#CH 251
[ZH] 之前临安的司理参军将朱晞颜巴结得甚好，一直想给他升职，苦于没有好的借口。仪王看上司理参军的活儿，朱晞颜再高兴不过，他可光明正大把人调走。
[ZH] 朱晞颜也仔细思量过，以后狱讼之事他一概不过问，仪王想怎么着都行，谁让现在的临安城仪王最大。贵人别没事挑他的毛病便万事大吉，他头上的乌纱帽也能戴得长久些。
[ZH] 白风驾着马车来到司理院，看到有一群人队列整齐，站在门口等待。他先跳下马车，将步梯放好：“王爷，到了。”
[ZH] 申简辰从马车里出来，踩着步梯走下。
[ZH] 那群人见状，上前行礼。
[ZH] 一身儒生装扮，外形文雅的中年汉子带头说：“我等在此恭候贵人多时，仪王能来，司理院蓬荜生辉。”
[ZH] 申简辰瞧瞧眼前之人：“你们是？”
[ZH] “见到贵人太过激动，竟然忘记介绍。”儒生装扮的中年汉子说，“小人是司理院的师爷，名唤韩谷；这位是温都头温信，后面全是司理院的差役。”
[ZH] 申简辰想起《水浒传》中的武松也曾担任过都头，不由对眼前的温都头产生兴趣：“不知温都头功夫如何？”
[ZH] 温都头回道：“回仪王，尚可。”
[ZH] 申简辰想试试对方的身手，待温信话音刚落，迅速打出一拳。温信反应敏捷，头向一边侧去，顺利躲开。
[EN] The previous Judicial Investigator of Lin'an had flattered Zhu Xiyan so well that the Prefect had long been itching to promote him, lacking only a clean excuse. Prince Yi taking a fancy to the post was a gift from heaven—Zhu Xiyan could transfer his protégé right out in the open. And he had already thought the rest through: from now on, he wouldn't poke his nose into a single criminal case. Prince Yi could run the courts however he liked—he was the biggest name in Lin'an now, after all. So long as the prince didn't go hunting for faults in him, everything was golden and his official's hat would sit safe on his head.
[ZH] 其他人见二人动起拳头，都向一旁闪开，将地方让给两位切磋之人。白风最喜看王爷与人打斗，在一旁津津有味地瞧着。
[EN] When Bai Feng drove the carriage up to the Judicial Office, a neat line of staff stood waiting outside the gates. Bai Feng hopped down and set out the footstool. "We're here, Your Highness."
[ZH] 很快温信由被动转为主动，以极快的速度挥出右拳。申简辰身体灵活地旋转，避开的同时使出一记勾拳打向温信的下巴，温信侧身躲避。两人你来我往，拳影交错，每一拳都蕴含着力量。最终申简辰使出一招连环拳，对方终于招架不住，连连后退。
[EN] Shen Jianchen stepped down from the carriage, and the crowd surged forward to bow.
[ZH] 再说温信也不敢赢贵人，于是拱手认输：“仪王拳法精妙，在下佩服佩服。”
[EN] A refined middle-aged man dressed like a scholar stepped ahead as spokesman. "We have been awaiting Your Highness's arrival. Your presence brings boundless luster to our humble Judicial Office!"
[ZH] 申简辰收起拳头：“温都头的拳法也不赖，看来能成为都头的人都不简单，不知温都头酒量如何？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen looked them over. "And you are?"
[ZH] 温信说：“不瞒仪王，在下的酒量至今未逢敌手。”
[EN] "Forgive me—so thrilled to meet Your Highness I forgot our introductions!" the scholar said. "I am Han Gu, clerk to the Judicial Office. Beside me is Chief Constable Wen Xin, and behind us are all the constables of the office."
[ZH] 申简辰说：“甚好！有时间你我斗斗酒，痛饮一回！”
[EN] Remembering Wu Song serving as a chief constable in *Water Margin*, Shen Jianchen eyed Wen Xin with sudden curiosity. "How are your martial arts, Chief Constable Wen?"
[ZH] 大家都豪爽地笑起来，气氛变得轻松许多。
[EN] "Passable, Your Highness," Wen Xin answered.
[ZH] 韩谷本以为来了位需供着的大神，却没想到如此亲民，拳法也厉害。
[EN] Wanting to test his mettle, Shen Jianchen threw a lightning punch the instant the words left Wen Xin's mouth. Wen Xin reacted in a blink, snapping his head aside to slip the blow.
[ZH] 总不能让贵人一直待在外面，韩谷赶快请仪王进入司理院堂中歇息，再将公务详情一一说明。
[EN] Everyone else scrambled back to clear a ring for the spar, while Bai Feng—who loved nothing more than watching his master trade blows—folded his arms to enjoy the show.
[ZH] 白风叫两名差役帮忙搬东西，住处早已安排好，温信带他们直接朝府衙后面拐去。
[EN] Wen Xin flipped from defense to offense, whipping a blistering right fist forward. Shen Jianchen spun inside the reach, ducked clear, and fired an uppercut toward Wen Xin's jaw; Wen Xin twisted clear in turn. Fists blurred back and forth, every strike packed with solid force, until Shen Jianchen unleashed a rapid chain of punches that finally forced Wen Xin staggering backward.
[ZH] 后面有一院子，主要供司理参军居住。挨着院子的东面是都头和师爷的住处，西边则是差役们群居之处。
[EN] Not that Wen Xin would have dared beat a prince anyway. He cupped his hands in concession. "Your Highness's boxing is extraordinary! I concede in total admiration."
[ZH] 司理院的人亦可选择回家住，只需每日按时前来点卯，尽心尽力办好差事即可。不过一旦有案情，往往没日没夜，大家渐渐形成大部分时间住在司理院的习惯。
[EN] Shen Jianchen lowered his fists. "Your own fists aren't half bad either, Chief Constable. No wonder you made chief constable. How well do you hold your liquor?"
[ZH] 首要需解决吃饭的问题，司理院也安置有后厨，安排专人负责采买做饭，做出来的饭食自然比不上幽林别居厨子的手艺，更无法与临安各大酒楼的名厨相比较，好在家常饭的味道较浓厚，吃到肚内也舒服。
[EN] "If Your Highness will forgive my boasting, I've yet to meet my match at the wine table."
[ZH] 申简辰在堂内坐好。
[EN] "Splendid! When we get a spare evening, you and I will drink till we drop!"
[ZH] 师爷韩谷不敢坐下，站着说道：“仪王殿下替官家体察民情，我等甚为感动。昨日知府大人派人前来叮嘱，往后临安刑狱诉讼之事不必上报，一切由仪王您裁夺。司理院从未有过此等殊荣，都是托殿下的福。”
[EN] Hearty laughter rippled through the courtyard, melting the stiffness in a heartbeat. Han Gu had braced himself for a haughty royal deity who had to be waited on hand and foot; instead, here was a down-to-earth prince with wicked fists.
[ZH] 对方一阵夸赞，搞得申简辰甚是不自在：“韩师爷还是坐下给我说说司理参军日常的事务。”
[EN] Mindful not to keep the prince standing outside, Han Gu hurried to invite him into the main hall for a rest and a briefing on official duties. Bai Feng enlisted two constables to haul in the trunks, and Wen Xin showed them straight to the rear quarters already prepared for the prince.
[ZH] 韩谷依然不敢坐下，生平第一次见身份如此高贵之人，总担心哪里做得不对：“小的还是站着回话心里舒坦些。”
[EN] Behind the court hall lay a private courtyard reserved for the Judicial Investigator. Immediately to its east were the quarters for the chief constable and clerk, and to the west the barracks for the constables. Staff were free to sleep at home so long as they made morning roll call and did their jobs, though active cases often meant working day and night, so most ended up living right at the Judicial Office. A back kitchen with a dedicated buyer and cook handled meals—nothing like the chef at Youlin Retreat or Lin'an's grand taverns, of course, yet the hearty home-style cooking sat warm and satisfying in the belly.
[ZH] 申简辰不再强求，认真听韩师爷说些什么。
[EN] Shen Jianchen took his seat in the main hall. Clerk Han dared not sit, staying on his feet as he spoke. "We are deeply moved that Your Highness would step down to look into the people's hardships on His Majesty's behalf. Yesterday the Prefect sent word that from now on, criminal cases and lawsuits in Lin'an need not be referred upward—everything rests solely on Your Highness's judgment. The Judicial Office has never known such an honor, all thanks to Your Highness."
[ZH] “参军平日要忙的事不少。首先需审理临安牢狱里的犯人，将陈年冤案理一理，各类卷宗殿下每日必然要抽时间阅览；其次新的案子也需受理，老百姓时常会有人前来报案，司理院只有受理的份儿，一旦接下便需帮百姓排忧解难。若受理的案件大些，面上还有光，可有时老百姓邻里、夫妻之间闹些小矛盾也会来报官，直接拉低我们的档次，不受理还不行。”
[EN] The deluge of flattery made Shen Jianchen squirm. "Sit down, Clerk Han, and walk me through the Judicial Investigator's day-to-day work."
[ZH] 申简辰笑道：“老百姓将朝廷官员称为‘父母官’，不就是希望官员为百姓做主？百姓之事无小事，哪怕夫妻、邻里之间的纠纷依然是大事。”
[EN] Han Gu stayed rooted on his feet—his first time ever dealing with someone of such lofty blood, and he was terrified of putting a foot wrong. "I feel far more at ease speaking on my feet, Your Highness."
[ZH] “仪王教训得对，小的谨记在心。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen stopped pressing and leaned in to listen.
[ZH] “还有呢？”
[EN] "An Investigator has plenty on his plate," Han Gu began. "First, there are the prisoners already in Lin'an's jail—old unresolved cases and appeals need sorting through, so Your Highness will have to set aside time each day to read through the dossiers. Second, new complaints arrive constantly. Whenever commoners come to file a case, the Judicial Office has no choice but to accept it and resolve their grievances. Big cases at least bring prestige, though half the time neighbors or husbands and wives show up squabbling over petty nonsense—drags down the dignity of the court, yet we still can't turn them away."
[ZH] “参军还需时常到百姓中走动，替朝廷和临安官员做些慈善。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen chuckled. "The common folk call officials 'father-and-mother officials' because they expect us to stand up for them. Nothing that weighs on the people is petty—even domestic or neighborly disputes matter."
[ZH] 申简辰心想，昏君和腐 败的官员竟然也会做慈善？他的历史知识有限，穿越前的历史课上老师也并未提及。
[EN] "Your Highness speaks true wisdom. I will take it to heart."
[ZH] “慈善？是官家允许的？”
[EN] "What else?"
[ZH] 韩谷诧异，眼前之人不是官家的儿子吗？怎么连官家重视慈善之事都不知？又不敢多问，只得费些口舌解释一番。
[EN] "The Investigator must also go out among the people now and then to oversee charity work on behalf of the court and Lin'an's officials."
[ZH] “崇宁年间官家便下旨命各地建居养院、安济坊、漏泽园，目的在于让鳏寡孤独者，有居养之法；疾而无医者，有安济之所；贫而不葬者，有安葬之地。官家重视慈善，一直施行至今，各地官员无不尽心竭力，因为官家将此事作为考核官员升迁的重要依据。”
[EN] Charity? Shen Jianchen blinked. That incompetent emperor and his corrupt mandarins actually ran public charities? His history classes back in the modern world had never mentioned that. "Charity? Ordered by His Majesty?"
[ZH] 申简辰心想：“昏君能有这么好？”紧接着问：“如此重大之事为何知府大人不亲力亲为，反而交给小小的司理参军？”
[EN] Han Gu looked startled—wasn't this man the emperor's own son? How could he not know about His Majesty's prized welfare system? Still, he dared not question him and launched into a patient explanation.
[ZH] 韩谷犹豫着，不敢开口。
[EN] "Back during the Chongning reign, His Majesty decreed that every prefecture establish three institutions: Juyang Yuan—poorhouses to shelter the widowed, orphaned, and destitute; Anji Fang—charity infirmaries for the sick who cannot afford doctors; and Louze Yuan—pauper cemeteries for the poor who cannot afford burial. His Majesty puts great store by these charities and has kept them going ever since. Local officials bend over backward over them because His Majesty uses them as a key yardstick for promotions."
[ZH] “放心大胆讲，本王既不会治你的罪，也不会讲出去，就当听听他人的故事。”
[EN] *Could that useless emperor actually be that decent?* Shen Jianchen wondered, before asking aloud, "If it's so important, why isn't the Prefect handling it himself instead of dumping it on a lowly Judicial Investigator?"
[ZH] 仪王如此说，韩谷轻松一些，再说面前的人虽是仪王，又是节度使，但也是司理参军，他有义务将实情告知，否则治他一个知情不报之罪，也担待不起。
[EN] Han Gu hesitated, lips pressed shut.
[ZH] “按说各地的居养院、安济坊、漏泽园均是知州或知府亲自管辖，可临安的知府大人政绩已捞够，这等烫手的山芋还是赶快丢给别人好，您之前的司理参军之所以能被知府大人赏识，就是因为他将烫手的山芋处理得好。”
[EN] "Speak freely," Shen Jianchen assured him. "I won't punish you, and not a word leaves this room. Pretend you're just telling me a story."
[ZH] “烫手的山芋？怎么个说法？”
[EN] That loosened Han Gu's shoulders. Besides, the man in front of him wasn't just Prince Yi and Military Commissioner—he was now the Judicial Investigator himself. Keeping the truth from him could get Han Gu slapped with concealing official facts.
[ZH] “一个居养院就需许多银子来养，何况再加上安济坊和漏泽园？朝廷每年拨下来的银两由上到下经过各人之手，到了地方所剩无几，总不能让知府大人自己掏腰包？他刚担任知府那会儿位置还未坐稳，除了需打通人脉，还需多多捞政绩，保住头上的乌纱帽，必然亲力亲为。殿下，您猜猜他用的银子从哪里来？”
[EN] "By rights, the Juyang Yuan, Anji Fang, and Louze Yuan fall under the Prefect's direct charge," Han Gu admitted. "Trouble is, Prefect Zhu has already racked up all the merit he needs, so he couldn't wait to offload such a hot potato. Your predecessor won the Prefect's favor precisely because he knew how to juggle that hot potato so neatly."
[ZH] “除了朝廷分派下来所剩无几的部分，难道大部分让临安的官员一起承担？”
[EN] "A hot potato? Explain."
[ZH] “殿下只猜对一半，一大半钱财由陈家承担，朱陈两家联姻的背后都是利益捆绑，陈家有钱帮知府大人稳住乌纱帽，知府大人利用职权帮陈家稳固临安首富的地位，因此临安一大半的银子都被陈家赚了。”
[EN] "Running a single Juyang Yuan burns through heaps of silver, let alone an Anji Fang and a Louze Yuan on top of it. The annual welfare funds the court sends down pass through layer after layer of sticky fingers; by the time anything reaches the prefecture, there's barely a scrap left. You don't expect the Prefect to dip into his own pocket, do you? Back when he first took office and still needed to secure his seat, build connections, and pile up political merit to keep his hat, he did run them hands-on. Care to guess where the silver came from, Your Highness?"
[ZH] 提到朱陈两家联姻，申简辰自然想到千里寻，难道她也无形中成为朱陈两家利益捆绑的纽带？还未听出“烫手的山芋”，申简辰无心多想，继续问：“这跟烫手的山芋有什么关系？”
[EN] "Aside from whatever crumbs trickled down from the court, did Lin'an's officials chip in together?"
[ZH] “殿下别急，听我慢慢道来。知府大人靠陈家和依附他的官员捞够政绩，也稳住在临安的地位，也许是看开，或是累了，不想再往上爬，没必要继续捞政绩，干脆捞钱，上面拨下来的善款层层盘剥，到达临安没剩多少，但也不少，最后一个子儿也到不了居养院、安济坊和漏泽园。他便把这三处交给司理参军管理，意思再明显不过。”
[EN] "Half right, Your Highness. More than half the money came straight out of the Chen family's pockets. The marriage alliance between the Zhus and the Chens is all about mutual gain—the Chens have the silver to keep Prefect Zhu's hat firm on his head, and Prefect Zhu uses his office to keep the Chens perched atop Lin'an as its richest clan. That's why half the silver in Lin'an flows into Chen coffers."
[ZH] “什么意思？”
[EN] Mention of the Zhu-Chen alliance sent Shen Jianchen's thoughts straight back to Qianli Xun. Had she unwittingly become just another link chaining the two families' interests together? Pushing the ache down, he asked, "And how does that tie into the hot potato?"
[ZH] “就是别当回事。”
[EN] "Bear with me, Your Highness. Once Prefect Zhu piled up enough merit through the Chens and his clique and locked down his grip on Lin'an, he either saw through the game or grew tired of climbing. With no urge to seek higher office, he stopped chasing merit and started chasing silver instead. Whatever welfare money survives the journey down from the capital still adds up to a tidy sum—and not a single copper of it reaches the poorhouses, infirmaries, or cemeteries anymore. Handing all three over to the Judicial Investigator was his way of sending a clear message."
[ZH] “难道朝廷不会派人来查？”
[EN] "Which is?"
[ZH] “唉，查有什么用？听说知府大人朝中有人，无人敢查，即使有人来查，也不怕。”
[EN] "Don't bother taking them seriously."
[ZH] “陈家难道也不再出善款？”
[EN] "Doesn't the court send inspectors to check?"
[ZH] “这陈家倒也不错，善款继续出，只是没有以前那么多。知府大人都不管，陈家也没必要多费心。其实，要我说不管也好。”
[EN] "Ha—what good are inspections? Word is Prefect Zhu has powerful backers at court. Nobody dares audit him, and even if someone did, he wouldn't break a sweat."
[ZH] “为何？”
[EN] "So the Chen family stopped donating too?"
[ZH] “老百姓中流行着一个谚语：不养健儿，却养乞儿；不理好人，却理病人；不管活人，只管死尸。一针见血，指出官家设立居养院、安济坊、漏泽园的弊端。”
[EN] "To give the Chens their due, they still pitch in—just nowhere near as much as before. If the Prefect doesn't care, why should they work themselves ragged? Truth be told, Your Highness, leaving them alone might be for the best."
[ZH] 申简辰听后，颇为感慨，昏君做个好事都会成为坏事，怪只怪这个时代如同根部腐朽的大树，土里的养分已无法通过根部传遍整个树干。
[EN] "Why?"
[ZH] “如此看来，这三个地方对知府大人来讲的确是烫手的山芋，不想管又不得不管，不愿花钱又不得不花钱，花了反而为社会滋生一大批蛀虫，不花又担心朝廷知道。你刚说之前的司理参军将烫手的山芋处理得好，不妨向本王说说他是如何做的？”
[EN] "There's a saying going around among the common folk: *Instead of feeding able soldiers, they feed beggars; instead of minding healthy folk, they mind the sick; instead of caring for the living, they care for corpses.* Hits the nail right on the head about how rotten His Majesty's Juyang Yuan, Anji Fang, and Louze Yuan have become."
[ZH] “朝廷的善款不可能拨到司理院，参军每个月的俸禄也不多，更不可能自己掏腰包，司理院是个穷衙门，银子都不够自己用的，哪管得了那三处？还好有陈家。之前的司理参军想到一个好办法，将原来的居养院、安济坊和漏泽园里的人全部赶出去，该怎么样就怎样。最多由陈家出些银子设个粥棚和医病的棚子，那些个被救济之人只要有口粥吃，有个医病的人就行，死了也有个乱坟岗埋即可。上面一旦差人来查，知府大人会提前得到消息，以前的参军才会临时将那三个地方重新布置，再让人住进去，当然银子也是由陈家出。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen let out a heavy breath. Even when that inept emperor tried to do something good, it curdled into rot—because the whole era was a massive tree rotten to its roots, unable to carry a drop of nourishment up the trunk.
[ZH] “难道老百姓不会乱说？”
[EN] "No wonder those three places turned into a hot potato for the Prefect," he murmured. "Doesn't want to manage them, yet can't drop them outright; hates spending money on them, yet spends it only to breed a swarm of parasites—and stops spending only to risk the court finding out. You said my predecessor handled the hot potato well. How did he do it?"
[ZH] “老百姓哪敢？司理院的杀威棒厉害着呢。”
[EN] "None of the imperial funds ever make it to the Judicial Office," Han Gu said, "and the Investigator's own salary is meager—no way he could pay out of pocket. Our yamen is dirt-poor; we barely scrape by on our own expenses, let alone run three charities. Thank heaven for the Chens. The previous Investigator hit on a neat trick: he evicted everyone from the Juyang Yuan, Anji Fang, and Louze Yuan and let them fend for themselves. At most, the Chens funded a plain congee shed and a makeshift sick-tent—just enough gruel to keep the destitute breathing, a doctor to hand out herbs, and a potter's field to dump the dead. Whenever word leaked ahead of time that an imperial inspector was coming, the old Investigator would scramble to refurbish all three buildings and herd people back inside for show—on the Chen family's dime, naturally."
[ZH] 韩谷一番介绍，申简辰算是了解到一些关于临安官府的情况。他来担任司理参军一职，难道朱晞颜不怕他的腐败之举被发现？
[EN] "And the townsfolk don't blab?"
[ZH] 申简辰仔细想想，也就明白，朱晞颜有什么好怕的？昏君的政策看似爱民，实则漏洞百出，他申简辰即使以仪王的身份诟病政策不好，也怪不到朱晞颜的头上，最终只能与官家对着干。
[EN] "Who'd dare? The beating rods of the Judicial Office don't joke around."
[ZH] 哪怕不诟病政策，只查贪污一事，办了朱晞颜，再顺藤摸瓜，摸出来的也不知会有多少个腐烂的大瓜，也许最后一个还得摸到官家的头上。
[EN] Han Gu's briefing pulled the curtain right back on Lin'an's officialdom. And why hadn't Zhu Xiyan worried that Prince Yi might expose his corruption? Thinking it over, Shen Jianchen saw the answer plain as day. What did Zhu Xiyan have to fear? The emperor's own welfare decree looked benevolent on paper yet leaked like a sieve in practice. Even if Shen Jianchen criticized the system as Prince Yi, none of the mud would stick to Zhu Xiyan—it would only pit Shen Jianchen against His Majesty. And if he bypassed policy and went straight after embezzlement, pulling the thread on Zhu Xiyan would unravel an endless vine of rotten gourds stretching all the way to the Dragon Throne itself.
[ZH] 申简辰不是怕事，是不想在毫无意义的事情上浪费时间，他决定既然已接下司理参军一职，不如在老百姓中做些更有意义的事。
[EN] Shen Jianchen wasn't afraid of trouble; he just refused to burn his energy on a lost cause. Since he had taken the Investigator's chair, he would rather do real, tangible good down among ordinary people.
[ZH] 正想着，温信进来报告：“仪王，竹里村的村长前来报案。”
[EN] Just as that thought settled, Wen Xin stepped in to report. "Your Highness, the headman of Zhuli Village is here to report a crime."
[ZH] 听到有案情，申简辰兴致倍增，在桌案前坐正：“把人叫进来。”
[EN] A real case! Shen Jianchen's eyes brightened as he squared his shoulders behind the desk. "Bring him in."
[ZH] 韩谷赶紧坐在师爷的位置，准备记录案情。
[EN] Han Gu hurried to the clerk's desk and picked up his brush.
[ZH] 温信到门口向外喊道：“进来吧，参军有话问你。”
[EN] Wen Xin called out through the doorway, "Come on in—the Investigator wants to question you."
[ZH] 村长小心走进堂内，原来是千里寻住过的竹林中的那位村长，他曾经来过司理院，也见过以前参军。新参军看着面生，他不敢多问，一走进跪在地上：“参军在上，小的是竹里村的村长，一大早去忙，结果在离村子不远的溪水旁发现一具女尸，便赶过来报案。”
[EN] The village headman stepped in with wary steps—the very same headman from the bamboo grove where Qianli Xun had once stayed. He had visited the Judicial Office before and knew the old Investigator; seeing a young stranger behind the desk, he kept his curiosity to himself and dropped straight to his knees. "Your Honor, I am the headman of Zhuli Village. I went out to work first thing this morning and found a woman's corpse beside the stream not far from the village, so I rushed straight here to report it."
[ZH] 申简辰虽没做过断案之事，但这方面的剧追过不少，经验还是有一些：“可破坏现场？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen had never tried a real murder case, though he had binge-watched enough detective dramas in his old life to know the basics. "Has the crime scene been disturbed?"
[ZH] 村长回道：“毕竟是死了人，大家都不敢靠近，现场自然完好。”
[EN] "A dead body's bad luck, Your Honor—nobody dared go near it. The scene's untouched."
[ZH] 申简辰即刻下令：“韩师爷、温都头随我去现场，记得带上仵作。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen barked his orders at once. "Clerk Han, Chief Constable Wen, come with me to the scene—and bring the coroner!"

#CH 252
[ZH] 千里寻陪完夏老太太和姚轻琰，回墨香苑的途中，守护陈府南门口的一名下人迎面走来。
[ZH] “二少夫人，外面有人前来送请帖，让您亲自去接。”
[ZH] 千里寻纳闷，往常的请帖都是交由陈府下人送进来即可，谁摆这么大谱？竟然让主人家亲自前去？
[ZH] “可知送帖子的人是谁？”
[ZH] “是个丫鬟，名叫喜兰。”
[ZH] “喜兰。”
[ZH] 千里寻重复着喜兰的名字，一定是梦容让她前来，难道梦容已原谅她？她顾不上多问，快速向南门而去。
[ZH] 到达南门外时，喜兰的的确确站在那里。
[ZH] “梦容……可好？”
[ZH] 喜兰简单行礼：“多谢幽栖居士挂念，仪王妃甚好。明日王妃要在幽林别居举办诗会，只请些闺中密友参加，王妃甚是看重幽栖居士，派我亲自送上请帖，还请幽栖居士届时光临。”
[ZH] 喜兰将请帖递上，苦荞上前接过。
[ZH] 千里寻品味着喜兰刚才的话，梦容只邀请闺中密友参加，说明她依然把她当朋友看待。
[ZH] “麻烦转告梦容，明日我定按时赴约。”
[ZH] 喜兰提醒道：“幽栖居士今后可别再唤王妃的闺名，她现在是仪王妃，比起以前来身份更加尊贵。”
[ZH] 能被梦容邀请，千里寻喜出望外，根本没有多想喜兰的提醒，仅仅认为她不过是好心嘱咐一下而已，凭自己与梦容以前的关系，不过是个称谓，又何足挂齿？
[ZH] “感谢提醒，以后我会注意。”
[ZH] 喜兰走后，千里寻从苦荞手中接过请帖，打开认真读着上面的字。
[ZH] 她在幽林别居时，曾瞧过一眼梦容写的字，娟秀轻柔，跟眼前的字迹一模一样。梦容实在有心，亲自提笔为她写下请帖，不是原谅又能是什么？
[ZH] 千里寻舍不得让苦荞帮忙拿请帖，一直小心捏在手掌中往回走，一路上思忖着，如果见到申简辰该如何表现才不会伤害梦容。
[EN] After keeping Old Madam Xia and Yao Qingyan company, Qianli Xun was strolling back toward Moxiang Courtyard when a gatekeeper from Chen Manor's south gate hurried up to meet her.
[ZH] 爱情已不可能得到，千里寻更加重视她与梦容之间的友情，希望明日的诗会成为她们友情重新开始的契机。
[EN] "Second Young Madam, someone is outside delivering an invitation card and insists you receive it in person."
[ZH] ……
[EN] Qianli Xun raised a brow. Invitations were always passed inside through the manor servants—who was putting on such airs as to make the mistress walk out to the gate herself? "Who's the messenger?"
[ZH] 话说申简辰带领司理院的人来到竹林的溪水旁，许多竹民站在不远处观看唏嘘，却无一人敢靠近。大家见官府来人，更是远远躲开。
[EN] "A maidservant. Calls herself Xilan."
[ZH] 仵作先将女尸查探一番，检查完后向参军汇报：“大人，死者是女子，看上去大约四十岁左右，因匕首插入心脏而致命。”
[EN] "Xilan." Qianli Xun echoed the name under her breath. It had to be Mengrong sending her—could Mengrong have forgiven her? Without asking another question, she hurried straight toward the south gate.
[ZH] 不等申简辰吩咐，温信已带人在四周寻找凶器。
[EN] Sure enough, Xilan stood waiting outside.
[ZH] 仵作接着说：“不过还需查验女尸的下 体，我不甚方便……”
[EN] "Mengrong... how is she?" Qianli Xun asked.
[ZH] 话未说完，传来一个妇人的声音：“我来！我来！”
[EN] Xilan offered a perfunctory curtsy. "Thank you for your concern, Youqi Jushi. Princess Consort Yi is quite well. Tomorrow Her Highness is hosting a poetry salon at Youlin Retreat for her closest female friends only. Out of high regard for Youqi Jushi, she sent me to deliver this invitation in person and hopes you will honor her with your presence."
[ZH] 申简辰循声瞧去，原来是位五十多岁的妇人，她顾不上行礼，将怀里的粗布展开，向旁边的差役说：“麻烦差役大哥帮个忙，扯扯布子。”
[EN] Xilan held out the card, and Kuqiao stepped forward to accept it.
[ZH] 两名差役轻车熟路，将布子扯开，挡住大家的视线，他们则将头扭向一边，省得看到不该看的。
[EN] Turning Xilan's words over in her mind, Qianli Xun felt a warm surge of hope—inviting only her closest friends meant Mengrong still counted her as one! "Please tell Mengrong I'll be there right on time tomorrow."
[ZH] 妇人来到粗布后面，开始在女尸的下 体检查。
[EN] "Youqi Jushi would do well not to call Her Highness by her given name anymore," Xilan reminded her. "She is Princess Consort Yi now—her rank stands far higher than before."
[ZH] 申简辰问：“韩师爷，这位是？”
[EN] Overjoyed just to be invited, Qianli Xun read nothing sinister into the reminder, taking it as a well-meaning nudge about etiquette. Between old friends like her and Mengrong, what was a title? "Thanks for the reminder. I'll be more careful from now on."
[ZH] 韩谷回道：“她是九婆，干的是坐婆和稳婆的营生，一般遇到女尸，也会请她前来。她和这位仵作都是咱们司理院的常客。”
[EN] Once Xilan departed, Qianli Xun took the invitation from Kuqiao, opened it, and read every stroke with care. She had glimpsed Mengrong's handwriting once before at Youlin Retreat—delicate, graceful, unmistakable, identical to the calligraphy in her hands. Mengrong had even written her card with her own brush! What else could that mean besides forgiveness?
[ZH] 温信走过来禀报：“仪王，周围没有发现凶器。”
[EN] Reluctant to hand the card back to Kuqiao, she kept it cradled in her palm all the way home, mulling over how to act if she ran into Shen Jianchen so she wouldn't hurt Mengrong again. Love was already beyond her reach; that only made her friendship with Mengrong twice as precious. With any luck, tomorrow's salon would mend the rift and give them a fresh start.
[ZH] 申简辰点点头，朝村长招招手。
[EN] Meanwhile, Shen Jianchen led his men from the Judicial Office down to the stream beside the bamboo grove. A cluster of villagers stood watching and murmuring from a distance, not one daring to edge closer—and at the sight of yamen runners, they backed away even farther.
[ZH] 村长小心过来：“参军有何吩咐？”
[EN] The coroner knelt to examine the corpse first, then rose to report. "Your Honor, the deceased is a woman of about forty. Cause of death is a dagger thrust straight into the heart."
[ZH] 申简辰问：“这具女尸你应该在报案前见过，不知她可否是你们村的？”
[EN] Without waiting for orders, Wen Xin already had constables fanning out to sweep the area for the murder weapon.
[ZH] 村长说：“竹里村的人我都熟识，死的那人看着面生，肯定不是本村人。”
[EN] "There is one more check," the coroner added, clearing his throat. "Her lower body needs to be examined, and as a man, it isn't proper for me to..."
[ZH] 九婆已查完女尸的下体，收了粗布，前来汇报：“参军大人，女子临死时并未与人发生关系，由此可推断不是情杀。”
[EN] Before he could finish, a woman's crisp voice rang out. "I'll do it! I'll do it!"
[ZH] 申简辰笑了笑：“由此推断不是情杀太过轻率。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen turned to see a woman in her fifties striding up. Skipping any bow, she shook out a bolt of coarse cloth from under her arm and called to the nearest constables, "Lend a hand here, boys—hold this screen up!"
[ZH] 接着命令道：“温都头，你派人将尸体先抬回司理院的停尸房，再让仵作认真验尸，也许会有新的发现。韩师爷，你查查近期有没哪家上报过家中有人走失之事。”
[EN] Two constables, clearly old hands at this, stretched the cloth wide to block everyone's view and turned their heads aside so they wouldn't peek. The woman stepped behind the screen to check the lower half of the body.
[ZH] 温信和韩谷齐声回道：“是！”
[EN] "Who is she, Clerk Han?" Shen Jianchen asked.
[ZH] 溪水两旁的竹林，让申简辰想起刚到临安时与千里寻不期而遇的情景，那时并不知幽栖居士就是千里寻，两人没说一句话便擦肩而过。此时想起竟也生起美好的情愫来。
[EN] "That's Granny Jiu, Your Honor. She works as a midwife and female examiner—whenever we turn up a female corpse, we call her in. Both she and the coroner are regulars at our yamen."
[ZH] 申简辰吩咐其他人先回司理院，他想到竹林走走。
[EN] Wen Xin strode back to report. "Your Highness, no weapon turned up anywhere around here."
[ZH] 申简辰沿着溪流找到第一次见千里寻时的那条小径，盯着千里寻坐过的石头看一会儿，想一会儿，然后顺着小径往村子里走去。
[EN] Shen Jianchen nodded and beckoned to the village headman, who hurried over. "What can I do for Your Honor?"
[ZH] 又从村子里走到村口，站在那里回想两人互相点头回应的瞬间……
[EN] "You saw the body before reporting it. Is she from your village?"
[ZH] 白风懒得理会王爷的心情，竹林和竹里村的风景不错，上次匆匆来，没好好欣赏，不如趁此机会一饱眼福。
[EN] "I know every soul in Zhuli Village, Your Honor. Never laid eyes on her before—she's definitely not one of ours."
[ZH] 正欣赏着，无意中瞧见一个身影躲在竹子的后面。白风使出轻功迅速抓住那人，把他从竹子后面拽出来一瞧，原来是他曾经抓过的苦蕨，苦荞的弟弟，手不由松开。
[EN] Granny Jiu finished her check, folded up her cloth, and stepped forward to report. "Your Honor, the woman had no relations with any man before she died. We can rule out a crime of passion."
[ZH] “你为何躲在竹子后面鬼鬼祟祟？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen gave a faint smile. "Ruling out a crime of passion from that alone is jumping the gun." Turning back to his officers, he issued his orders. "Chief Constable Wen, have the body carried back to the morgue at the Judicial Office and tell the coroner to perform a thorough autopsy—he may spot something we missed out here. Clerk Han, check whether any household has reported a missing person lately."
[ZH] 苦蕨还不知道姐姐与白风的关系，小心说：“原来那位是参军大人，你该不会也是当官的吧？”
[EN] "Yes, Your Honor!" Wen Xin and Han Gu answered together.
[ZH] “我叫白风，难道没听你姐姐提起过我？”
[EN] Gazing at the bamboo flanking the stream, Shen Jianchen remembered his very first chance encounter with Qianli Xun right after arriving in Lin'an—back when he didn't even know Youqi Jushi was her, and they had brushed past each other without a single word. Remembering it now stirred a tender ache in his chest.
[ZH] 苦蕨摇摇头：“从来没听我姐说起过你，你认识我姐？”
[EN] Sending the rest of the team back to the Judicial Office ahead of him, he stayed behind for a walk through the grove. He followed the stream until he found the narrow path where he had first laid eyes on her, stood gazing at the boulder she had sat on, and drifted down the lane into Zhuli Village until he reached the village entrance—lingering right where they had exchanged that quiet nod.
[ZH] 白风倍感失落，苦荞竟然从未向她的家人提起过他，于是强调道：“你给我记好了，以后我就是你的姐夫，见到我必须叫姐夫。”
[EN] Bai Feng had no patience for his master's lovelorn brooding. The bamboo grove and village were gorgeous, and he had rushed through last time without taking in the sights; might as well feast his eyes now. As he scanned the towering green stalks, a figure ducked behind a cluster of bamboo.
[ZH] 苦蕨“嘿嘿”笑道：“没想到我有这么厉害的姐夫，我爹娘要是知道肯定高兴。”
[EN] In a flash of light-foot technique, Bai Feng swooped over, seized the lurker, and dragged him out—only to blink and loosen his grip. It was Kujue, Kuqiao's younger brother, whom he had collared once before.
[ZH] 被苦蕨认可，白风不再失落，继续问：“你还没回答我为何鬼鬼祟祟躲在竹子后面？”
[EN] "What are you sneaking around behind the bamboo for?"
[ZH] 苦蕨这才说：“我有要事与参军大人说，是关于死的那人。”
[EN] Unaware of his sister's romance with Bai Feng, Kujue eyed him with caution. "So that gentleman is the Investigator... don't tell me you're an official too?"
[ZH] 与案情有关，白风不敢怠慢：“跟我过去把你知道的一五一十向王爷说明。”
[EN] "I'm Bai Feng. Hasn't your sister ever mentioned me?"
[ZH] “王爷？”苦蕨露出惊讶的表情，“竟然会有王爷来竹里村？王爷身边的人是我姐夫？我是不是做梦？”
[EN] Kujue shook his head. "Never heard my sis say a word about you. You know my sis?"
[ZH] 白风又体一会儿优越的感觉，才带着苦蕨来到仪王身边：“王爷，关于那具女尸，苦蕨应该知道些什么。”
[EN] A stab of hurt hit Bai Feng—Kuqiao had never once mentioned him to her family! He puffed out his chest. "Remember this good and clear: from now on I'm your brother-in-law. Whenever you see me, you call me Brother-in-law."
[ZH] 申简辰不能再继续回忆，转过身，面前站着一位憨憨的小伙子：“你叫苦蕨？苦荞的弟弟？”
[EN] Kujue broke into a goofy grin. "Hehe! Never dreamed I'd land a brother-in-law this impressive! Wait till Ma and Pa hear—they'll be over the moon!"
[ZH] 苦蕨憨憨一笑，没想到王爷竟然也知道自己的姐姐，姐姐在城里混得好，家人面上也有光，他犹豫要不要也像白风一样称呼眼前的贵人为“王爷”，可这两个字怎么都吐不出口，半天才说：“回参军大人，我是苦荞的弟弟苦蕨，那女的是被人杀死的，用车拉过来，扔在溪水旁。”
[EN] Validated, Bai Feng let his sulk vanish. "You still haven't told me why you were skulking behind the bamboo."
[ZH] 刚担任司理参军一职就碰到大案，在溪水旁时，申简辰还在揪心案子怎么个破法。当放下案子，思念心中之人时，就碰到目击者，他顽固地认为是千里寻给他带来的幸运。
[EN] "I've got something important to tell the Investigator—about the dead woman."
[ZH] “把你看到的从头到尾详细说一遍。”
[EN] A witness! Bai Feng snapped straight to business. "Come with me and tell His Highness every single detail."
[ZH] 苦蕨邀请道：“大人可否到我家坐坐？我再慢慢给您讲。”
[EN] "His Highness?" Kujue's jaw dropped. "A real prince came to Zhuli Village? And the prince's right-hand man is my brother-in-law? Am I dreaming?"
[ZH] 申简辰也觉村口不合适，时不时有村民经过：“好，就去你的竹屋坐坐。”
[EN] Basking in a few seconds of sheer glory, Bai Feng marched Kujue over to Shen Jianchen. "Your Highness, Kujue here knows something about that corpse."
[ZH] 苦蕨已成亲，搬进新的竹屋居住，正是千里寻在竹林时住过的竹屋。里面已重新布置，没有了质朴中尽显雅致的美感，反而充满乡间生活的气息。
[EN] Pulled out of his reverie, Shen Jianchen turned to face the earnest young villager. "You're Kujue? Kuqiao's brother?"
[ZH] 苦蕨带着贵人走了不多会儿，来到竹屋前，竹屋的院子里放着一张用竹子打制的矮桌，四周放着四个竹凳。
[EN] Kujue grinned bashfully, tickled pink that even a prince knew his sister—she must be doing mighty well in the city to bring the family such face. He wondered if he ought to call the noble gentleman "Your Highness" like Bai Feng did, yet the words stuck in his throat. At last he blurted, "Reporting to the Investigator—yes, I'm Kuqiao's brother Kujue. That woman was murdered, hauled out here in a carriage, and dumped by the stream."
[ZH] “大人，请坐。”
[EN] Landing a murder case on his very first day as Investigator had left Shen Jianchen racking his brains back by the creek over how to crack it. Yet the moment he paused to walk in Qianli Xun's footsteps and think of her, an eyewitness fell straight into his lap. Stubbornly, he decided Qianli Xun had brought him this luck.
[ZH] 申简辰挑了一个竹凳坐下，抬头满眼是茂密的竹林，为了解案情，他顾不上欣赏：“你与白风都坐下，将你看到的详细道来。”
[EN] "Tell me everything you saw, from start to finish."
[ZH] 苦蕨见白风毫不客气把一个竹凳拉到一边坐下，便也大胆坐下，边回忆边说：“我娘想吃鲤鱼，子时抓鲤鱼最合适。竹林外有个池塘，里面有鲤鱼，我便趁天黑出去抓……”
[EN] "Would Your Honor care to sit at my house? I can tell you the whole story there."
[ZH] 子时，苦蕨成功从池塘里抓好鲤鱼装在竹筐里往村子走，结果听到一阵马车的声音，心想，这个点儿怎么还有人赶马车出去？借着月色仔细一瞧，原来是从外面回来的，便没当回事，继续朝村口走。
[EN] Glancing at the villagers passing the entrance now and then, Shen Jianchen agreed. "Lead the way to your bamboo house."
[ZH] 走着走着，听不到马车的声音，转身看去，车已停下。苦蕨很纳闷，既然是回村的，为何不继续往村口走？因好奇，躲在一旁偷看。
[EN] Since marrying, Kujue had moved into a newer bamboo cottage—the exact one Qianli Xun had stayed in during her retreat here. Re-furnished for newlyweds, its spare, poetic elegance had given way to cozy country clutter. Kujue led them into the yard, where a low bamboo table sat surrounded by four bamboo stools. "Please sit, Your Honor."
[ZH] 夜色中一个男子从马车里拽出一个东西来，当他把那东西扛在肩膀上时，苦蕨才看清楚是一个大袋子，里面装的是什么，他一时猜不到。
[EN] Shen Jianchen chose a stool. Looking up, lush bamboo filled the sky, though he set sightseeing aside for the case. "Both of you sit down, too. Now tell me everything."
[ZH] 此人鬼鬼祟祟，引起苦蕨极大的好奇心，便悄悄尾随来到溪水旁，躲在一个石头后面偷看。
[EN] Seeing Bai Feng pull up a stool without ceremony, Kujue sat down and gathered his memories. "My mother had a craving for carp, and midnight is the best hour to catch them. There's a carp pond just outside the bamboo grove, so I headed out after dark..."
[ZH] 当那男人把袋子去掉，趁着月色才看清是一具女尸。苦蕨被吓坏，后悔跟着前来，可此时离开，容易被对方发现，他便继续躲着。
[EN] Around midnight, Kujue had landed his carp, packed them in his bamboo basket, and started back toward the village when the rattle of a carriage caught his ear. *Who's driving a carriage out at this hour?* Peering through the moonlight, he saw it was coming *toward* the village from outside, thought nothing of it, and kept walking.
[ZH] 那男的收了布袋，对着女尸开始说话：“你虽是我杀的，但也不能怪我，要怪就怪方计远。”
[EN] A few paces on, the wheels went silent. Looking back, he saw the carriage had halted. Curious why someone headed for the village would stop short of the entrance, he ducked behind cover to watch.
[ZH] 说完那男人匆匆离开。
[EN] In the dark, a man dragged something bulky out of the carriage and hoisted it onto his shoulder—a large cloth sack, though Kujue couldn't guess what was stuffed inside. His curiosity piqued by the man's furtive air, he crept after him to the stream and hid behind a boulder.
[ZH] 回忆完，苦蕨继续说：“那人走后，我到女尸旁看了一眼，被吓坏，赶紧跑回家中，第二天村长也发现，便赶着去报了官。”
[EN] When the man pulled the sack off, moonlight revealed a woman's corpse. Terror froze Kujue in place; he cursed himself for following, yet running now would only give him away, so he stayed crouched behind the rock.
[ZH] 申简辰重复着名字：“方计远？虽然不知道是哪三个字，有了这名字，案子向前迈进一大步，都是苦蕨的功劳。”
[EN] Rolling up the sack, the man spoke straight to the dead woman: *"I'm the one who killed you, sure, but don't blame me—blame Fang Jiyuan."* With that, he hurried off.
[ZH] 苦蕨继续憨憨地笑着：“只要大人觉得有用就好。大人认识我姐，应该也认识东家，您要是见到东家，告诉她什么时候有时间再回竹林看看，村子里的人都念着她的好儿，盼着她回来。”
[EN] "After he left," Kujue finished, "I crept over for one look, nearly jumped out of my skin, and bolted straight home. Next morning the headman found her too and went to report it."
[ZH] 申简辰并不知竹林的东家是谁，疑惑地问：“东家？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen repeated the name under his breath. "Fang Jiyuan... I don't know which characters write it yet, but having a name moves this case forward by leaps and bounds. All thanks to you, Kujue."
[ZH] “我姐姐伺候的人，就是朱家姑娘，听说嫁到陈家。这事儿闹得，转了一圈儿，竹林还是归东家，村里人都高兴坏了。”
[EN] Kujue scratched his head with a bashful grin. "Glad it helps, Your Honor. Since you know my sister, you must know our Proprietor too. Next time you see her, tell her to come visit the bamboo grove whenever she has time—everyone in the village remembers her kindness and misses her."
[ZH] 申简辰虽听不懂苦蕨话中的意思，但也明白，他口中的东家正是他日思夜想之人：“不知朱家姑娘来竹林时住在哪里？”
[EN] "Proprietor?" Shen Jianchen blinked, baffled.
[ZH] “东家就住在我的竹屋，她回城里后，我也成了亲，与我娘子住到这里。”
[EN] "The lady my sister serves—Miss Zhu, who married into the Chen family! Funny how things turned out: went all the way around in a circle, and the bamboo grove ended up belonging to her again. Whole village was thrilled!"
[ZH] 原来此处正是千里寻曾经住过的地方，申简辰这才看向眼前茂密的竹林，想象她曾住在这里欣赏竹林时的心情。
[EN] Shen Jianchen couldn't follow all the twists behind that remark, yet he knew at once who Kujue meant. "Where did Miss Zhu stay when she came to the bamboo grove?"
[ZH] 那时她刚刚成为朱淑真，还未摆脱与林觉的婚姻，来到此处应该是散心。
[EN] "Right here in this bamboo house! After she went back to the city, I got married and moved in with my wife."
[ZH] 白风见王爷又陷入个人的情绪中，起身把苦蕨拉到一边，小声问：“我是你未来的姐夫，今儿既然来了，不如带我去见见岳父岳母？”
[EN] So *this* was where Qianli Xun had lived. Shen Jianchen lifted his eyes to the dense sea of bamboo swaying overhead, imagining her gaze resting on these same leaves. Back then she had only just woken up as Zhu Shuzhen, still trapped in her marriage to Lin Jue, escaping out here for breathing room.
[ZH] 苦蕨难为情道：“带你见见我爹娘也没什么，只是他们一起去我外公家，我娘子也跟着去认认门。”
[EN] Seeing his master sink back into silent longing, Bai Feng got up, pulled Kujue aside, and dropped his voice. "Since I'm your future brother-in-law and I'm already here, why not introduce me to my future father- and mother-in-law?"
[ZH] 白风又是一阵失望：“你娘子都去了，你怎么不跟着？却一个人留在家里？”
[EN] Kujue looked sheepish. "I'd love to, except they went over to my maternal grandfather's place today, and my wife went along to meet the relatives."
[ZH] 苦蕨“嘿嘿”笑道：“家里总得留人干活，我是干活的。”
[EN] Bai Feng deflated. "Your wife went and you stayed home by yourself?"
[ZH] 很快白风不再失望，起码见了未来的小舅子。
[EN] "Hehe—somebody's gotta stay behind and do the chores. That's me."
[ZH] 他开始关心起王爷，来到竹林，除了办案分走心神外，他的心思基本被幽栖居士带走，往后跟郡主的日子可怎么过？
[EN] Still, Bai Feng cheered up fast; at least he'd met his future brother-in-law. His thoughts swung back to his master—aside from brief flashes of focus on the murder case, the prince's whole mind had wandered straight back to Youqi Jushi. How on earth was he going to build a life with Princess Jiahe like this?
[ZH] 欣赏完竹林后，申简辰站起来：“白风，把你身上的那袋银子全部给苦蕨。”
[EN] After taking in the bamboo grove a while longer, Shen Jianchen stood up. "Bai Feng, give Kujue the entire pouch of silver you're carrying."
[ZH] 给苦荞的家人银两，白风自然乐意，将一袋银子取出送到苦蕨手中：“拿着，王爷赏你的。”
[EN] Glad to put money in Kuqiao's family's hands, Bai Feng pulled out a heavy pouch and pressed it on Kujue. "Take it—a reward from His Highness."
[ZH] 苦蕨是老实人，无功不受禄，况且那么多银子：“不不不！我说的那些话不用给银子！”
[EN] Honest to the bone, Kujue waved both hands in alarm. "No, no, no! I don't want silver just for telling what I saw!"
[ZH] 申简辰说：“不是因为你对案情有帮助才给你银子，我打算在竹林建竹屋，你帮我找块地方，要风景美的，再找些人，帮我把竹屋建起来。跑腿、选地、找人不得花银子？这些你先拿着，不够我会让白风送过来。”
[EN] "It isn't payment for your testimony," Shen Jianchen said. "I want to build a bamboo cottage here in the grove. Pick out a spot with a fine view and hire builders to put it up for me. Running errands, securing the plot, hiring hands—all of that takes silver. Take this for now, and if it's not enough, I'll have Bai Feng bring more."
[ZH] 苦蕨这才放心接到手中：“大人放心，我定帮您办妥。”
[EN] Only then did Kujue accept the pouch with relief. "Leave it to me, Your Honor—I'll see it done right!"
[ZH] 白风小声对苦蕨说：“往后我会常来竹林，不愁见不到未来的岳父岳母。”
[EN] Bai Feng leaned in to whisper, "I'll be swinging by the grove plenty from now on. I'll meet my future in-laws sooner or later."

#CH 253
[ZH] 一早陈浩宇在门外道完别离开后，千里寻用过早饭，简单装扮完，换上一身素淡的衣裙，先去婆婆房中告假。
[ZH] 既是仪王妃邀请参加诗会，必然不能阻拦，姚轻琰只有同意的份儿：“仪王妃主动邀请，怠慢不得，我也替你感到高兴。”
[ZH] 婆婆的话语中流露出长辈也一直担心她与梦容的关系，毕竟梦容在仪王抢亲那日受了伤，任何人都会认为从此一对闺中密友形同陌路。
[ZH] 千里寻也无时无刻不在担心与梦容的关系无法恢复到从前，梦容主动邀请她参加诗会，才暂时放下忧虑。
[ZH] 得到长辈允许，千里寻拜别后，赶往幽林别居。
[ZH] 马车里，苦荞一直观察小姐，她脸上挂着笑容。苦荞能够感受到小姐并不是因为可能会遇见仪王而高兴。
[ZH] “小姐，郡主人真好，举办诗会，还主动邀请您参加，给两人一个重修旧好的机会，明摆着不再计较以前的事，我真替小姐和郡主感到高兴。”
[ZH] 此刻千里寻觉得委屈自己嫁给陈浩宇是最正确的选择，在心里默默告诫自己，今后不要再去念着申简辰，也不要再与他相见。
[ZH] “还称呼郡主？应该称仪王妃。”
[ZH] “对，应该是仪王妃，我可得记牢，不能给小姐丢脸。”
[ZH] 到达后，马车刚在幽林别居门口停下，李管家迎上来：“少夫人来了，仪王妃正在里面候着，其他人也都到齐。”
[ZH] 不等苦荞先下车，千里寻迅速跳下马车：“我出发得不晚，大家怎么都到了？倒显得我不积极。梦容，不，仪王妃会不会怪我？”
[ZH] 李管家并不知情，安慰道：“仪王妃性情最好，少夫人又不是不知，她怎么会怪您？”
[ZH] 是啊，梦容向来性情好，那么大的事她都不计较，主动邀请她参加诗会，又怎么会计较这等小事？千里寻轻松许多。
[ZH] 苦荞已从马车上下来，跟着小姐往里走，李管家也跟在后面。
[ZH] 苦荞趁机悄悄问：“李管家，白风可在？”
[ZH] “白侍卫不在，他跟着仪王去了司理院。”
[EN] Right after Chen Haoyu said his morning farewell outside her door and left for work, Qianli Xun ate breakfast, dressed simply in a muted, understated gown, and went to ask her mother-in-law's leave.
[ZH] “司理院？是什么地方？他为何要去那里？”
[EN] Since Princess Consort Yi herself had sent the invitation, Yao Qingyan could hardly say no. "When Princess Consort Yi invites you in person, you must not keep her waiting. I'm truly glad for you."
[ZH] “仪王要当司理参军，估计是喜欢审理案子，王爷去哪里白侍卫都得跟着。”
[EN] Her mother-in-law's relief showed how worried the elders had been about the rift with Mengrong—after Prince Yi's wedding crash left Mengrong wounded, anyone would assume the two bosom friends had become strangers for good. Qianli Xun had fretted over the same thing every waking day, and Mengrong's personal invitation had lifted that stone right off her chest.
[ZH] 苦荞瘪起嘴：“哼，这么大的事他都不告诉我，等下次见到他，我一定要好好批评。”
[EN] With Yao Qingyan's blessing, she took her leave and rode to Youlin Retreat.
[ZH] 两人的对话千里寻一字不落听进耳朵里，申简辰不在，她失落之余也有轻松，可他跑去做司理参军，又是几个意思？
[EN] Inside the carriage, Kuqiao studied her mistress's smiling face and sensed at once that this glow had nothing to do with hoping to glimpse Prince Yi. "Miss, Princess Jiahe really has a heart of gold—hosting a poetry salon and inviting you herself to mend fences! Clearly she's put the past behind her. I'm so happy for the two of you."
[ZH] 唉！想那么多干什么？他是仪王想做什么便做什么，跟自己一点儿关系都没有。
[EN] In that moment, Qianli Xun felt that swallowing her pride to marry Chen Haoyu had been the wisest choice she could have made. Silently, she vowed to stop thinking about Shen Jianchen and stop seeing him altogether. "Still calling her Princess Jiahe? It's Princess Consort Yi now."
[ZH] 幽林别居对千里寻来讲并不陌生，她沿着小路准备往梦容的小院拐去，被李管家拦住。
[EN] "Right—Princess Consort Yi! Better drill that into my head so I don't embarrass you, Miss."
[ZH] “少夫人走错了，两位贵人回来后住一处，诗会在仪王住的院子里举行。”
[EN] The instant their carriage halted outside Youlin Retreat, Butler Li stepped out to greet them. "Second Young Madam has arrived. Princess Consort Yi is waiting inside—all the other guests are already here."
[ZH] 千里寻收住脚步：“瞧我这记性，他们都已成亲，仪王妃怎么可能还住在以前的小院儿？”
[EN] Without waiting for Kuqiao to step down first, Qianli Xun hopped straight out of the carriage. "I didn't leave late—how did everyone else beat me here? Makes me look rude! Will Mengrong—I mean, Princess Consort Yi—be upset?"
[ZH] 她改变方向，一边走着，一边想起申简辰说过守身如玉的话来。
[EN] In the dark about any tension, Butler Li smiled reassuringly. "You know as well as anyone what a sweet temper the Princess Consort has. Why would she be upset with you?"
[ZH] 心想：“你若真的为我守身如玉，我岂不是更加对不起梦容？我心里虽然还念着你，却也希望你跟梦容能相守一生。你们能住在一起，看来你对她不错，难怪梦容会原谅我，主动邀请我参加诗会。申简辰，我们一起努力忘记彼此吧。”
[EN] True enough. Mengrong had always been gentle—if she could forgive something as huge as that wedding mess and invite her over, why would she sweat a trifle like arriving last? Qianli Xun's shoulders loosened.
[ZH] 心中想着事，不知不觉已来到正院门外。
[EN] Kuqiao climbed down and trailed behind her mistress, with Butler Li following along. Seeing her chance, Kuqiao leaned over to murmur, "Butler Li, is Bai Feng here?"
[ZH] 李管家说：“我就送到这里，少夫人自行进去，院中自有下人招呼。”
[EN] "Guard Bai isn't here—he went with Prince Yi to the Judicial Office."
[ZH] 待李管家去后，苦荞推开院门，一阵“嘎吱”声，传达出千里寻对友情的期待。
[EN] "Judicial Office? What's that? Why'd he go there?"
[ZH] 她小心走进去，院子里竟然异常安静，没有一丝热闹的气息，她甚至怀疑梦容为了见她找了诗会的借口而已。早知如此，她应主动前来向她忏悔。
[EN] "Prince Yi took on the post of Judicial Investigator—must have a taste for solving cases. Wherever His Highness goes, Guard Bai has to follow."
[ZH] 刚走没几步，喜兰从屋里出来：“幽栖居士可来了，众人正在里面等着，都盼您快些到来，尤其是仪王妃。”
[EN] Kuqiao stuck out her lower lip. "Humph! Something that big and he didn't even tell me! Wait till next time I see him—I'm giving him an earful."
[ZH] 原来还有其他人，千里寻有些许失望。失望过后，仔细品喜兰说话的语气和表情，很正常，没有什么异样，足以说明梦容对她的心思还和以前一样。
[EN] Every word reached Qianli Xun's ears. Knowing Shen Jianchen wasn't here brought a twinge of emptiness mixed with genuine relief—though what on earth was he doing playing Judicial Investigator? Oh, forget it! He was Prince Yi; he could do whatever he pleased, and none of it was her business anymore.
[ZH] 千里寻快走几步，踏进屋内，只觉眼前一片红色。不是诗会吗？怎么布置得跟新婚似的？仔细一想，梦容与申简辰不就是新婚，如此布置再正常不过。
[EN] Familiar with Youlin Retreat's paths, she started turning down the lane toward Mengrong's old side courtyard, only for Butler Li to stop her. "Wrong way, Second Young Madam. Since returning, Their Highnesses live together—the salon is in Prince Yi's main courtyard."
[ZH] 一片红光过后，清楚地看到梦容身着一身王妃的服饰，端坐在正位，面色平静中带着威严，见她走进，只是略微抬眼瞧来。
[EN] Qianli Xun halted with a sheepish smile. "Look at my memory. They're married now—of course the Princess Consort wouldn't still live in her old side courtyard."
[ZH] 那表情令千里寻产生从未有过的距离感，准备了一肚子的话说不出来，只能站在原地沉默不语。
[EN] Changing course, she remembered Shen Jianchen's vow to stay chaste for her. *If you really did keep yourself untouched for me, wouldn't I owe Mengrong an even heavier debt? Even if a piece of my heart still aches for you, I truly want you and Mengrong to grow old together. Moving into the same courtyard means you're treating her right—no wonder she forgave me and invited me today. Shen Jianchen, let's both try our hardest to forget each other.*
[ZH] 梦容则微启双唇：“幽栖居士不愧是江南女词人，架子就是大，这么多人早早到来，你却是最后一个，该不会根本没把我放在眼里？”
[EN] Lost in thought, she reached the gate of the main courtyard before she knew it.
[ZH] 从昨日接到请帖到刚才进入幽林别居，所有美好的畅想被梦容冷冷的表情和语气击得粉碎。
[EN] "I'll leave you here, Second Young Madam," Butler Li said. "Go right on in—servants inside will see to you."
[ZH] 千里寻缓了缓心绪，瞧瞧在座的人。左边坐着苗知瑜和她的一位闺中密友，还有一个位置空出，应该是给她留的。右边坐着白梅若与两位没见过的女子，用脚趾头都能猜到应该也是苗知瑜的闺中密友。
[EN] Once he withdrew, Kuqiao pushed the wooden gate open. Its low creak carried all of Qianli Xun's quiet hope for restored friendship. Stepping inside, she blinked at the eerie hush hanging over the courtyard—not a shred of party chatter anywhere. For half a heartbeat, she wondered if Mengrong had only used a poetry salon as an excuse to see her alone. Had she known, she would have come knocking first to beg forgiveness.
[ZH] 千里寻敏锐地感觉出今日诗会不是那么简单，既然来了，该面对的还需面的。仪王妃已发话，她再不接显得不守规矩。
[EN] A few paces in, Xilan emerged from the hall. "At last Youqi Jushi has arrived! Everyone's waiting inside, eager to see you—especially Princess Consort Yi."
[ZH] 千里寻不由自主躬身行礼：“请仪王妃海涵，紧赶慢赶还是最后一个，好在没有迟到。”
[EN] So there *were* other guests. A flicker of disappointment passed over her, though studying Xilan's calm tone and smooth expression reassured her—nothing looked amiss, so Mengrong's feelings must be just as warm as before.
[ZH] “既然你没迟到，我也不与你计较。在座诸位来时都为我送上祝福，幽栖居士却一句祝福的话都没有，难道不希望我与仪王百年好合？”
[EN] Quickening her step across the threshold, Qianli Xun met a blaze of wedding red. Wait—wasn't this a poetry salon? Why was the hall decked out like a bridal chamber? Well, Mengrong and Shen Jianchen *were* newlyweds, so red hangings made sense enough.
[ZH] 现实总是与想象中有差距，千里寻失落的同时，无法埋怨梦容，她受过的剑伤虽好，内心的伤口应该没有那么容易愈合，怪只怪命运捉弄。
[EN] As her eyes adjusted to the scarlet glow, she saw Qiao Mengrong seated in the place of honor, arrayed in full Princess Consort regalia. Her face held a cool, regal stillness; as Qianli Xun entered, she lifted her eyes by no more than a fraction.
[ZH] “回仪王妃，百年好合怎么能够？我时刻在心中祝福您与仪王相濡以沫，白头偕老，生生世世不离不弃。”
[EN] That single look opened a chasm between them Qianli Xun had never felt before. Every warm word she had rehearsed dried up in her throat, leaving her rooted in place.
[ZH] “哼！心中祝福，说得轻巧，谁知你心中作何想法？希望幽栖居士不要口是心非，更希望你时刻记着你已嫁人，不要再惦记不该惦记的人。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong parted her lips. "Youqi Jushi truly lives up to her name as the Poetess of Jiangnan—what grand airs! Everyone else arrived bright and early, yet you're dead last. Or did you simply not think me worth respecting?"
[ZH] 乔梦容的意思再明显不过，千里寻又岂能不知？她心里即使想过申简辰也不能承认。并不是虚伪，是怕再次伤害梦容。
[EN] Every bright hope Qianli Xun had nursed from yesterday's card straight up to this threshold shattered under that glacial tone.
[ZH] “我心中一直放着一位闺中密友，不管她如何看待我，我绝不会做对不起她的事，对她我永远不会口是心非，心中只惦记着她，希望她越来越好。只要她好……”
[EN] Steadying her breathing, she swept her gaze around the room. To the left sat Miao Zhiyu and one of her close friends, with a single empty seat beside them—clearly meant for her. To the right sat Bai Meiruo alongside two unfamiliar young women who, even a fool could tell with her eyes shut, had to be more of Miao Zhiyu's circle.
[ZH] “不必多言！”乔梦容认为朱淑贞不过是巧舌雌黄，若真的心里有她，为何要偷偷勾引哥哥？她稳住情绪，“既然来了，落座吧，我的诗会怎么能少了江南女词人？”
[EN] Today's poetry salon was no friendly tea party, that much was crystal clear. Still, she was already here; no sense flinching from what had to be faced. Princess Consort Yi had spoken, and staying mute would only look defiant.
[ZH] 千里寻行完礼，坐在左边的空位上。那位置是下位中的下位，很显然在梦容心中她已不重要。千里寻一阵落寞，心中自问：“她既不看重我？为何邀请我前来参加诗会？”
[EN] Qianli Xun bowed low. "Please forgive me, Princess Consort Yi. I hurried as fast as I could and still ended up last—thankfully, not past the appointed hour."
[ZH] 她抬眼再次瞧一遍在场的人，她们个个脸上露出不怀好意的笑容，尤其是白梅若。由此看来诗会也许会成为批判她的大会，不如找理由溜掉？
[EN] "Since you aren't technically late, I won't hold it against you," Mengrong said. "Still, every lady here offered me her wedding blessings upon arriving, yet Youqi Jushi hasn't uttered a single word of congratulation. Don't you wish Prince Yi and me a hundred years of harmony?"
[ZH] 正想着，门从外面被下人关上。
[EN] Reality hit colder than she had feared, yet Qianli Xun couldn't bring herself to resent Mengrong. The sword wound on her shoulder might have healed, but the scar on her heart clearly hadn't. Blame fate's cruel jokes. "How could a mere hundred years be enough, Your Highness? In my heart I pray every day that you and Prince Yi share every joy and hardship, grow white-haired together, and never part through life after life."
[ZH] 梦容连溜掉的机会都不给她，既如此还是做好被众人批判的准备。只要梦容心中的怨气能消，被人骂两句也没什么，去年被那么多人骂，不都挺过来，今日就这几人，又能有多大力度。
[EN] "Humph! Easy enough to claim you pray 'in your heart'—who knows what you're really thinking in there? I only hope Youqi Jushi isn't speaking sweet words while harboring foul thoughts, and that you keep firmly in mind that you are a married woman now. Stop coveting what doesn't belong to you."
[ZH] 苦荞站在小姐身后很是担忧，仪王妃说话的语气和表情跟以前大不相同，不仅有距离感，还充满情绪。恐怕小姐今日在劫难逃，怎么办？
[EN] Every barb struck straight at the mark, and Qianli Xun caught every syllable. Even if Shen Jianchen still crossed her thoughts, she could never admit it—not out of hypocrisy, but because she refused to knife Mengrong again. "There is one dear friend I have always carried in my heart. No matter how she views me, I would never betray her, never lie to her, and only ever wish her happiness. So long as she is happy—"
[ZH] 都怪那个杀千刀的仪王，放着那么好的妹妹不好好爱，偏偏招惹自家小姐，才搞成今日局面。他倒好，自己远远躲开当什么司里参军，害得白风也跟着去，错过一次见他的机会。
[EN] "Save your breath!" Qiao Mengrong cut in. Silver-tongued hypocrisy—if she truly cared about her, why had she seduced Brother behind her back? Reining in her temper, she gestured to the empty chair. "Since you're here, sit. How could my poetry salon go on without the Poetess of Jiangnan?"
[ZH] 苦荞担忧小姐，必然观察她的表情，发现小姐失落之后倒安静坦然，她心里也平静许多。
[EN] After bowing again, Qianli Xun took the lone seat on the left—the lowest spot at the lowest end of the room, signaling plain as day how little she meant to Mengrong now. A hollow chill crept into her chest. *If she despises me this much, why invite me at all?* Looking around the circle again, she met smug, venomous smiles on every face—Bai Meiruo's sharpest of all. This wasn't a salon; it was a public lynching. Should she invent an excuse and slip away?
[ZH] 从后门进来五位幽林别居的丫鬟，手中端着笔墨纸砚，分别放在其他五人的桌上。
[EN] Right on cue, servants pulled the main doors shut from outside.
[ZH] 千里寻心中纳闷，第一次见诗会如此操作，既如此操作为何她面前的桌上不给摆同样的物件？
[EN] Mengrong wasn't even giving her an exit. Fine—might as well brace herself for the verbal firing squad. If letting them tear into her drained some of Mengrong's bitterness, what were a few insults? She had survived an entire city slandering her last year; how much bite could half a dozen noblewomen have?
[ZH] 乔梦容向苗知瑜使眼色。
[EN] Standing behind her mistress, Kuqiao's stomach knotted. Princess Consort Yi's icy tone and hostile stare were nothing like the sweet girl she used to be. Miss was walking straight into an ambush! Curse that rotten Prince Yi—ignoring such a devoted little sister to chase after Miss and landing everyone in this mess, while he hid away playing Judicial Investigator and dragged Bai Feng off with him so she didn't even get to see her sweetheart! Watching her mistress's profile, though, Kuqiao saw the initial hurt settle into quiet composure, and her own nerves steadied a notch.
[ZH] 苗知瑜迅速领会，第一个开口说话：“幽栖居士不必疑惑，今日主要品鉴你的诗词，大家必然佳句频出，不记下来实在浪费。此等劳烦事，不必幽栖居士亲自动笔，我等代劳即可。”
[EN] Five maids filed through the rear door carrying the Four Treasures of the Study—brushes, inksticks, paper, and inkstones—and set them on the tables before the other five guests, skipping Qianli Xun's table altogether.
[ZH] 千里寻再笨也能听出，她们是要记录今日品诗的内容。已猜出她们品诗的话语定是以批判为主，将批判的内容记下来到底意欲何为？难道也要像陈浩宇传播她谣言一样宣扬出去？
[EN] Qianli Xun frowned. Strange setup for a poetry salon—and if they were writing, why give everyone else brushes and paper except her?
[ZH] 面前的人要是别人，搁千里寻的性格，必然大骂一顿冲出去，凭她的拳头谁能拦得住？可谁让请她的人是梦容，谁让她的的确确惦记过她的哥哥，一个人终要为自己的错误付出代价。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong shot Miao Zhiyu a glance.
[ZH] 不如听听她们到底要说些什么，再做决断。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu picked up the cue at once. "No need to look puzzled, Youqi Jushi. Today we are here specifically to critique your poetry. Brilliant commentary is bound to pour forth from everyone present, and failing to record it would be a pity. Naturally we wouldn't trouble you to take notes yourself—we'll handle the writing."
[ZH] 苗知瑜对身后的幼凡说：“把幽栖居士写的新词拿出来。”
[EN] Even a dimwit could read between those lines: they were going to transcribe every word of their hatchet job. Why write it all down? Were they planning to broadcast it all over town, just like Chen Haoyu had spread rumors about her? If anyone else had pulled this stunt, Qianli Xun's temper would have snapped on the spot—she'd have cursed them sideways and punched her way straight out the door. Who here could stop her fists? Yet Mengrong was the host, and she *had* coveted Mengrong's brother. Sooner or later, a person had to pay for her own mistakes. Better sit tight, hear what they had to say, and decide her move after.
[ZH] 幼凡手中早已拿着一个卷轴，只见她点点头，转向苦荞：“既是幽栖居士的词，你帮我一起展开。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu nodded to the maid behind her. "Youfan, bring out Youqi Jushi's new ci."
[ZH] 众目睽睽下，苦荞无法拒绝，点头答应，与幼凡走到中间，将卷轴展开。
[EN] Youfan already held a rolled scroll. Stepping forward, she turned to Kuqiao. "Since this is Youqi Jushi's lyric, help me unroll it."
[ZH] 苦荞认得这首词，正是小姐新写的《减字木兰花·春怨》。南双跑来替乔公子讨要诗词，她便将这首给了。
[EN] With every eye locked on them, Kuqiao couldn't refuse. She stepped to the center of the hall beside Youfan and stretched the scroll wide. She recognized it at once—her mistress's new *"Jianzi Mulan Hua: Spring Resentment"*, the very piece she had handed Nan Shuang when Young Master Qiao asked for a fresh lyric.
[ZH] 苗知瑜说：“这首词是幽栖居士新写的，文渊派南双从幽栖居士那里讨要来不久，还热乎着。我无意中从文渊的书桌上发现，誊抄来，正好拿到今日诗会，供大家品鉴。”
[EN] "This is Youqi Jushi's latest work," Miao Zhiyu announced. "Wenyuan sent Nan Shuang to beg it from her just the other day—the ink's barely dry! I happened to spot it on Wenyuan's desk, copied it out, and brought it along today for everyone to appreciate."
[ZH] 乔梦容说：“既是苗夫人拿来，就由苗夫人第一个品鉴，帮大家开个场，也好让诸位知道今日该如何品鉴幽栖居士的佳作。”
[EN] "Since Madam Miao brought it," Qiao Mengrong said, "you shall open the critique—show everyone how to properly appraise Youqi Jushi's masterpiece."
[ZH] “谨遵仪王妃吩咐。”苗知瑜起身，走到卷轴跟前，念了两句，“独行独坐，独唱独酬还独卧。”
[EN] "As Your Highness commands." Miao Zhiyu rose, stepped up to the scroll, and read the opening lines aloud: *"I walk alone, sit alone, sing alone, drink alone, and sleep alone."* [TN: Translated by Tony Barnstone and Chou Ping]
[ZH] 念完，转向朱淑贞：“幽栖居士，恕我冒昧，这两句只有十一个字，一个‘独’字竟然出现五次，难道幽栖居士才尽词穷？”
[EN] Turning toward Zhu Shuzhen, she arched a brow. "Forgive my bluntness, Youqi Jushi, but those opening lines contain eleven characters, and the single character 'alone' (独) shows up five times! Has the great Youqi Jushi finally run dry of talent and words?"
[ZH] 千里寻正想解释，重复不仅能将内心的孤独感表达得淋漓尽致，而且每个“独”字程度不同，表达的意思也是同中有异。嘴巴刚刚张开，话还未出口，被苗知瑜的话给打了回去。
[EN] Qianli Xun opened her mouth to explain how the repetition drove home an inescapable loneliness, with each "alone" carrying a distinct shade and deepening intensity—yet Miao Zhiyu cut straight across her before a syllable escaped.
[ZH] “我看幽栖居士一定是才尽词穷，像幽栖居士这等有名的女词人，随便写两句，哪怕是重复，别人都会认为是佳句，而我等即使佳句频出，别人也不会拿我们写的诗词当回事。”
[EN] "Oh, she must have run dry! Still, for a celebrity poetess like Youqi Jushi, people will hail any sloppy repetition as genius, whereas no matter how many sparkling lines the rest of us write, nobody spares us a glance."
[ZH] 苗知瑜坐回桌前，继续说：“我诗兴大发，仿幽栖居士的句子也写两句，大家听听，给品评品评：喜行喜坐，喜唱喜酬还喜卧。大家觉得如何？”
[EN] Returning to her chair, Miao Zhiyu smirked. "In fact, inspiration just struck me! I composed a parody in her exact style—tell me what you think, ladies: *I walk in joy, sit in joy, sing in joy, drink in joy, and sleep in joy.* Well? How does it sound?"
[ZH] 苦荞心中十分不满：“好个屁！跟我家小姐写的句子比差远了！原来她们是想借着诗会批判我家小姐，早知如此，就不该来。”
[EN] *Sounds like hot garbage!* Kuqiao raged in silence. *Doesn't hold a candle to my mistress's lines! So they really did drag Miss here just to tear her apart—we never should've come!*
[ZH] 其他几人边笑边提笔在纸上记录着。
[EN] Giggling, the other women dipped their brushes and began scribbling on their sheets.
[ZH] 千里寻发现梦容的脸上露出满意的笑容，本来可借此空档说两句，梦容的笑容搞得她更加失落，失落得一句话也不想说。
[EN] Qianli Xun glanced up and caught a gleam of pure gratification on Mengrong's face. She could have jumped in right then to shut Miao Zhiyu down, yet seeing that satisfied smile on Mengrong's lips drained every word out of her.
[ZH] 白梅若放下笔：“依我看还是苗夫人的句子更好，起码让人觉得喜庆，人生总须往好处想，不能老是悲悲切切的，好像别人总是欠着她似的。幽栖居士，你说是也不是？”
[EN] Bai Meiruo set down her brush. "If you ask me, Madam Miao's version is far superior. At least it sounds cheerful! People ought to look on the bright side of life instead of moping and weeping all day as if the whole world owes them a debt. Wouldn't you agree, Youqi Jushi?"

#CH 254
[ZH] 对方的语气酸中带刺，千里寻多少被刺到。目光瞟向梦容，她一脸冷笑，端起酒酿仔细品着，那表情使得她只能将骂白梅若的话憋回去。
[ZH] 白梅若继续说：“幽栖居士不说话便是默认，可你为何总写些悲悲戚戚的诗词？你不必回答，我来帮你说。诸位！你们猜猜男子是喜欢长得美柔柔弱弱又悲戚可怜的女子？还是喜欢长得美又厉害的母老虎？”
[ZH] 千里寻旁边的姑娘说：“当然是喜欢第一种。”
[ZH] 白梅若否认道：“不对。”
[ZH] 另一姑娘问：“难道是第二种？”
[ZH] 白梅若摇摇头：“也不对。”
[ZH] 第三个姑娘说：“白夫人，你就别卖关子，赶紧说吧。”
[ZH] 白梅若站起来，走到中间，向仪王妃行礼后，面向大家：“男子最喜欢二者兼有之人，幽栖居士正是二者兼有之人。幽栖居士拳头厉害，大家有所耳闻，苗夫人也曾亲眼见识过，连我家那位都不是她的对手，昨日还念叨来者，批评我什么都不懂，既不会写诗作词，还不懂功夫。你们听听，都和离了，还对前妻念念不忘，这不正说明幽栖居士是有魅力之人吗？”
[ZH] 千里寻终于忍不住：“白梅若，这是诗会，你们家那档子破事少拿来炫耀！”
[ZH] 乔梦容也开口说话：“幽栖居士何必恼她？因为你，她在家里过得不舒坦，借着诗会发泄发泄也是好的。怪我，忘记告诉你，今日诗会大家畅所欲言，既品诗亦可将心中不快吐出来。你呀，都是江南女词人，怎么如此不大度？只允许你在诗词中无病呻 吟，就不允许大家借着你的诗词吐露心中不快？”
[EN] The venomous edge in Bai Meiruo's voice pricked Qianli Xun's pride. She flicked a glance toward Mengrong—who sat sipping fermented rice wine with a cold smirk—and swallowed the curse perched on her tongue.
[ZH] 所有人齐声说：“请让我们借着你的诗词吐露心中不快！”
[EN] "Silence means consent," Bai Meiruo went on. "Still, why do you insist on writing nothing but gloomy, tear-soaked verses? Don't bother answering—I'll tell everyone for you. Ladies! Guess which sort of woman men adore most: a fragile, weeping beauty who looks ready to faint, or a fierce, gorgeous tigress?"
[ZH] 苦荞很是为难，大家批判自家小姐，她还帮忙扯着卷轴的一角，不如放手，拉着小姐离开。她回头瞧瞧关着的大门，现在才明白为何那门被紧紧关起来。
[EN] The young woman seated beside Qianli Xun answered, "The first kind, obviously."
[ZH] 千里寻注意到苦荞不对劲，向她摇摇头。
[EN] "Wrong," Bai Meiruo declared.
[ZH] 苦荞才又扯好卷轴，小姐有多生气，她便有多生气。
[EN] "The second, then?" another guest asked.
[ZH] 如此看来，今日显然是梦容借着大家的言辞将情绪发泄在她的身上。千里寻决定说完一句话，暂时紧闭嘴巴，任由他人言语攻击。
[EN] Bai Meiruo shook her head. "Wrong again."
[ZH] “大家请便！”
[EN] "Stop keeping us in suspense, Madam Bai—spit it out!" cried the third girl.
[ZH] 乔梦容装作夸赞的样子：“幽栖居士不愧是江南女词人，就是大度。白夫人，继续。”
[EN] Rising, Bai Meiruo sauntered to the center of the hall, dipped a curtsy to Princess Consort Yi, and faced the room. "Men crave a woman who is *both* at once—and Youqi Jushi is the living embodiment! Everyone's heard how wicked her fists are, and Madam Miao has seen them firsthand. Even my own husband is no match for her—just yesterday he was griping again, scolding me for knowing nothing about poetry or martial arts! Imagine that: mutually divorced and still hung up on his ex-wife! Doesn't that prove how irresistible Youqi Jushi is?"
[ZH] 白梅若向仪王妃行礼后，继续面向大家。
[EN] Qianli Xun snapped. "Bai Meiruo, this is a poetry salon—stop airing your family's dirty laundry in here!"
[ZH] “我听说幽栖居士嫁到陈家把陈家二公子收拾得服服帖帖，现在跟变了个人似的，这不就是母老虎的魅力吗？再瞧瞧这首词，‘伤神’‘轻寒’‘泪洗残妆’‘愁病相仍’，啧啧啧啧，好一个幽怨我见犹怜的病西施，太容易勾起男子的保护欲。尤其是那个‘独’字，还重复了五遍。大家猜猜幽栖居士是想告诉别人什么吗？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong stepped right in. "Why snap at her, Youqi Jushi? She's miserable at home because of you—letting her blow off a little steam here does no harm. My mistake, I forgot to mention today's house rules: everyone speaks freely, critiquing your poetry while getting whatever grievances they have off their chests. Really, as the famed Poetess of Jiangnan, how can you be so narrow-minded? You're allowed to wallow in fake anguish in your poems, yet nobody else is allowed to vent their real frustrations over them?"
[ZH] 众人笑而不语，有的提笔记录着。
[EN] The whole room chimed in at once. "Please, let us use your lyric to vent our frustrations!"
[ZH] 白梅若并不希望大家回答，仍旧说道：“这首词还新鲜着，说明是幽栖居士嫁到陈家后所作。都嫁人了，夫君也已变好，怎么还不满足？又是宣扬孤独，又是病西施的，搞得别人以为你嫁到陈家是跑去受罪。”
[EN] Holding up one corner of the scroll while everyone gang-attacked her mistress made Kuqiao itch to drop the roller, grab Miss by the hand, and storm right out. One look over her shoulder at the latched front doors reminded her why they had been shut. Catching Kuqiao tensing, Qianli Xun gave a slight shake of her head. Kuqiao tightened her grip on the scroll, seething right alongside her mistress.
[ZH] 苗知瑜趁机说：“据我所知，幽栖居士嫁到陈家日子过得相当舒坦，既霸占了陈公子的院子，还得了他的财产，也将夏老太太哄得高兴，还遇到难得的好公爹和好婆婆，现在夫君也被她管教好，真是羡煞旁人，又岂会跑去受罪？”
[EN] So this whole spectacle was Mengrong using the other women's mouths to bleed off her rage. Fine—Qianli Xun would say one line, then zip her lips and take whatever venom they threw. "By all means, ladies. Help yourselves!"
[ZH] 白梅若说：“答案只有一个。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong offered mock praise. "Spoken like the grand Poetess of Jiangnan—magnanimous indeed. Continue, Madam Bai."
[ZH] 众人问：“什么答案？”
[EN] Dipping another curtsy to the Princess Consort, Bai Meiruo pivoted back to her audience. "Word has it Youqi Jushi marched into the Chen household and whipped Second Young Master Chen right into line until he's practically a brand-new man—there's the tigress charm in action! Now look at this lyric: *my spirit hurts*, *a light cold*, *tears have washed off half my makeup*, *sorrow and sickness have joined hands*... tsk, tsk, tsk! What a pathetic, drooping Xi Shi—guaranteed to trigger every man's urge to sweep in and protect her! Especially that word *alone*, hammered home five times in a row. Care to guess what message Youqi Jushi is trying to broadcast?"
[ZH] 白梅若接着说：“试想想，在座几位若是男子，看到幽栖居士这首词，是不是既心疼，又被那‘独’字勾 引得蠢蠢欲动？”
[EN] Smirks rippled around the tables as brushes scratched across paper.
[ZH] 众人听了，都掩鼻而笑。
[EN] Not waiting for an answer, Bai Meiruo pressed on. "This lyric is fresh off the brush, meaning she wrote it *after* marrying into the Chen family. You're already wed, and your husband has already reformed—what more could you possibly want? Yet here you are broadcasting your loneliness and playing the fragile invalid, making everyone think marrying into the Chen family is some sort of torture!"
[ZH] 千里寻攥紧拳头，真想一拳打上去，将白梅若打得稀巴烂。可看到梦容满足的样子，她又松开拳头。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu jumped in on cue. "From what I hear, Youqi Jushi is living like a queen over at Chen Manor. She took over Young Master Chen's courtyard, pocketed his assets, charmed Old Madam Xia, landed the kindest in-laws imaginable, and even whipped her husband into shape. Every woman in town envies her—what 'torture' could she possibly be suffering?"
[ZH] 苦荞真是佩服这位白夫人，好好一首词，被她说得一无是处，还成为勾 引的句子。小姐的诗词被如此诟病，她心里肯定怒不可遏。她看向小姐，怒色虽有，却也渐渐从她脸上消失。小姐看重仪王妃，应该是因为她，才一忍再忍。
[EN] "There is only one explanation," Bai Meiruo declared.
[ZH] 白梅若面向苗知瑜：“苗夫人，这首词是你从乔公子那里得的，他已读过，你可得把他看紧了，省得他被别人把魂儿勾走。”
[EN] "What is it?" the others chorused.
[ZH] 苗知瑜放下手中的笔：“我家文渊以前是喜欢过一些花花草草，都是过去的事，自打成亲后，他对我一心一意，又岂会被一首词所蛊惑？只不过他是品词高手，需经常搜集新作，省得参加各类诗会时没有作品拿出，被大家笑话。”
[EN] "Picture yourselves as men for a moment," Bai Meiruo purred. "Reading a lyric like this from Youqi Jushi, wouldn't your hearts melt—and wouldn't all those whispers of being *alone* set your blood itching with desire?"
[ZH] 白梅若装模作样：“恭喜苗夫人觅得佳偶。”
[EN] Tittering behind their sleeves, the guests hid their grins.
[ZH] 白梅若说完，坐回位置，今日真是一吐为快，将心中积压的情绪一股脑儿倒出来。
[EN] Qianli Xun's fists clenched white. Every muscle begged to stride over and smash Bai Meiruo's smug face flat—yet one glance at Mengrong's glowing satisfaction made her uncurl her fingers.
[ZH] 苗知瑜向斜对面的闺中密友使眼色。
[EN] Kuqiao could hardly believe her ears. Turning a heartfelt lyric into cheap seduction—how shameless could this Madam Bai get? Her mistress had to be boiling inside! Yet glancing sideways at Qianli Xun, she saw the flash of fury ebb away into calm restraint. Miss valued Princess Consort Yi too much; that was the only reason she kept swallowing this abuse.
[ZH] 那姑娘意识到该自己说话，放下手中的笔：“仪王妃，诸位，适才听到仪王妃说起一词‘无病呻 吟’，我以为十分恰当，就以此为主题品品幽栖居士的词。”
[EN] Bai Meiruo turned toward Miao Zhiyu. "Madam Miao, you got this poem from Young Master Qiao, which means he's already read it. Keep him on a tight leash, lest someone hook his soul right out of his chest."
[ZH] 她故意端起酒杯：“幽栖居士得罪了。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu laid down her brush with cool poise. "My Wenyuan may have been fond of a few stray flowers in his youth, but that's ancient history. Ever since our wedding, he has had eyes only for me—hardly the sort to be bewitched by one lyric. He's simply a connoisseur of verse who collects fresh pieces so he never turns up empty-handed at literary gatherings."
[ZH] 千里寻懒得理会，对方肯定又会说些难听的话，心想：“暂且让你们过过嘴瘾，等你们一个个说完，理穷词尽，无话可说时，我再一个一个教训。”
[EN] "Congratulations on finding such a devoted husband, Madam Miao," Bai Meiruo simpered, then returned to her seat, glowing with triumph after dumping months of pent-up spite.
[ZH] 那姑娘一饮而尽，放下酒杯说道：“虽值暮春时节，也是大好春 光，幽栖居士不赞春 色之美，却‘伫立伤神’，还谈什么‘轻寒’，实在矫情。明明自己名声不好，随随便便当众就能坐在男子的怀里，还有脸说‘此情谁见，泪洗残妆无一半’，搞得好像你很委屈，不明白的人还以为幽栖居士深受情伤。只有我等明白之人才清楚，你善用媚术，又长了一双多情的眼睛，男子只要看那么一眼，魂儿都被你勾走，你令许多女子受尽情伤，又何必在诗词里矫揉造作？还‘愁病相仍’。诸位都瞧瞧，她那张脸白里透红，气色好得很，真是无病呻 吟，博取他人同情罢了。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu tipped a glance toward the friend sitting diagonally across from her.
[ZH] “说得好！”乔梦容赞道，“尤其是‘善用媚术’四个字，最适合幽栖居士，她能化有形为无形，不知不觉中勾走男子的魂儿，苗夫人和白夫人可得把自己的夫君看好，你们三个虽没成亲，听苗夫人说也刚定过婆家，也需将自己的未婚夫婿看好。”
[EN] Catching the signal, that young woman set down her brush. "Princess Consort Yi, ladies—just now Her Highness used the phrase *wallowing in fake anguish*. I couldn't think of a sharper verdict, so let me use that as my theme to dissect Youqi Jushi's lyric." Raising her wine cup with theatrical mock-courtesy, she nodded at Qianli Xun. "My apologies in advance, Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 众人回道：“仪王妃叮嘱得极是，我等牢记在心。”
[EN] Qianli Xun didn't spare her a blink. *Have your fun running your mouths,* she thought. *Once every last one of you runs out of spit and venom, I'll take you apart one by one.*
[ZH] 苦荞心中骂道：“仪王妃与这些人实在过分！如此不堪入耳的话都能说出口。”
[EN] Draining her cup, the young woman set it down and launched in. "Late spring is still gorgeous weather, yet instead of celebrating the season's beauty, Youqi Jushi stands around moaning that *standing lone, my spirit hurts*, and whines about *a light cold*—how affected! With a reputation already in tatters—plopping herself right into a man's lap in public without a shred of shame—she still has the nerve to write, *Who can see how tears have washed off half my makeup*, acting as though she's the poor wronged victim! Strangers might think she's nursing a broken heart, whereas those of us who see right through her know she's a master of seduction with flirtatious eyes that steal a man's wits in a single glance. After breaking so many other women's hearts, why put on this simpering act in verse—claiming *sorrow and sickness have joined hands*? Look at her cheeks, ladies! Rosy and glowing with health! Pure fake anguish cooked up to fish for sympathy."
[ZH] 苦荞看向小姐，她看似面色平静，实则努力忍着怒气，不发作则罢，若发作只怕不会顾及仪王妃的身份。
[EN] "Well said!" Qiao Mengrong applauded. "Especially *master of seduction*—fits Youqi Jushi to a tee! She hooks men's souls out of thin air without even leaving a mark. Madam Miao and Madam Bai had best watch their husbands closely—and you three girls, since Madam Miao tells me you've just been betrothed, had better keep a close eye on your fiancés too."
[ZH] 苦荞再看她旁边的幼凡，跟她的主人一样满脸得意的笑容。便气呼呼地瞪了她一眼，幼凡将头扭向一边，从鼻子里“哼”了一声。苦荞无奈，继续扯着卷轴心中骂着眼前之人，包括仪王妃在内。
[EN] "Your Highness's warning is wise indeed!" the guests chorused. "We will take it to heart."
[ZH] 其他人说完开始提笔在纸上写着，千里寻身边的姑娘反而搁下笔，转向她：“幽栖居士，我一直很喜欢读你的诗词，今日这首实在不敢恭维。字数、取韵和平仄规律均符合‘减字木兰花’的规则，只是立意差了许多，与易安居士和东坡居士的词比起来相差甚远。仔细想来，不过是写一位闺中怨妇。不知幽栖居士怨什么？是怨所嫁非人，还是别的？”
[EN] *Princess Consort Yi and this pack of vipers have gone way too far!* Kuqiao cursed silently. *How do such filthy words even leave their mouths?* Shooting a worried glance at her mistress, she saw calm on the surface, yet sensed a volcano banked underneath—if Miss blew her lid, even Princess Consort Yi's royal title wouldn't save them. Glancing next at Youfan, who wore the same smug smirk as her mistress, Kuqiao shot her a scorching glare; Youfan merely tossed her head and gave a haughty sniff. Stuck holding the scroll, Kuqiao silently cursed every soul in the room, the Princess Consort included.
[ZH] 千里寻一字未回，用沉默和假装的平静暂时回怼一切。
[EN] As the others scribbled furiously, the young woman seated right next to Qianli Xun put down her brush and turned to face her. "Youqi Jushi, I used to enjoy reading your verses, yet today's piece leaves me speechless. Sure, the character count, rhyme scheme, and tonal patterns obey *Jianzi Mulan Hua*—but the artistic vision is pathetic, leagues beneath Yi'an Jushi or Dongpo Jushi. Strip it down, and it's just a bitter wife whining in her boudoir. What are you so resentful over, Youqi Jushi? Regretting who you married, or something else?"
[ZH] 姑娘面向大家：“我看多半是怨所嫁非人。‘剔尽寒灯梦不成’，幽栖居士已嫁过两回，竟然还像少女一样喜欢做梦，也不知这梦中梦到的人是谁？或者说幽栖居士到底想要梦见何人？何人又是幽栖居士想梦又梦不到的人？是那个让幽栖居士情不自禁‘和衣睡倒人怀’之人？还是那位‘天易见，见伊难’之人？”
[EN] Qianli Xun stayed silent, answering every jab with cool stillness.
[ZH] 一直未开口的姑娘应和道：“说不定是同一个人。”
[EN] The girl pivoted to address the room. "Regretting her husband, most likely! *I trim the lamp's wick till it's gone and dream still does not come*—married twice already and still mooning over dreams like a lovesick maiden! Who is it you're dreaming of—or longing to dream of and failing to reach? The man you couldn't resist *falling asleep fully dressed in his arms* for? Or the one from your line, *It's simple enough to watch the sky, hard to catch a glimpse of him*?" [TN: Translated by Sophie Volpp]
[ZH] 千里寻身边姑娘说：“不是没有这种可能，可若是同一个人，又岂能展示幽栖居士的魅力？”
[EN] The final guest, who hadn't spoken yet, chimed right in. "Maybe it's the exact same man!"
[ZH] 大家听了都呵呵笑起来。
[EN] "Possible," the girl beside Qianli Xun snickered, "though if it were only one man, how could Youqi Jushi showcase her irresistible charm?"
[ZH] 千里寻觉得自己快要忍不住，还有最后一位没说，等她说完，定要将在座每一个人骂得狗血喷头！
[EN] Snickers burst around the room.
[ZH] 大家笑够后，最后一位姑娘，也就是那位应和之人开口说：“该我谈谈看法，大家一一品鉴完，可以得出一个结论，幽栖居士实在有辱江南女词人的美名，不如主动让位，省得诗词写得越来越差，被大家诟病，到时再退出词坛恐怕面子上挂不住。再说说这首词，明明有‘春怨’之名，却看不到一丝春日的气息，只感受到一个‘怨’字，若不知题目只看句子，还以为写的是深秋之情。真没想到堂堂女词人竟然与黄毛小儿一样写起诗词来会跑题，传出去谁会信？可真真实实发生在我们眼前。我好心建议，幽栖居士还是乖乖退出词坛得好。”
[EN] Qianli Xun's patience hovered at the snapping point. One last speaker left—the second she finished, Qianli Xun was going to roast every single person in this hall till their ears smoked!
[ZH] 其他人都在奋笔疾书，记录着。
[EN] When the laughter died down, the last young woman stepped into her turn. "My turn to weigh in. Summing up everyone's critiques, one verdict is obvious: Youqi Jushi is an embarrassment to the title Poetess of Jiangnan. Better step down on your own before your verses sink even lower and you get chased out of literary circles with no face left. Look at this poem's subtitle—*Spring Resentment*—yet there isn't a breath of spring anywhere in it, nothing but *resentment*! Cover the title and anyone would swear it's about late autumn. Who'd believe a famous poetess goes off-topic like a clueless toddler? Yet here it is, right under our noses! Take a bit of friendly advice, Youqi Jushi: quit poetry while you still can."
[ZH] 乔梦容瞟了一眼朱淑真，她装作平静的样子，令她极其不爽：“我比诸位有先见之明，早已看出幽栖居士写诗作词缺乏境界，立意不高，便将我搜集过的所有她写的诗词统统烧毁，建议大家效仿。”
[EN] Brushes flew across the remaining sheets, recording every barb.
[ZH] 众人立刻放下笔，恭敬地回道：“我等必然效仿仪王妃！”
[EN] Eyeing Zhu Shuzhen's unflappable mask with growing irritation, Qiao Mengrong cut in. "I showed better foresight than all of you. I saw ages ago that Youqi Jushi's verses lacked vision and depth, so I burned every single poem of hers I had ever collected. I suggest you all do the same."
[ZH] 乔梦容将手一挥，幼凡拽过卷轴，卷起来，回到苗知瑜身后。
[EN] Every guest dropped her brush and bowed in unison. "We will follow Princess Consort Yi's example!"
[ZH] 手中的卷轴冷不防被拽走，苦荞很生气，又不能发作，满心憋屈走回到小姐身后站好。
[EN] With a wave of Qiao Mengrong's hand, Youfan yanked the scroll out of Kuqiao's grip, rolled it shut, and stepped back behind Miao Zhiyu. Caught off guard by the rude snatch, Kuqiao bristled with pent-up rage, biting her tongue as she marched back to stand behind her mistress.
[ZH] 乔梦容问：“幽栖居士可有话说？”
[EN] "Does Youqi Jushi have anything to say?" Qiao Mengrong asked.
[ZH] 主动给她说话的机会再好不过，也好将心中的怒气发泄一番，重要的是要帮朱淑真正名，省得一不小心穿越而来，给她丢了江南女词人的美称。
[EN] Handing her the floor? Nothing could suit Qianli Xun better. Time to vent the bile churning in her gut—and more importantly, clear the real Zhu Shuzhen's literary name so her transmigration didn't trash the Poetess of Jiangnan's legacy!
[ZH] 千里寻站起来，走到中间，向梦容行礼，此时她心甘情愿地接受“仪王妃”的称呼。
[EN] Standing up, Qianli Xun strode to the center of the room and bowed to Mengrong—for the first time, fully accepting the cold gulf of the title *Princess Consort Yi*.
[ZH] “仪王妃在上，我今日应邀前来，本以为是一场友好的诗会，没想到大家都将矛头指向我，一个一个言辞犀利，将我说得一无是处，我一直忍到现在，已忍无可忍，不回怼几句心中不畅快。”
[EN] "Princess Consort Yi, I accepted your invitation expecting a friendly poetry gathering. Instead, every single speaker trained her knives on me, ripping me apart until I wasn't worth a copper. I've bit my tongue up to now, and I've reached my limit—if I don't fire back, I'll choke on it."
[ZH] “难道你也想怼我不成？”
[EN] "Oh? Do you mean to fire back at *me* too?"
[ZH] “仪王妃说得对，我自然毫无怨言，不对之处还请让我自证清白。”
[EN] "Whatever Your Highness spoke true, I accept without complaint. Wherever you spoke false, allow me to clear my name."
[ZH] “哼！好个自证清白！有什么不满随便说。”
[EN] "Humph! Clear your name, indeed! Say whatever you please." Qiao Mengrong flashed the guests a knowing smile, and the women smiled right back.
[ZH] 乔梦容说完，用微笑向众人示意，大家都心知肚明，也以微笑回应。
[EN] Qianli Xun swung straight onto Miao Zhiyu. "Madam Miao, you and I attended Young Master Dongfang's poetry salons together plenty of times. Why didn't you ever trash my poetry back then?"
[ZH] 千里寻先面对苗知瑜：“苗夫人，你我二人经常参加东方公子的诗会，那时怎不见你在诗会上诟病我的诗词？”
[EN] "Back then, your poetry was decent enough," Miao Zhiyu countered.
[ZH] 苗知瑜回道：“那时你的诗词还算不错。”
[EN] "What a joke! My poetry has *always* been good—the only thing that changed is your attitude toward me! Just because Wenyuan and I had a past, you've been nursing a grudge! Don't forget this, Madam Miao: if I hadn't stepped aside and let Wenyuan go, you wouldn't even be sitting in the Qiao Manor as Young Madam today!"
[ZH] “笑话！我的诗词一直都不错！是你对我态度和想法改变了而已。只怪我与文渊有过一段情，你心中不爽，才对我心存芥蒂。今日你可记好了，若不是我主动放弃文渊，恐怕你也成不了乔家少夫人。”
[EN] "You—!" Flushed with rage, Miao Zhiyu grabbed her brush and scribbled aloud, *"Youqi Jushi flies into a shameful rage!"*
[ZH] “你！……”苗知瑜被气到，边写边说，“幽栖居士，恼羞成怒。”
[EN] "Write whatever you want—today I'm tearing into you till I've had my fill!" Qianli Xun snatched up the wine cup on Miao Zhiyu's table and poured fermented rice wine straight across her fresh ink. "You call yourself a literary talent who's read loads of classics and attended dozens of salons, yet today you're talking like a complete idiot!"
[ZH] “随你怎么写，我今日定要骂到爽为止。”千里寻端起苗知瑜桌上酒杯，将里面的酒酿倒在她刚刚写的字上，“你怎么说也是才女，读过不少书，也多次参加诗会，今日却跟个白痴一样！”
[EN] "You—!" Miao Zhiyu slammed her brush down. "Youfan, clean this up right now and bring fresh paper!"
[ZH] “你！……”苗知瑜将笔搁下，“幼凡，赶紧收拾一下，重新更换纸张。”
[EN] Youfan scrambled forward to whisk away the soaked sheets, mop the tabletop dry with a cloth, and lay out fresh paper.
[ZH] 幼凡上前拿走湿了的纸张，用布子把桌子擦干净，又将干净的纸放好。
[EN] Setting the cup down with a sharp *clack*, Qianli Xun turned to face the entire room. "The *Book of Songs* is packed with refrains and repeated stanzas—a classic device prized for its echoing musical beauty:
[ZH] 千里寻将酒杯重重放在桌上，面向众人：“《诗经》中重章叠句实在多见，反而成为一种独特的表达手法，带给人一种回环往复之美：桃之 夭夭，灼灼其华。之子于归，宜其室家。桃之 夭夭，有蕡其实。之子于归，宜其家室。桃之 夭夭，其叶蓁蓁。之子于归，宜其家人。……”
[EN] *The peach tree is young and elegant; / Brilliant are its flowers. / This young lady is going to her future home, / And will order well her chamber and house. / The peach tree is young and elegant; / Abundant will be its fruits. / This young lady is going to her future home, / And will order well her house and chamber. / The peach tree is young and elegant; / Luxuriant are its leaves. / This young lady is going to her future home, / And will order well her family.*" [TN: Translated by James Legge]
[ZH] 白梅若插嘴道：“你写的句子岂能跟《诗经》中的诗句相比？”
[EN] Bai Meiruo jumped in. "How dare you compare your little lines to the *Book of Songs*?"
[ZH] 千里寻转向白梅若：“现在你最好闭嘴！待会儿有你哭的时候！”
[EN] Qianli Xun whipped around to lock eyes with her. "You'd better shut your mouth right now—your turn to cry is coming up next!"

#CH 255
[ZH] 白梅若撇撇嘴，提起笔：“什么大家闺秀，就写你粗鲁又无礼！哼！”
[ZH] 千里寻懒得理会，继续面向苗知瑜：“苗夫人不会连《诗经》都没读过？”
[ZH] 苗知瑜露出骄傲的神色：“当然读过，我十三岁时，便能倒背如流。”
[ZH] “倒背如流又如何？感受不出其中的美来，背再多也不过给脑子里装些文字垃圾而已。”千里寻瞧着苗知瑜气愤的面庞，甚是开心，“古诗十九首苗姑娘也应该听说过，第一首诗中首句‘行行重行行’，不也用了重复的手法？一句五个字，‘行’字出现四次，苗姑娘难道也要批判写这首诗的文人也是才尽词穷？”
[ZH] 苗知瑜十分不屑：“古诗十九首乃五言之冠冕，地位无比尊贵，幽栖居士的句子如何能与其相比？”
[ZH] “我写的句子怎敢与五言之祖相比？我想说的是重复的手法。苗夫人是才女都搞不懂，在座诸位恐怕更不懂，当然仪王妃定是懂得，可她身份尊贵，不便开口，那就由我在此肩负起扫盲的重任。”
[EN] Bai Meiruo curled her lip and snatched up her brush. "Call yourself a refined lady? I'm writing down *vulgar and ill-mannered*! Humph!"
[ZH] 听到“扫盲”二字，众人十分不满，都撇撇嘴。
[EN] Ignoring her, Qianli Xun turned back to Miao Zhiyu. "Don't tell me Madam Miao has never even read the *Book of Songs*?"
[ZH] 千里寻面向大家，她清楚在座诸位心中明明懂得，都是故意歪曲而已，这种情况下更应该替朱淑真的句子正名。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu lifted her chin with pride. "Of course I've read it. I could recite it front to back by age thirteen."
[ZH] “行行重行行，前后两个‘行行’意思上尽管一致，表达情感的程度差别甚大，后者显然更加沉痛。我写的句子有异曲同工之妙：独行独坐，独唱独酬还独卧。一个人独行时尚可与风景相伴，倒也有一种风雅孤寂之美；独坐家中无人陪伴畅聊，心思无从表达，有种落寞孤寂之感；干脆唱首歌吧，却无人聆听，有种知音难觅的孤寂之叹；自己给自己敬酒，则有种愁闷难遣的孤寂之悲；到了晚上，一个人独卧，床铺凄冷，又有种黯然泪下的孤寂之痛。”
[EN] "And what good is reciting it front to back if you can't grasp a shred of its beauty? Cramming lines into your skull without understanding them just turns your brain into a trash bin of dead words!" Watching fury darken Miao Zhiyu's cheeks felt downright delicious. "Surely Miss Miao has also heard of the *Nineteen Old Poems*. The very opening line of the first poem—*On and on, always on and on* [TN: Translated by Arthur Waley]—also uses repetition! Five characters in that one line, and the character *xing* (行, meaning 'to go on') appears four times! Are you going to lecture that poet for running dry of words too?"
[ZH] 千里寻又转向苗知瑜：“如此解释，苗夫人还会认为我是才尽词穷吗？”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu sniffed with disdain. "The *Nineteen Old Poems* are the crown jewel of five-character verse—unmatched in prestige. How could Youqi Jushi's lines possibly be mentioned in the same breath?"
[ZH] 苗知瑜没有言语，心中冷笑：“我当然明白你的词甚妙，谁让你得罪仪王妃。说得那么好，我还是会给你记下才尽词穷四个字。”苗知瑜提笔写下四字。
[EN] "Who's comparing my lines to the ancestor of five-character poetry? I'm talking about the *technique* of repetition! If even a 'literary talent' like Madam Miao can't wrap her head around it, the rest of you must be even more clueless. Of course, Princess Consort Yi certainly understands, only her exalted rank makes it beneath her to lecture—so I'll gladly shoulder the heavy burden of giving you all a basic literacy lesson!"
[ZH] 是该收拾白梅若的时候，千里寻转向白梅若：“白梅若！”
[EN] At the words *basic literacy lesson*, disgusted scowls broke out across the room.
[ZH] 对方直接唤大名，白梅若很不爽：“有什么指教？”
[EN] Qianli Xun faced them all squarely. She knew perfectly well these women understood the craft and were twisting it out of pure malice—all the more reason to set the record straight for Zhu Shuzhen's verse.
[ZH] “你是我见过的最蠢的女人！”
[EN] "In *On and on, always on and on*, the first *on and on* and the second mean the same thing on the surface, yet their emotional weight is worlds apart—the second cuts far deeper with grief. My lines work the exact same way: *I walk alone, sit alone, sing alone, drink alone, and sleep alone.* Walking alone outdoors, at least you still have the scenery for company—a quiet, graceful solitude. Sitting alone indoors with no one to talk to, your thoughts trapped inside—a bleak, hollow solitude. Trying to lift your spirits by singing, only for no one to listen—the aching solitude of lacking a kindred soul. Pouring wine and toasting yourself—the bitter solitude of sorrow that won't dissolve. And at last, lying down alone at night on a freezing bed—the sharp, tear-soaked solitude of pure heartbreak."
[ZH] 千里寻冷不丁一句话，将白梅若搞得懵住，她缓了缓，怼道：“污蔑！纯属污蔑！”
[EN] She swung back to Miao Zhiyu. "After hearing that, Madam Miao, do you still claim I've run dry of talent and words?"
[ZH] “常言道家丑不可外扬，可你白梅若没事总喜欢把家里的丑事往外说，不是笨又是什么？搞得所有人都知道你家那档子破事，还把老娘给牵扯出来。告诉你，老娘根本瞧不上林觉，若能瞧上还有你什么事？不好好在家想着如何笼络林觉的心，跑到这里来撒野，对老娘的词品头论足，你有什么资格？”
