[EN] Miao Zhiyu kept her mouth shut and sneered inwardly. *Of course I know your lyric is brilliant—blame yourself for crossing Princess Consort Yi! Talk as pretty as you like, I'm still writing down "run dry of talent and words."* Dipping her brush, she wrote those exact characters onto her sheet.
[ZH] “你管得着吗？仪王妃请我来的，她还没批评我，你倒批评起我来？”
[EN] Now it was Bai Meiruo's turn. Qianli Xun pivoted straight toward her. "Bai Meiruo!"
[ZH] 乔梦容见两人吵得激烈，心想：“请你来诗会，既要借你的诗词好好宣泄宣泄不满，更要激得你恼羞成怒，大家也好有诋毁你的说辞。我暂且好好看戏，你今日的表现定将助我毁了你江南女词人美名。”
[EN] Hearing her full name barked like that made Bai Meiruo bristle. "What do you want?"
[ZH] “老娘才懒得管你，可你欺负到老娘的头上，胡说八道，毁老娘声誉，老娘不得不管。”千里寻说着用手抓起白梅若的手腕，稍微使出力气捏去。
[EN] "You are the dumbest woman I have ever met in my life."
[ZH] 白梅若“哎吆”地叫着，疼得流出眼泪：“仪王妃救我！”
[EN] The blunt bolt struck Bai Meiruo speechless for two seconds before she sputtered, "Slander! Pure slander!"
[ZH] 其他三位姑娘心中害怕，若幽栖居士待会儿也捏她们的手腕，岂不疼死？一个个不由自主放下笔用衣袖将手腕盖好。
[EN] "Ever heard the proverb *don't air dirty laundry outside the family gate*? Yet you love blabbing every ugly mess inside your house to half the city—if that isn't sheer stupidity, what is? Now everyone knows your domestic circus, and you even dragged *me* into it! Let me spell it out for you: I couldn't stand Lin Jue! If I'd wanted him, you wouldn't even be in the picture! Instead of staying home figuring out how to win your husband's heart, you strut over here running wild and picking apart *my* poetry—who gave you the nerve?"
[ZH] 乔梦容说：“幽栖居士，这里是诗会，你面前的都是文弱女子，又何必动用武力？”
[EN] "What business is it of yours? Princess Consort Yi invited me—she hasn't scolded me, so where do you get off lecturing me?"
[ZH] 千里寻松开手，面向梦容，她心里清楚今日这些人所作所为不过都是梦容允诺而已，还是强辩道：“世间最厉害的并不是武力，而是口诛笔伐。适才白梅若用‘勾引’二字诋毁我，我捏疼她的手腕已是最轻的惩罚。”
[EN] Watching the shouting match flare up, Qiao Mengrong smiled inwardly. *Inviting you here wasn't just to vent over your poem—it was to goad you into snapping so everyone has fresh ammunition to smear you! Go right ahead—put on a show for me. Your meltdown today will strip you of that "Poetess of Jiangnan" crown for good.*
[ZH] 乔梦容说：“记得我刚才也如此说过你，难道你也要捏我的手腕不成？”
[EN] "I couldn't care less about your mess—until you walk all over my name, spout filth, and trash my reputation! That I *will* answer for!" In one swift move, Qianli Xun grabbed Bai Meiruo's wrist and put just enough squeeze into her fingers.
[ZH] 千里寻沉默片刻，说道：“草民不敢。”
[EN] Bai Meiruo shrieked, tears bursting down her cheeks. "Ow! Princess Consort Yi, save me!"
[ZH] 白梅若眼泪啪嗒地揉着手腕：“仪王妃，您可得替我做主。”
[EN] Panic rippled through the other three young women—if Youqi Jushi squeezed *their* wrists next, their bones would snap! One after another, they dropped their brushes and tucked their wrists under their sleeves.
[ZH] “行了，忍忍就好。幽栖居士继续训斥她们，我正好在一旁看热闹。”
[EN] "Youqi Jushi," Qiao Mengrong said coolly, "this is a poetry salon, and every lady here is fragile and unarmed. Why resort to brute force?"
[ZH] 乔梦容的话像一盆凉水，浇灭千里寻怒骂的热情，也让她明白即使把她们每个人骂一顿又有何意义？关键还在梦容。
[EN] Qianli Xun let go and turned toward Mengrong. She knew full well every stunt today had Mengrong's blessing, yet she argued back anyway. "The deadliest weapon in this world isn't a fist—it's character assassination. Bai Meiruo just smeared me with the word *seduction*. Pinching her wrist is the mildest slap on the wrist she could ask for."
[ZH] “我有话与仪王妃单独说。”
[EN] "As I recall, I used the exact same word about you a moment ago," Qiao Mengrong said. "Are you planning to grab *my* wrist too?"
[ZH] “我与你无话可说。”
[EN] Qianli Xun held silent for a beat. "This commoner would not dare."
[ZH] “以前的梦容单纯善良，绝不会领着一群人说些污秽之语。”
[EN] Rubbing her red wrist with tears still spilling, Bai Meiruo whined, "Princess Consort Yi, you must uphold justice for me!"
[ZH] “你还有脸跟我提以前？”乔梦容站起来，走到朱淑真面前，“以前的梦容已被你亲手毁掉。”
[EN] "Enough, bear with it. Carry on scolding them, Youqi Jushi—I'm quite enjoying the show."
[ZH] “我很珍惜与梦容之间的友情，很怀念与她一起躺在床上聊天儿，一起睡懒觉，一起坐在火炉旁，怀念她在我受伤时前来安慰我。”
[EN] Mengrong's frigid sarcasm hit like a bucket of ice water, quenching all of Qianli Xun's fire. What was the point of tearing into these lackeys? The real knot sat right in front of her—Mengrong.
[ZH] 一幕一幕出现在眼前，乔梦容眼中含着泪水，担心被大家看到，向众人挥挥手。
[EN] "I need to speak with Princess Consort Yi alone."
[ZH] 大家会意，都起身带着丫鬟从后门离开。
[EN] "I have nothing to say to you."
[ZH] 走出后，白梅若一边揉着手腕一边担心地问：“苗夫人，你说仪王妃会不会与幽栖居士和好？”
[EN] "The Mengrong I knew was pure and kind. She would never herd a pack of people together to sling filth like this."
[ZH] 苗知瑜说：“把心放在肚子里，任凭幽栖居士能言善辩，也不可能再恢复到从前，这是夺人所爱的仇恨。你想想，若是有人夺走林统制的心，你还会与她做朋友吗？”
[EN] "You still have the nerve to bring up the past?" Qiao Mengrong rose and stepped right up to Zhu Shuzhen. "You destroyed the old Mengrong with your own two hands!"
[ZH] 白梅若立刻否认：“当然不会！我之所以讨厌她，就是因为我家那位心里还念着她，总拿我跟她比。”
[EN] "I treasure our friendship," Qianli Xun said, her voice softening. "I miss lying on the same bed talking half the night, sleeping in late together, sitting side by side by the brazier—and I miss how you rushed over to comfort me when I was hurt."
[ZH] 其他人听了都在心里偷笑，这位白夫人没事老提自家的事，搞得大家都知道她家里发生些什么。
[EN] Every memory flashed across Qiao Mengrong's eyes, stinging them with tears. Unwilling to let the room see her crack, she waved a hand in dismissal. Taking the cue, the guests rose and filed out through the rear door with their maids.
[ZH] 苗知瑜说：“我们暂且在幽林别居欣赏欣赏风景，等幽栖居士离开，再拜见仪王妃，看她还有什么吩咐。”
[EN] Outside, Bai Meiruo rubbed her bruised wrist with a nervous frown. "Madam Miao, do you think Princess Consort Yi might patch things up with Youqi Jushi?"
[ZH] 众人无不赞同，带着丫鬟去各处转悠。
[EN] "Rest easy," Miao Zhiyu scoffed. "Talk as slick as she wants, they'll never go back to how they were. Stealing the man a woman loves leaves blood-deep hatred. Ask yourself: if another woman stole Commander Lin's heart, would you ever stay friends with her?"
[ZH] 屋内剩下千里寻和乔梦容，还有她们的贴身丫鬟。
[EN] "Not in a million years!" Bai Meiruo snapped. "The whole reason I can't stand her is that my husband still pines for her and measures me against her!"
[ZH] 乔梦容颤抖着嘴唇：“你说这些有什么用？难道想让我感动？然后原谅你？”
[EN] Behind her back, the other women hid their snickers—this Madam Bai really couldn't shut up about her own marriage; five minutes with her and you knew every scandal under her roof.
[ZH] 千里寻走到梦容面前：“你流泪了？”
[EN] "Let's stroll around Youlin Retreat and enjoy the gardens for a bit," Miao Zhiyu suggested. "Once Youqi Jushi leaves, we'll check back in with Princess Consort Yi for further orders."
[ZH] 一滴眼泪滚落下来，乔梦容用丝帕轻轻擦去：“我是流泪，为我付出的友情被人践踏而流泪。”
[EN] Everyone agreed and wandered off down the paths with their maids.
[ZH] “我珍惜还来不及，又怎么会践踏？一切不过是命运捉弄。”
[EN] Inside the hall, only Qianli Xun, Qiao Mengrong, and their two personal maids remained.
[ZH] “哼！命运捉弄，好一个借口！”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong's lips trembled. "What's the point of bringing all that up? Hoping to touch my heart so I'll forgive you?"
[ZH] “今日我毫无保留将我与仪王之间的事向你说明。”
[EN] Qianli Xun stepped closer. "You're crying."
[ZH] “你不仅懂得如何抢走哥哥的心，还能找出理由来，我倒要听听你如何为自己辩解？”
[EN] A single tear slipped down Mengrong's cheek; she dabbed it away with a silk handkerchief. "Yes, I'm crying—crying for the sincere friendship I gave you, only to watch you stomp it into the mud!"
[ZH] “我根本不想辩解，只想让你明白事实，才不至于对我误会太深。”
[EN] "I couldn't cherish it enough—how could I ever stomp on it? Fate played a cruel joke on all of us."
[ZH] “少废话，有话快说。”
[EN] "Humph! *Fate played a joke*—what a convenient excuse!"
[ZH] 千里寻露出回忆往事的表情：“可还记得我失忆之事？”
[EN] "Today I'll tell you everything about Prince Yi and me, holding nothing back."
[ZH] “记得又如何？”
[EN] "Not only do you know how to steal Brother's heart, you've got excuses ready too? Go on—let's hear how you talk your way out of this."
[ZH] “我来自另一个时代，在地震中丧生，被一股力量带到这个时代，醒来时就成为朱淑真，她的记忆我一点儿都没有。”
[EN] "I'm not making excuses. I only want you to know the truth so you won't misunderstand me so deeply."
[ZH] 乔梦容想起哥哥起死回生后也说过同样的话，冷笑道：“你们编造的谎言都一模一样。”
[EN] "Cut the preamble and talk."
[ZH] 原来申简辰也对梦容说过。
[EN] A distant look entered Qianli Xun's eyes. "Do you remember when I lost my memory?"
[ZH] “我说的事实，我知道你难以相信，可的确是事实。”
[EN] "What if I do?"
[ZH] “你写诗作词的本领怎么解释？”
[EN] "I come from another era. I died in an earthquake, and some force pulled me into this world. When I woke up, I was Zhu Shuzhen, without a single shred of her memories."
[ZH] “朱淑真的诗词流传后世，我在那个时代背过不少，凭记忆写出不在话下。”
[EN] Remembering Brother saying the exact same thing after coming back from the dead, Qiao Mengrong gave a cold laugh. "Even the lies you two spin match word for word!"
[ZH] “你编谎话的本领不比你写诗作词的本领差。”
[EN] So Shen Jianchen had told Mengrong too. "It's the truth. I know it sounds impossible to believe, yet every word is real."
[ZH] “仪王起死回生过，活过来的仪王和我来自同一个时代。发生地震时我们正在神柳下比试拳法，地震来临，比试无法继续，眼看神柳就要倒下，他赶过来救我，与我一起丧生。去年冬季我知道仪王就是他，才慢慢产生与以前不一样的情愫来。也许是在陌生的时代遇见熟悉的人才会这样。”
[EN] "Then explain how you write poetry and ci like that!"
[ZH] 乔梦容闭上眼睛，再次挤出两滴泪水。朱淑真终于承认爱上她的哥哥，仔细想想，在捶丸场时她还傻乎乎地真心待她，而她已偷偷喜欢上哥哥。
[EN] "Zhu Shuzhen's poems survived into my era. I memorized heaps of them back then, so writing them down from memory isn't hard."
[ZH] 她睁开眼盯着朱淑真：“你不顾友情，抢走哥哥的心已是事实，何必找诸多借口？别以为你找出许多说辞，我就会同情和理解，告诉你，从今往后，你我形同陌路！永远不可能再成为闺中密友！”
[EN] "Your skill at spinning tall tales rivals your skill at verse."
[ZH] 千里寻也不由红了眼：“不管你如何看待我，你永远是我最好的朋友，为了你，我即使有机会逃离父母之命媒妁之言，也最终放弃，心甘情愿嫁到陈家，嫁给散播我谣言之人。目的就是为逼自己忘记这段不该属于自己的感情。”
[EN] "Prince Yi came back from death—and the man who woke up in his body came from the exact same era as me. When the earthquake struck, we were sparring beneath the Divine Willow. The ground split, the willow started toppling, and he threw himself over to save me—so we died together. Only last winter did I discover Prince Yi was him, and that's when my feelings began to change. Perhaps stumbling across a familiar face in a strange world does that to a person."
[ZH] 乔梦容并没有被感动，冷笑道：“说得你好像很可怜，我才是那个可怜人，你成亲前一晚，哥哥撇下我要带你离开，成亲那日还跑去抢亲，让我沦为笑柄，被全临安城的人笑话，此仇不报，我心中难平。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong squeezed her eyes shut, forcing out two fresh tears. At last Zhu Shuzhen had admitted loving her brother! Thinking back to the chuiwan field, she had treated this woman with wide-open trust while she was already stealing Brother's heart behind her back. Opening her eyes, she drilled a glacial stare into Zhu Shuzhen.
[ZH] 此刻千里寻才真正了解梦容已将伤痛转化为仇恨，她心中为之一颤：“梦容，我该怎么做你才能放下仇恨？”
[EN] "You trampled our friendship and stole Brother's heart—that is the sole fact that matters. Why bury it under fancy stories? Don't imagine a pack of excuses will buy my pity or understanding! Hear me well: from this day on, we are strangers! We will never be friends again!"
[ZH] “除非你消失，或者回到你自己杜撰的那个时代。”乔梦容冷漠的目光中充满杀气，“否则我心头难安，你也别想过得舒坦。”
[EN] Qianli Xun's own eyes reddened. "No matter what you think of me, you'll always be my best friend. For your sake, even when I had a chance to escape my parents' arranged marriage, I walked away from it and willingly married into the Chen family—married the very man who slandered me—just to force myself to give up a love that didn't belong to me."
[ZH] 威胁并不可怕，可怕的是梦容对她的恨。
[EN] Not a flicker of pity touched Qiao Mengrong's face; she only sneered. "Listen to you painting yourself as the martyr! *I* am the victim here! The night before your wedding, Brother abandoned me to run off with you. On your wedding day, he stormed in to steal you away, turning me into the laughingstock of all Lin'an! Until that humiliation is paid back, my heart will never rest."
[ZH] 千里寻小心说道：“你和仪王已成亲，我也老实嫁给陈浩宇，你我各自安好，难道不好吗？”
[EN] Only now did Qianli Xun grasp the horrifying truth: Mengrong's pain had curdled into pure hatred. A chill rippled through her chest. "Mengrong... what do I have to do for you to let go of this hatred?"
[ZH] “不好！只要你在，哥哥就无法忘记你，他忘不了你，我便难安。你要识趣的话要么自缢，要么吞食毒药，要么跳钱塘江，手段任由你选。如若你自己下不了狠手，就等着我如何一步一步将你逼上绝路！”
[EN] "Disappear—or crawl back to that imaginary era you invented!" Murderous frost glinted in Qiao Mengrong's eyes. "Until you do, I'll know no peace—and you won't have a single comfortable day either."
[ZH] 梦容充满恨意的双眼令千里寻不寒而栗，她怀疑有人给梦容下了蛊，要不怎么好好一个人变得可怕？
[EN] The threat didn't frighten Qianli Xun; what chilled her bone-deep was seeing such hatred in Mengrong's eyes.
[ZH] “梦容，你怎么会变得如此狠毒？”
[EN] "You and Prince Yi are already married," Qianli Xun pleaded softly, "and I'm settled down with Chen Haoyu. Can't we each just live our own lives in peace?"
[ZH] “要怪就怪你自己，是你亲手毁了以前的我。”乔梦容走回正位坐下，端出威严来，“说吧，你准备怎么个死法？”
[EN] "No! So long as you draw breath, Brother will never forget you—and so long as he remembers you, I can never breathe easy! If you know what's good for you, pick your own end: hang yourself, swallow poison, or jump into the Qiantang River. Choose whichever method you prefer. If you haven't got the guts to finish yourself, sit back and watch me drive you into a dead end, step by step!"
[ZH] 看样子梦容不可能再原谅她，友情之路真的走到尽头，既如此还是那句话，自己犯的错误还需自己承担相应的后果。
[EN] A shiver crawled down Qianli Xun's spine under that venom-soaked glare. Had someone put a curse on Mengrong? How had such a gentle girl turned this terrifying? "Mengrong, how did you become so ruthless?"
[ZH] “怎么个死法？我从未想过，也不想选，交由老天，我只管做好自己，走好每一步。”
[EN] "Blame yourself—you murdered the girl I used to be." Qiao Mengrong returned to the main seat, settling back into regal coldness. "Well? How do you plan to die?"
[ZH] “既如此，回去耐心等待，我会替老天帮你选一个死法。”
[EN] Forgiveness was dead and buried; their friendship had reached the end of the road. Fine. A person paid for her own mistakes. "How I'll die? Never thought about it, and I'm not choosing. I'll leave that to Heaven. All I'll do is stay true to myself and walk each step straight."
[ZH] 乔梦容的声音冷静中带着冷漠，千里寻只觉两个人之间的距离越来越远，远到想要努力跨出一步都难。
[EN] "In that case, go home and wait. I'll gladly play Heaven and choose your death for you."
[ZH] “拜别……仪王妃。”
[EN] Mengrong's voice fell flat and arctic, stretching the gulf between them until no step could ever cross it.
[ZH] 正门被打开，外面的光线立刻透了进来。也许是刚才在梦容冰冷的话语中浸泡的时间过长，千里寻竟然没有燥热的感觉，反而有股舒适的暖意。
[EN] "Farewell... Princess Consort Yi."
[ZH] 她跨出这道门，走出院子，再坐上马车离开幽林别居。希望而来，失望而归。为何事情会成这样？她在心中不断自问。
[EN] The front doors swung wide, letting daylight flood across the threshold. After soaking so long in Mengrong's freezing hatred, the warm sun outside felt neither harsh nor stifling—just a welcome relief.
[ZH] 苦荞十分担忧：“刚才仪王妃好可怕，小姐，我好担心。”
[EN] She stepped across the sill, crossed the courtyard, and boarded her carriage out of Youlin Retreat. She had come brimming with hope and left empty-handed. *How on earth did it come to this?* the question echoed in her chest all the way home.
[ZH] 千里寻安慰道：“有什么好怕的？她要是能取我性命还能放我走？说明她现在还没有能力取我性命。长得那么柔弱，我一拳就能打倒，只是我打谁都不会打她。再说她一个王妃，也没有调遣临安士兵的权利，想杀我没那么容易。不过是说出来吓唬吓唬人，发泄发泄而已，别太当回事。”
[EN] Beside her, Kuqiao fretted aloud. "Princess Consort Yi looked terrifying just now, Miss! I'm scared to death!"
[ZH] “小姐这么一说我就放心了。”苦荞埋怨道，“要我说，都怪仪王，一直都是他跑来招惹小姐，事情才变得如此复杂。”
[EN] Qianli Xun patted her hand. "What's there to be scared of? If she had the power to kill me, she wouldn't have let me walk out that door. Means she can't touch me yet. Fragile as she is, one punch from me would knock her flat—not that I'd ever lay a finger on her. Besides, a princess consort can't commandeer Lin'an's garrison; knocking me off isn't that easy. She's just venting and trying to rattle me—don't lose sleep over it."
[ZH] “事情已发生，怪谁都没用，只能走一步看一步，兵来将挡水来土掩，只要我不再与他相见，认真过我的日子，便一切都好。”
[EN] "Hearing you say that makes me feel better," Kuqiao sighed, then muttered darkly, "If you ask me, it's all Prince Yi's fault! He's the one who kept coming after you until everything tangled into this mess."
[ZH] 千里寻决定不如试着学习如何接纳陈浩宇。
[EN] "What's done is done. Pointing fingers won't fix anything. We'll take it one step at a time—meet every blow as it comes. So long as I stay away from him and live my own life right, things will settle down." Maybe it really was time she tried learning how to accept Chen Haoyu.
[ZH] 苗知瑜等人逛完回到原处拜见仪王妃。
[EN] Back inside Youlin Retreat, Miao Zhiyu and the others finished their stroll and returned to pay their respects.
[ZH] 乔梦容吩咐道：“你们下去将记录的内容整理，再添油加醋一番，拿到印书局刊印出来，找些下等小民，到处分发，相信不出一个月便摘了她江南女词人的美称。”
[EN] "Take everything you wrote down," Qiao Mengrong ordered, "polish it up, spice it up even sharper, and take it to a printing house to run off booklets. Hire street urchins to hand them out everywhere. Within a month, we'll rip that 'Poetess of Jiangnan' crown straight off her head."
[ZH] 众人回道：“是！”
[EN] "Yes, Your Highness!" they chorused.

#CH 256
[ZH] 回到陈府南门口，千里寻刚走下马车，冲上来一群人，队列两边，从衣着判断应是府衙之人。
[ZH] 正纳闷，瞧见申简辰向这边走来，他的身边跟着白风和两个人，那两人看着面生。他现在是司理参军，应该是与他一起办差之人。
[ZH] 一再叮嘱过自己不要再见眼前之人，还是不期而遇。
[ZH] 申简辰想过好几种遇见千里寻的方式，唯独没有预设是在办理公务之时。见到日思夜想之人，他不由停住脚步，注视一会儿，才走上前。
[ZH] “你……可好？”
[ZH] 他眼中依然饱含感情，与以前相比丝毫不差，难怪梦容今日会如此对待她。
[ZH] 千里寻从心底根本无法对眼前之人生起埋怨之情，谁让她的内心依然还有无法向人道出的情感。已决定要放下他，自然不能再有任何流露。
[ZH] 千里寻恭敬行礼：“仪王殿下，民妇自从嫁到陈家一切安好，劳烦贵人挂念。”
[ZH] 如此充满距离的话语，申简辰早已预料到，还是忍不住说：“我会继续寻找会爬树的猪。”
[ZH] 千里寻心中为之一动，他怎么还如此执着？必须打击两句，让他清醒清醒。
[EN] The moment Qianli Xun stepped out of her carriage at Chen Manor's south gate, a column of men rushed up and flanked both sides of the entrance—yamen runners, judging by their uniforms.
[ZH] “殿下的爱好实在独特，民妇难以理解。别说我没见过会爬树的猪，即使真有，殿下找到又能如何？不如珍惜当下，不要把时间浪费在毫无意义的事情上。”
[EN] Blinking in puzzlement, she saw Shen Jianchen striding straight toward her, flanked by Bai Feng and two unfamiliar men—colleagues from his new Judicial Investigator post, no doubt. She had just sworn up and down never to see this man again, and here they bumped right into each other.
[ZH] “无论你说什么，都无法动摇我的想法，否则我都不知道来到这个时代还有什么意义？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen had imagined a dozen ways he might run into Qianli Xun again, never once picturing an official case call. Seeing the woman who filled his waking thoughts stopped him dead in his tracks; he gazed at her for a quiet beat before stepping forward.
[ZH] “殿下身为仪王又是临安节度使，现在又是司理参军，只要用好其中任何一个身份，都可做出有意义之事，不愁在这个时代实现不了自身的价值。”
[EN] "Are you... well?"
[ZH] “我当然会做有意义的事，但我依然不会放弃。”
[EN] The devotion burning in his eyes hadn't dimmed by a spark—no wonder Mengrong had lashed out like a cornered beast today. Deep down, Qianli Xun could never bring herself to resent him, since her own heart still harbored feelings she couldn't speak aloud. Yet she had vowed to let go, so not a crack could show.
[ZH] 正说着，陈江林从陈府出来，行礼道：“不知仪王驾到，有失远迎。”
[EN] She dropped into a formal, respectful curtsy. "Your Highness Prince Yi. Since marrying into the Chen family, this humble wife has fared very well. Thank you for your gracious concern."
[ZH] 仪王显然有公务，千里寻不便多言。已说得够多，再说下去恐怕被人误会。今日诗会被人辱骂之语还萦绕在耳边，往后需老实窝在陈府写话本，少出去招惹是非。
[EN] He had braced himself for that polite wall of ice, yet the words still slipped out of him. "I'm going to keep looking for a pig that can climb trees."
[ZH] 千里寻又屈膝行礼后走进陈府。
[EN] Her heart gave a sudden lurch. How could he still cling to that? She had to dash cold water on him and snap him awake. "Your Highness has quite an eccentric hobby—beyond my simple understanding. Forget whether I've ever seen a tree-climbing pig; even if one did exist and you found it, what good would it do? Better cherish the life right in front of you instead of wasting time on pointless pursuits."
[ZH] 苦荞趁机瞧向白风，白风对她笑笑，张嘴摆着口型，说些她猜不明白的话。苦荞皱皱眉头，不予理会，快步跟上小姐。
[EN] "Nothing you say can change my mind. Otherwise, I don't even know what meaning my coming to this era has left."
[ZH] 申简辰目送千里寻进入陈府，才看向陈江林：“陈老爷，我今儿是以司理参军的身份来你府上寻一人。”
[EN] "You are Prince Yi, Military Commissioner of Lin'an, and now Judicial Investigator too. Put any one of those titles to real use, and you'll accomplish plenty of meaningful work—no shortage of ways to prove your worth in this era."
[ZH] 陈江林仍旧弯腰而立：“不知仪王要寻何人？”
[EN] "Oh, I'll do meaningful work," he answered. "Still, I'm not giving up."
[ZH] “听说陈府的兴隆阁有位管账的方管事名叫方计远？”
[EN] Right then, Chen Jianglin hurried out of the manor and bowed low. "I was unaware Your Highness had arrived—forgive me for failing to greet you at the gate!"
[ZH] “方管事是叫方计远，不知仪王找他何事？”
[EN] Prince Yi was clearly here on official business, and she had already said more than enough; lingering risked wagging tongues. With today's vicious salon insults still ringing in her ears, she meant to hole up inside Chen Manor writing huaben and stay clear of trouble. Dipping another curtsy, Qianli Xun turned and walked through the gate.
[ZH] “此处不便多说，陈老爷先带我进去，把方计远唤来，有些事需要当面问他。”
[EN] Kuqiao stole a glance at Bai Feng, who grinned and mouthed silent words she couldn't make head or tail of. Frowning, she ignored him and hurried after her mistress.
[ZH] “仪王，里边请。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen watched Qianli Xun disappear inside before turning to Chen Jianglin. "Master Chen, I'm here today in my capacity as Judicial Investigator looking for someone in your household."
[ZH] 陈江林不敢怠慢，将仪王请进兴隆阁的会客室，吩咐伙计摆上茶点和水果，然后派人去叫方管事。
[EN] Still bent in deference, Chen Jianglin asked, "Whom does Your Highness seek?"
[ZH] 方管事正在打算盘核对账目，有伙计前来传话：“方管事，大老爷请你去会客室。”
[EN] "I hear Xinglong Pavilion has an accountant named Steward Fang—full name Fang Jiyuan?"
[ZH] 方管事停下打算盘的动作：“可知找我何事？”
[EN] "Steward Fang's name is indeed Fang Jiyuan. May I ask what Your Highness wants with him?"
[ZH] 伙计说：“是司理院的人找你。”
[EN] "Not a conversation for the street. Lead me inside and summon Fang Jiyuan—I have questions to ask him face to face."
[ZH] 方管事纳闷：“司理院的人找我何事？我又没做什么伤天害理的事。”
[EN] "Right this way, Your Highness!"
[ZH] 纳闷归纳闷，放下账目，乖乖来到会客室。
[EN] Wasting no time, Chen Jianglin ushered the prince into the reception room of Xinglong Pavilion, ordered clerks to bring tea, pastries, and fruit, and sent someone to fetch Steward Fang.
[ZH] 只见会客室的门口站着差役，一个差役问：“你是方计远？”
[EN] Steward Fang was clicking away at his abacus checking ledgers when a clerk hurried in. "Steward Fang, Eldest Master wants you in the reception room."
[ZH] 方管事回道：“正是。”
[EN] His fingers paused over the beads. "Know what it's about?"
[ZH] 差役进去禀报完，出来说：“大人让你进去。”
[EN] "Folks from the Judicial Office are asking for you."
[ZH] 方计远小心进入。
[EN] "The Judicial Office? What do they want with me? I haven't done anything crooked!" Baffled as he was, he laid down his ledger and headed straight over.
[ZH] 正面坐着一位年轻英俊的公子，从那穿着看不出是官府之人，既坐在正位应该是参军大人。两边分别站着两人，右边那位他见过，是司理院的都头。下位的左边坐着大老爷陈江林，右边坐着的应该是师爷，手中执笔准备记录案情。
[EN] Constables flanked the doorway; one stopped him. "Are you Fang Jiyuan?"
[ZH] 方计远心中不由紧张，难道自己不小心犯了案子？仔细回想，没做过什么错事。赶紧上前拜见：“大人，老爷，不知唤我何事？”
[EN] "Yes, that's me."
[ZH] 申简辰问：“你是方计远？”
[EN] The constable stepped in to announce him, then returned. "His Honor says go on in."
[ZH] “小的是方计远。”
[EN] Fang Jiyuan stepped inside with trepidation. Seated in the main chair was a handsome young nobleman whose robes didn't look like standard yamen attire—yet sitting center stage meant he had to be the Investigator. Flanking him stood two men, the one on the right familiar as the Judicial Office's chief constable. On the lower left sat Eldest Master Chen Jianglin, and on the right sat a clerk holding a brush poised over paper.
[ZH] “家中可有四十岁左右的女人失踪？”
[EN] Nerves churned in Fang Jiyuan's stomach. Had he somehow gotten tangled in a lawsuit? Racking his brain, he couldn't think of a single offense. He hurried forward to bow. "Your Honor, Master—may I ask why you summoned me?"
[ZH] 方计远想想，自家夫人正是这个年纪，确实不在家，只是回了娘家，于是回道：“没有。”
[EN] "You are Fang Jiyuan?" Shen Jianchen asked.
[ZH] 申简辰向旁边的温信看一眼，温信会意，拿出一个玉镯，举在手中，展示给大家。
[EN] "Yes, Your Honor, I'm Fang Jiyuan."
[ZH] 申简辰继续问：“可认得此物？”
[EN] "Is a woman around forty years old missing from your household?"
[ZH] 方计远瞅去，那玉镯翠绿，看着眼熟，便走近瞧一眼，心中更加紧张，将那玉镯接在手中，翻看一遍，当看到镯子上刻的一个“玉”字，急切地问：“此玉镯正是我家娘子的，怎么会在大人手中？”
[EN] Fang Jiyuan blinked—his wife was right around forty, and she wasn't home, though she had only gone back to visit her parents. "No, Your Honor."
[ZH] 申简辰说：“确定是你家娘子之物？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen glanced at Wen Xin beside him. Taking the cue, Wen Xin held up a jade bangle for everyone to see.
[ZH] “小人确定，我家娘子过四十岁生辰时，我买来此物送她，她的名字中有一‘玉’字，我便麻烦工匠刻在玉镯上。”
[EN] "Do you recognize this?" Shen Jianchen asked.
[ZH] “既然是你娘子之物，为何要说没有女人丢失？”
[EN] Spotting the vivid emerald jade, Fang Jiyuan leaned in with rising dread. Taking the bangle into his hands, he turned it over—and froze at the character *yu* (玉, jade) carved inside. "This bangle belongs to my wife! How did it end up in Your Honor's hands?"
[ZH] “我娘子只是回娘家而已。”
[EN] "You're certain it's your wife's?"
[ZH] “你娘子已被人杀害，抛尸在竹林边的溪水旁，现在尸体正放在司理院的停尸房，仵作在停尸房继续查验尸体时，从她身上搜得。奇怪，你家娘子为何不将手镯戴在手上，偏偏装进怀里？”
[EN] "Positive! I bought it for her fortieth birthday. Her given name has the character *yu* in it, so I paid the jeweler to engrave it right on the jade."
[ZH] 方计远听到娘子被人杀害，震惊之余，伤心不已，不由跪在地上，摸着玉镯哭起来：“呜呜……，我娘子平日舍不得戴，只有回娘家时才会揣进怀里，呜呜……”
[EN] "If it's hers, why did you claim no woman was missing from your home?"
[ZH] 陈江林安慰道：“方管事，事已至此，哭也没用，还是配合大人查找凶手。”
[EN] "My wife only went to visit her parents' home!"
[ZH] 申简辰问：“为何你会说你娘子回了娘家？”
[EN] "Your wife has been murdered," Shen Jianchen said flatly. "Her body was dumped beside the stream near the bamboo grove and is now lying in the Judicial Office morgue. When the coroner inspected her body again there, he found this tucked inside her robe. Strange—why didn't she wear the bangle on her wrist instead of hiding it inside her clothes?"
[ZH] 方管事止住哭声：“前日我从兴隆阁忙完事回到家中，不见我家娘子，便到处找，没找到人。正生气时，我家下人孙二告诉我她回娘家几日。我埋怨两句，便没说什么。”
[EN] Shock gave way to crushing grief. Fang Jiyuan collapsed to his knees, clutching the bangle as tears poured down his face. "My wife hated wearing it for daily chores—she only ever tucked it inside her blouse when going back to her parents' house... *sob*..."
[ZH] 申简辰敏锐地感觉到孙二不对：“孙二？他说的话你为何如此相信？”
[EN] Chen Jianglin tried to steady him. "What's done is done, Steward Fang. Crying won't bring her back—help His Honor catch the killer."
[ZH] “孙二是我远房亲戚的孩子，家住在山里，来临安城混口饭吃，我家正好缺下人，便把他留下，帮夫人干些粗活。这孩子嘴巴甜，整日叔叔婶婶地叫着，我就没把他当外人，他的话自然信。”
[EN] "Why did you believe she had gone to her parents' house?" Shen Jianchen pressed.
[ZH] “此人可还在你家中？”
[EN] Swallowing his sobs, Fang Jiyuan answered, "Two days ago, I came home from Xinglong Pavilion and couldn't find her anywhere. Just as I was getting irritated, my house servant Sun Er told me she'd gone home to her parents for a few days. I grumbled a bit and thought nothing more of it."
[ZH] “自然在，他也没别的去处。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen's instincts zeroed straight in on that name. "Sun Er? Why did you take his word so readily?"
[ZH] 申简辰迅速起身：“陈老爷，我需到方计远家中走一趟，将兴隆阁的管事带走，你应该不会反对吧？”
[EN] "Sun Er is a distant relative's boy from the hills. Came into Lin'an looking to earn his keep, and since my household was short-handed, I took him in to help my wife with heavy chores. He's got a sweet tongue—always calling us Uncle and Auntie—so I treated him like family and never doubted a word he said."
[ZH] 陈江林赶紧站起来行礼：“仪王尽管带走，不必知会我。”
[EN] "Is he still at your house?"
[ZH] 陈江林侧身站立，恭送贵人。等贵人走出，才又跟上，准备将贵人送至大门口。
[EN] "He must be—he's got nowhere else to go."
[ZH] 刚走出兴隆阁，申简辰突然停住脚步对白风说：“不必继续跟着，去见你想见的人。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen rose at once. "Master Chen, I need to visit Fang Jiyuan's house. You don't object if I take your Xinglong Pavilion steward along?"
[ZH] 白风高兴道：“谢王爷！”
[EN] Chen Jianglin sprang up and bowed. "Take him as you please, Your Highness—no need to ask my leave!" Stepping aside to let the prince exit first, he followed along to see him to the main gate.
[ZH] 白风目送着王爷等人往陈府外而去，他则站在原地不知该如何去找苦荞，第一次进陈府，道路一点儿都不熟悉。
[EN] Just outside Xinglong Pavilion, Shen Jianchen paused and looked at Bai Feng. "No need to tag along. Go see who you want to see."
[ZH] 正犹豫着，走来一个下人：“白侍卫是想找二少夫人身边的丫鬟苦荞吗？”
[EN] Bai Feng's face lit up. "Thank you, Your Highness!"
[ZH] 白风不解：“是的，你怎么知道？”
[EN] Watching his master and the constables head out the gate, Bai Feng stood in place scratching his head—his first time inside Chen Manor, and he had no clue where to find Kuqiao.
[ZH] 下人回道：“我家大老爷说的，吩咐我帮您将苦荞叫出来。”
[EN] Just then a servant walked up. "Is Guard Bai looking for Kuqiao, Second Young Madam's maid?"
[ZH] 白风觉得这位陈老爷真不简单，连他跟苦荞的事情都知道：“你去告诉苦荞，我在那个亭子里等她。”
[EN] "Yes—how did you know?"
[ZH] 下人去后不多会儿，苦荞来到亭子里，见白风正站在亭子里赏花，没好气地说：“你不跟着王爷去忙，找我干什么？”
[EN] "Eldest Master guessed and ordered me to fetch her for you."
[ZH] 白风转过身：“你怎么看上去不高兴？”
[EN] *This Master Chen doesn't miss a trick—even knows about me and Kuqiao,* Bai Feng marveled. "Tell Kuqiao I'll wait for her in that pavilion over there."
[ZH] “有什么好高兴的？你跟着王爷跑去什么司理院，以后还不得有很多事情忙？想见你都难。”
[EN] A few minutes later, Kuqiao marched into the pavilion, where Bai Feng stood admiring the flowers. "Shouldn't you be off working with Prince Yi?" she asked tartly. "What do you want with me?"
[ZH] “原来因为这个，放心，刚到司理院一切还没理顺，等过了这一阵，我还会和以前一样三天两头过来见你。你瞧，今日王爷主动允许我留下，他对我多好。”
[EN] Bai Feng spun around. "Why the long face?"
[ZH] 苦荞走到亭子中间，叹口气：“唉，仪王的小心思以为我不知？他不过是想让你从我口中打听些有关我家小姐的事。说得好听些，叫痴情。说得难听些，叫风流。都已成亲还惦记我家小姐，他是男子又是王爷，别人不敢将他怎样，可我家小姐是女子，名声对女子多重要？你回去告诉仪王，我家小姐已打算跟姑爷好好过日子，让他别再惦记。”
[EN] "What's there to smile about? You followed Prince Yi off to that Judicial Office—you'll be buried in work from now on, and I'll barely ever get to see you!"
[ZH] 白风警惕地瞧瞧周围：“小声些，小心隔墙有耳，被人听去，对幽栖居士不利。”
[EN] "So *that's* what's eating you! Relax—we just moved into the Judicial Office, so everything's still chaotic. Once things settle down, I'll be sneaking over every few days just like before. Look, His Highness even told me to stay behind today—isn't he good to me?"
[ZH] 苦荞才知自己因为不满有所失言，也向亭子周围瞧一遍，还好没有别人。
[EN] Stepping to the center of the pavilion, Kuqiao let out a long sigh. "Oh, please—think I don't see right through Prince Yi's little scheme? He only let you stay so you could pump me for news about Miss! Call it *devotion* if you want to be polite; call it *philandering* if you're honest! He's already married, yet he keeps hanging onto my mistress. He's a man and a prince—nobody dares lay a hand on him—whereas Miss is a woman, and a woman's reputation is her life! Go back and tell Prince Yi that Miss has decided to settle down and live right with her husband, so tell him to leave her alone."
[ZH] 白风说：“放心，我会把你的话转达给王爷。”
[EN] Bai Feng cast a wary eye around the garden. "Keep your voice down! Walls have ears—if someone overhears that, it'll hurt Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 苦荞轻声说：“是我家小姐的话。”
[EN] Realizing frustration had made her careless, Kuqiao glanced around too; thankfully nobody was nearby.
[ZH] “幽栖居士能这么想，说明她人品不错。”
[EN] "Don't worry," Bai Feng said. "I'll pass your words along to His Highness."
[ZH] “说什么呢？我家小姐向来人品不错，只是别人总是误解她，把她说得一无是处，就今日……”
[EN] "They're *Miss's* words," Kuqiao murmured.
[ZH] 苦荞话说一半，想起小姐的叮嘱，不敢继续往下说。
[EN] "If Youqi Jushi truly feels that way, it speaks well of her character."
[ZH] 白风好奇地问：“今日怎么了？”
[EN] "What kind of nonsense is that? My mistress has *always* had sterling character! It's everyone else who keeps twisting things and slandering her—just like today..." Catching herself mid-sentence, she remembered Qianli Xun's strict orders and bit her tongue.
[ZH] “没什么。”苦荞掩饰道，迅速转移话题，“不要老说小姐的事，说说我们吧。”
[EN] Bai Feng caught the slip. "What happened today?"
[ZH] 白风拉起苦荞的手：“王爷已成亲，我也想向你提亲，娶你过门。”
[EN] "Nothing!" Kuqiao covered up, swerving straight off the subject. "Enough about Miss—let's talk about us."
[ZH] 苦荞羞涩后，为难道：“我要是嫁给你，岂不是要跟你住在幽林别居？仪王妃是那里的主人，她肯定不会喜欢我，到时我恐怕没好日子过？”
[EN] Bai Feng reached out and took her hand. "His Highness is wed now, so I want to ask for your hand and marry you too."
[ZH] “仪王妃人很好。”
[EN] A blush warmed Kuqiao's cheeks, then faded into a troubled frown. "If I marry you, won't I have to move into Youlin Retreat with you? Princess Consort Yi runs that house—she's bound to despise me. Won't my life there be pure misery?"
[ZH] “那是以前，现在不见得，人总是会变。”
[EN] "Princess Consort Yi is a kind soul."
[ZH] “难道你不想嫁给我？”
[EN] "Maybe she used to be. Not anymore—people change."
[ZH] “我当然想！只是现在……，要不我们再等等？等仪王妃跟小姐的关系变好。”
[EN] "Don't you want to marry me?"
[ZH] “那得等到什么时候？如果好不了呢？”
[EN] "Of course I do! Just... not right now. Can't we wait a little longer? Wait until things thaw between Princess Consort Yi and Miss."
[ZH] “我也不知道，起码要变得不像现在这样。”
[EN] "How long would *that* take? What if they never make up?"
[ZH] “她们现在不过是互相不往来，其实也没什么，是你想得太多。”
[EN] "I don't know—at least until things aren't as bad as they are right now."
[ZH] 小姐叮嘱过，不能将今日诗会之事告诉白风，苦荞欲言又止。白风哪里清楚，仪王妃连逼死小姐的心都有，她们的关系已经十分糟糕。
[EN] "They're only avoiding each other—hardly the end of the world. You're overthinking this."
[ZH] “你怎么了？”
[EN] Bound by her promise not to breathe a word about today's salon, Kuqiao swallowed back the truth. How could Bai Feng know Princess Consort Yi literally wanted her mistress dead?
[ZH] “白风，我们再等等，小姐正遇到难处，我不能撒手，等她过了这个坎儿，我再嫁给你。”
[EN] "What's wrong?" he asked, noticing her silence.
[ZH] 白风不解：“幽栖居士的拳头多厉害，什么坎儿过不去！多你一个不多，少你一个不少。再说她能有什么坎儿？”
[EN] "Please wait a little longer, Bai Feng. Miss is facing a rough patch right now—I can't walk out on her. Once she gets over this hurdle, I'll marry you."
[ZH] “你不懂，有些事不是用拳头就能解决。总之，现在不是我嫁给你的最佳时机，再给我些时日，好不好？”
[EN] Bai Feng frowned in confusion. "With Youqi Jushi's fists, what hurdle could stop her? One maid more or less won't matter—and what trouble could she even be in?"
[ZH] 白风看着苦荞诚恳的样子，很是心疼：“好吧，我答应你，但不要让我等得太久。”
[EN] "You don't understand. Some problems can't be punched away. All I know is now isn't the right time for me to marry you. Give me a little more time, please?"
[ZH] 苦荞点点头，想起仪王妃让小姐选择死亡的方式，她脸上不由挂满愁容。
[EN] Seeing the honest plea in her eyes softened his heart. "All right, I promise. Just don't make me wait too long."
[ZH] 话说申简辰与众人来到方管事家中，前前后后找那位叫孙二的下人，却已不见踪影，到他的住处一瞧，个人之物已收拾干净。
[EN] Kuqiao nodded, though remembering Princess Consort Yi telling Miss to pick her own way to die left a heavy cloud over her face.
[ZH] 方管事说：“今早我离开时，他还在，该不会临时回山里了？”
[EN] Meanwhile, Shen Jianchen and his men arrived at Steward Fang's home and searched high and low for Sun Er—not a hair of him remained, and his bedroom had been stripped clean of every personal belonging.
[ZH] 申简辰说：“方管事到现在还替孙二说话，难道就不怀疑他是杀死你娘子的凶手？”
[EN] "He was still here when I left for work this morning," Steward Fang stammered. "Could he have gone back to the hills on a sudden whim?"
[ZH] 方管事难以置信：“怎么可能？这孩子一直很老实，只知道干活，我和娘子对他都不错，他没有理由做出这等凶狠之事。”
[EN] "You're still making excuses for him, Steward Fang?" Shen Jianchen asked. "Doesn't it cross your mind that he might be the one who killed your wife?"
[ZH] 申简辰没有说话，仔细观察周围。
[EN] "How could he?" Steward Fang shook his head in disbelief. "That boy's always been honest and hard-working. My wife and I treated him well—he had no reason on earth to commit a crime like that!"
[ZH] 温信已吩咐差役到处搜寻凶器。孙二住的屋子被翻了个遍，并未找到凶器。
[EN] Shen Jianchen said nothing, scanning the premises with sharp eyes while Wen Xin had constables tear apart Sun Er's room hunting for the murder weapon. Nothing turned up inside.
[ZH] 申简辰走出屋子，瞧见院子里堆着码得整整齐齐的柴火：“温都头，派人翻翻那堆柴火。”
[EN] Stepping out into the yard, Shen Jianchen spotted a neat stack of firewood piled against the wall. "Chief Constable Wen—have your men pull that woodpile apart."
[ZH] 温信一摆手，差役上前立刻搜寻起来，将那堆柴火搞得乱七八糟，终于看到一把匕首，一名差役捡起，送到温信面前。
[EN] At Wen Xin's gesture, constables pitched into the logs, scattering them across the dirt until a gleam of steel flashed free. One constable scooped up a dagger and brought it over to Wen Xin.
[ZH] 他接过后，仔细瞧一遍：“王爷，这把匕首的大小与妇人的伤口很吻合，不过还需仵作进一步验证。”
[EN] Studying the blade, Wen Xin nodded. "Your Highness, the blade's width matches the woman's fatal wound—though the coroner will still need to confirm it."
[ZH] 方管事已受不了，瘫坐在地上：“真是知人知面不知心啊！呜呜……”
[EN] The last of Steward Fang's strength gave out; he slumped onto the ground weeping. "You know a man's face, never his heart! *Sob*..."
[ZH] 申简辰说：“现在还不是哭的时候，你可知孙二会去哪里？”
[EN] "Save your tears for later," Shen Jianchen said. "Where would Sun Er run to?"
[ZH] 方管事抹抹眼泪：“回大人，他无处可去，肯定是回山里。”
[EN] Wiping his eyes, Fang Jiyuan choked out, "Nowhere else to go, Your Honor—he must've fled back to the hills!"
[ZH] 问清山里的具体位置后，申简辰带着众人离开方管事家中，派温信带上几名差役迅速赶往山里捉拿孙二。
[EN] Pinning down the exact village in the hills, Shen Jianchen led his group out of Fang's house and dispatched Wen Xin with a squad of constables to ride straight into the mountains and capture Sun Er.

#CH 257
[ZH] 众人离开后，乔梦容坐在屋内，不想说一句话，刚才大家帮着诋毁朱淑真时的开心已荡然无存。
[ZH] 她抬眼瞧瞧哥哥的屋子，被她布置成洞房的样子，可新郎并不在身边。她擦掉眼泪，心中更生起恨意。
[ZH] 喜兰说：“王妃，今日诗会甚是成功，大家都替王妃宣泄了心中不满。”
[ZH] “只宣泄还不够，我说过要让她消失。”乔梦容说完，问道，“你觉得我狠心吗？”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷正好从外面进来，听到王妃的话，强调道：“王妃是何等身份？想让谁消失，谁还不得立刻消失。”
[ZH] “邹嬷嬷快坐，我正好心中烦闷，陪我说说话。”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷坐在诗会时苗知瑜坐过的位置：“众人刚刚替王妃骂过那个狐狸精，您应该高兴。”
[ZH] “哥哥不在，我高兴不起来。”
[ZH] “王妃要对仪王有耐心，男人就像风筝，随他去飞，只要线拽在王妃手里就行。”
[ZH] “可哥哥不是风筝，是个大活人，再说我手中没有一根线。”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷见仪王妃说得可怜，心中对朱淑真更是不满：“王妃刚才提到想让她消失，不知王妃有没有什么好办法？”
[ZH] “让她消失的想法也是今日当着她的面临时起意，既已说出，不好退缩，可我想来想去不知该如何做。一来我不会功夫，即使会也不一定能打得过她；二来我一个妇道人家哪有权利调遣兵力？即使有，她爹是临安知府，也未必把我放在眼里，再说哥哥是节度使，事情闹大必然会惊动他。”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷叹道：“王妃是良善之辈，自然想得都是正大光明的途径。虽说碾死她跟碾死一只蚂蚁一样简单，也需想些见不得光的方法。”
[ZH] “嬷嬷说来听听。”
[ZH] “此事王妃不能亲自动手，也不能被王爷知道，我们需假借他人之手。”
[ZH] “假借何人？”
[EN] Once the guests cleared out, Qiao Mengrong sat alone in the hall without a word. Every drop of petty triumph she had felt while they tore Zhu Shuzhen to shreds had evaporated. Lifting her gaze around Brother's room—decked out like a bridal chamber, yet missing its groom—she brushed away a hot tear as fresh venom surged inside her.
[ZH] “我回临安，还未去拜见过乔老爷和夫人，不如今日就去。”
[EN] "Your Highness," Xilan ventured, "today's poetry salon went splendidly. Everyone helped you vent your anger."
[ZH] 乔梦容立刻明白：“有劳邹嬷嬷。”
[EN] "Venting isn't enough. I told her I'd make her vanish." Qiao Mengrong turned her eyes on her maid. "Do you think I'm cruel?"
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷在丫鬟的陪同下，乘坐马车来到乔府。
[EN] Right then, Matron Zou stepped inside and caught her last words. "With Your Highness's rank, if you want someone to vanish, who on earth wouldn't vanish on the spot?"
[ZH] 当乔守仁和钱氏听到邹嬷嬷前来拜见，十分欣喜。她曾是乔府的丫鬟，后来去了汴梁，待在乔贵妃身边，还成为仪王的奶娘。女儿走后，是她把仪王抚养成人，对乔家贡献极大，他们决不能怠慢，必须亲自出去迎接。
[EN] "Come sit, Matron Zou. My mind is in knots—keep me company."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷站在乔府主人的院子门口，见二位主人亲自前来相迎，便知自己在他们心中的地位，作为下人能获此等礼遇，实属难得。
[EN] Matron Zou took the seat Miao Zhiyu had occupied earlier. "Everyone just tore that little vixen to strips for you. You ought to be smiling."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷赶紧行礼：“老爷，夫人，我随仪王回到临安，帮王爷和王妃处理完事情，就跑来看望你们。”
[EN] "With Brother gone, I can't bring myself to smile."
[ZH] 钱氏上前拉住邹嬷嬷的手：“不必多礼。”不觉想起女儿，鼻子一酸，眼睛也跟着变红。
[EN] "Be patient with Prince Yi, Your Highness. Men are like kites—let them soar all they like, so long as the string stays in your fist."
[ZH] 乔守仁说：“别站着，进屋说话。”
[EN] "Brother isn't a kite—he's flesh and blood, and I don't hold a single string."
[ZH] 进入屋内，三人落座后，先寒暄些往事。有乔贵妃当姑娘时在临安的趣事，还有乔贵妃进宫成了贵妃后的不易，再到生下仪王，很快撒手人寰之事。
[EN] Her mistress's misery stoked Matron Zou's hatred for Zhu Shuzhen even hotter. "You just said you want her to vanish, Your Highness. Do you have a plan?"
[ZH] 说着说着，三人都忍不住悲伤起来。
[EN] "The thought only popped out in the heat of facing her today. Now that I've said it aloud, I can't back down—yet I'm stumped on how to pull it off. First, I don't know martial arts, and even if I did, I couldn't beat her. Second, as a woman I have no authority to mobilize troops—and even if I tried, her father is the Prefect of Lin'an and wouldn't necessarily roll over for me. Worse, Brother is Military Commissioner; any big commotion is bound to reach his ears."
[ZH] 乔守仁第一个控制好情绪：“唉，都是过去的事，贵妃泉下有知，见仪王身体康健，又来到临安成为节度使，必然欣慰。”
[EN] Matron Zou gave a wry sigh. "Your Highness is too kind-hearted—you only think of open, aboveboard paths. Squashing her is as easy as crushing an ant, yet it calls for methods that stay out of the daylight."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷擦完眼泪，趁机引出来意：“仪王现在得官家重用，来到临安担任要职，也与郡主成亲，人生可谓圆满，郡主却高兴不来。”
[EN] "Tell me."
[ZH] 钱氏也擦去眼泪：“为何？”
[EN] "Your Highness cannot dirty her own hands, nor can Prince Yi ever catch wind of it. We need to borrow someone else's blade."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷说：“亲是成了，可未圆房，仪王还跑去司理院忙活，都好几日没回幽林别居。”
[EN] "Whose blade?"
[ZH] 乔守仁与钱氏对视一番，仪王抢亲之事他们听说过，只是不大明白朱家姑娘能有这么大魅力？
[EN] "Since returning to Lin'an, I still haven't called on Master Qiao and Madam Qian. Why not pay them a visit today?"
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷继续说：“仪王心里装着别人，王妃整日以泪洗面，我看着心疼。”
[EN] Understanding dawned on Qiao Mengrong at once. "I'll trouble you to handle it, Matron Zou."
[ZH] 乔守仁说：“邹嬷嬷多劝劝仪王妃，让她别难过，男人总有不开窍的时候，仪王还年轻，将个把女子放心上再正常不过，等醒悟后，自然会好。”
[EN] Accompanied by a maid, Matron Zou rode by carriage straight to the Qiao Manor.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷瞧瞧屋子里站着乔府的下人：“乔老爷，让下人回避一下，我有话跟二位说。”
[EN] Hearing Matron Zou had come calling, Qiao Shouren and Madam Qian lit up with welcome. She had started out as a maid right here in the Qiao household before following Consort Qiao to Bianliang and becoming Prince Yi's wet nurse. After their daughter passed away, Matron Zou had raised the prince to manhood—her service to the Qiao clan was beyond measure. They couldn't dream of treating her lightly and hurried out to greet her in person.
[ZH] 乔守仁向下人吩咐道：“都下去吧，出去时把门关好。”
[EN] Standing outside the masters' courtyard, Matron Zou saw both host and hostess rushing out to receive her and felt a quiet swell of pride. For a servant to command such respect was rare indeed. She dropped into a crisp bow. "Master, Madam—now that I've returned to Lin'an with Prince Yi and settled things for His Highness and the Princess Consort, I came straight over to see you."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷也对跟着自己的丫鬟示意，丫鬟明白，与乔家的下人们一起出去把门从外面关好。
[EN] Madam Qian hurried forward to clasp her hands. "No need for all that ceremony!" Thinking of her late daughter made her nose prickle and her eyes turn red.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷这才说：“老爷和夫人应该清楚仪王心中之人是谁，只要她还在，王妃心中难安。若是在京城，得罪王妃，只有死路一条，仪王府的侍卫便能将她处理干净……”
[EN] "Don't stand out in the breeze—come inside and talk," Qiao Shouren urged.
[ZH] 听到这话，乔守仁和钱氏心中害怕。
[EN] Once the three sat down indoors, they drifted into memories: Consort Qiao's girlhood pranks in Lin'an, the harsh trials after entering the palace as an imperial consort, giving birth to Prince Yi, and slipping away far too young. Before long, all three were dabbing damp eyes.
[ZH] 钱氏小心说：“她也已嫁人，王妃没必要跟她计较。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren pulled himself together first. "All water under the bridge now. If the Consort knows from the netherworld that Prince Yi is healthy again and back in Lin'an as Military Commissioner, she'll rest in peace."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷说：“仪王因她跑去抢亲，令王妃颜面何存？现在又因心里念着她，连洞房都未入，王妃更是难过，只有她消失，王妃才能心安。可王妃在临安没有可用之人，我便替她前来拜见老爷和夫人。”
[EN] Wiping her tears, Matron Zou seized her cue. "Prince Yi enjoys His Majesty's favor, holds a grand post here, and has wed Princess Jiahe—his life looks complete on paper, yet the Princess Consort can't find a shred of joy."
[ZH] 乔守仁头上冒出冷汗，毕竟是条人命：“王妃会不会想得太严重？都是过去的事儿，听说她在陈家过得不错，还把陈家二公子给管教好，看得出是想安心过日子。再说她爹是临安知府，若女儿出事，必然彻查到底。”
[EN] Madam Qian blotted her eyes. "Why is that?"
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷很是不屑：“哼！临安知府又如何？见到仪王妃还不得跪拜？谁让他们的女儿不开眼得罪贵人！”
[EN] "They held the wedding, yet they still haven't consummated the marriage. Now Prince Yi has moved over to the Judicial Office—hasn't set foot back in Youlin Retreat for days."
[ZH] 乔守仁试探地问道：“邹嬷嬷前来，想让乔家做什么？”
[EN] Qiao Shouren and Madam Qian traded an uneasy look. They had heard about Prince Yi crashing Zhu Shuzhen's wedding, though neither could fathom what spell that Zhu girl had cast on him.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷说：“仪王妃现在只能靠乔家，乔家家大业大，为了看家护院收取租子，养着不少打手，希望乔老爷帮王妃解决心腹之患。”
[EN] "His Highness carries another woman in his heart," Matron Zou sighed. "The Princess Consort cries herself sick every day—breaks my heart to watch."
[ZH] 钱氏吓得不敢说话。
[EN] "Do try to soothe her, Matron," Qiao Shouren said. "Men are slow to come to their senses. Prince Yi is young—getting infatuated with a girl here or there happens. Once the fog clears, he'll come right back around."
[ZH] 乔守仁头上继续冒着冷汗，心下思忖：
[EN] Matron Zou cast a meaningful glance at the Qiao servants lining the walls. "Master Qiao, send the servants out. I have something private to say to you both."
[ZH] 乔家仰仗的是仪王而并不是仪王妃，此事仪王若知，肯定会对乔家不满。乔家再看不上朱家姑娘，也不能干伤天害理之事，何况是一条人命？
[EN] Qiao Shouren waved a hand. "All of you leave, and shut the doors behind you."
[ZH] 即便帮仪王妃，将此事做得天衣无缝，凭朱晞颜的本事最终也能查到他的头上，只怕到时仪王妃不认账，乔家岂不是成替死鬼？﻿可乔家也是万万不能得罪仪王妃。
[EN] Matron Zou nodded to her own maid too, who filed out with the Qiao servants and pulled the doors tight.
[ZH] 乔守仁犹豫片刻想到一个好办法，他继续擦擦额头的冷汗：“乔家帮仪王妃自然义不容辞，可让人消失的事儿乔家从未做过，下不去手。再说我养的那些个打手吓唬吓唬人还可以，让他们干要命的事儿，估计逃跑的速度比兔子还快。不过，我倒有个主意……”
[EN] Only then did Matron Zou lean forward. "Master and Madam both know who sits in Prince Yi's heart. So long as she lives, the Princess Consort will never know peace. Back in the capital, crossing a princess consort meant instant death—the prince's guards would have made her disappear without a trace..."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷算是听出来，乔守仁不乐意，那就听听他会出什么主意：“乔老爷说来听听。”
[EN] A cold knot gripped Qiao Shouren and Madam Qian.
[ZH] “嬷嬷说的事儿还需找黑道人做，临安城有个黑帮叫钱塘帮，听说也做收钱取人性命的买卖，不如找找他们？”乔守仁又担心邹嬷嬷帮仪王妃麻烦乔家帮忙，紧接着说，“乔家只跟白道人往来，黑道从未接触过。”
[EN] "She's already married to another man," Madam Qian ventured timidly. "Surely the Princess Consort needn't bother with her?"
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷心想：“看来乔家铁定心不愿帮忙，不过出的主意倒不错，可以试试。”然后叮嘱道：“乔老爷既然帮不了这个忙，就当我的话没说，你们还需将今日听到的烂在肚子里。”
[EN] "Prince Yi crashed her wedding over that woman, leaving the Princess Consort with not a scrap of face! Now he's still pining for her and won't even enter the bridal chamber! Only if that woman vanishes will the Princess Consort breathe easy. Since Her Highness has no trusted hands of her own in Lin'an, I came on her behalf to ask Master and Madam for help."
[ZH] 两人起身将邹嬷嬷送出后，钱氏差点倒下，还好被乔守仁扶住。
[EN] Cold sweat beaded on Qiao Shouren's brow—this was a human life she was talking about! "Isn't the Princess Consort taking this too far? It's all in the past. Word is the girl's settled down nicely at Chen Manor and even reformed Second Young Master Chen—clearly she intends to live a quiet married life. Besides, her father is the Prefect of Lin'an! If anything happens to his daughter, he'll tear this city apart hunting the culprit!"
[ZH] “老爷，她看着柔弱，怎么会如此想？”
[EN] Matron Zou let out a contemptuous snort. "Humph! What's a Lin'an Prefect? He still has to kneel before Princess Consort Yi! Blame their blind daughter for daring to cross royalty!"
[ZH] “人是看不出来的。”
[EN] Testing the waters, Qiao Shouren asked, "What exactly does Matron Zou want the Qiao family to do?"
[ZH] “要不要告诉仪王？”
[EN] "The Princess Consort has nobody to lean on here except the Qiaos. Your family owns vast estates and keeps plenty of hired thugs to guard the property and collect rents. Her Highness hopes Master Qiao will clear this thorn out of her side."
[ZH] “还是不要瞎掺和。”
[EN] Terror froze Madam Qian's tongue.
[ZH] 钱氏站稳后，乔守仁松开手。
[EN] Beads of sweat kept trickling down Qiao Shouren's temples as his mind raced. *The Qiao family's real patron is Prince Yi, not Princess Consort Yi! If the prince ever finds out, we're finished! No matter how little I like that Zhu girl, I won't touch murder! Even if we pulled it off cleanly, Zhu Xiyan's sharp enough to trace it straight back to my door—and if the Princess Consort plays innocent, the Qiaos take the fall!* Yet he couldn't afford to flat-out offend Princess Consort Yi either.
[ZH] “老爷，毕竟是人命……”
[EN] After a tense pause, a way out struck him. Swiping the sweat off his brow, he said, "Serving Princess Consort Yi is our duty, of course—only the Qiao household has never made anyone disappear, and we don't have the stomach for it. Those thugs on my payroll are fine for roughing up rent-skippers, yet tell them to spill blood and they'll bolt faster than rabbits! Still... I do have a suggestion."
[ZH] 乔守仁用手捂住钱氏的嘴巴：“烂在肚子里，对谁都不要提起。我会让文渊多劝劝仪王，不要再想着朱家姑娘，况且她已成为陈家二少夫人。”
[EN] Matron Zou saw right through his dodge, yet she leaned in anyway. "Let's hear it, Master Qiao."
[ZH] 钱氏点点头。
[EN] "Work like that takes underworld hands. Lin'an has a syndicate called the Qiantang Gang—rumor says they take silver for wet work. Why not sound them out?" Afraid she might ask him to broker the deal, he added in a hurry, "The Qiaos only deal with respectable folks—we've never crossed paths with the underworld."
[ZH] 乔守仁拿开手，对外面的下人说：“去，把公子叫来。”
[EN] *Clearly the Qiaos refuse to lift a finger,* Matron Zou thought, *still, that lead is worth checking.* "Since Master Qiao can't help," she warned darkly, "forget I ever opened my mouth. Take every word you heard today to your grave."
[ZH] 没过多久，乔文仙前来，拜见父母：“不知父亲和母亲唤儿子前来有何事？”
[EN] The instant they saw Matron Zou out the gate, Madam Qian's knees buckled; only Qiao Shouren's arm kept her upright.
[ZH] 儿子与去年闹绝食时完全不同，钱氏用满意的笑容掩饰掉刚才的惊恐：“来，坐下说。”
[EN] "Husband—she looks so gentle and fragile! How could she plot something like that?"
[ZH] 乔文仙在旁边的椅子上坐下，等待父母发话。
[EN] "You can never judge a heart by its face."
[ZH] 乔守仁说：“文渊啊，你现在是好了，妻妾都有，还心甘情愿接纳她们。可仪王……，刚才邹嬷嬷来过，从她口中得知仪王还未跟王妃圆房，长此以往，哪个女人能受得了？何况还是仪王妃？你跟仪王无话不谈，抽个时间找他聊聊，劝说劝说。”
[EN] "Should we warn Prince Yi?"
[ZH] “他回来那日，儿子亲自去幽林别居已劝说过，无济于事。”
[EN] "Stay out of it!" Once Madam Qian caught her footing, he released her arm.
[ZH] 钱氏说：“你再去劝说劝说，说多了，他的观念自然会被改变过。”
[EN] "Husband, it's a human life—"
[ZH] “人的观念哪有那么容易改变？”
[EN] Qiao Shouren clapped a hand over her mouth. "Bury it in your belly! Not a peep to anyone! I'll have Wenyuan keep working on Prince Yi to give up that Zhu girl—she's Second Young Madam Chen now, for heaven's sake!"
[ZH] 乔守仁说：“你不是已经改变过来？爹相信仪王也会慢慢改变。不变又能如何？总不能利用手中的权势把人家抢过去当他的侧妃？”
[EN] Madam Qian gave a shaky nod. Letting go, Qiao Shouren barked at a servant outside, "Go fetch the Young Master!"
[ZH] “好吧，我答应爹娘今日找仪王聊聊。”
[EN] Before long, Qiao Wenxian arrived and bowed. "Father, Mother—what did you summon me for?"
[ZH] 两人齐声说：“这就对了！”
[EN] Looking at her son—nothing like the lovesick wreck who had starved himself last year—Madam Qian hid her lingering panic behind an approving smile. "Come, sit down."
[ZH] 乔文仙拜别父母回到自己的院子。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian took a chair and waited.
[ZH] 自成亲后，乔守仁给他换了更大的院子，毕竟娶了一妻一妾，以前的小院子根本住不下。
[EN] "Wenyuan," Qiao Shouren began, "you've sorted yourself out nicely—wife and concubine both, and you've come to accept them willingly. Prince Yi, on the other hand... Matron Zou just stopped by and let slip that His Highness still hasn't consummated his marriage with the Princess Consort! How long do you expect any woman to stomach that, let alone Princess Consort Yi? You and the prince tell each other everything—find a chance to talk some sense into him."
[ZH] 院子的正屋属于男主人，东厢房住着苗知瑜，西厢房住着夏果，下人们则住在后院。
[EN] "I already tried the day he got back at Youlin Retreat," Qiao Wenxian sighed. "Didn't budge him an inch."
[ZH] 每次踏进院子，乔文仙心中不免变得沉重，总怕见到两个女人为他争风吃醋。这两个女人他并不喜欢，与她们圆房也只是屈从于生活而已，当然也有身体萌生的欲望。
[EN] "Try again!" Madam Qian urged. "Keep hammering at it and his mind will come around."
[ZH] 当他被身体的欲望支配时，觉得自己跟动物没有什么区别。自从放下淑真，他心甘情愿接受自己变成动物，获得身体快乐的同时，别人也快乐，一家人都高兴。
[EN] "People don't change their hearts that easily."
[ZH] 说是放下淑真，只是讲给别人听而已。其实他把淑真藏在心底最深处，他人都无法窥探到。
[EN] "Didn't *you* change yours?" Qiao Shouren countered. "I believe Prince Yi will come around too. What choice does he have? He can't exactly use his title to kidnap another man's wife and make her his secondary consort!"
[ZH] 每当回忆起以前的经历，想淑真想得难以入眠时，他都会去找夏果。她与淑真有几分相似，乔文仙只能用这几分相似慰藉思念之情，故而他去夏果的房中居多。
[EN] "All right," Qiao Wenxian relented. "I'll go talk to him today."
[ZH] 苗知瑜很不开心，为了院中安宁，他又不得不去苗知瑜房中。
[EN] "Now you're talking!" both parents beamed.
[ZH] 若说他跟夏果在一起主动居多，跟苗知瑜则相反，他懒得主动。倒是苗知瑜，跟他在一起时一点儿不像淑女，令乔文仙汗颜。第一晚时，的确将乔文仙给惊到。苗知瑜解释说，新婚前家人找来有经验的嬷嬷，将方法传授给她。
[EN] Taking leave of them, Qiao Wenxian walked back to his own courtyard. Since marrying, his father had moved him into a larger compound—one wife and one concubine couldn't squeeze into his old bachelor quarters. The main hall belonged to him, the east wing to Miao Zhiyu, the west wing to Xiaguo, and the rear quarters to the servants.
[ZH] 苗知瑜的主动渐渐会将乔文仙搞得躁动不安，这时他便会由被动变为主动，享受暂时成为动物的快乐。
[EN] Every time he stepped through his own gate, a dull weight pressed on his chest, dreading fresh jealousy between his two women. He loved neither of them; bedding them was simply surrendering to life—alongside plain physical appetite. Whenever bodily urge took over, he felt no better than an animal. Ever since letting Shuzhen go, he had resigned himself to being that animal—he got his release, the women stayed content, and his parents beamed.
[ZH] 今日苗知瑜回娘家，夏果也去参加妾室的聚会，乔文仙走进院子有种说不出的轻松。
[EN] Claiming he had "let Shuzhen go" was just a line for everyone else. In truth, he had locked her away in the deepest corner of his chest where nobody could peek. Whenever old memories kept him tossing sleepless at night, missing Shuzhen till his ribs ached, he drifted to Xiaguo's room. She bore a faint likeness to Shuzhen, and he borrowed that shadow to soothe his longing—which meant he spent most nights in the west wing.
[ZH] 幼凡跟南双正站在院中说话。
[EN] That left Miao Zhiyu sulking, so to keep the peace he had to visit the east wing too. If he usually took the lead with Xiaguo, with Miao Zhiyu he couldn't muster the spark—yet Miao Zhiyu shocked him breathless by throwing all ladylike reserve out the window. On their wedding night she had nearly bowled him over, explaining later that her family had hired a seasoned matron beforehand to drill her in bedroom arts. Her bold advances soon got his blood racing anyway, flipping him from passive to eager as he lost himself in brief, mindless pleasure.
[ZH] 乔文仙很纳闷：“幼凡，你不是跟少夫人回娘家了吗？怎么回来？”
[EN] Today Miao Zhiyu was visiting her parents and Xiaguo was out at a concubines' gathering, leaving the courtyard wonderfully quiet. Stepping inside, Qiao Wenxian spotted Youfan and Nan Shuang chatting by the veranda.
[ZH] 幼凡行礼道：“回姑爷，少夫人忘记带东西，让我回来取，取完还得赶回苗府。”
[EN] "Youfan?" he asked, puzzled. "Didn't you go with Young Madam to her parents' house? Why are you back?"
[ZH] 幼凡说完，对南双笑笑，向东厢房走去。
[EN] Youfan dropped a hasty curtsy. "Young Madam forgot something, Young Master, so she sent me back to fetch it before hurrying back to Miao Manor." Flashing Nan Shuang a quick smile, she hurried into the east wing.
[ZH] 南双上前拉住公子的手臂：“公子，能不能让我快些娶幼凡？我也想……”
[EN] Nan Shuang sidled up and tugged his master's sleeve. "Young Master, couldn't you let me marry Youfan sooner? I want to—"
[ZH] 乔文仙用手敲敲南双的脑门儿：“别以为我不知，都传到我耳朵里，你们两个注意点儿，别把我的院子搞得乌七八糟，小心我棒打鸳鸯。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian rapped his knuckle on Nan Shuang's forehead. "Don't think I haven't heard the rumors! Behave yourselves and stop turning my courtyard into a scandal, or I'll break you two lovebirds up!"
[ZH] 南双埋怨道：“公子只顾自己快乐，都不管我。”
[EN] "Young Master only cares about his own fun and leaves me hanging!" Nan Shuang pouted.
[ZH] 乔文仙笑道：“行了，明年给你们成亲。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian chuckled. "Fine, fine—I'll marry you two off next year."
[ZH] 南双高兴道：“谢公子！”
[EN] "Thank you, Young Master!" Nan Shuang whooped.
[ZH] 幼凡手里拿着卷好的纸张，从东厢房出来，能看得出上面写有字迹。
[EN] Just then Youfan emerged from the east wing clutching a roll of paper covered in ink.
[ZH] 乔文仙十分纳闷：“怎么回娘家还要带这些东西？”
[EN] "Why does she need written sheets at her parents' house?" Qiao Wenxian wondered aloud.
[ZH] 幼凡看上去很紧张，并未回答，行完礼匆匆离开。
[EN] Looking flustered, Youfan didn't answer—she dipped a quick curtsy and hurried straight out.
[ZH] 乔文仙并未多想，对南双说：“进屋换身衣裳，再陪我去趟司理院拜见仪王。”
[EN] Thinking little of it, Qiao Wenxian told Nan Shuang, "Come help me change robes, then walk with me to the Judicial Office to see Prince Yi."
[ZH] 两人进入屋内，南双帮公子换完衣裳后出来，夏果刚好从外面回来，身后跟着丫鬟艾墨。
[EN] They had barely changed and stepped back out when Xiaguo returned through the gate with her maid Aimo trailing behind.
[ZH] “文渊这是要出门？”
[EN] "Heading out, Wenyuan?"
[ZH] “你回来得倒快？”
[EN] "Back already?"
[ZH] 夏果搀住文渊，故意撒娇：“怎么？嫌我回来得早？你穿得这么整齐，该不会是出去会情人？”
[EN] Linking her arm through his, Xiaguo put on a syrupy pout. "What, disappointed I came home early? Dressed up so sharp—don't tell me you're sneaking out to meet a sweetheart?"
[ZH] 夏果撒娇的样子，乔文仙无法与她置气：“别胡说，我去见仪王。”
[EN] Her playful coaxing melted any edge off him. "Stop talking nonsense. I'm going to see Prince Yi."
[ZH] 夏果放开文渊，继续用撒娇的声音说：“既是见贵人，快去快回，我在家里等着你。”
[EN] Letting go of his sleeve, Xiaguo kept her honeyed purr going. "If it's His Highness, hurry back—I'll be waiting right here for you."

#CH 258
[ZH] 夏姨娘的声音令南双听了都觉酥软，何况是公子。
[ZH] 乔文仙本能地喜欢听这种撒娇的声音，每每此时，他都会想起淑真坐在他的怀里撒娇的情景，那时她说爱他，要嫁给他，还要给他生很多很多孩子。
[ZH] 他的脸上露出笑容，声音不由变得温柔：“好。”上前抱了抱夏果，才带着南双离开。
[ZH] 艾墨说：“夏姨娘的声音公子根本招架不住。”
[ZH] 夏果目送文渊出去后，转头对艾墨说：“学着点儿，男人都喜欢女子撒娇，像小姐那样不会撒娇就能笼络住男子的没几人。文渊以前对她痴情的样子你我都知道，现在怎么样？还不是抵抗不了我娇滴滴的声音？他早把以前的情抛到九霄云外。”
[ZH] 艾墨望向东厢房：“那位不会撒娇，为何公子时常去她房中？”
[ZH] 夏果面露不满：“听说她那方面厉害。”
[ZH] 艾墨不解：“哪方面？”
[ZH] 夏果凑近艾墨耳边：“床上功夫。”
[EN] Concubine Xia's syrupy purr made even Nan Shuang go weak at the knees, let alone his master. Qiao Wenxian had a natural soft spot for that coaxing tone—every time she spoke like that, memories of Shuzhen curling in his lap surfaced, whispering that she loved him, wanted to marry him, and wanted to bear him heaps of children.
[ZH] 艾墨的脸顿时变得通红，不再言语。
[EN] A warm smile broke across his face, and his voice gentled. "All right." Giving Xiaguo a brief embrace, he strode out with Nan Shuang.
[ZH] 夏果吩咐道：“你私下悄悄打听，看哪里有这方面懂得多的嬷嬷，请来给我传授传授。”
[EN] "Young Master doesn't stand a chance against your voice, Concubine Xia," Aimo giggled.
[ZH] 艾墨不明白，夏姨娘都跟公子圆了房，公子也多次去她房中，怎么还需人指点？她不敢多加质疑，乖乖回道：“是。”
[EN] Watching Wenyuan vanish down the path, Xiaguo turned to her maid. "Take notes. Every man melts for a woman who knows how to coax and pout. Precious few women can hook a man without it like Miss used to. You and I both saw how madly in love Wenyuan was with her—and look at him now! One sweet purr from me and he's putty in my hands. That old flame is ancient history."
[ZH] 话说邹嬷嬷回到幽林别居，将乔老爷的态度说了。
[EN] Aimo glanced toward the east wing. "That one never coaxes or pouts—so why does Young Master still spend plenty of nights in her room?"
[ZH] 乔梦容一阵失落，只怪自己在朝中没有大靠山，连乔家都不把她放在眼里。她想起梁内侍，虽与梁师成能说上话，可要说他是自己的靠山，还差得很远，
[EN] Xiaguo's smile soured. "Word is she's skilled in *that* department."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷继续说：“乔家虽不愿帮忙，倒出了一个不错的主意。”
[EN] "Which department?" Aimo blinked.
[ZH] “什么主意？”
[EN] Xiaguo leaned close to her ear. "Bedroom skills."
[ZH] “找钱塘帮。”
[EN] Aimo's cheeks flamed scarlet, cutting off every word.
[ZH] “钱塘帮是做什么的？”
[EN] "Ask around on the quiet," Xiaguo ordered. "Find out where to hire a matron who knows all those tricks and bring her here to teach me."
[ZH] “乔老爷说是黑帮，也做收钱杀人的买卖。”
[EN] Aimo couldn't fathom why Concubine Xia needed tutoring when Young Master already bedded her half the week, yet she dared not question her and replied, "Yes, Concubine Xia."
[ZH] “甚好。”乔梦容不再失落，“嬷嬷走后我仔细思量，得先让她没好日子过，需寻些事端折磨折磨她，令她心中不快。第一件便是毁了她江南女词人的美名，该计划正在实施，下一步还需有人在陈府给她制造些事端，等折磨够了，我心中满意，再让她彻底消失也不迟。”
[EN] Back at Youlin Retreat, Matron Zou reported Master Qiao's lukewarm brush-off.
[ZH] “王妃所言甚是，不过，如何才能给她寻些事端？王妃身份尊贵，总不能为整那狐狸精往陈府跑？”
[EN] A wave of bitterness hit Qiao Mengrong—all because she lacked a towering patron at court, even the Qiaos dared shrug her off! She thought of Imperial Attendant Liang; sure, Liang Shicheng listened to her now and then, yet calling him a rock-solid backer was stretching the truth.
[ZH] “嬷嬷忘了？你给我出了一个假借他人之手的主意。”
[EN] "Even though the Qiaos won't lift a finger," Matron Zou added, "Master Qiao did toss out a promising lead."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷露出醒悟的表情：“王妃打算假借何人？”
[EN] "What lead?"
[ZH] “陈浩东。”
[EN] "The Qiantang Gang."
[ZH] “陈家人？”
[EN] "Who are they?"
[ZH] 乔梦容点点头：“是陈家大公子。苗夫人的母亲秦夫人几次约陈家的二夫人包氏打马吊，从她口中打听到些许信息，再经过一番分析，猜测陈浩东想要成为陈家产业继承人，可陈浩宇突然变好，还主动参与陈家生意，令陈浩东心中难安。”
[EN] "An underworld syndicate, Master Qiao says—they take silver to kill."
[ZH] “消息若可靠，不妨用用陈浩东。王妃身边的确需有一位用着趁手的男子，外头的事总得有人替王妃跑腿。”
[EN] The gloom cleared from Qiao Mengrong's face. "Good. While you were out, I thought it over. First, I want to ruin her life—stir up trouble, torment her, and make every day miserable. Step one is stripping her of that 'Poetess of Jiangnan' title, which is already underway. Next, we need someone inside Chen Manor making her life hell. Once I've watched her suffer to my heart's content, making her vanish for good won't be too late."
[ZH] “苗知瑜心甘情愿帮我，应该不会骗我。”
[EN] "Well reasoned, Your Highness. Still, how do we stir up trouble inside Chen Manor? Someone of your exalted station can't very well march over there to mess with that vixen."
[ZH] “既如此，我差人打听打听这位陈家大公子，然后找机会与他聊上几句。”
[EN] "Forgot already, Matron? You're the one who suggested borrowing someone else's blade."
[ZH] “有劳嬷嬷。”
[EN] Realization lit Matron Zou's eyes. "Whom does Your Highness have in mind?"
[ZH] 司理院内，申简辰在参军居住的小院子里坐着，方形的木桌上放着酒酿，他边饮边想心事。
[EN] "Chen Haodong."
[ZH] 小小的院落打扫得干净，风景却差了许多。花坛里的植物一看就知无人打理，里面长满杂草，倒也令简陋的院落充满生机。
[EN] "A Chen?"
[ZH] 温都头带人去山里捉拿孙二，若顺利的话明日可归来。申简辰想，等该案子了结，再回幽林别居看望梦容。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong nodded. "Eldest Young Master Chen. Madam Miao's mother, Madam Qin, invited Second Madam Bao to play madiao several times and pried out plenty of gossip. Put the pieces together, and it's clear Chen Haodong has his eye on inheriting the Chen empire—and Chen Haoyu's sudden reformation and foray into family business has him sweating bullets."
[ZH] 他既借着赵朴的身体活过来，帮他照顾梦容也是应该，可要圆房……
[EN] "If that tip holds water, Chen Haodong could be very handy indeed. Your Highness really does need a capable man outside running errands for you."
[ZH] 正百无聊赖地想着，文渊前来。他一走进院子，满面春风，看不到一丝去年绝食时留下的痕迹。
[EN] "Miao Zhiyu is eager to serve me—she wouldn't feed me lies."
[ZH] 申简辰很是奇怪，这家伙去年爱得死去活来，现在竟然跟变了个人似的，看来人的感情是会发生变化。而自己何时会变？申简辰一时无法预知。
[EN] "Then I'll have someone look into Eldest Young Master Chen and arrange a quiet chat with him."
[ZH] “仪王一个人喝闷酒多没意思，白风，再取只酒杯来。”乔文仙不请自坐，“堂堂仪王跑到司理院当差亏你想得出！”
[EN] "I'll leave it in your hands, Matron."
[ZH] 白风已将酒杯摆好，向南双指指门口，两人便走出院子，一起去玩耍。
[EN] Over at the Judicial Office, Shen Jianchen sat alone in the Investigator's tiny rear courtyard, sipping fermented rice wine from a square wooden table as dark thoughts drifted through his head. The small yard had been swept clean, yet the scenery left plenty to be desired—the flowerbeds had gone wild with weeds, lending the humble yamen a scrappy sort of life.
[ZH] 申简辰放下酒杯：“你哪只眼睛瞅见我喝闷酒？不过是闲来无事饮几杯。倒是你，不好好在家陪你的妻妾，跑到我这里来做什么？”
[EN] Chief Constable Wen had ridden into the hills to arrest Sun Er; with any luck he'd be back tomorrow. Once this murder case wrapped up, Shen Jianchen figured he ought to return to Youlin Retreat and visit Mengrong. Since he had come back to life inside Zhao Pu's body, looking after Mengrong was only right—still, consummating the marriage...
[ZH] 乔文仙自斟一杯，再给仪王斟满：“今日邹嬷嬷来乔家，说了你与郡主之事，父亲差我前来劝说，我便来司理院转转。司理院比幽林别居差远了，你还是回去住吧。”
[EN] Just as he brooded aimlessly, Qiao Wenxian strolled through the gateway looking radiant, not a trace left of the hollowed-out wretch who had starved himself last year. Shen Jianchen marveled inwardly: how could a man who swore undying love transform so completely? People's hearts really did shift—though when his own might shift, he hadn't the foggiest clue.
[ZH] “你来当说客？”
[EN] "Drinking all alone like a brooding hermit? Where's the fun in that! Bai Feng, fetch another cup!" Qiao Wenxian dropped into a chair without waiting for an invite. "Leave it to you—a full-blooded imperial prince playing clerk at the Judicial Office!"
[ZH] “要不然呢？”
[EN] Bai Feng brought a second cup, jerked his chin toward the doorway at Nan Shuang, and the two young attendants slipped out to wander the grounds.
[ZH] “又不是没劝过我，何必再多费口舌？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen set his cup down. "Which eye saw me brooding? Just passing a slow afternoon with a drink. What about you—shouldn't you be home keeping your wife and concubine happy instead of dropping in on me?"
[ZH] “那日劝说如同蜻蜓点水，今日我想与你聊些更深入的话题。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian poured himself a cup and topped off the prince's. "Matron Zou came by Qiao Manor today and brought up you and Princess Jiahe. My father sent me over to talk sense into you, so I figured I'd inspect your new digs. This Judicial Office is a dump compared to Youlin Retreat—move back home already."
[ZH] 申简辰端起酒酿，品了一口：“聊你放下感情如何接纳一妻一妾的心路历程？我看还是算了，我跟你不一样。”
[EN] "So they sent you as a lobbyist?"
[ZH] 一下子被对方猜中，乔文仙一路上想好的劝说之语，无法按照计划的顺序来，他迅速调整次序，不如先让对方感到出乎意料：“错！我根本没有真正放下淑真。”
[EN] "What else?"
[ZH] 还真管用，仪王放下酒杯，一言不发。
[EN] "You already gave me the speech once. Why waste your breath again?"
[ZH] “我不可能忘记她，只能把她藏在心底，委屈自己接纳不爱的人。只有这么做，周围的人才会开心，一家人才会其乐融融。否则，我既失去淑真，还搞得两个女人不开心，父母也不开心，我会更加罪过。”
[EN] "Last time I only skimmed the surface. Today I want to dig deeper."
[ZH] “你可开心？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen lifted his bowl and took a sip. "Another lecture on how you let go and embraced your wife and concubine? Save it—you and I aren't built the same."
[ZH] “不能跟心爱的人在一起，当然难以开心。可我渐渐发现，不同的女子有不同的好。既然无法体会与心爱的人在一起的好，不如接纳其他女子的好。夏果喜欢撒娇，只要她对我撒娇，我便忍不住不对她好，何况她还有几分像淑真；苗知瑜虽不爱撒娇，可与她圆房时，她比我懂得多，我竟然出乎意料地很快乐……”
[EN] Having his prepared script guessed on line one threw Qiao Wenxian off stride, so he flipped his opening straight for shock value. "Wrong! I never truly let Shuzhen go."
[ZH] 申简辰听不下去：“为何要对我提起你房中的私密之事？”
[EN] Bullseye. Shen Jianchen set his cup down and went still.
[ZH] “难道你听不明白？”
[EN] "I'll never forget her," Qiao Wenxian confessed quietly. "All I can do is bury her deep inside my heart and swallow my pride to accept women I don't love. That's the only way the people around me smile and the house stays warm. Otherwise, I've already lost Shuzhen—and making two other women and my parents miserable on top of it only piles sin on sin."
[ZH] “明白什么？”
[EN] "Are you happy?"
[ZH] “男女就那么回事，仪王只要与郡主圆房，便知她的好。仪王肯定会像我一样欲罢 不能，有了第一次便会有第二次，甚至无数次，你开心，郡主也开心。心中的人既得不到，不如像我一样把她藏在心底，安心接受身边的人，只有这样大家才能各自安好。”
[EN] "How could anyone be truly happy apart from the woman he loves? Still, I've learned that every woman has her own kind of charm. Since I can't have the joy of being with the one I love, I might as well accept what other women offer. Xiaguo loves to pout and coax—one sweet whine from her and I melt, plus she even looks a bit like Shuzhen. Miao Zhiyu isn't the pouting sort, yet once we're in bed she knows far more tricks than I do, and—much to my surprise—I actually enjoy myself..."
[ZH] 申简辰不是没有冲动，不是没想过将梦容变成自己的女人，只是他难以跨越心中那道道德的门槛儿。不爱，便与其圆房，他如何都做不到，何况他只把梦容当妹妹看待。
[EN] Shen Jianchen cut in with a grimace. "Why are you telling me about your bedroom affairs?"
[ZH] “她唤我一声哥哥，在我心里她只是妹妹，我怎么能轻易动自己的妹妹？那样我会觉得自己猪狗不如。”
[EN] "Don't you get my point?"
[ZH] “你生病时爱着她，现在又娶了她，她早已不是你的妹妹，而是你的妻子，你动她天经地义，不违背任何伦常。相反，你不动她，才会令她心中不安，说不定还会给淑真带来不利。”
[EN] "What point?"
[ZH] 申简辰想起抢亲那日，梦容狠狠扇了千里寻一巴掌，还说：“朱淑真！我恨你！”
[EN] "Men and women are simple at the end of the day. Once you consummate your marriage with Princess Jiahe, you'll discover how sweet she is—and just like me, you won't want to stop! Once leads to twice, twice leads to a hundred times; you're content, and she's overjoyed. Since you can't have the woman in your heart, lock her away deep inside like I did and embrace the wife right beside you. That's the only way everyone comes out in one piece."
[ZH] 又想起刚回临安那日，乔梦容说：“你心里还是放不下她，你越放不下她，我就越恨她。”
[EN] It wasn't that Shen Jianchen lacked normal desires or had never thought of taking Mengrong to bed—he simply couldn't step over the moral line inside his own head. Bedding a woman he didn't love felt wrong to the bone, especially when he had only ever viewed Mengrong as a little sister.
[ZH] 他才品出自己执着于对千里寻的爱，无形中会给梦容造成伤害。
[EN] "She calls me *Brother*," he said grimly. "In my head she's my little sister. How could I lay hands on my own sister? I'd feel lower than a beast."
[ZH] 他端起酒酿一饮而尽，将酒杯重重地放在桌上，艰难地说：“文渊，我……我听你的，等我了结完刚接手的案子，就回幽林别居，与……与梦容圆……圆房。”
[EN] "Back when you were sick, you loved her—and now you've married her! She stopped being your sister ages ago; she's your wedded wife! Touching your own wife is Heaven's law, not some taboo. On the contrary, *refusing* to touch her leaves her frantic and insecure—and before you know it, that bitterness might spill straight onto Shuzhen!"
[ZH] 仪王答应，乔文仙却高兴不起来，自己屈服于生活，还劝说仪王与他一样屈服于生活。在现实面前，一个人无论地位有多尊贵，也不得不向生活低头。
[EN] The memory flashed red before Shen Jianchen's eyes: Mengrong slapping Qianli Xun across the face on her wedding day and shrieking, *"Zhu Shuzhen! I hate you!"* Followed by her icy threat the day they returned to Lin'an: *"You still can't let her go—and the harder you hold onto her, the more I hate her!"*
[ZH] 乔文仙又为二人先后斟满酒酿：“这就对了。……。她已嫁人，听说陈浩宇也变好，你我把她放在心底，默默祝她幸福，只要她过得好，你我便都好。来，干了这杯，为不得不屈服的生活。”
[EN] Only now did it sink in how his stubborn devotion to Qianli Xun was twisting the knife inside Mengrong—and putting Qianli Xun in the crosshairs.
[ZH] 申简辰也端起酒杯，与文渊互相碰了一下，二人一起饮下。
[EN] Throwing back his entire cup in one gulp, Shen Jianchen set the vessel down with a heavy *thud*. Every word scraped out of his throat like gravel. "Wenyuan... I'll listen to you. Once I close this murder case I just picked up, I'll go back to Youlin Retreat and... consummate my marriage with Mengrong."
[ZH] 乔文仙放下酒杯慨叹道：“你说这世上有没有人不必屈服于生活，过得自由自在？官家呢？他会不会是唯一一位不用屈服于生活的人？”
[EN] Getting the prince to cave brought Qiao Wenxian no triumph at all. First he had bowed his neck to reality, and now he had talked his best friend into bowing too. Against the weight of the real world, even an imperial prince had to bend the knee.
[ZH] 申简辰沉浸在刚刚答应与乔梦容圆房的想法中，没想到他这么快改变观念。还曾没脸没皮地在千里寻面前说出要为她守身如玉，现在想来觉得自己既虚伪又可笑。
[EN] Refilling both cups, Qiao Wenxian raised his. "That's the right move... She's married now, and word is Chen Haoyu really has straightened up. Let's both lock her in our hearts and wish her well in silence—so long as she's happy, we'll be fine. Come on, bottoms up—to the life we're forced to bow to!"
[ZH] 文渊提到官家，官家正是他不得不屈服的现实，一道圣旨，他随缘娶了梦容，离爱的人越来越远。
[EN] Shen Jianchen clinked his cup against his friend's, and both drank deep.
[ZH] 昏君呢？终有一日也不得不在他该面对的现实前低下头颅，申简辰竟然希望那一日来得快些。
[EN] Setting down his empty cup, Qiao Wenxian sighed. "Tell me—is there anyone on earth who doesn't have to bow to life and gets to live completely free? What about His Majesty? Is he the one man alive who never has to bend?"
[ZH] “只要是人都有不得不屈从的现实，官家也是，昏聩的朝政，时代的变迁，会搞得他无比卑微。”
[EN] Still reeling from his own pledge to consummate his marriage—how hollow and laughable his bold oath to stay chaste for Qianli Xun felt now!—Shen Jianchen heard the mention of the emperor. Wasn't His Majesty the very hammer of reality that had forced this wedding decree on him and driven him away from the woman he loved? As for that incompetent sovereign, sooner or later history would force *him* onto his knees too—and part of Shen Jianchen almost wished that day would hurry up and arrive.
[ZH] 乔文仙难以预料以后的变化，他以为仪王说这些不过是想表达不满。自从仪王起死回生后，变得喜欢抨击亲生老子的朝政。还好他知分寸，知道在什么人面前可以抨击，否则脑袋都不知掉落几回。
[EN] "Every human being faces a reality he has to bow to—His Majesty included," Shen Jianchen said darkly. "A rotting court and the turning tide of an era will grind him lower than dust."
[ZH] 白风带着南双在司理院到处参观一圈儿后，两人返回，参军居住的院子门口有两个石墩，他们正好坐在那里聊天。
[EN] Unable to foresee the storm history held in store, Qiao Wenxian chalked it up to his friend blowing off steam. Ever since coming back from the dead, the prince loved trashing his own father's court—good thing he had the sense to watch who was in the room, or his head would have rolled ten times over.
[ZH] “司理院的环境太一般，仪王能住得习惯？”
[EN] Meanwhile, after touring the Judicial Office grounds together, Bai Feng and Nan Shuang returned to perch on a pair of stone stools outside the Investigator's courtyard gate.
[ZH] “我家王爷对环境不挑，该睡睡该吃吃，没什么不习惯。”
[EN] "This place is pretty bare-bones," Nan Shuang observed. "Can Prince Yi really stomach living here?"
[ZH] “仪王这么金贵的人竟然跟我一样不挑地方。”
[EN] "My master isn't picky—eats whatever's served, sleeps wherever there's a bed. Doesn't bother him a bit."
[ZH] “少拿我家王爷跟你比，不是一个档次。”
[EN] "Whoa—a noble prince as easygoing as me?"
[ZH] 南双并不生气，满脸堆笑：“问你个事儿，你跟苦荞那个没？”
[EN] "Quit comparing yourself to His Highness—you're not even in the same league."
[ZH] 白风一脸疑惑：“哪个？”
[EN] Unfazed, Nan Shuang flashed a cheeky grin. "Hey, tell me something—have you and Kuqiao done *that* yet?"
[ZH] 南双凑近白风耳边，悄悄说了一会儿，然后又是满脸笑容。
[EN] "Done *what*?" Bai Feng blinked.
[ZH] 白风不满道：“你们还没成亲就这样，也太早了些。”
[EN] Nan Shuang leaned over and whispered straight into his ear, then leaned back grinning ear to ear.
[ZH] “公子说明年让我娶幼凡。”
[EN] Bai Feng scowled. "You two aren't even married yet! Bit early for that, isn't it?"
[ZH] “上次你不是说你家公子要等你长两岁再说吗？”
[EN] "Young Master says I can marry Youfan next year."
[ZH] “没办法，我跟幼凡在丫鬟房里偷偷私会被人发现，告到公子那里，他今日才说明年就给我娶幼凡。”
[EN] "Didn't you tell me he was making you wait two years?"
[ZH] 白风用手指指着南双：“你比我还小一岁，竟然如此猴急，幼凡也是，就不怕自己吃亏？”
[EN] "Well, somebody caught me and Youfan sneaking around in the maids' quarters and snitched to Young Master—so today he caved and promised next year!"
[ZH] “不是我猴急，是幼凡。”
[EN] Bai Feng jabbed a finger at him. "You're a year younger than me and already this impatient! And what's Youfan thinking—isn't she worried about getting taken advantage of?"
[ZH] 白风瞪大眼睛：“什么？南双，你是男人吗？竟然污蔑人家女孩子？”
[EN] "Hey, I wasn't the impatient one—*she* was!"
[ZH] 南双再次凑近白风的耳边，小声说：“我家少夫人成亲前，苗家请过一位经验丰富的嬷嬷，把毕生所学倾囊传授。幼凡在一旁伺候，多少学了些。后来少夫人嫁过来，用此法将我家公子拿得服服帖帖。每次公子去少夫人房中，幼凡都要在一旁伺候，她听得心热，找我练练。我也是血气方刚的男儿，哪经得起她诱惑？必然屈服。”
[EN] Bai Feng's eyes went wide. "What?! Call yourself a man, pinning the blame on a girl?"
[ZH] 白风批评道：“还有脸说自己是血气方刚的男儿，我看你就是经不起诱惑的色鬼！”
[EN] Nan Shuang leaned back in, dropping his voice to a conspiratorial whisper. "Before my Young Madam married in, the Miao family hired some veteran matron who taught her every trick in the book. Youfan was right there waiting on her and picked up a fair bit. Ever since Young Madam used those moves to wrap Young Master around her finger, Youfan has to attend outside the bed curtains every night—gets all worked up listening and drags me off to practice! I'm red-blooded flesh and bone—how am I supposed to say no to that?"
[ZH] 南双依然不生气：“你敢说你能经得起诱惑？”
[EN] "Red-blooded my foot!" Bai Feng scoffed. "You're just a little lecher with zero willpower!"
[ZH] “我……我当然能。”
[EN] Nan Shuang kept grinning. "Oh yeah? Swear you could resist?"
[ZH] “若是苦荞诱惑你呢？”
[EN] "Of... of course I could!"
[ZH] “苦荞才不是那种随随便便的女孩子，我也不会像你那样随便。”
[EN] "What if Kuqiao came tempting you?"
[ZH] “你好，你不随便，可你却体会不到我的快乐。不如下次见到苦荞，把她带到没人的地方体验一回？保准你终生难忘。”
[EN] "Kuqiao isn't some loose girl, and I'm not careless like you!"
[ZH] 白风将南双推到一边：“胡说什么？以为我跟你一样？你小子以后可别干出什么坏事来，我现在跟着王爷在司理院办差，小心到时办了你。”
[EN] "Sure, sure, you're a saint—and you're missing out on heaven! Next time you see Kuqiao, drag her somewhere quiet and try it once. Guarantee you'll never forget it!"
[ZH] “放心，我专一着呢，只跟幼凡。”
[EN] Bai Feng shoved him sideways. "Shut your dirty mouth! Think I'm like you? Don't go crossing any lines out there, kid—I work for the Judicial Office now, so watch yourself or I'll lock you up!"
[ZH] “你今日说话色眯眯的样子，恐怕以后会出事。”
[EN] "Relax, I'm loyal to a fault—Youfan's the only one for me."
[ZH] “能出什么事？我都快跟幼凡成亲。”
[EN] "With that sleazy look in your eye today, trouble's bound to find you sooner or later."
[ZH] 听了这话，白风认为自己一定是太多疑。虽然他一直批判南双，但不得不承认，南双的话搞得他心里痒痒的，真想快些娶苦荞过门。想起苦荞让他再等等的话，失落的情绪出现在脸上。
[EN] "What trouble? I'm practically married to her already!"
[ZH] 也不知幽栖居士遇到什么坎儿？以至于苦荞暂时不让他上门提亲，实在想不通。
[EN] Listening to him, Bai Feng decided he was probably just worrying too much. Still, much as he scolded Nan Shuang, the boy's gossip left an undeniable itch in his chest, making him eager to marry Kuqiao soon. Remembering her asking him to wait dimmed his mood again. What rough patch could Youqi Jushi possibly be facing that made Kuqiao hold off on his proposal? He couldn't figure it out for the life of him.

#CH 259
[ZH] 晚上，陈浩宇从绸缎庄回来，按时在门外报告。
[ZH] “娘子，我回来了，今日我进步很大，伙计的活儿已十分熟悉……”
[ZH] 话还未说完，苦荞从里面把门打开：“姑爷，小姐请您进来。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇受宠若惊，几日的努力终于有成果：“我这就进去。”
[ZH] 走进后发现娘子并未正襟危坐，而是站着，像是专门等他一般，更是心花怒放。
[ZH] “娘子，没想到这么晚，你还在等我，我好感动。”
[ZH] 搁往常，千里寻必然将陈浩宇的话怼回去。既打算学着接受他，怼他并不合适。
[ZH] “你忙一整天很辛苦，苦荞刚做好一盏茶，把它喝了。”
[ZH] 苦荞已将茶盏端到姑爷面前：“姑爷，小姐算好时间，让我提前做好等你回来喝，你刚刚在外面说话时，我也刚做好。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇二话不说，立刻端起来一口喝下，再将空茶盏递给苦荞。
[ZH] 好心情的作用下，陈浩宇只觉一盏茶下肚，浑身舒坦：“带着娘子心意的茶喝起来感觉就是好！”
[ZH] 千里寻说：“你累了一天，赶紧休息吧。”
[ZH] 又是给他准备茶，又是关切的语气，陈浩宇以为娘子已经接纳他，不免想入非非：“娘子，我能不能今晚住在你这里？”
[ZH] 青瓜和苦荞的脸上都露出诧异的表情，更别说千里寻。她试着学习接受对方，刚刚开始，甚是不习惯，对方就提出如此要求，千里寻立刻原形毕露。
[ZH] “陈浩宇！你满脑子都想些什么？老娘刚对你好些，就得寸进尺！你在青楼里干的那些个龌龊事，老娘想起来就觉恶心！还想跟老娘睡一个屋子，下辈子吧！”
[ZH] 陈浩宇意识到火候还未到，他不小心用力猛了些，还是赶紧服软：“娘子别生气，怪我太喜欢你，心急了些，你继续对我好，以后我不敢再提无礼的要求。”
[EN] That evening, Chen Haoyu came home from the silk emporium and made his usual check-in outside her door. "Wife, I'm back! Made huge progress today—I've pretty much got the hang of working the counter..."
[ZH] 对方态度端正，千里寻也不好一直生气，语气缓和许多：“走吧，别杵在这儿烦我。”
[EN] Before he could finish, Kuqiao pulled the door open from inside. "Young Master, Miss invites you in."
[ZH] “这就走，这就走，祝娘子晚上做个好梦，最好梦里有我。”陈浩宇担心娘子再生气，迅速带着青瓜离开。
[EN] Catching his breath in delighted shock—days of dogged effort had finally paid off!—Haoyu blurted, "Coming right in!"
[ZH] 回后院的路上，青瓜一直“嘿嘿”笑着。
[EN] Stepping inside, he found Qianli Xun standing there rather than sitting stiffly at her table, looking as though she had been waiting just for him. Joy burst through his chest. "Wife, you stayed up waiting for me this late? I'm so touched!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇站住，转过身：“你笑什么？”
[EN] Any other evening, Qianli Xun would have snapped back a cutting remark. Since she had resolved to try accepting him, biting his head off didn't fit the plan. "You worked hard all day. Kuqiao just whisked a fresh bowl of tea—drink it."
[ZH] 青瓜忍住笑声：“没什么。”
[EN] Kuqiao presented the bowl to him. "Young Master, Miss timed it so I'd have this ready right as you got back. You were talking outside just as I finished."
[ZH] “不说小心我踢你。”
[EN] Without a second's hesitation, Haoyu drained the entire bowl in one gulp and handed it back to Kuqiao. Riding high on pure joy, he felt warmth spread from head to toe. "Tea made with my wife's care hits different!"
[ZH] “公子怎么还想踢我？您又不是不知道我现在躲避的本领超强。”
[EN] "You've been on your feet all day," Qianli Xun said. "Go get some rest."
[ZH] “赶紧说，否则今晚去西院睡觉。”
[EN] Hot tea waiting for him, soft words of concern—convinced his wife had finally thawed, Haoyu let hope run away with his tongue. "Wife... could I sleep in here with you tonight?"
[ZH] “好好好，我说。”青瓜说着又乐起来，“嘿嘿嘿，我是笑少夫人，她明显是想对公子您好些，可却不知道怎么对公子好。”
[EN] Qinggua and Kuqiao both froze in shock—and Qianli Xun blew straight through her new resolve, showing her true claws in half a heartbeat.
[ZH] “她给我准备了茶，还说些关心的话，难道不对吗？”
[EN] "Chen Haoyu! What filth is rattling around inside that skull?! I act halfway civil for five seconds and you push for a mile! Every nasty thing you did in those brothels still turns my stomach! Sleep in the same room with me? Wait for your next lifetime!"
[ZH] “公子觉得够吗？嘿嘿嘿……”
[EN] Realizing the iron wasn't hot yet and he had jumped the gun, Haoyu backpedaled fast. "Don't be mad, Wife—my fault for liking you so much I got ahead of myself! Keep treating me nice, I swear I won't push my luck again!"
[ZH] “是不够，不过需慢慢来。你想想，她以前跟我可是死对头，又知道是我散播她的谣言，能像今日这般对待我，已是不易。”陈浩宇脸上露出高兴的神色，“说明她想跟我好好过日子，我也需努力努力，明日也该去永泰茶楼拜师学艺。”
[EN] With him backing down so meekly, Qianli Xun couldn't stay steamed and dialed her tone down a notch. "Get out of here. Stop standing around getting on my nerves."
[ZH] 青瓜不再继续笑，公子一本正经想入非非的样子，让他觉得继续笑下去，太不尊重少夫人。
[EN] "Leaving right now, leaving right now! Sweet dreams, Wife—hope I'm in them!" Afraid of setting her off again, Haoyu whisked Qinggua right out the door.
[ZH] 姑爷走后，苦荞边为小姐宽衣边说：“您开始表现挺好，后面怎么又发起火来？”
[EN] Halfway back toward the rear quarters, Qinggua couldn't stop snickering under his breath.
[ZH] “你还问我？他提那要求搁谁谁不生气。”外衣已脱掉，千里寻身着睡衣躺在床上，将薄薄的蚕丝被盖在身上。
[EN] Haoyu spun around. "What's so funny?"
[ZH] 苦荞边挂衣裳边说：“姑爷的要求确实唐突，可仔细一想也没什么不对，毕竟已成亲，在外人看来，他要求住在素心草堂再正常不过。”
[EN] "Nothing!" Qinggua choked back a laugh.
[ZH] “你竟然帮他说话？”
[EN] "Spill it or you're getting a kick."
[ZH] 苦荞走到床边：“我不是帮姑爷，是帮小姐，想提醒您以后别老对姑爷发火，要不真成姑爷说的母……”
[EN] "Still trying to kick me, Young Master? You know my dodge footwork is untouchable now!"
[ZH] “母老虎？”
[EN] "Talk, or you're sleeping in the West Courtyard tonight."
[ZH] 苦荞点点头：“再把变好的姑爷给吓跑，他纳一两个妾室，墨香苑会变得鸡飞狗跳，到时恐怕小姐不得安生。”
[EN] "All right, all right, I'll talk!" Qinggua broke into another grin. "Hehehe—I'm laughing at Young Madam! She's clearly trying to be nice to you, only she has no clue *how* to be nice to a man!"
[ZH] “我倒希望她找妾室，可陈浩宇不乐意。”
[EN] "She made me tea and checked if I was tired—what's wrong with that?"
[ZH] 苦荞又替小姐将蚕丝被盖好：“我怎么觉着小姐说这话有些得意？”
[EN] "Think that's enough, Young Master? Hehehe..."
[ZH] “你哪只眼睛看到我得意了？目前他说的话还让我得意不起来。你家小姐我都想好了，无论陈浩宇纳不纳妾都得跟他过日子。他要是纳妾日子还好过些，有妾室陪他省去我不少麻烦；可他要不纳妾，我可就难了。”
[EN] "No, it's not enough, though we have to take it slow. Think about it—we used to be sworn enemies, and she knows I spread those rumors about her. Treating me even this well is already huge." A bright grin spread across Haoyu's face. "Means she's willing to give our marriage a shot. I need to step up my game too—tomorrow I'm heading to Yongtai Teahouse to find a tea master and learn to whisk tea."
[ZH] “这有什么难的？小姐只要拿出当初跟乔公子在一起的劲头来，不就万事大吉？”
[EN] Seeing his master grinning earnestly over such tiny crumbs stopped Qinggua's chuckles; laughing any further felt disrespectful to Young Madam.
[ZH] 千里寻早已放下文渊，苦荞提起并不会再次引起她的伤感之情。
[EN] Inside Suxin Thatched Hall, Kuqiao helped Qianli Xun out of her outer robe. "You started out doing so well, Miss—why did you snap at him at the end?"
[ZH] “那时我喜欢文渊自然愿意跟他亲近，可我根本不喜欢陈浩宇，估计拉他的手全身都会起鸡皮疙瘩，恋爱的劲头用在他身上不合适。”
