[ZH] 乔梦容在哥哥躺过的地方又趴了一会儿，通过枕头和被子再嗅一嗅哥哥身上的味道，那味道刺激着她的嗅觉，进而刺激她的大脑，昨晚的情景一幕一幕呈现在脑海……
[ZH] 哥哥对待她既温柔又粗鲁。温柔时像一只绵羊，在她耳边咩咩地叫着，声音低沉而轻柔，她整个身心就像平静的湖水不断泛起粼粼的水波。粗鲁时，像惊涛骇浪，带着她一起向岸边的岩石拍打而去，直到撞击出无数的水花……
[ZH] 正当乔梦容沉浸在昨晚情景的回味中时，喜兰将床幔挂起：“王妃，该起床了。”
[ZH] 乔梦容不舍地从哥哥睡过的地方爬起，用手摸摸枕头，仿佛只有如此才能将哥哥的气味留在她的指间。
[ZH] 喜兰见仪王妃懒懒的样子，心中觉得好笑，定是昨晚与王爷折腾得太累。
[ZH] 她一边为仪王妃整理衣装，一边说：“王爷关心王妃，走时吩咐奴婢让厨房给王妃熬了滋补粥，刚熬好，已放在桌上，王妃梳洗完毕，刚好食用。”
[ZH] 哥哥如此关心她，乔梦容更觉幸福，一扫懒懒的状态，变得精神起来。
[ZH] 梳洗完毕，整理好妆容后，坐在桌前用心享受哥哥为她安排的滋补粥，甜而不腻，还有股淡淡的中草药的味道，并不苦，反而更觉爽口。
[ZH] 享用完滋补粥后，乔梦容不想待在幽林别居，没有哥哥的地方实在无趣。
[ZH] “喜兰，我今日无事，你陪我去趟司理院。”
[ZH] 正要起身带着喜兰去司理院找哥哥，有丫鬟进来禀报：“仪王妃，苗夫人和白夫人前来拜见。”
[ZH] 乔梦容心中埋怨二人来得不是时候，又一想她们应该是为书卷之事而来，也不知进展得如何，不妨接见接见，与她们聊完再去司理院不迟。
[ZH] 她继续坐好：“请二位夫人进来。”
[ZH] 丫鬟去后，两位夫人一起走进，向仪王妃请安之后，各自落座。
[ZH] 白梅若已打定主意，绝不先开口，一坐下便紧闭嘴巴。
[ZH] 苗知瑜发现白梅若一句话不说，总不能尴尬地坐着，于是先开口：“我与白夫人来幽林别居是有要事与王妃禀报。”
[ZH] 乔梦容问：“可是书卷之事？”
[ZH] 白梅若以为仪王已将昨日之事告知仪王妃，立刻插嘴道：“王妃已知昨日之事？”
[ZH] 乔梦容疑惑：“昨日之事？发生了什么？”
[ZH] 对方还不知，白梅若后悔主动问出，又不敢言语，尴尬地低下头。
[ZH] 苗知瑜瞧出白梅若的小心思，林觉帮王爷破坏王妃的计划，她担心被王妃批评，一直不敢主动说出昨日之事。她苗知瑜可不怕，再说只有讲出来，仪王妃不仅不会怪罪她们办事不力，还会将一切罪过归咎到幽栖居士身上，恐怕她在劫难逃，也好帮自己出口气。
[ZH] 苗知瑜主动说道：“诗会后，我与白夫人尽心竭力将诗会的内容整理在册，又添油加醋一番，找到书局刊印成书卷，又花钱找人在街上免费发放。此事不知怎么被仪王殿下发现，他派人将所有书卷收回，还抓了书局的管事。林统制也参与其中，白夫人最清楚不过。”
[ZH] 苗知瑜提到她，白梅若必须开口：“我家夫君只是个统制，官职不高，仪王的话不得不听。此事已被殿下破坏，毁掉江南女词人美名的计划恐怕得泡汤。”
[ZH] 计划会不会泡汤，已不是乔梦容关注的焦点，关键是哥哥竟然参与此事。他为何要这么做？
[ZH] 既然书局的管事被抓，哥哥应该已知背后操纵之人是她乔梦容，为何昨晚回来只字不提？还对她那么好？
[ZH] 哥哥若不爱她，又为何与她圆房？若爱她，为何要帮朱淑真？难道他的心里还装着女词人？
[ZH] 眼泪已在乔梦容的眼中打转，哥哥人已属于她，可心并未全在她的身上，想让哥哥心中只有她，必须让另一个人消失。
[ZH] 仪王妃眼眶变红，沉默不语。两位夫人更是紧张不已，担心贵人怪罪她们。
[ZH] 还是苗知瑜最有办法：“请王妃明示，下一步该怎么做？”
[ZH] 乔梦容略微平复心情，忍住即将掉下的泪水：“感谢二位夫人如实相告，计划失败与二位无关。哥哥既然把书局的管事抓了，必然已从他口中得知与此事相关之人。他昨晚回幽林别居只字未提，应该是看在我的面子上并不想将此事闹大。二位夫人也已暴露，还是收敛些好，你们先回去好生候着，若需要，我会差人上门去请。”
[ZH] 二位夫人再笨，也能听得出，仪王妃不想与她们继续聊下去，两人很知趣，起身行礼离开。
[ZH] 走出正院，白梅若才大胆问：“苗夫人，仪王妃到底什么意思？”
[ZH] 苗知瑜对白梅若刚才自保的举动甚是不满：“难道白夫人听不出？仪王妃是让我们暂时不要来打扰她。不打扰也好，省得白夫人为自保一言不发。”
[ZH] 被对方看出，白梅若一脸尴尬：“苗夫人别跟我计较，毕竟我家那位帮着破坏了王妃的计划，我担心贵人怪罪。没想到仪王妃通情达理，这下我就放心了。”
[EN] The moment the three scholars walked out, Bai Feng winced for his master's wallet. "Why are *you* footing the bill for Young Master Dongfang's salon?"
[ZH] 苗知瑜懒得再理会白梅若，继续往出走。
[EN] "Promised him before Bianliang—can't go back on my word."
[ZH] 二人来到幽林别居门口，白梅若没有上自己的马车，而是跟着进了苗知瑜的马车。
[EN] "Out of all things to promise, a money pit with zero return? Total loss!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜没好气地问：“为何上我的马车？”
[EN] "Some things matter more than silver."
[ZH] 白梅若坐好后，厚着脸皮说：“想和苗夫人多聊会儿，进了城我再下来不迟。”
[EN] "Easy for a rich prince to say! To me, silver's king."
[ZH] 苗知瑜懒得与她说话，装作闭目养神的样子。
[EN] "Oh? What's worth more to you—Kuqiao or silver?"
[ZH] 白梅若主动说：“我这人笨，还得苗夫人帮我分析分析仪王妃的意思。”
[EN] Stumped for three seconds, Bai Feng declared, "Equal!" Then, remembering Nan Shuang's and Xilan's warnings, he probed cautiously, "Your Highness... if I marry Kuqiao, will the Princess Consort object?"
[ZH] 苗知瑜继续闭着眼：“刚不都给你说过，怎么还问？”
[EN] "Why would she?" Shen Jianchen didn't look up from his dossier.
[ZH] “你说仪王妃以后是不是不会再找幽栖居士麻烦？”
[EN] "Do I need the Princess Consort's permission?"
[ZH] “不好说。”
[EN] "You're my guard—my permission is all you need."
[ZH] “为何？”
[EN] "If I marry her, could we live outside Youlin Retreat? Buy our own little house so Kuqiao stays comfortable—without slacking on guarding you, of course!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜睁开双眼：“不找，像吃了只苍蝇，恶心着呢；找吧，又担心仪王插手。可仪王插手，王妃更会找幽栖居士麻烦，所以幽栖居士还是没好日子过。”
[EN] "I'll buy the house for you," Shen Jianchen said smoothly.
[ZH] 白梅若十分不解：“那为何不让我们两个来幽林别居？没有我们两个，谁帮仪王妃？”
[EN] "Thank you, Your Highness!" *Problem solved!* Bai Feng beamed—working for Prince Yi really paid off.
[ZH] “你啊，别把自己想得太重要。她是贵人，找个人帮忙还不是轻轻松松的事儿？再说我们两个已暴露，稍有举动，仪王必然会猜到王妃的身上，我们还是老老实实在家待着。”
[EN] Back at Youlin Retreat, Qiao Mengrong woke to find Brother already gone without a sound. Bliss mixed with a hollow ache—how she wished his face were the first thing she saw every dawn. Back when he was sick, he couldn't eat, walk, or dress without her; she held every thread of his days. Now, healthy and strong, he slipped right past her grasp, and after tasting him last night, her craving to hold him tight only grew fiercer—terrified that pushing too hard would drive him away.
[ZH] 白梅若终于明白：“还是苗夫人聪慧，刚才的事就别跟我计较。”
[EN] Rolling onto his warm pillow, she breathed in his scent as last night's memories washed over her—his gentle murmurs rippling through her like breeze across a quiet lake, then his fierce, surging passion crashing over her like waves on rock...
[ZH] “我若计较呢？”
[EN] Xilan hooked back the bed curtains. "Time to rise, Your Highness."
[ZH] “算我错了，不如我请客，把你的闺中密友都叫上，一起去永泰茶楼。”
[EN] Lingering one last second to brush her fingertips over his pillow, Mengrong sat up. Smiling knowingly at her mistress's languid glow, Xilan helped her dress. "His Highness had the kitchen simmer nourishing porridge specially for you before he left—waiting hot on the table."
[ZH] “还是算了，永泰茶楼现在生意不好，字画好久没更换过，听说陈家马上接手。先给你记在账上，等陈家接手后步入正轨我们再去不迟。”
[EN] That perked Mengrong right up. Washing, dressing, and savoring the lightly herbal, sweet porridge, she felt too restless to sit around an empty house. "Xilan, let's go visit the Judicial Office."
[ZH] “好，就这么说定了。”
[EN] Just as she stood, a maid announced Madam Miao and Madam Bai. Irritated at the interruption yet curious about the pamphlets, Mengrong sat back down. "Show them in."
[ZH] 两位夫人走后，乔梦容继续伤心，已没有心情去司理院。她流了一会儿泪，吩咐喜兰将邹嬷嬷找来。
[EN] Both women curtsied and sat—Bai Meiruo promptly sealing her lips shut, leaving Miao Zhiyu to carry the ball.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷来后，见仪王妃眼圈红红的，猜到与仪王有关，坐下说道：“王爷昨晚回来陪伴过王妃，为何你看上去仍旧不高兴？”
[EN] "We came to report something urgent, Your Highness."
[ZH] “哥哥虽与我圆房，可他心里还装着女词人，我又如何能高兴？”
[EN] "The pamphlets?"
[ZH] “王妃会不会要求得太多？”
[EN] "Wait—Your Highness already knows what happened yesterday?" Bai Meiruo blurted, immediately wishing she could swallow her tongue when Mengrong blinked in confusion.
[ZH] “我只要求哥哥心中只有我一人，全心全意待我，难道多吗？”
[EN] *Coward!* Miao Zhiyu sneered silently, seizing control cleanly. "After the salon, Madam Bai and I compiled the critiques, spiced them up, printed them, and paid folks to hand them out. Somehow Prince Yi caught wind of it, confiscated every copy, and arrested the print manager—Commander Lin helped round them up, as Madam Bai knows best."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷点点头：“是多了些，对男子可要求这，要求那，就是不能要求他心中只有你一人，何况还是王爷？王妃仔细想想，官家那些个成年的皇子哪个身边只有一个女子？”
[EN] Dragged into the spotlight, Bai Meiruo stammered, "My husband's just a garrison commander—couldn't refuse Prince Yi's order! Looks like ruining her reputation hit a snag."
[ZH] “哥哥身边有其他女子我都能忍，唯独无法忍受他的心里还装着朱淑真。”
[EN] The ruined scheme didn't even register with Mengrong—*Brother* had intervened! If he arrested the print manager, he *knew* his own wife pulled the strings—so why hadn't he breathed a word last night while holding her so sweetly?! Did he still love Zhu Shuzhen?! Tears burned behind her eyes. She held his body, not his whole heart—and the only way to claim his heart was wiping that other woman off the map.
[ZH] 仪王妃说这话时面露愤恨之色，邹嬷嬷便知仪王妃眼中根本容不下这位女词人。
[EN] Seeing the Princess Consort's reddening eyes and icy silence, both visitors held their breath until Miao Zhiyu ventured, "What are your next orders, Your Highness?"
[ZH] “该怎么做？仪王妃请说。”
[EN] Forcing her tears back down, Mengrong said evenly, "Thank you both for telling me straight. This failure isn't on you. Since Brother questioned the print manager, he already knows who was involved; staying silent last night means he doesn't want a scandal on my account. Since you two are exposed, lie low for now—wait at home until I send word."
[ZH] “诗会后我让苗夫人和白夫人去做的事已失败，最可恨的，是因哥哥而失败。辛苦嬷嬷告诉陈家大公子，该他出场，让他帮忙与蒋爷搭上关系，我要借蒋爷之手除掉朱淑真。”
[EN] Taking the clear dismissal, both women curtsied and retreated.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷完全理解仪王妃，毫不犹豫地答应：“我这就去找陈家大公子。”
[EN] Outside the main courtyard, Bai Meiruo whispered, "What did she mean by that?"
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷乘坐马车，来到西湖边的永泰茶楼。进入后发现里面生意惨淡，伙计都懒得招呼她。
[EN] "She means stay out of sight—just as well, since someone went mute to save her own skin!" Miao Zhiyu snapped.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷径直走上二楼，来到一个包间外，只见门上有句诗“一览众山小”。邹嬷嬷虽识字，可对诗词没有研究，也不感兴趣，示意身边的丫鬟将门推开。
[EN] "Don't hold it against me—my husband helped wreck her plot! Good thing Her Highness is reasonable." Climbing uninvited right into Miao Zhiyu's carriage at the gate, Bai Meiruo pushed on despite Miao Zhiyu's closed eyes. "Think Her Highness will drop going after Youqi Jushi now?"
[ZH] 丫鬟乖乖上前推开门，一股茶香扑面而来，也传来一阵琴声。
[EN] "Hard to say." Miao Zhiyu cracked her eyes open. "Dropping it feels like swallowing a fly; moving again risks Prince Yi stepping in—and the more he steps in, the harder Her Highness will go after her. Either way, Youqi Jushi's in for rough weather."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷走进后，瞧见陈浩东坐在桌前品茶，女茶师在茶桌旁做茶，男琴师则专注地抚琴。
[EN] "Then why bench us? Who else will help her?"
[ZH] “陈大公子好雅兴！”
[EN] "Don't flatter yourself—she's royalty, she can snap her fingers for new hands anytime! We're burned; stay home and keep quiet."
[ZH] 陈浩东见邹嬷嬷进来，赶紧起身行礼：“嬷嬷大驾光临，有失远迎。”
[EN] "Smart as ever, Madam Miao! Let me treat you and your girlfriends to tea at Yongtai Teahouse to make up for today!"
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷毫不客气，坐在为她准备的桌旁：“大公子不必客气，你我坐下聊。”
[EN] "Pass—their art's stale and business is dead. Put it on my tab until the Chen family finishes taking over and freshens it up."
[ZH] 陈浩东继续坐下：“嬷嬷别急，先听听曲儿，品尝一下女茶师做的茶，再聊正事不迟。”
[EN] "Deal!"
[ZH] 要与陈浩东谈的事毕竟见不得光，有他人在场十分不便，不如等等。邹嬷嬷点点头，表示同意。她对乐曲一窍不通，也不感兴趣，倒是女茶师做茶的动作饶有趣味，她定睛瞧着……
[EN] Back inside, Mengrong wept briefly before summoning Matron Zou.
[ZH] 女茶师终于做完茶，最有趣的是，茶做完，乐曲刚好演奏完。女茶师示意身旁的丫鬟将茶盏为邹嬷嬷端到桌前摆好，才与琴师一起行礼离开，将门从外面关好。
[EN] "His Highness spent last night with you—why the red eyes?" Matron Zou asked gently.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷瞧着茶盏中的画作，疑惑道：“茶师的手艺不错，为何茶楼的生意如此惨淡？”
[EN] "He bedded me, yet his heart still clings to that poetess! How can I be happy?"
[ZH] 陈浩东回道：“陈家正接手永泰茶楼，七日后这里会恢复如初。”
[EN] "Aren't you asking too much, Your Highness? Demand anything from a man—just never demand sole ownership of his heart, especially an imperial prince! Name one grown son of His Majesty who only has one woman."
[ZH] “原来如此，难怪陈大公子约我来此。”邹嬷嬷端起茶盏品了一口，真是沁人心脾。
[EN] "I could stomach ten other women beside Brother—anyone except Zhu Shuzhen!" Venom hardened her face. "The pamphlet plan failed because *Brother* crushed it! Go tell Eldest Young Master Chen it's his turn—have him link us with Master Jiang to get rid of Zhu Shuzhen for good."
[ZH] 陈浩东笑道：“不是因陈家要接手永泰茶楼我才约嬷嬷来这里，以前我也常来，这个包间我常年包着，用来洽谈生意或是与朋友相聚。”
[EN] Unable to refuse her, Matron Zou rose at once. "I'll go find him right now."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷放下茶盏：“你我闲话少叙，还是说正事。”
[EN] Matron Zou's carriage pulled up at Yongtai Teahouse by West Lake—so empty the waiter didn't even bother greeting her. Climbing straight to the second floor, she paused outside a booth marked *"Dwarfing All Other Peaks"* and nodded for her maid to push the door open.
[ZH] “嬷嬷请讲。”
[EN] Tea aroma and guqin music drifted out. Inside sat Chen Haodong sipping tea while the female tea master whisked fresh bowls and a male musician strummed softly.
[ZH] “王妃说该你出手。”
[EN] "How refined, Eldest Young Master Chen!"
[ZH] “我早已安排好，只等王妃一声令下。”
[EN] Haodong rose bowing. "Forgive me for failing to greet you downstairs, Matron."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷很是疑惑，她还未说如何做，对方竟然已准备好。
[EN] "No ceremony—let's sit." Agreeing to wait out the music before discussing dirty business, Matron Zou watched the tea master's crisp, fluid motions until the last note of the guqin faded right as the finished bowl landed on her table. Once the two performers bowed out and shut the door, Matron Zou admired the foam painting and took a fragrant sip. "Fine skill—why is this place so empty?"
[ZH] “不知陈大公子如何安排？”
[EN] "The Chen family is taking over Yongtai Teahouse—in seven days it'll be booming again," Haodong chuckled. "Not that I picked it for that; I've kept this booth reserved year-round for private deals."
[ZH] “上次嬷嬷说先给她制造些麻烦，我早已安排好，只等贵人的指令。”
[EN] Setting her cup down, Matron Zou came straight to the point. "The Princess Consort says it's your move."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷摇摇头：“制造麻烦还不够，仪王妃想让她消失。”
[EN] "Already set up—just waiting for her word."
[ZH] “消失？”陈浩东没想到仪王妃如此心狠，“上次嬷嬷说需徐徐图之，怎么又变了？”
[EN] "Wait—what did you set up?"
[ZH] “仪王妃已等不及，眼中钉肉中刺不拔掉，她不舒服。需要陈大公子帮着找一人。”
[EN] "Two headaches for her, just like you asked last time."
[ZH] “谁？”
[EN] Matron Zou shook her head. "Headaches aren't enough anymore. Her Highness wants her gone."
[ZH] “蒋爷。”
[EN] "Gone?" Haodong blinked at the sudden ruthlessness. "Thought we were taking it slow?"
[ZH] “我与此人打过交道，也有些交情，找他做此事再好不过。只是这位蒋爷认钱不认人。”
[EN] "Her Highness is out of patience. She needs you to hire one man: Master Jiang."
[ZH] “只要认钱就好，银子不成问题。”
[EN] "I've dealt with him—we're on decent terms. Only Master Jiang answers to silver, nothing else."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷向丫鬟示意，只见丫鬟拿出一个木盒，走上前放在陈浩东的桌上。
[EN] "Silver is no problem." At Matron Zou's nod, her maid placed a wooden box packed thick with banknotes onto Haodong's table.
[ZH] 陈浩东打开，里面是厚厚一沓银票。将盖子盖好后，说道：“嬷嬷转告仪王妃，此事交给我尽管放心。只是我费了些心思准备好两个麻烦事儿，总得让我尽兴一回。”
[EN] Peeking inside and snapping the lid shut, Haodong smiled. "Tell Her Highness to rest easy. Still, since I put real effort into those two little headaches, let me enjoy watching them play out first."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷笑道：“你自行安排，只要不影响仪王妃关心的大事即可。”
[EN] "Suit yourself, so long as nothing delays Her Highness's main business."
[ZH] “绝不会影响，反而锦上添花。”
[EN] "Quite the opposite—it'll be icing on the cake."
[ZH] 两人商议好后，陈浩东离开茶楼去忙制造麻烦之事，还需去钱塘帮走一遭。
[EN] Parting ways, Chen Haodong strode off to light his two fuses—and pay a call on the Qiantang Gang.

#CH 270
[ZH] 懒人村的事有人出面解决，陈家的施粥棚只剩些老弱病残的流浪之人前来讨要粥吃，人数一下子减少一大半，管理起来甚是容易。千里寻决定明日让红豆带几个下人前来即可，她来不来已经不重要。
[ZH] 想到施粥棚的问题能够解决与仪王息息相关，千里寻的内心依然会萌生特殊的情愫。可梦容愤恨的眼神，还有昨日看到的书卷，她不敢沉迷下去，还是想想陈浩宇。
[ZH] 昨晚被他亲到，那种感觉她从未体验过，真是妙不可言。既然已经不是那么讨厌他，不如与他圆房。
[ZH] 想到圆房两个字，她依然感到别扭，还是等等吧。陈浩宇都说了，他愿意等，不能辜负他。
[ZH] 正想着，红豆走来：“小姐，施粥棚比昨日好多了，不如回府忙您的事，这里交给我。若您不愿回府，到街上逛逛也行。”
[ZH] 千里寻正有此意，她站起来：“此处交给你，我去逛街。”
[ZH] 红豆继续请示：“要不要随身带两个人？没了懒人村的人，施粥棚现在用不了这么多人。”
[ZH] 千里寻想独自逛街散散心：“不必，都留在这里，明日你少带几人来即可。”
[ZH] 显然小姐想一个人逛，红豆不再强求：“一切听小姐的。”
[ZH] 千里寻离开施粥棚，没有乘坐马车，既是逛，还需依赖双脚。她决定等逛累了再租辆马车回府。
[ZH] 街上虽不是人挤人，也足以显示出街市的繁华。
[ZH] 街市越热闹，越能衬托出一个人逛街的落寞。即使千里寻并未带着落寞的情绪而来，身旁经过的一对对情侣足以令她感到落寞。
[ZH] 经历过与文渊的初恋，还悄悄喜欢上仪王，现在又嫁给陈浩宇，昨晚还被他亲，逛个街竟然还是孤身一人。
[ZH] 去年时，身边还有文渊、仪王和梦容陪伴。真是时过境迁，每个人都有了自己的归宿，不管这归宿你乐不乐意，冥冥中已替每个人安排得妥妥当当。
[ZH] 她突然想去找陈浩宇，现在也只能找他陪伴，否则她便是不守妇道。
[ZH] 经历过流言蜚语，又在诗会上被众人辱骂，千里寻觉出人言可畏，她不想再给他人留下辱骂她的机会。
[ZH] 记得今早陈浩宇走时，在门外说他还去绸缎庄忙活，不如去那里找他，顺便瞧瞧他如何当伙计。
[ZH] 千里寻租辆马车，车夫专门干拉人的活儿，对临安城各地儿无不熟悉，尤其是陈家的产业，那是出了名的，自然清楚走哪条道最方便。
[ZH] 到达陈家绸缎庄，千里寻走下马车，向车夫付了钱，望向匾额上写的“陈家绸缎庄”五个大字，心生一计，不如装作买料子的顾客，检验一下陈浩宇当伙计的成效。
[ZH] 她掏出丝帕，绑在脸上，只露出两只大眼睛，这样进去，只要不开口说话，陈浩宇一时半会儿应该认不出。
[ZH] 装扮好后，大胆走进去。里面很大，顾客不少，显然生意十分红火。再仔细瞧，各种花色和质地的绸缎应有尽有。
[ZH] 千里寻搜寻着陈浩宇的身影，只见各位伙计忙着招呼客人，大家穿一样的衣装，一时半会儿还真瞧不出哪位是陈浩宇。
[ZH] 倒是先瞧见青瓜，他的身形稍胖些，算是特点比较鲜明。青瓜也在招呼客人，满脸堆笑，给客人介绍这个又介绍那个，嘴皮子也顺溜。从跟班的身上就能瞧出主人的变化。
[ZH] 陈浩宇的个头不低，不如专挑个头高的伙计瞧，果然很快陈浩宇映入眼帘，他正将包装好的绸缎递给一位女顾客。
[ZH] “姑娘，您要的丝绸已装好，请您拿好，若有需要，再来陈家绸缎庄。”
[ZH] 那位姑娘将纸盒接在手中：“你这伙计懂得不少，下次我来还找你接待。”
[ZH] “荣幸之至，姑娘请。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇将那位姑娘送至门口，瞧见一位用丝帕蒙着面的姑娘盯着自己瞧，身形像极了家中的娘子，难道是她？又一想怎么可能？她在施粥棚忙活，不可能来这里。
[ZH] 青瓜也将顾客送至门口，见公子盯着美女瞧，没有接待的意思，干脆由他来。
[ZH] “姑娘想买什么？我给您一一介绍，定能让您选到满意的。”
[ZH] “去去去！她是我的客人，少跟我抢！”陈浩宇将青瓜推到一边。
[ZH] 青瓜很不满，凑近公子耳边：“小心我回去告诉少夫人，公子在外面盯着美女看。”
[ZH] 青瓜说完，担心公子揍他，迅速撤离，去迎接别的客人。
[ZH] 千里寻心中不满：“哼！好色之徒！见着美女就走不动！一双眼睛色眯眯地盯着我，还想跟我圆房，门儿都没有！”
[ZH] 只听陈浩宇说：“姑娘的身形酷似我家娘子，我不由多看几眼，还请姑娘见谅。”
[ZH] “心里还有我，那我就暂时原谅你。”千里寻想着，一句话不说，往里走。
[EN] With Lazybones Village off the scene, only genuinely frail, elderly, and crippled paupers remained at the Chen congee shed—cutting the crowd in half and making order effortless. Qianli Xun figured Hongdou and a few servants could handle it solo starting tomorrow.
[ZH] 陈浩宇赶紧跟上：“姑娘，您气质高雅，定是识货之人。陈家绸缎庄有各式各样的丝绸，不知您想挑选哪种质地的？”
[EN] Knowing Prince Yi had cleared that mess still tugged a subtle ache in her chest, yet Mengrong's venomous glare and yesterday's smear pamphlet snapped her straight back to Chen Haoyu. That surprise kiss last night had caught her utterly off guard—and felt weirdly good. Maybe she really could consummate the marriage soon... no, the mere word still felt awkward. He'd promised to wait; let him wait a little longer.
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“我偏不说话，看他如何应对。”
[EN] Hongdou walked over. "Things are smooth today, Miss. Why not head back—or stroll the streets for a bit? I've got this covered."
[ZH] 蒙面姑娘不言语，陈浩宇只能继续介绍：“我们店的丝绸种类繁多，质地优良，无论是贡品，还是民间用品，都能满足您的需求。请问您想了解哪种？”
[EN] Qianli Xun stood up right away. "I'll go take a walk. Keep everyone here—and bring fewer hands tomorrow."
[ZH] 蒙面姑娘依然不言语，陈浩宇仔细观察，发现她的目光在各色丝绸上流转，似有几分犹豫。
[EN] Skipping a carriage to stretch her legs, she drifted into Lin'an's bustling markets—only for the lively crowd and strolling couples to highlight how solitary she felt. First love with Wenyuan, hidden longing for Prince Yi, wed to Haoyu, kissed last night—yet still walking the street all alone. Last year Wenyuan, Prince Yi, and Mengrong had filled her days; now everyone had landed in whatever slot fate picked for them.
[ZH] 陈浩宇心领神会，开口说：“姑娘想必是为挑选哪种丝绸而烦恼？这绫罗绸缎，绫之纹理细密，光滑柔软，若制成衣裳，穿在身上，行动之间，流光溢彩；罗则轻薄透气，触感细腻，最是适合夏日穿戴，清凉又舒适；绸之质地厚实，光泽温润，做裙装端庄大气；缎则光滑如镜，色泽明艳，做披风定能让姑娘在人群中脱颖而出。”
[EN] Suddenly she wanted Haoyu's company—and he was the only man she *could* walk with in public without fueling fresh venom after all the gossip and Mengrong's salon. Remembering he was at the silk emporium, she hired a carriage straight there.
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“这家伙懂得倒挺多，伙计的活儿没白干。”
[EN] Paying the driver outside the grand *Chen Silk Emporium* signboard, a playful scheme struck her: test-drive Haoyu's shop-clerk skills incognito! Tying a silk handkerchief across her lower face so only her bright eyes showed, she marched inside.
[ZH] 姑娘依然不言语，继续往里走，陈浩宇仍旧跟上，如数家珍：“我们这里有蜀锦、宋锦、缎子、罗、绫、纱等。蜀锦以色彩艳丽、图案精美著称；宋锦则质地细腻，光泽柔和；缎子光滑柔软，适合做高档衣装；罗则轻盈透气，适合夏天穿着；绫和纱则各有特色，既可做衣料，也可做装饰。”
[EN] Business buzzed across the sprawling sales floor packed with lustrous bolts. Scanning identical clerk tunics, she spotted Qinggua's rounder frame first—beaming and rattling off pitches like a pro! Next she scanned for tall clerks and caught Haoyu handing a wrapped box to a beaming female shopper.
[ZH] 姑娘还没有要买的意思，陈浩宇继续耐心介绍：“丝绸的质地主要取决于原料和工艺。比如，缎子是用桑蚕丝织成的，质地柔软，光泽好；而罗则是用麻、蚕丝织成的，轻盈透气。此外，我们还根据顾客的需求，采用不同的工艺，如缂丝、刺绣等，使丝绸更具特色。”
[EN] "Here's your silk, Miss. Please come again!"
[ZH] 对方依旧没有要购买的意思，难道今日要失败？陈浩宇打算使出最后一招：“姑娘高贵典雅，浑身上下透着书卷之气，若是配上这淡青色的绫，再绣上几株兰花，那定是清新脱俗，宛如仙子下凡。或者这月白色的罗，做一件里衣，贴身穿戴，肌肤的触感就如同春风拂面，轻柔至极。”
[EN] "You really know your fabrics—I'll ask for you next time!"
[ZH] 千里寻觉得自己被说动，转向陈浩宇：“那便依你所言，选淡青色的绫。”
[EN] "My honor, Miss!"
[ZH] 这声音不正是娘子的声音吗？陈浩宇激动的同时，又不敢相信，直到千里寻将丝帕取下，娘子的容颜出现在他面前，陈浩宇竟然忍不住抱住她。
[EN] Seeing her to the door, Haoyu spotted a veiled young woman watching him—her silhouette screaming his wife! Wait, couldn't be; she was running the congee shed.
[ZH] “娘子！真的是你！没想到你跑来看我！我……我……我高兴得都不知道该说什么！”
[EN] Qinggua stepped up too, eager to grab the shopper. "Looking for silk, Miss? Let me show you—"
[ZH] 众目睽睽之下被他抱住，千里寻虽不习惯，又不好拒绝，怎么说也需给他留些面子。小声在他耳边说：“大众场合，你这样抱住我不太好，不如今日请假陪我去逛街。”
[EN] "Shoo! My customer!" Haoyu shouldered Qinggua aside.
[ZH] “娘子有任何需求我无不满足。”陈浩宇放开娘子，“我这就找秋管事请假，娘子在此稍候片刻。”
[EN] "Careful or I'll tell Young Madam you're ogling pretty strangers!" Qinggua hissed, ducking off to another buyer before Haoyu could kick him.
[ZH] 他转过身，见所有伙计和客人瞧向这边，于是笑嘻嘻地向大家介绍：“我娘子！她来绸缎庄看我，嘿嘿嘿，大家继续，继续，嘿嘿嘿……”
[EN] *Filthy flirt!* Qianli Xun fumed behind her veil. *One pretty silhouette and his eyes bug out!*
[ZH] 青瓜心想：“公子一脸幸福的样子真令人羡慕，什么时候丑丫也能来绸缎庄瞧瞧我。”想归想，还得上前问候少夫人。
[EN] Then Haoyu spoke with warm earnestness. "Forgive me for staring, Miss—your figure reminds me so much of my wife."
[ZH] 其他伙计也都一一上前问候完少夫人，继续招待客人。
[EN] *Fine—reprieve granted!* Saying nothing, she wandered deeper into the racks to see how he handled a silent customer.
[ZH] 秋管事再严厉，也不能阻止人家小两口约会，要不大老爷会批评他。再说二公子的业务进展飞速，不在乎少学一两日。因此满口答应，还嘱咐二公子用心陪少夫人，其他不用放在心上。
[EN] Haoyu fell smoothly into stride. "You carry yourself like a true connoisseur, Miss. What weave are you looking for? From imperial-grade silks to everyday cloth, we stock the finest—which catches your eye?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇换过衣裳，拉起娘子的手往外走。青瓜见状跟上去。
[EN] Still silence. Catching her gaze drifting indecisively across the bolts, Haoyu launched effortlessly into his pitch.
[ZH] 走出绸缎庄，陈浩宇感觉身后跟着人，转过头，一脸不满：“别跟着，回去继续当伙计。”
[EN] "Trying to pick the right weave? Look at these four staples: *ling* twill has a fine, liquid sheen that ripples with light when you walk; *luo* gauze is sheer, breathable, and silky—unbeatable for summer; *chou* silk is thick and softly lustrous, ideal for stately skirts; and *duan* satin gleams like a mirror in bold hues, guaranteed to turn heads as a cloak!"
[ZH] 青瓜很委屈，眼睁睁瞧着公子牵着少夫人的手越走越远，自语道：“公子有了少夫人，都不让我跟着，下次我也约丑丫逛街，把公子撇在一边。”
[EN] *Not bad—he actually studied!*
[ZH] 埋怨归埋怨，他毕竟是下人，还得听主人吩咐，继续回去当伙计。想到当伙计有银子赚，青瓜立刻又高兴起来，打算拿到银子后给丑丫买件礼物。
[EN] Keeping mum, she walked further while he rattled on without missing a beat. "We carry Sichuan brocade, Song brocade, satin, gauze, twill, and sheer silk. Sichuan brocade dazzles with bold patterns; Song brocade glows with refined subtlety... Satin uses pure mulberry silk for softness and gloss; gauze blends hemp and silk for airy weightlessness—plus we offer *kesi* tapestry weave and custom embroidery."
[ZH] 手被陈浩宇牵着，千里寻并未拒绝。一来在外面要给夫君面子，当众拒绝，他脸上会挂不住；二来，既打算与他进一步发展，牵手是必然之事，何况昨晚他已亲过她，牵手对她来讲已不算什么。
[EN] Still no bite! Pulling out his closer, Haoyu smiled warmly. "With your refined, scholarly air, Miss, this pale celadon *ling* twill embroidered with a few sprays of orchids would look downright celestial—or this moon-white gauze for an inner robe, soft as a spring breeze against the skin."
[ZH] 只是感到惋惜，为何牵着他的手没有怦然心动的感觉？
[EN] Actually sold, Qianli Xun turned to him. "Then I'll take the pale celadon twill."
[ZH] 常言道十指连心，难道是因为她一直委屈自己的感情迎合不得不接受的婚姻？看来是这样，她还未从心底真正爱上陈浩宇。
[EN] That voice! Haoyu froze in stunned disbelief until she pulled down her silk veil—and without thinking twice, he swept her straight into a bear hug!
[ZH] 可为何昨晚他亲她时，她会有感觉？人们常说口腹之欲，难道是女性荷尔蒙作祟？千里寻越想脸越红，还未真正爱上陈浩宇，却对他产生感觉，实在不雅。
[EN] "Wife! It really *is* you! You came all this way to see me—I don't even know what to say!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇并未察觉娘子的变化，沉浸在牵着她的手逛街的满足感中，边走边说：“我还是第一次陪女子逛街，娘子累了告诉我，你要是不想坐马车，我背你。”
[EN] Flustered in front of a crowded shop yet unwilling to embarrass him, she whispered in his ear, "Public place, buddy—let go. Ask for the rest of the day off and walk around town with me."
[ZH] 千里寻安慰自己，他是她的夫君，对他产生情感以外的感觉应该是正常的，她的脸色逐渐恢复正常。
[EN] "Your wish is my command!" Letting go, he grinned sheepishly at the staring clerks and shoppers. "My wife! Dropped by to check on me—hehe, carry on, everybody!"
[ZH] “我的确不想坐马车，可也轮不上你背我，试想想大街上你背着我多难看。”
[EN] Qinggua hurried up to bow while secretly wishing Chouya would come visit *him* at work too, and the other clerks chimed in greetings. Strict as Steward Qiu was, no steward dared block a young master's date with his wife—especially when Haoyu was flying through training—and happily waved them off.
[ZH] “我不觉得，背自己的娘子幸福着呢，别人羡慕还来不及，怎么会难看？”
[EN] Changing robes, Haoyu clasped her hand and strode out—spinning around to scowl at Qinggua tagging behind. "Go back inside and work!"
[ZH] “你背不动我。”
[EN] Left pouting as the couple walked away hand in hand, Qinggua muttered, "Gets his wife and ditches me! Next time I'm taking Chouya shopping and ditching *him*!" Still, earning real clerk's wages cheered him right back up—he'd buy Chouya a nice gift.
[ZH] 陈浩宇停下，转向娘子：“要不现在试试？你的拳头力气虽大，身体的重量肯定比不上我，背你小菜一碟。”
[EN] Qianli Xun let him hold her hand—partly to save his face outdoors, partly because after last night's kiss, holding hands was mild. Still, a quiet regret nagged her: why didn't his hand make her heart race? Was it because she was still forcing herself to settle? Wait—then why had his kiss stirred her last night?! *Must be plain hormones!* Heat crept up her cheeks at how improper that sounded.
[ZH] “才刚逛，我还不累。”千里寻只觉脸上又有温热的感觉，她竟然在陈浩宇面前害羞起来。
[EN] Oblivious, Haoyu beamed down at her. "First time I've ever gone shopping with a girl! Tell me the second your feet get tired—if you hate carriages, I'll carry you on my back."
[ZH] 娘子不再粗鲁，脸还变得粉红粉红，像春天盛开的桃花，难道她真的爱上自己？
[EN] Steadying her blush, she replied, "Don't want a carriage, and definitely not riding piggyback down Main Street—how ridiculous would that look!"
[ZH] “你对我说话越来越温柔，我现在感觉自己是世上最幸福的人。有了娘子后我突然觉得以前的日子都是白过，以后我会堂堂正正做人，谁让我娶了一位好娘子。”
[EN] "Who cares? Carrying my own wife—folks will just turn green with envy!"
[ZH] 千里寻不知该如何回应，曾经的她哪会想到有一日陈浩宇会站在街上对她说情话。街上人多，还是不要让他继续盯着自己说些让人面红耳赤的话。
[EN] "You couldn't even lift me."
[ZH] “我们还是继续逛吧？”
[EN] Stopping dead, he turned. "Try me! Wicked fists don't make you heavy—carrying you's a breeze!"
[ZH] “逛街必须买东西，娘子想买什么尽管告诉我。”
[EN] "We just started walking—I'm not tired yet." Another warm flush crept up her cheeks. *Wait—am I actually blushing in front of Chen Haoyu?!*
[ZH] “谁说逛街必须买东西？我什么都不想买，只想体验用双脚走路的感觉。”
[EN] Watching his fierce wife turn soft and rosy as spring peach blossoms sent Haoyu soaring into the clouds. "You're talking so gently to me now—I feel like the luckiest man alive. Ever since marrying you, my whole past feels wasted. From now on I'll walk straight and proud—all because I married a wife like you."
[ZH] “娘子想走多久，我陪你多久。”
[EN] Never in a million years had she pictured Chen Haoyu confessing sweet nothings in broad daylight. Too many eyes on the street—better keep moving before her ears caught fire. "Let's keep walking."
[ZH] 陈浩宇舍不得放开娘子的手，继续拉着她逛街。
[EN] "Shopping means buying—name whatever you want!"
[ZH] 千里寻从开始不习惯，逐渐变得用心享受起被牵着逛街的感觉。
[EN] "Says who? I don't want to buy a thing—just feel my own two feet on the street."
[ZH] 陈浩宇一直牵着她，没有丝毫放松的想法，仿佛担心她会从身边溜走似的。
[EN] "Walk as far as you like—I'm right beside you."
[ZH] 千里寻的脸上露出笑容，为她不再讨厌陈浩宇，心甘情愿跟着他逛街而高兴。只有这样她才会好，所有人都会好。
[EN] Refusing to loosen his grip for a second, as if she might slip away if he blinked, Haoyu walked on—and bit by bit, Qianli Xun's stiffness melted into genuine quiet pleasure. A genuine smile curved her lips. Walking willingly beside him like this felt right—good for her, and good for everyone.

#CH 271
[ZH] 陈浩宇牵着娘子的手，一刻也不愿松开，带着她看这瞧那，不亦乐乎。以前他总觉得逛街很无趣，现在却兴致勃勃。
[ZH] 千里寻的话语少了许多，也许是不知与陈浩宇逛街该说些什么。不过看到有趣的玩意儿，她还是会开心地笑出声来。
[ZH] 她开始想陈浩宇不要再牵着她，一个动作时间久了多少会不舒服，可陈浩宇没有松开的意思。她不愿扫对方的兴致，也就忍了。
[ZH] 逛起街来时间过得快，不知不觉已是午时，也到饭点儿，千里寻觉出腹中饥饿：“我饿了，找个地方吃点东西。”
[ZH] 她实在想把手抽出来，陈浩宇掌心中的汗搞得她的手又湿又热。吃起东西来，他必然会把手松开，千里寻对她的建议甚觉满意。
[ZH] 陈浩宇欣然答应：“此处离聚香楼最近，我带你去那里用饭。”
[ZH] 听到“聚香楼”三个字，千里寻回想起陈浩宇有一次口误将“聚香楼”说成“春香楼”。牵她手的人以前常去青楼，不知做过多少令人恶心之事，她不由自主使劲将手抽出，独自向聚香楼的方向走去。
[ZH] 陈浩宇纳闷，紧追上去，边走边说：“你怎么了？难道不愿去聚香楼？若不想去，我们可以去别的地儿。”
[ZH] 千里寻没好气地说：“谁说我不想去？没瞧见我正往聚香楼的方向走？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇既紧张又疑惑：“那你为何生气？”
[ZH] 千里寻站住，盯着陈浩宇：“老娘讨厌那种不小心将聚香楼说成春香楼的人！讨厌以前的你！”
[ZH] 陈浩宇先是一愣，接着高兴地抱住娘子：“我很开心你开始介意，说明你心里有我。”
[ZH] 千里寻没有立刻推开陈浩宇，不是因为她喜欢被对方抱着，而是暂时被陈浩宇的话影响，认真研究自己。
[ZH] 以前陈浩宇去青楼整日不着家，她毫无感觉，甚至希望他永远不着家才好。现在竟然介意起来？看来她的的确确开始在意他。
[ZH] 这份在意搞得千里寻不好再继续生气：“好啦好啦，街上这么多人，别再抱着我，快些去聚香楼，我的肚子快饿扁了。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇放开娘子，想继续牵她的手，结果娘子将手收到一边。
[ZH] “怎么？不愿让我牵着你？”
[ZH] “不是，你的手心都是汗，很不舒服。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇这才意识到他只顾自己开心，没有顾及娘子的感受：“好吧，你既然不舒服，手我可以不牵，但你必须跟我走近些，要不然别人怎么知道你是我的娘子？”
[ZH] 千里寻点点头：“好吧。”
[ZH] 两人肩并肩向聚香楼的方向走去，要不是因为靠得太近走起路来不方便，陈浩宇一定会将手臂紧挨着娘子。
[ZH] 并肩走的过程中，一旦发现两人之间的空隙变大，他一定会特意将空隙变小，小到不能再小。
[ZH] 不知不觉来到聚香楼，两人正要走进，仪王迎面从聚香楼出来，他的身后跟着白风、韩谷、温信，还有四五名差役押着两名伙计，其中一名身上明显有伤，还好不严重。
[ZH] 原来仪王正在司理院阅览卷宗，聚香楼有人前来报案，两名伙计因同时喜欢一女子打起来，甚至动了刀子，其中一位手臂被砍伤。
[ZH] 申简辰带人前来受理案子，询问完情况后，收了凶器，准备带两名伙计回司理院继续审问，一踏出聚香楼千里寻出现在眼前。
[ZH] 陈浩宇站在她身边，离她很近，申简辰还是习惯将目光投放在她的身上。
[ZH] 千里寻感受到申简辰的目光，紧张之余下意识抓起陈浩宇的手，这一刻，她忘记眼前的人是仪王，需要拜见。
[ZH] 娘子抓他的手，陈浩宇求之不得，当着仪王的面将娘子的手抓得更紧，带着她一起上前拜见：“在此遇到仪王殿下真是有缘，相请不如偶遇，我与娘子来此用饭，不知殿下可愿与草民一同用饭？”
[ZH] 申简辰将目光由千里寻挪到他们牵在一起的手，心中有说不出的滋味，他掩饰好情绪：“本王还有公务在身，不便在聚香楼耽搁时间，祝陈公子与夫人用餐愉快。”
[ZH] 申简辰说完，走到一边，让出路来。王爷如此，跟着的人也都闪到一边。
[ZH] 陈浩宇继续牵着娘子行礼：“殿下既有正事要忙，草民和娘子不便多打扰，我们先进去了。”
[ZH] 千里寻虽不说话，也跟着行礼。然后被陈浩宇牵着就要踏进聚香楼，一只脚刚要跨入，身后传来仪王的声音。
[ZH] “幽栖居士请留步！”
[ZH] 陈浩宇牵着娘子的手与她一起转过身，仪王独唤他的娘子，他不好主动回话，低眉瞧向她。
[ZH] 她垂下眼帘，恭敬地问道：“仪王殿下有何吩咐？”
[ZH] 充满距离感的语气对申简辰来讲还需慢慢适应，可他却伪装成早已适应的样子：“听说乡下的竹林又归到幽栖居士名下？”
[ZH] “是。”
[ZH] “幽栖居士既是竹林的主人，有一事还需征求你同意。”
[ZH] “仪王请说。”
[ZH] “竹林景色宜人，我甚是喜欢。上次去竹林办案，麻烦苦蕨帮我在那里找片景色好的地儿建造竹屋。占用幽栖居士的地盘，还望你不要见怪。”
[EN] Haoyu refused to let go of her hand, pulling her from stall to stall with boyish glee. Qianli Xun stayed quieter than usual—still figuring out what to chat about on a date with him—yet laughed aloud whenever some quirky trinket caught her eye. By noon, her fingers were getting damp and cramped in his sweaty grip, though she endured it rather than kill his mood until her stomach saved her.
[ZH] 千里寻本想在竹林为自己建竹屋，竟然被仪王抢先，他既想建，她还是不建为妙。
[EN] "I'm starving. Let's grab lunch." Eating meant he *had* to let go!
[ZH] “贵人能看上竹林是竹林百姓之福，也是淑真之福，怎敢见怪？仪王若需要帮忙尽管吩咐。”
[EN] "Juxiang Tower's right around the corner—let's eat there!"
[ZH] “幽栖居士不见怪我便放心，等竹屋建好后，还请陈公子与幽栖居士赏光去坐坐。”
[EN] The syllable *Juxiang* instantly recalled the day he'd slipped and called it *Chunxiang Brothel*. All his gross brothel past flashed back, and she yanked her hand free, marching ahead alone.
[ZH] 陈浩宇赶紧说：“仪王邀请怎敢不去？等建好后，我与娘子必然前去叨扰。”
[EN] Haoyu jogged right after her. "Hey, what's wrong? Don't like Juxiang Tower? We can pick somewhere else!"
[ZH] 申简辰这才带着人满意地离开。
[EN] "Who said I don't want Juxiang Tower? Aren't I walking straight toward it?"
[ZH] 目送仪王走后，陈浩宇终于可以牵着娘子毫无障碍走进聚香楼。
[EN] "Then why are you mad?"
[ZH] 司理院已将闹事的伙计带走，聚香楼很快恢复正常。正值饭点儿，一楼的顾客真不少。
[EN] Stopping dead, she glared at him. "I hate guys who accidentally call Juxiang Tower *Chunxiang Brothel*! I hate the old you!"
[ZH] 千里寻小心将手抽出：“一会儿要用饭，还是不牵着好。”
[EN] Haoyu blinked—then beamed and pulled her into a hug. "I'm thrilled you actually care! Means I'm finally in your heart!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇并不介意：“娘子总是考虑周到。我的包间在二楼，我带娘子上去。”
[EN] Qianli Xun didn't shove him off right away, startled by her own reaction. Back when he camped out at brothels all week, she couldn't have cared less—now a passing memory actually stung! That meant she *was* starting to care about him. Hard to stay furious after realizing that. "All right, all right, people are staring—let go! My stomach's growling!"
[ZH] 两人向二楼走去，一名伙计过来拦道：“陈公子慢着！”
[EN] When he reached for her hand again, she tucked it behind her back. "Your palm's drenched in sweat—gross."
[ZH] 陈浩宇停下脚步，转向伙计：“本公子今日带娘子前来品尝聚香楼的美食，还不快上二楼把本公子的包间打开。”
[EN] "Fair enough—no hand-holding, just walk right next to me so everybody knows you're my wife!"
[ZH] 伙计赔着笑脸说：“陈公子有所不知，包间已到期，您自从成亲后好久没来，银子续不上，您常用的包间已被他人给包了。”
[EN] "Fine."
[ZH] 陈浩宇本想骂人，娘子在跟前，还是忍了：“本公子没来，那是有正事耽搁，现在不是来了吗？去给那人说说，把包间给我让出来，银子少不了你们的。”
[EN] Shoulder to shoulder—with Haoyu edging back in whenever an inch of air opened between them—they reached Juxiang Tower's entrance just as Prince Yi walked straight out, followed by Bai Feng, Han Gu, Wen Xin, and constables escorting two brawling waiters, one nursing a knife gash on his arm from fighting over a girl.
[ZH] 伙计为难道：“没法让，那人是蒋爷，狠着呢，我们得罪不起。”
[EN] The instant Shen Jianchen stepped through the doors, his gaze instinctively snapped straight onto Qianli Xun standing tight against Haoyu.
[ZH] 陈浩宇生气道：“你们怕得罪他？难道就不怕得罪我？”
[EN] Feeling his eyes on her, Qianli Xun tensed and instinctively seized Haoyu's hand—forgetting for half a second even to curtsy.
[ZH] 伙计赶紧解释：“怕，当然怕，你们这些爷聚香楼都得罪不起，只是那包间只有一个，我们掌柜的也很为难。”
[EN] Over the moon, Haoyu locked his fingers tight around hers and stepped forward bowing. "Fancy meeting Your Highness here! My wife and I came for lunch—would you honor us by joining our table?"
[ZH] 见伙计为难，千里寻劝道：“还是算了，在哪里吃饭都一样，我没那么多讲究。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen's gaze dropped from her face to their joined hands. Swallowing the sharp ache, he kept his voice smooth. "Official business awaits—I won't hold you up. Enjoy your meal, Young Master Chen, Madam." Stepping aside to clear the entrance, he led his staff to yield the way.
[ZH] “听娘子的。”娘子发话，陈浩宇的情绪立刻变好，对伙计说，“还有没有其他包间？给本公子重新换一个。”
[EN] Haoyu bowed with her hand still clasped, Qianli Xun dipping a silent curtsy—yet as her foot crossed the threshold, Prince Yi spoke behind them.
[ZH] 伙计更加为难：“楼上的包间一间不剩，都已有主人。”
[EN] "A moment, Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 陈浩宇气得伸出拳头，结果被娘子挡住：“楼下挺好，没必要为这种小事生气。”
[EN] Turning together, Haoyu stayed silent while Qianli Xun kept her eyes lowered. "Yes, Your Highness?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇立刻满脸堆笑：“娘子说得对，我不计较。”
[EN] Masking how much that icy formality stung, Shen Jianchen said evenly, "I hear the bamboo grove outside town is back under your name?"
[ZH] 娘子的手就在眼前，此时不抓更待何时？
[EN] "Yes."
[ZH] 他继续抓起娘子的手：“那边有地方，我们去那边坐。”
[EN] "I'm quite fond of that grove. Last time a case took me out there, I asked Kujue to pick a scenic spot and build me a bamboo cottage. Hope you don't mind my borrowing a corner of your land."
[ZH] 千里寻没有拒绝，待会儿他必然放开，何必在乎一时半刻。
[EN] She'd once thought of building a cottage there herself—better drop that idea now. "That Your Highness favors the grove honors both the villagers and me. Need anything, just say the word."
[ZH] 两人坐到桌前面对面，陈浩宇还没有想要放开的意思，反而将另一只手伸过来，一起握住她的手。
[EN] "Good. Once it's finished, you and Young Master Chen must drop by as my guests."
[ZH] “有娘子在身边，我的脾气都变好，娘子对我来讲实在太重要。”
[EN] "We wouldn't miss it, Your Highness!" Haoyu chimed in briskly. Satisfied, Shen Jianchen marched off with his prisoners.
[ZH] 千里寻不再去管手被握着的事，终于问出心中疑惑的问题：“陈浩宇，你为什么会喜欢我？我离过婚，比你大三岁，以前还经常揍你，难道你有受虐倾向？”
[EN] Inside, the bustling ground floor had already bounced back from the waiters' brawl. Qianli Xun slipped her hand free for lunch while Haoyu led her toward the stairs—"My private booth's on the second floor"—only for a waiter to step in apologetically.
[ZH] “什么受虐倾向？娘子说得也太难听，我就是喜欢你，没有任何道理可言。我觉得自己实在幸运，娶的娘子竟然我是喜欢的人，嘿嘿嘿……！”
[EN] "Forgive me, Young Master Chen—since you haven't dropped by since your wedding, the lease lapsed and somebody else booked your booth."
[ZH] 陈浩宇笑得投入，千里寻十分羡慕，这家伙的确幸运。她呢？是幸运还是不幸？也许时间可以慢慢说明一切。
[EN] Keeping his temper checked in front of his wife, Haoyu snapped, "Got tied up! Go tell whoever's in there to clear out—silver's no issue."
[ZH] 刚才的伙计第一次见不一样的陈家二公子，心想，男人娶了娘子果真会变。他站着欣赏一会儿，见陈公子抓着娘子的手傻乐，便走上前：“二位吃些什么？”
[EN] "Can't touch him—it's Master Jiang! Mean customer, we can't afford to cross him."
[ZH] 陈浩宇不得不放开娘子的手，脸上依然挂满笑容：“我听娘子的，嘿嘿嘿……”
[EN] "Scared of crossing him and not scared of crossing *me*?!"
[ZH] 伙计又问：“少夫人想吃些什么？”
[EN] "We daren't cross either of you—there's just only one booth, and all the others upstairs are full too!"
[ZH] 千里寻忽然想让自己与陈浩宇在一起有甜蜜的感觉，怎么说两人也算是正式约会。
[EN] Seeing Haoyu's fist cock back, Qianli Xun caught his wrist. "Downstairs is fine—don't blow up over a table."
[ZH] 她想起去年与文渊约会时的情景，尽管文渊已无法占据她的内心，可与他约会时甜蜜的感觉依然让她回味无穷，不知以后还能否与他人再次体验那种感觉？
[EN] Instantly beaming, Haoyu seized her hand again. "Wife's right—I won't sweat it! Come on, open table over there."
[ZH] 现在她很想与陈浩宇体验体验，不如先从食物做起。
[EN] Sitting face to face, he clasped her hand in *both* of his. "Having you beside me even fixed my temper—you're everything to me."
[ZH] 记得当时文渊带来三种食物，千里寻一边想一边说出：“桂花糯米藕、葱花肉、刺毛肉圆。”
[EN] Ignoring his grip, Qianli Xun finally voiced the puzzle chewing at her. "Chen Haoyu, why on earth do you like me? I'm divorced, three years older than you, and used to beat you black and blue—are you a masochist?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇脸上挂着笑容：“看来娘子真是饿了，专点些容易饱腹的吃食，我再添几道，才不枉带娘子来趟聚香楼。再来道西湖醋鱼，一盘杭菊鸡丝和两盘清爽的素菜，我常点的，你们都知道。”
[EN] "Masochist? What an ugly word! I just love you—no rhyme or reason! I'm the luckiest guy alive marrying the very woman I fell for—hehehe!"
[ZH] 伙计说：“好的！二位稍候，菜很快上齐！”
[EN] Envy touched her—he really *was* lucky. What about her? Time would tell.
[ZH] 等待上菜期间，陈浩宇用手撑着下巴，呆呆地看着娘子：“真好看。”
[EN] Watching the notorious playboy grin like a love-struck pup, the waiter stepped over marveling at what marriage did to a man. "What'll you two have?"
[ZH] 千里寻“哼”了一声：“我要是长得丑，估计你也不会喜欢我。”
[EN] "Whatever my wife wants!" Haoyu beamed, releasing her hand at last.
[ZH] 陈浩宇毫无避讳：“那是当然。”
[EN] Wanting a taste of real romance—remembering how sweet her first picnic with Wenyuan had felt—Qianli Xun ordered the exact three dishes from that memory: "Osmanthus glutinous rice lotus root, minced pork with scallions, and fuzzy meatballs."
[ZH] “所以说，你就是好色之徒！”
[EN] "Starving, huh? Going straight for the hearty plates!" Haoyu grinned. "Add West Lake vinegar fish, shredded chicken with Hangzhou chrysanthemum, and two crisp greens—my usuals."
[ZH] 陈浩宇不再撑着下巴，强调道：“我不是！长得漂亮的姑娘多了，我不能个个都喜欢，我只喜欢娘子这样的。”
[EN] While waiting, Haoyu propped his chin in his hands staring at her. "So pretty."
[ZH] “你喜欢的还少吗？”
[EN] "Humph! Bet if I were ugly you wouldn't look twice."
[ZH] 娘子的反问搞得陈浩宇十分自责：“我错了还不行吗？再说以前我也没真正喜欢过谁，都是瞎玩儿，你是我第一个动真情之人。”
[EN] "Well, yeah."
[ZH] “瞎玩儿？还有脸说？哼！”
[EN] "Knew it—total shallow flirt!"
[ZH] “娘子别揪着我以前的事儿不放，好不好？总得允许我改邪归正，我保证以后再也不瞎玩儿。”
[EN] Sitting straight, he protested, "Not true! Plenty of pretty girls out there, yet I only want *you*."
[ZH] 不知为什么，越是想对陈浩宇动感情，越计较以前的他。几位伙计端着菜肴过来，千里寻不好继续计较。
[EN] "Oh, like you didn't chase plenty before!"
[ZH] 待所有菜上齐后，千里寻瞧着那盘刺毛肉圆，冷不丁问：“陈浩宇，你可愿意吃我剩下的食物？”
[EN] Guilt flashed across his face. "My fault, okay? Never actually loved any of them—just messing around. You're the first woman I've ever felt real love for."
[ZH] 陈浩宇先是一愣，很快回道：“当然！”
[EN] "Just messing around—and you say it proud!"
[ZH] 千里寻嘴角露出满意的微笑，她夹起一颗肉圆，咬去一半，然后将剩下的一半伸到陈浩宇嘴边，陈浩宇毫不犹豫吃进口中，津津有味地嚼着。
[EN] "Quit digging up my old dirt, Wife? Give a reformed guy a break—I swear I'm done fooling around forever."
[ZH] 这一刻千里寻似乎找到恋爱的感觉，继续说：“我虽在你眼中长得不错，可我有时也懒得梳洗打扮，你会不会介意？”
[EN] Oddly enough, the more she tried opening her heart to him, the sharper his past pricked her. When the waiters set the dishes down, Qianli Xun picked up a fuzzy meatball, bit off half, and suddenly asked, "Chen Haoyu, would you eat my leftovers?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇摇摇头：“怎么会？你怎样我都不介意。”
[EN] "Of course!" Without a second's hesitation, he leaned over and munched down the other half straight from her chopsticks.
[ZH] “我也会打嗝、磨牙、放臭屁，早晨醒来眼角都是眼屎，你会不会介意？”
[EN] A real flutter of date-night sweetness warmed her chest. "I might look decent now, yet half the time I can't be bothered washing up or fixing my hair—mind that?"
[ZH] “绝对不会，因为我也这样，娘子太完美，我还担心自己配不上你，你有这些缺点我们才算相配。”
[EN] "Never. Love you however you look."
[ZH] 千里寻又露出满意的笑容：“若你的朋友邀请你参加宴会，要求你必须带上我，你会不会在意我的装扮？”
[EN] "I burp, grind my teeth, pass gas, and wake up with crust in my eyes—still don't mind?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇想想说：“我在宴会中多次见到娘子，每次都打扮得体，相信你与我出去也会如此，我为何要特别在意？”
[EN] "Not a bit—I do all that too! You're so spotless I worry I'm out of my league; flaws make us a matched pair."
[ZH] “如若我不特意打扮呢？”
[EN] "And if your friends insist you bring me to a banquet, would you fuss over how I dress?"
[ZH] “娘子即使不打扮也艳压群芳。”
[EN] "You're always sharp at banquets—why worry? Even plain-faced without a hairpin you'd outshine every woman in the room."
[ZH] 千里寻终于“噗嗤”笑出声来：“算你过关，还不赶紧吃，热菜变凉会不好吃。”
[EN] A bright laugh broke out of her. "Fine—you pass! Eat up before it gets cold."
[ZH] 陈浩宇这才拿起筷子，夹了一筷子西湖醋鱼放在娘子的碗中，又将其他菜放了一些，他才大口嚼起来。
[EN] Grinning, Haoyu loaded West Lake vinegar fish and side dishes into her bowl before digging in himself.

#CH 272
[ZH] 两人用完饭，又逛一会儿消消食，逛得差不多时，终于逛累，才租辆马车回陈府。
[ZH] 今日陪娘子逛，没有时间去永泰茶楼跟女茶师学习做茶，就当是体验生活。
[ZH] 女茶师说过，做茶的方法基本传授给他，还需多体验生活，将生活的体验融入茶技中，做出的茶会更加高级。
[ZH] 陈浩宇不大懂，不过以此为理由不去找女茶师倒也冠冕堂皇。
[ZH] 马车到达陈府南面离门口不远处时，一群丫鬟装扮的女子排成一排站在大门外。
[ZH] 陈浩宇从马车里探出脑袋一瞧，全是他以前从乡下抢来的。心中纳闷，不是把她们安置在陈家的其他宅子里吗？怎么一个个都跑到这里来？
[ZH] 因担心娘子生气，陈浩宇匆忙间找出理由：“娘子，我突然想起来还要到永泰茶楼跟女茶师学习做茶，要不你陪我一起去？”
[ZH] 千里寻敏锐地觉察出不对劲，也向马车外瞧，虽不知发生了什么，大略已猜到与陈浩宇有关。
[ZH] “今日逛得累，不必再去学习做茶。不如与我下马车，看看外面到底发生了什么？”
[ZH] 已无法避免，陈浩宇只能答应：“娘子别生气，我这就跟你一起下去。”
[ZH] “看来那些人与你有关？”
[ZH] 从陈浩宇的表情能判断出，外面的人是来找他。
[ZH] 千里寻想看看到底发生了什么，她起身走下马车。
[ZH] 陈浩宇担心不已，没敢尾随娘子，继续躲在马车里。
[ZH] 千里寻没有急着上前打扰，她想站在原地观察一会儿再做打算。
[ZH] 那些丫鬟一个个长得不敢说花容月貌，也颇具姿色。
[ZH] 千里寻朝马车里小声批判：“好色之徒！”
[ZH] 陈浩宇小声回道：“娘子批评得是。”
[ZH] 王管家带着几名下人从陈府走出来，因着急应对丫鬟们，并未注意到不远处的马车和少夫人。
[ZH] 他来到丫鬟们面前劝道：“大家回去吧，二公子不在府中，即使在也不会见你们，能在陈府当丫鬟已经不错，难道还想登堂入室不成？赶紧回其他宅子去，该干啥干啥。”
[ZH] 丫鬟们纷纷说：
[ZH] “今日不见到陈公子我们绝不走！”
[ZH] “他把我们抢来不能就这么算了！”
[ZH] “就是！不能就这么算了！”
[ZH] 一个丫鬟哭着从队伍中走出来：“呜呜，王管家有所不知，二公子把我们抢来，已将我们……，呜呜，他只顾自己娶娘子，我们往后可怎么办？呜呜……”
[ZH] 许多丫鬟跟着哭起来，陈府外立刻一片哭声。
[ZH] 王管家与几个下人左哄右哄不见成效，干脆站在一旁干瞪眼。
[ZH] 刚对陈浩宇产生的好感立刻去大半，千里寻很想将陈浩宇从马车里揪出来让丫鬟们一个个将他狠狠揍一顿。
[ZH] 毕竟已开始承认作为陈家二少夫人的身份，千里寻警告过自己，往后做事不能太鲁莽。
[ZH] 她向马车里说：“陈浩宇，你要是承认自己是男人就下来，当着我的面将此事解决。”
[ZH] 娘子的话，陈浩宇必须听。何况他要不出面处理就不是男人，以后岂不是要在娘子面前矮半截？
[ZH] 他从车里下来，掏出碎银，给了车夫：“赶紧走，别在一旁看热闹。”
[ZH] 车夫接过银子，已对热闹不感兴趣，把马车赶走。
[ZH] 众人听到赶马车的声音，才向这边瞧来。
[ZH] 还没等王管家上前行礼，众位哭闹的丫鬟一起涌上去，将陈浩宇围住。
[ZH] 作为正主的千里寻竟然被挤到一边。
[ZH] 她心里更不爽，倒要见识一下陈浩宇怎么处理眼前的麻烦事。哼！自己拉的屎，还是自己擦屁股得好！
[ZH] 众位丫鬟哭哭啼啼：
[ZH] “陈公子，你是不是不要我了？呜呜……”
[ZH] “陈公子，你不是说以后会把我放在身边做妾室吗？为何成亲了不兑现承诺？呜呜……”
[ZH] “陈公子，我把自己都给你了，你要是不要我，我就不想活了，呜呜……”
[ZH] “陈公子，你既把我抢来，就应该对我负责，否则你抢个什么劲儿啊？呜呜……”
[ZH] “陈公子，我不图别的，只想跟着你，你可千万不要不管我，呜呜……”
[ZH] “陈公子，听说二少夫人是只母老虎，你这么久没来找我，该不会是她把你管得太严，呜呜……”
[ZH] 陈浩宇抢来的丫鬟们你一言我一语，加上哭声，搞得陈府大门外甚是热闹。
[ZH] 陈浩宇人被围着，眼睛却看向娘子，担心自己的努力因此情此景而泡汤。娘子的脸色并不好看，却也没要走的意思，她应该是想看他如何解决麻烦。
[ZH] 如何解决？陈浩宇根本不知，除了后悔仍是后悔，总得说些什么。
[ZH] “别哭了！再哭我可谁都不理！”
[ZH] 这话果真管用，丫鬟们都不再大声哭泣，抹着眼泪小声抽泣。
[EN] After lunch they strolled a bit more to walk off the meal before hiring a carriage back to Chen Manor. Skipping today's tea lesson had a convenient excuse—the tea master had said real tea artistry required soaking up real life.
[ZH] “都站好了！”
[EN] Nearing Chen Manor's south gate, a whole row of maids stood lined up outside. Peeking out the window, Haoyu recognized every last face—girls he had snatched from the countryside back in his wild days! Wait—weren't they tucked away in the Chen family's side estates? Why were they camped at the front gate?!
[ZH] 丫鬟们还真听话，整整齐齐站成一排，继续抹眼泪。
[EN] Panic hit. "Wife! Just remembered I've got a tea lesson at Yongtai Teahouse—come along with me?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇指着丫鬟们：“你！你！你！还有你！向前一步！”
[EN] Sensing trouble instantly, Qianli Xun leaned out to look. "Too tired to watch you whisk tea. Come step down and see what's going on."
[ZH] 被指的四个丫鬟都向前迈出一步。
[EN] Cornered, Haoyu groaned, "Don't get mad, Wife..."
[ZH] 陈浩宇训斥道：“你们四个本公子一根汗毛未动，凑什么热闹？今日我就把卖身契还给你们，从哪里来回哪里去，别在这里缠着本公子。”
[EN] "So they're here for *you*." Stepping out of the carriage while Haoyu stayed huddled inside, she eyed the pretty row of maids and muttered back through the window, "Shameless skirt-chaser!"
[ZH] 四个丫鬟哭起来：“陈公子！求求你留下我们吧？我们已习惯在陈府当丫鬟，回乡下会不适应，呜呜……”
[EN] "Guilty as charged, Wife," Haoyu whimpered from inside.
[ZH] “别哭啦！”
[EN] Unaware of the carriage nearby, Butler Wang hurried out with servants trying to shoo the girls off. "Go back to the side estates! Second Young Master isn't home—and wouldn't see you if he were! Being maids in a rich house is good enough; don't dream of climbing higher!"
[ZH] 四个丫鬟立刻停止哭声。
[EN] "Not budging till we see Young Master Chen!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇接着训斥：“只要你们四个乖乖的，别瞎闹事，留你们继续当丫鬟没什么不可以，但需给我记好了，本公子不纳妾，别再有这种想法，听到没？”
[EN] "He snatched us—can't just walk away!"
[ZH] 四个丫鬟齐声说：“听到了。”
[EN] One maid stepped out bawling, "You don't understand, Butler Wang! Second Young Master snatched us and... *sob*... took our innocence! Now he marries a fine wife and leaves us ruined—what happens to us?!"
[ZH] “听到了还不滚！”
[EN] Half the row burst into wails, turning the south gate into a weeping chorus while Butler Wang stood helpless.
[ZH] 四个丫鬟迅速离开，不再参与闹事。
[EN] Half the warm feelings Qianli Xun had just built for Haoyu evaporated on the spot. Swallowing the urge to drag him out for them to beat senseless—she was Second Young Madam Chen now, no acting rash—she rapped on the carriage door. "Chen Haoyu! If you call yourself a man, get down here and handle this right in front of me!"
[ZH] 剩下的丫鬟听到陈浩宇不愿纳妾，伤心不已，可不敢哭出声来。
[EN] Refusing meant losing every shred of manhood before his wife. Climbing out, Haoyu tossed silver to the driver—"Beat it, show's over!"—sending the carriage rattling away.
[ZH] 陈浩宇走到跟前，一脸愧疚，拱手向剩下的丫鬟行礼：“几位，我陈浩宇当初不懂事，冒犯各位。可我已有娘子，又不打算纳妾，只能委屈大家。你们出个价，多少银子我都给，只要断了给我当妾室的想法即可。”
[EN] At the sound, the wailing crowd spun around and swarmed straight onto Haoyu, shoving the actual wife clean to the sidelines!
[ZH] 千里寻心中冷笑道：“哼！有钱就了不起？你以为女子的贞洁能用钱买回来？好色之徒！”
[EN] *Fine—let's see how you wipe your own mess!* Qianli Xun crossed her arms darkly.
[ZH] 几位丫鬟并不买账，都跪下哭道：“我等已失贞于陈公子，往后没有人愿意再要我们，要那么多银子有何用？还望陈公子把我们留在身边伺候，呜呜……”
[EN] "Young Master Chen, are you dumping us?! *Sob*..."
[ZH] 陈浩宇用几乎乞求的语气说：“你们还是要银子吧？银子多实惠。”
[EN] "You promised to make me a concubine—why break your word?!"
[ZH] 几位丫鬟坚持道：“我们不要银子，就要待在陈公子身边，呜呜……”
[EN] "I gave myself to you—if you abandon me I'll kill myself!"
[ZH] 这时过来一辆马车停在陈府外，陈浩东扶着族中的一位长辈从车上下来。
[EN] "Word is Second Young Madam's a fierce tigress keeping you locked down—is that why you vanished?!"
[ZH] 这位长辈与夏老太太年纪相仿，名唤陈陆，在陈家的产业中占有一定份额。陈家选择产业的继承人，他持有重要的一票，而且此人在其他族人中影响较大。
[EN] Pinned under the shrieking storm, Haoyu kept darting nervous glances at his wife's chilly face. Seeing she wasn't storming off—she wanted to see him handle it—he barked, "Shut it! Cry one more tear and I won't deal with any of you!"
[ZH] 陈浩东从钱塘帮办完事专程将陈陆接来，为的就是让他看到堂弟不堪的一面。
[EN] The wails shrank to sniffles. "Line up!" They snapped into a neat row.
[ZH] 果然不负陈浩东一番筹谋，陈陆被扶着来到陈浩宇面前：“浩宇啊！听说你刚好些，我还替你高兴，又惹下这等事，陈家是指望不上你。唉！家门不幸！以后要好好跟浩东学！”
[EN] Jabbing a finger down the line, he barked, "You, you, you, and you—step forward!" Four girls stepped out. "I never laid a finger on any of you four—what are you jumping on the bandwagon for?! I'm handing back your deeds of sale today—go home to your villages and stop pestering me!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇赔着笑脸：“陆爷批评得对，您快进去，别让这事儿污了您的眼。”
[EN] All four burst into fresh tears. "Please don't kick us out, Young Master! We're used to serving in the Chen estates—we can't go back to the dirt!"
[ZH] 陈陆叹口气不再说话。
[EN] "Stop crying! Behave yourselves and you can stay on as maids—only get it through your heads I'm *never* taking concubines! Got it?!"
[ZH] 陈浩东装作关心的样子：“要是处理不了，交给大哥，不就是银子的事儿。”
[EN] "Yes, Young Master!" They scrambled off at once.
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“东哥，还真不是银子的事儿，你赶紧扶陆爷进去，我自己的事儿自己处理。”
[EN] Turning to the six remaining girls, Haoyu bowed with genuine shame. "I was a reckless fool back then and wronged you all. Yet I have a wife now and won't take concubines. Name your price—whatever silver you want, I'll pay, just give up being my concubines."
[ZH] 千里寻没见过陈陆，对方留着山羊胡，看上去年纪不小，陈浩宇还称呼他陆爷，应该是位有身份的长辈，作为陈家二少夫人，不能失了礼数，便上前拜见：“陆爷，晚辈这厢有礼。”
[EN] *Think silver buys back a girl's honor? Filthy flirt!* Qianli Xun scoffed silently.
[ZH] 陈陆将陈家二少夫人上下瞧一遍，没好气地说：“浩宇与你订亲我老大不乐意，可我做不了主。你既嫁给浩宇，应该尽做娘子的职责，他遇到此等事，你却站在一旁看热闹，成何体统？浩宇娶了你，更别想继承陈家产业！”
[EN] All six dropped to their knees weeping. "You already took our virtue—no decent man will marry us now! What good is silver? Just keep us beside you!"
[ZH] 陈浩东窃喜不已，扶着陈陆往府内走。
[EN] "Come on—silver's way more practical!" Haoyu practically begged.
[ZH] 千里寻最讨厌这种什么都不了解，胡乱批评人的长辈，见到必然要怼回去：“陆爷留步！”
[EN] "We don't want silver—we want to stay with you!"
[ZH] 陈陆停下来，转过身：“二少夫人有何指教？”
[EN] Right then a carriage halted nearby, and Chen Haodong stepped out assisting an elderly clansman with a goatee—Chen Lu, an elder of Old Madam Xia's generation who held a major share in the Chen enterprises and swung heavy clout over the heir vote. Haodong had fetched him straight from his errands specifically to catch Haoyu knee-deep in scandal.
[ZH] 千里寻走上前，继续行礼：“指教谈不上，陆爷刚才的话晚辈听着甚不顺耳，需辩解几句。”
[EN] "Haoyu!" Granduncle Lu tutted in disgust. "Just heard you were straightening up, and here's another disgrace! Hopeless—pure family misfortune! You ought to learn from Haodong!"
[ZH] “你这晚辈，不想着处理自己夫君惹下的丑事，还想跟长辈辩解？实在可笑，我可没工夫听。”
[EN] Haoyu forced an apologetic grin. "You're right, Granduncle Lu. Go on inside before this eyesore spoils your day."
[ZH] 陈浩东扶着陆爷继续向府内走去。
[EN] "Need me to settle this with silver, little brother?" Haodong offered smoothly.
[ZH] 千里寻大胆挑衅道：“陆爷刚说自己做不了主，看来你在陈家族人中并不重要，又何必倚老卖老？作威作福？一见面毫无道理批评晚辈？”
[EN] "Silver won't fix this one, Brother Dong—help Granduncle inside, I'll handle my own mess."
[ZH] 陈浩东更是开心，弟妹实在给力，竟然敢得罪手中握有重要一票的陆爷，设计逼浩宇娶她此时算是起到良好的效果。还需故意装作好意帮忙说话的样子：“陆爷，弟妹不懂事，您老别跟她计较。”
[EN] Mindful of manners toward an elder, Qianli Xun stepped forward and curtsied. "Greetings, Granduncle Lu."
[ZH] 陈陆被成功气到：“她既不懂事，我更要替陈家好好教育她。”
[EN] Granduncle Lu eyed her up and down with disdain. "I was dead set against Haoyu marrying you, only nobody asked my leave. Now that you're his wife, standing around gawking while he's knee-deep in scandal—what kind of wife are you? With you married to him, Haoyu can forget ever inheriting the Chen estates!"
[ZH] 陈陆彻底转过身：“我早听说你厉害，果然脾气不小，我倒要听听你如何替自己辩解。”
[EN] Gleaming inside, Haodong started steering the old man indoors—until Qianli Xun's sharp voice halted them.
[ZH] 陈浩宇担心娘子吃亏，满脸堆笑地解释道：“陆爷，您错怪我娘子，她是要管来着，我不让，自己惹的事儿怎能让娘子替我擦屁股？否则别人会笑话我不是男人。”
[EN] "Hold on, Granduncle Lu."
[ZH] “以为我不知？跟你爹一样天生怕老婆，一心维护自己的娘子。从现在起，你靠边站，不准说一句话，我只听她说。”陈陆指着那几个丫鬟，“老实跪着，不许哭出声来！”
[EN] "What do you want?" the elder snapped.
[ZH] 丫鬟们吓得更是忍住哭声。
[EN] "Just a word in my own defense—your remarks didn't sit right with me."
[ZH] 陈陆又指着王管家和几名下人：“你们几个好好站在这里，一个都不许进去替她找帮手，听到没？”
[EN] "Too lazy to clean up your husband's disgrace yet bold enough to talk back to elders? Absurd—I haven't got time for this!"
[ZH] 王管家很为难，不敢说一句话。
[EN] "Since Granduncle Lu admitted he can't call the shots anyway," Qianli Xun jabbed boldly, "looks like you don't carry much weight in the clan—so why strut around throwing your age at a junior you just met?"
[ZH] 陈陆这才面向二少夫人：“浩宇的娘子，该你说话了。”
[EN] Haodong nearly laughed out loud—his fiery sister-in-law insulting the swing-vote elder straight to his face! Putting on a righteous peacemaker act, he chimed, "Don't mind her, Granduncle—she's young and brash."
[ZH] 千里寻早已等不及，一肚子话给他准备着：“陆爷既不愿我嫁给陈浩宇，为何当初不出面阻止？您作为长辈应该知道父母之命媒妁之言，婚姻我也做不了主，嫁给陈浩宇不是我的错，现在却拿没有意义的话来批评我，实在毫无道理可言！”
[EN] Hooked straight into rage, Granduncle Lu whirled back. "Brash? Then I'll educate her on the Chen clan's behalf! Speak up—let's hear your 'defense'!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇心中难过，难道娘子还认为嫁给他是一种错误？他看看跪在地上的丫鬟，如此看来娘子嫁给他的确是错误的，乔文仙和仪王从来没做过此等事。
[EN] Haoyu jumped in grinning nervously. "You've got her wrong, Granduncle! She offered to handle this—I stopped her! What kind of man makes his wife wipe his own messes?"
[ZH] 陈陆辩解道：“我已说过，我做不了主，难道你白白长了一双耳朵？”
[EN] "Save it—henpecked just like your father! Step aside and shut your mouth! You six—kneel still and quit sniveling! Butler Wang, nobody goes inside for backup! Now talk, Haoyu's wife!"
[ZH] “依晚辈看，不尽然，以陆爷的年纪和说话的口气在陈家族人中地位应该不低，否则堂兄也不可能请您来，又是搀又是扶的。”
[EN] Locked and loaded, Qianli Xun stepped forward. "If Granduncle Lu hated my marrying Haoyu, why didn't you block it beforehand? You know marriage goes by parents' orders—I didn't choose it, so blaming me for marrying him makes zero sense!"
[ZH] 千里寻怼人的话中多少有些奉承的意味，说得陈陆面露自豪。
[EN] Haoyu winced—did she still view marrying him as a mistake? Looking at the six kneeling girls, maybe it *was*.
[ZH] “你这晚辈倒也有些眼色，即使我在族人中地位不低，可浩宇娶媳妇的事我也不好参与，才让你这么顺利嫁到陈家。浩宇本来就跟浩东没法比，你这一嫁更是令他与陈家的产业毫无瓜葛。”
[EN] "Already told you nobody asked me—are your ears decorative?!"
[ZH] 陈浩东刚慨叹过弟妹厉害，能从他又搀又扶看出陆爷的地位。陆爷的话又令他兴奋不已，弟妹最厉害的还是帮了他，呵呵……
[EN] "Hardly," she parried smoothly with a sliver of flattery. "Judging by your age and how carefully Cousin Haodong escorts you arm in arm, you clearly hold high rank in the clan."
[ZH] 陈浩宇忍不住又替娘子说话：“陆爷说的不对，我娘子无可挑剔，是我的问题，我不争气，还好陈家有东哥。”
[EN] Puffing up slightly, Granduncle Lu sniffed, "At least you have sharp eyes. High rank or not, I don't meddle in Eldest Brother's son's wedding—which is how you slipped in. Haoyu never held a candle to Haodong anyway, and marrying *you* sealed him clean out of the inheritance!"
[ZH] “无可挑剔？哼！她的问题大了！陈家产业继承人的条件里有一条规定，继承人娶的娘子必须出自清白人家，贤良淑德，名声要好。而你这位娘子不仅和离过，去年满城人议论她难道你不知？”
[EN] "Granduncle's wrong!" Haoyu burst out. "My wife is flawless—I'm the useless one, thank goodness we've got Brother Dong!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇此时更加悔恨，不该散播娘子的谣言，最终害人害己。还好他对陈家产业没有兴趣，害己已不重要，重要的是害得娘子被人诟病。显然陆爷并不知道他是散播谣言之人，不如当众承认，也省得他怪罪娘子。
[EN] "Flawless?! Ha! Clan law states the heir's wife must come from an unblemished house, virtuous and spotless in reputation! Yours is divorced—and last year half the city was gossiping about her!"
[ZH] “陆爷，去年的谣言是我让人胡乱散播的，我娘子根本不是那样的人。”
[EN] Remorse tore through Haoyu. Never mind losing an inheritance he didn't want—watching his wife smeared for *his* crime burned like fire! "Granduncle Lu, *I'm* the one who paid people to spread those rumors last year! My wife is completely innocent!"

#CH 273
[ZH] 谁知陈陆根本不相信：“浩宇，我知道你疼媳妇，也不至于什么事都往自己身上揽。去年的事不提也罢，就昨日，还有人在街上免费发放书卷，我的下人拿回来一卷，上面全是骂她的话。一个女人家做什么不好？偏偏没事写诗作词，矫揉造作，招惹是非，实在不雅！”
[ZH] 陈浩东明明知道谣言是浩宇散播，并不帮着说话，而是一副看热闹的样子。
[ZH] 陆爷提到书卷时，他猜测应是仪王妃派人做下的事，她连除掉朱淑真的心都有，什么事做不出来？陈浩东心中更喜，早已把那位萍水相逢的颜兄交代的话忘到九霄云外。
[ZH] 陈浩宇急了：“什么书卷？为何要骂我娘子？”
[ZH] 千里寻不愿大家关注书卷之事：“书卷之事不必理会，不过是别有用心之人诋毁我而已。”
[ZH] “不行！我不能让娘子的声誉再被人诋毁，现在就去报官！”
[ZH] 陈浩宇说着便打算去司理院。
[ZH] 传来姐姐陈文心的声音：“不必报官！此事已有人出面解决！”
[ZH] 原来朱晞颜已知书卷之事，让陈文心前来劝说女儿往后不要再写诗作词。陈文心刚到，正好瞧见眼前的一幕，在一旁看了一会儿，才开口说话。
[ZH] 陈浩宇见到姐姐甚是开心：“姐，你来得正好，快劝劝陆爷，别让他为难淑真。”
[ZH] 千里寻上前行礼：“让嫂嫂见笑。”
[ZH] 陈文心扶住淑真：“你啊，何必跟老人家计较？”
[ZH] 陈文心放开淑真，走到陈陆面前，行完礼说道：“陆爷，您别跟淑真计较，我扶您进去。”
[ZH] 陈陆并不给面子，嫁出去的姑娘泼出去的水，他根本不会当回事：“二少夫人眼中没我，我自然要跟她计较，你还是靠边站，不要管陈家的事。”
[ZH] 陈文心气不打一处来，想骂两句，被千里寻拦住。
[EN] Chen Lu refused to believe a word of it. “Haoyu, I know you dote on your wife, yet you needn’t take the blame for everything onto yourself. Never mind last year’s mess—just yesterday people were handing out free pamphlets on the street! My servant brought one home, and every page was packed with abuse aimed at her. Of all things a woman could do, why must she sit around writing poetry and ci lyrics, putting on pretentious airs and stirring up scandals? How indecent!”
[ZH] “嫂嫂，我来。”
[EN] Chen Haodong knew full well Haoyu had started the old rumors himself, yet he held his tongue and stood back to enjoy the show.
[ZH] 陈文心不再阻拦，不如让淑真替她好好教训一下陆爷。
[EN] When Granduncle Lu mentioned the pamphlets, Haodong guessed Princess Consort Yi’s people were behind it. A woman ruthless enough to want Zhu Shuzhen dead would stop at nothing. Delight bubbled inside him, and the warning from his passing acquaintance Brother Yan flew straight out of his mind.
[ZH] 千里寻上前说：“正好，我也想跟陆爷计较。那我就跟您掰扯掰扯。您说我影响陈浩宇继承陈家产业，真是荒唐至极！且不说陈浩宇并无此心，即使有，凭他以前的做派陈家长辈也瞧不上。那时朱陈两家还未订婚，此事与我何干？后来老太太与我公婆愿意定下这门亲事，足以说明他们是明理之人，知道外面关于我的传言子虚乌有，难道陆爷连老太太和我公婆也要怀疑？我嫁到陈家只想安安稳稳过日子，至于陈浩宇是否继承陈家产业，我并不在乎。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu bristled. “What pamphlets? Why are they slandering my wife?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇立即应和道：“我与娘子一样，都不在乎。”
[EN] Unwilling to draw any more eyes to the pamphlets, Qianli Xun cut in. “Ignore them. Just someone with an ax to grind trying to smear me.”
[ZH] 千里寻对陈浩宇露出满意的笑容：“既然我们都不在乎，又何来他娶了我更别想继承陈家产业的说法？还请陆爷收回此想法，千万不要再提，只怕您提多了，有人会以为陈浩宇觊觎陈家产业，心中不满。”
[EN] “No way! I won’t let anyone trash my wife’s reputation again—I’m going straight to report this to the authorities!” Turning on his heel, Chen Haoyu made to head for the Judicial Office.
[ZH] 陈浩东听出这是内涵他，很是不屑。不过弟妹刚才说的话他甚是舒心，最好别觊觎陈家产业，没什么好果子吃。又一想谁知她是不是口是心非？还是继续听陆爷如何教训她。
[EN] His sister Chen Wenxin’s voice rang out. “No need to report it! Someone has already stepped in and handled it!”
[ZH] 陈陆瞧一眼身旁的陈浩东，才说道：“你们两口子有自知之明再好不过，老太太都不嫌弃你，我又何必管江林房中的事？只是替浩宇惋惜啊。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan had caught wind of the pamphlets and sent Chen Wenxin over to warn his daughter to quit writing poetry. Arriving right in the middle of the standoff, she had watched from the sidelines for a moment before speaking up.
[ZH] 陈浩宇趁机说：“陆爷不必替我惋惜，娶到淑真我高兴着呢。”
[EN] Relief lit Chen Haoyu’s face. “Sister, thank goodness you’re here! Hurry and talk Granduncle Lu down before he gives Shuzhen any more grief.”
[ZH] “你既护着媳妇，不认为她拖你的后腿，我也不好说什么，此事到此为止。不过……”陈陆扫视一遍跪在地上的丫鬟，“你作为浩宇的娘子，对她们置之不理，在一旁看热闹，又作何解释？”
[EN] Qianli Xun stepped forward with a curtsy. “I’m sorry you had to witness this spectacle, Sister-in-law.”
[ZH] 千里寻觉得这位陆爷真是多管闲事，不如打击打击他，让他明白爱管闲事不是什么好习惯。
[EN] Chen Wenxin caught her elbow. “Really, why bother locking horns with an old man?” Letting go of Shuzhen, she walked over to Chen Lu, curtsied, and offered her arm. “Granduncle Lu, don’t hold a grudge against Shuzhen. Let me help you inside.”
[ZH] “此乃我的家事，陆爷最好别管。”
[EN] To Chen Lu, a married-out daughter was spilled water, not worth his regard. He brushed her off. “Second Young Madam shows me zero respect, so of course I’m taking issue with her. Step aside and keep your nose out of Chen family affairs.”
[ZH] 陈陆被成功气到：“我乃族中长辈！晚辈的家事我也管得！”
[EN] Fury flared in Chen Wenxin’s chest. She opened her mouth to snap back, only for Qianli Xun to stop her.
[ZH] 此话正中下怀，千里寻立即说：“陆爷既然要管，不如来点儿实惠的？”
[EN] “Leave this to me, Sister-in-law.”
[ZH] 陈陆不解：“什么意思？”
[EN] Chen Wenxin stepped back—better to let Shuzhen give the old coot a proper tongue-lashing on her behalf.
[ZH] 千里寻没有立即回答，走到几位跪着的丫鬟面前，将她们个个瞧一遍：“陈浩宇以前做事荒唐，冒犯你们是他不对，依我的意思，他就应该被千刀万剐……”
[EN] Qianli Xun stepped up to face him. “Suits me fine—I’ve got a bone to pick with Granduncle Lu too, so let’s lay it all out. Claiming I ruined Chen Haoyu’s chances of inheriting the Chen businesses is pure nonsense! Even setting aside the fact that Chen Haoyu has zero interest in it, with his old track record the elders wouldn’t have looked twice at him anyway. Back then the Zhus and Chens weren’t even betrothed—what did any of that have to do with me? Later, Old Madam and my parents-in-law agreed to this match because they’re sensible people who knew the gossip about me was groundless. Is Granduncle Lu saying he doubts Old Madam and my in-laws too? I married into the Chen house to live a quiet life. Whether Chen Haoyu inherits the family business or not couldn’t matter less to me.”
[ZH] 娘子的话吓得陈浩宇吐吐舌头，不敢言语，还是继续听娘子说些什么。
[EN] Chen Haoyu chimed in at once. “Same here—I couldn’t care less either.”
[ZH] “我也想让他纳你们为妾，可他不乐意，我又不愿为难他。你们说，除了给他做妾，还愿意选择哪条路？只要在我能力范围之内，自然尽心尽力帮忙解决。”
[EN] Qianli Xun shot him a pleased smile. “Since neither of us cares, where does this talk about his marrying me ruining his inheritance come from? Please take that notion back and quit bringing it up, Granduncle Lu. Keep harping on it, and folks might get the wrong idea that Chen Haoyu is eyeing the family business and grow resentful.”
[ZH] 几位丫鬟面面相觑，早已哭得差不多，刚刚流过的眼泪也被擦干，头脑清楚许多。很显然想给陈公子做妾已不可能，不如图点别的。
[EN] Chen Haodong caught the jab aimed straight at him and scoffed under his breath. Still, his sister-in-law’s words pleased him—good, let them stay clear of the inheritance, because fighting him for it wouldn’t end well. Then again, who knew if she meant a word she said? Better keep listening to Granduncle Lu put her in her place.
[ZH] 一位丫鬟带头说：“我们几个最担心以后嫁不出去，若少夫人能帮着解决终身大事，我们保证以后再也不会前来闹事。”
[EN] Chen Lu shot a sideways glance at Chen Haodong before answering. “Good that the two of you know your place. If Old Madam doesn’t look down on you, why should I meddle in Jianglin’s branch? I just feel it’s a pity for Haoyu.”
[ZH] 千里寻继续问：“你们对未来夫君有什么要求？比如年纪、家世等等。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu jumped right in. “Save your pity, Granduncle Lu. I’m thrilled to be married to Shuzhen.”
[ZH] 另一位丫鬟带头说：“若家中不缺银子，其他不计较。若家境一般，还需与我们年纪相仿，力气大些。”
[EN] “Since you’re determined to shield your wife and don’t think she’s dragging you down, I’ve nothing more to say—we’ll drop it.” Chen Lu’s eyes swept over the kneeling maids. “Still, as Haoyu’s wife, how do you explain standing back and enjoying the show while ignoring these girls?”
[ZH] 其他丫鬟无不点头认可。
[EN] What a busybody, Qianli Xun thought. Time to knock him down a peg and teach him not to poke his nose where it didn’t belong. “This is my household business, Granduncle Lu. Best stay out of it.”
[ZH] “如此甚好！”千里寻面向陈陆，“陆爷听到了吧？我现在帮着夫君解决难题，陆爷作为长辈喜欢管小辈的闲事，不如帮个忙？”
[EN] Right on cue, Chen Lu bristled. “I am a clan elder! I have every right to meddle in a junior’s household affairs!”
[ZH] 陈陆依然十分疑惑：“什么意思？”
[EN] Walked straight into the trap. Qianli Xun smiled. “Since Granduncle Lu insists on stepping in, why not offer something practical?”
[ZH] 千里寻故意把几个丫鬟数一遍：“一、二、三、四、五、六，共六人，陆爷随便挑，帮着解决几人，做妾也好，登堂入室也好，反正陆爷不缺银子，她们不会计较你的年纪，作为长辈帮晚辈解决难题是应该的，也算闲事没白管！”
[EN] Chen Lu blinked. “What do you mean?”
[ZH] 娘子捉弄陆爷，陈浩宇觉得好玩儿，可面对长辈他不好笑出声来。
[EN] Instead of answering him right away, Qianli Xun walked up to the kneeling maids and looked each one over. “Chen Haoyu acted like a fool in the past, and wronging you was his fault. If it were up to me, he’d deserve death by a thousand cuts...”
[ZH] 陈文心觉得解气，噗嗤笑了一声。
[EN] Hearing that, Chen Haoyu stuck his tongue out in fright and kept his mouth shut to hear what came next.
[ZH] 就连陈浩东也被逗乐，长辈是他请来的，总不能笑他，故意看向一边缓解缓解情绪。
[EN] “I’d even let him take you as concubines,” she went on, “except he refuses, and I’m not about to force him. Tell me: aside from becoming his concubines, what other path would you choose? Anything within my power, I’ll do my best to arrange.”
[ZH] 陈陆被气得说不出话：“你……你……，哼！”
[EN] The maids exchanged glances. By now their crying had tapered off, their cheeks were dry, and their heads were clear. Becoming Young Master Chen’s concubines was off the table; might as well angle for the next best thing.
[ZH] 不用陈浩东扶，陈陆自顾转身往陈府里走。
[EN] One maid spoke up for the group. “What worries us most is never finding husbands. If Young Madam can arrange good marriages for us, we swear we’ll never come back to make trouble.”
[ZH] 陈浩东忍着笑快步跟上。
[EN] Qianli Xun asked, “What do you look for in a future husband? Age, family background, that sort of thing?”
[ZH] 这些丫鬟能来闹事，是陈浩东私下派人蛊惑又塞些银子的，才有了今日的热闹。
[EN] Another maid spoke up. “If his family has silver, nothing else matters. If his family is ordinary, he should be close to our age and strong.”
[ZH] 丫鬟们算是起到作用，起码弟妹得罪了陆爷，浩宇变得再好，没有陆爷支持，许多长辈也会放弃支持他。
[EN] Every other maid nodded in agreement.
[ZH] 唯一令他不满的是，看上去浩宇的娘子已想到解决难题的办法。没关系，他还有下一招，走着瞧！
[EN] “Splendid!” Qianli Xun pivoted toward Chen Lu. “Heard that, Granduncle Lu? I’m right in the middle of solving my husband’s headache. Since you’re so fond of meddling in your juniors’ affairs, why not lend a hand?”
[ZH] 陈文心竖起大拇指：“你真厉害！佩服佩服！”
[EN] Chen Lu stayed baffled. “What are you getting at?”
[ZH] 面对嫂嫂的夸奖，千里寻根本高兴不起来：“还是你弟弟最厉害，招惹来这么大的麻烦事，还得我出面解决。”
[EN] Qianli Xun pointed and counted them off one by one. “One, two, three, four, five, six—six girls in all. Take your pick, Granduncle Lu! Settle a few of them yourself, whether as concubines or proper wives. You’re never short on silver, so they won’t mind your age. An elder helping a junior out of a jam is only right—and that way all your meddling won’t go to waste!”
[ZH] 陈文心上前用手指在陈浩宇的额头上点了一下：“你啊，以前净不干好事，现在还得淑真替你擦屁股。”
[EN] Watching his wife tease Granduncle Lu made Chen Haoyu fight to keep a straight face out of respect for his elder.
[ZH] 陈浩宇肠子都快悔青，又不知该如何安慰娘子，此时任何话都毫无意义，只能沉默不语。
[EN] Chen Wenxin let out an amused snort of pure satisfaction. Even Chen Haodong bit back a chuckle, turning his head away to keep from laughing at the elder he himself had invited.
[ZH] 千里寻对六位丫鬟说：“我答应帮你们解决终身大事，以后不许再来闹事，听到没？”
[EN] Chen Lu spluttered, purple in the face. “You—you—hmph!”
[ZH] 丫鬟们齐声回道：“听到了！”
[EN] Without waiting for Chen Haodong’s arm, he spun around and stalked into Chen Manor. Chen Haodong suppressed his grin and hurried after him.
[ZH] “起来吧！再跪下去，别人还以为我欺负下人。”
[EN] It was Chen Haodong who had slipped those maids silver and goaded them into making a scene today. They had done their job well enough: at the very least, his sister-in-law had alienated Granduncle Lu, and without Granduncle Lu’s backing, plenty of other elders would drop Haoyu no matter how much he reformed. The only snag was that Haoyu’s wife already looked close to sorting out the mess. No matter—he had another move waiting up his sleeve.
[ZH] 丫鬟们拜谢后，都起身离开。
[EN] Chen Wenxin gave her a thumbs-up. “Brilliant! Hats off to you!”
[ZH] 千里寻拉起嫂嫂：“嫂嫂，到我屋里坐坐，陪我说说话。”
[EN] Qianli Xun could not summon a smile. “Your brother’s the brilliant one—stirring up a mess this big and leaving me to clean it up.”
[ZH] 陈文心说：“等等，我去给他们叮嘱一番。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin reached out and poked Chen Haoyu’s forehead. “You—never did a shred of good before, and now Shuzhen has to wipe your backside for you.”
[ZH] 陈文心走到王管家等人身边：“今日之事奶奶与各位老爷可知？”
[EN] Regret gnawed at Chen Haoyu’s guts, yet no words could soothe his wife right now. He kept his mouth shut.
[ZH] 王管家回道：“并不知。”
[EN] Qianli Xun turned to the six maids. “I’ve given my word to arrange marriages for you. No more coming here to make scenes, understood?”
[ZH] “不知甚好，你们在府里不要乱说话，听到没？”
[EN] “Understood!” they chorused.
[ZH] “大小姐放心，我等有分寸。”
[EN] “Get up, then. Keep kneeling and people will think I’m bullying the help.”
[ZH] 陈文心这才放心地拉起淑真的手进入府内。
[EN] The maids thanked her and rose to leave.
[ZH] 陈浩宇被落在后面，心情落寞。经过今日一事，他的努力全部泡汤，以后想让娘子心中有他，恐怕难上加难。
[EN] Qianli Xun tugged her sister-in-law’s sleeve. “Come sit in my room and chat with me, Sister-in-law.”
[ZH] 他在自己脸上扇了一巴掌，懊悔又有什么用？还是跟上娘子，找机会再讨好她。
[EN] “Hold on—let me have a word with them first.” Chen Wenxin walked over to Butler Wang and the gatekeepers. “Do Grandmother and the Masters know about today’s scene?”
[ZH] 进入墨香苑后，陈浩宇想与她们一起走进素心草堂，结果脚还未踏入，门“砰”的一声被关上。他向后退了一步，失落地向后院走去，还是等姐姐走后，再向娘子请罪。
[EN] “No, Miss,” Butler Wang answered.
[ZH] 丫鬟们的事让千里寻肚子里窝着火，顺手将陈浩宇关在门外。
[EN] “Keep it that way. Not a loose word inside the manor, hear me?”
[ZH] 陈文心不请自坐，笑道：“你啊，进步不少。”
[EN] “Rest assured, Eldest Miss. We know our place.”
[ZH] “嫂嫂这话说得好不应景，我正生气着呢！”没瞧见苦荞，千里寻喊道：“苦荞！你在吗？”
[EN] Reassured, Chen Wenxin took Shuzhen’s hand and walked inside.
[ZH] 苦荞正在里屋忙着收拾屋子，听到小姐的呼唤，从里面出来：“小姐今儿回来得早？少夫人也来了，我给您做茶。”
[EN] Left trailing behind, Chen Haoyu felt his spirits plummet. All his hard work over the past weeks had gone up in smoke; winning a place in his wife’s heart would be ten times harder now. He slapped his own cheek—what good did regret do? Better trail after her and look for a chance to get back in her good graces.
[ZH] 千里寻唤苦荞本也是此意，苦荞既然心领神会，她也没什么好吩咐的。
[EN] Inside Moxiang Courtyard, he made to follow them into Suxin Thatched Hall, only for the door to slam right in his face. He took a step back and trudged off toward the rear courtyard, resigned to apologizing once his sister left.
[ZH] 心里纠结书卷之事，还是向嫂嫂问个明白。
[EN] Still simmering over the maids, Qianli Xun had shut him out on instinct.
[ZH] 她走到嫂嫂身边：“刚在大门外，嫂嫂说书卷之事已有人出面解决，不知谁这么好心？”
[EN] Chen Wenxin sat down without waiting to be asked and laughed. “My, you’ve come a long way.”
[ZH] “你坐下，听我慢慢给你说。”
[EN] “Not the time for jokes, Sister-in-law—I’m fuming!” Not seeing her maid, Qianli Xun called out, “Kuqiao! Are you there?”
[ZH] 千里寻坐下后，用期待的目光盯着嫂嫂。
[EN] Busy tidying the inner room, Kuqiao stepped out at her call. “Back so soon, Miss? Young Madam is here too—I’ll whisk some tea right away.”
[ZH] “听公爹说，仪王派林统制和司理院的人一起将大部分书卷收回，一把火给烧了，还抓了书局的管事和分发书卷的人，他们也没犯什么大事，很快又给放了。”
[EN] That was all Qianli Xun wanted; since Kuqiao read her mind, there was nothing more to order. Still uneasy over the pamphlets, she walked over to her sister-in-law. “Outside the gate, you said someone already dealt with the pamphlets. Who was kind enough to step in?”
[ZH] 原来是仪王，千里寻感激的同时，根本高兴不起来，只怕梦容知道破坏计划的人是她的哥哥，更不会原谅她。
[EN] “Sit down and I’ll tell you the whole thing.”
[ZH] “你怎么了？”淑真没有露出高兴的神情，是意料之外的事，陈文心不明所以，继续说，“公爹让我转告你，以后别再写诗作词。”
[EN] Qianli Xun sat and watched her in anticipation.
[ZH] 千里寻失落地说：“即使我不写诗作词，想找我麻烦的人还会有其他办法，挡也挡不住。”
[EN] “Father-in-law told me Prince Yi sent Commander Lin and men from the Judicial Office to round up most of the pamphlets and burn the lot. They arrested the bookshop manager and the men handing them out too, though since it wasn’t a major crime, they released them soon after.”
[ZH] “听这话，你应该知道书卷之事与何人有关？”
[EN] Prince Yi again. Gratitude mixed with dread in Qianli Xun’s chest—if Mengrong learned her own brother had wrecked her scheme, she would hate her even more.
[ZH] 千里寻掩饰道：“我怎么会知道？要是知道还能让那人为非作歹？”
[EN] “What’s wrong?” Surprised that Shuzhen did not look cheered at all, Chen Wenxin added, “Father-in-law also told me to tell you to stop writing poetry and ci lyrics from now on.”
[ZH] “也是，你的拳头甚是厉害，要是知道还不得把那人打得屁滚尿流。”陈文心说完，又觉不对，“仪王已审讯过书局的管事，应该知道是谁人所为，为何没有把那人抓起来？”
[EN] Qianli Xun’s shoulders slumped. “Even if I quit writing, anyone out to make my life miserable will find another way. There’s no stopping them.”
[ZH] 千里寻担忧地问：“我爹可知是谁人所为？”
[EN] “Sounds like you know who’s behind those pamphlets.”
[ZH] “公爹并不知，他也不好直接问仪王。”
[EN] Qianli Xun covered up at once. “How could I know? If I knew, would I just sit back and let them run wild?”
[ZH] “林觉亲自去抓的，肯定知道些什么，难道他没对我爹说起？”
[EN] “True—with those fists of yours, you’d have beaten them silly.” Chen Wenxin paused, a thought crossing her mind. “Wait. Prince Yi questioned the bookshop manager, so he must know who ordered them printed. Why didn’t he arrest whoever it was?”
[ZH] “公爹问过林统制，他只说一概不知。”
[EN] Qianli Xun asked in alarm, “Does Father know who did it?”
[ZH] 千里寻松口气，她不想家人知道梦容因仪王对她充满怨恨，甚至想让她消失。自己惹的事，不必家人替她担忧。
[EN] “No, and he couldn’t well ask Prince Yi straight out.”
[ZH] “我怎么感觉你好像很担心公爹知道是谁？”
[EN] “Lin Jue made the arrests himself, so he must know something. Didn’t he tell Father?”
[ZH] 千里寻继续掩饰：“哪有？嫂嫂不要瞎想。”
[EN] “Father-in-law asked Commander Lin, and he claimed he knew nothing.”
[ZH] 陈文心突然说道：“自从你与仪王妃疏远，苗知瑜和白梅若倒与仪王妃来往密切。也能理解，乔家本来就是皇亲国戚，不来往才不正常。林统制则希望利用他娘子与贵人走近，好继续往上爬。”
[EN] Qianli Xun breathed out in relief. She did not want her family finding out that Mengrong despised her over Prince Yi—enough to wish her dead. Troubles of her own making were not for her family to shoulder.
[ZH] 嫂嫂的话令千里寻担心不已，难道嫂嫂知道了什么？
[EN] “Why do I get the feeling you’re terrified Father-in-law might find out who it is?”
[ZH] “嫂嫂怎么突然提起此事？”
[EN] “Not at all! Stop imagining things, Sister-in-law.”
[ZH] “不过是随口说说。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin switched tack out of the blue. “Ever since you drifted apart from Princess Consort Yi, Miao Zhiyu and Bai Meiruo have grown thick with her. Makes sense, I suppose—the Qiaos are imperial kin, so it’d be stranger if they didn’t socialize. And Commander Lin clearly wants to use his wife to get closer to the royals and climb higher.”
[ZH] 千里寻松口气。
[EN] Qianli Xun’s heart skipped a beat. Did her sister-in-law know something? “Why bring that up all of a sudden?”
[ZH] “想想去年仪王妃还是郡主时与你那个亲密劲儿，真是羡煞旁人，谁知说变就变，都怪……”
[EN] “Just passing chatter.”
[ZH] 陈文心话到嘴边，不敢吐出那两个字，毕竟人家是贵人，背后埋怨贵人是大不敬。
[EN] Qianli Xun exhaled.
[ZH] “嫂嫂不要再提及此事，已经过去，应该向前看。”千里寻说这话，与其说是宽慰嫂嫂，不如说是宽慰自己。
[EN] “To think how close you and the Princess Consort were last year when she was still Princess Jiahe—everyone envied you two! Who knew things could sour overnight, all because of...” Chen Wenxin swallowed the last two words; complaining about royals behind their backs was asking for trouble.
[ZH] 陈文心笑道：“是得向前看，你今儿生浩宇的气，挺让人意外。”
[EN] “Drop it, Sister-in-law. What’s past is past—we have to look forward.” Saying it comforted herself as much as her sister-in-law.
[ZH] “我生他的气，是常有之事，嫂嫂何来意外？”
[EN] Chen Wenxin chuckled. “Look forward, yes! Still, seeing you lose your temper with Haoyu today took me by surprise.”
[ZH] “不一样，以前你何时因其他女子生过他的气？刚嫁过来时，他整日待在青楼，你跟个没事人似的。现在倒好，几个丫鬟就搞得你把他关在门外，说明你开始在乎他。”
[EN] “I get mad at him all the time. What’s surprising about that?”
[ZH] 嫂嫂的一番话，更让千里寻明白她的确开始在乎陈浩宇。
[EN] “It’s different! When did you ever get mad at him over other women before? Right after the wedding he spent all day in brothels and you didn’t bat an eye. Now a handful of maids has you slamming the door in his face—that means you’re starting to care about him.”
[ZH] “淑真，你别在乎浩宇以前的事儿，多看看现在的他，他为你改变，证明他真心待你，也说明他本质上并不坏。”
[EN] Her sister-in-law’s words drove the truth home: she really was starting to care about Chen Haoyu.
[ZH] 嫂嫂的劝解之语不无道理，千里寻点点头表示认可。 丫鬟的事还未解决，不如求嫂嫂帮忙。
[EN] “Don’t dwell on Haoyu’s past, Shuzhen. Look at who he is now. Changing for your sake proves he’s sincere—and shows he’s not bad at heart.”
[ZH] “嫂嫂，我有一个办法可以解决几个丫鬟的终身大事，你能不能帮我个忙？”
[EN] Unable to fault the logic, Qianli Xun nodded. The six maids still needed husbands, so she might as well ask her sister-in-law for help. “Sister-in-law, I’ve got a way to settle those maids’ marriages. Could you do me a favor?”
[ZH] “跟我还客气，尽管说。”
[EN] “No need to stand on ceremony with me. Name it.”
[ZH] “林觉手底下的兵不少，应该有很多人没成亲，总会有人不嫌弃那些丫鬟，我想请他出面帮忙解决此事。碍于我跟他以前的关系，不好亲自去见他，更怕见了被白梅若知道搞得他家宅不宁。恳请嫂嫂帮我转告哥哥，让他出面，给林觉说说。”
[EN] “Lin Jue commands plenty of soldiers, and lots of them must still be unmarried. Some of them won’t mind those maids’ pasts, so I want to ask him to help match them up. Given my history with him, I can’t go see him myself—and if Bai Meiruo caught wind of it, she’d raise a storm in his house. Could you ask my brother to step in and put in a word with Lin Jue?”
[ZH] “为何不让浩宇去？”
[EN] “Why not send Haoyu?”
[ZH] “他跟林觉没什么交情，恐怕林觉不会给他面子。相反由哥哥出面，他多少会顾及知府大人的面子，肯定不会果断拒绝。”
[EN] “He has no pull with Lin Jue—Lin Jue wouldn’t give him the time of day. If my brother goes instead, Lin Jue will at least respect Father’s position as Prefect and won’t turn him down flat.”
[ZH] “你倒谋算得好，用朱家人的关系替自己夫君擦屁股。”
[EN] “Clever little scheme—using the Zhu family’s connections to clean up your husband’s mess.”
[ZH] “陈浩宇也是嫂嫂的弟弟，难道嫂嫂不希望他好？”
[EN] “Chen Haoyu is your younger brother too! Don’t you want things to go well for him?”
[ZH] “呵呵呵……”陈文心笑得很开心，“看在你如此为浩宇考虑，就是拿刀架在脖子上，也要逼你哥哥把这件事情办成功。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin laughed in delight. “Seeing how hard you’re looking out for Haoyu, I’d hold a knife to your brother’s throat if I had to make him pull this off!”

#CH 274
[ZH] 林觉派人守好懒人村，又带领临水县县令勘察完周围的土质后，从山里回到临安城，天色已晚，他打算等明日再向知府大人和仪王汇报懒人村的情况。
[ZH] 骑着马刚到林府外，听到有人喊他：“林统制可回来！让我久等！”从声音判断出是朱景元。
[ZH] 林觉跳下马，过来一名下人将马牵了。他才循声看去，朱家的马车停在那里，朱景元从马车里探出个脑袋。
[ZH] 朱景元靠着知府大人的关系在府衙谋了个文职，没有官阶，说白了就是打杂的，大家都是看在知府大人的面子上才会把他放在眼里，也因这层关系林觉不敢怠慢。
[ZH] 他走上前：“难道朱兄一直在外面候着？”
[ZH] 对方没有官职不能称他为大人，还好以前是他的妹夫，“朱兄”倒称呼得顺口。
[ZH] “不算太久，大概等了两刻钟。你现在是大忙人，替仪王和知府大人忙些大事，人实在难找，我便想了到林府门口堵你的法子，还真管用。”
[ZH] “朱兄既然来，去府里坐坐。”
[ZH] “我就不进府打扰，烦请林统制上马车来与我一叙。”
[ZH] 林觉想不通朱景元找他何事？能耐心堵他，应该不是小事。带着好奇的心情登上马车，在朱景元对面坐好。
[ZH] “我猜朱兄找我定有要事，不妨直说。”
[ZH] “不是府衙的事，是私事，此事非林统制莫属。”
[ZH] 林觉更加好奇：“朱兄请讲。”
[ZH] “你是统制，管着临安的士兵，说句话肯定比以前当练兵官时更管用。再说是好事，士兵们不会不同意。”
[ZH] 林觉疑惑道：“请朱兄明说。”
[ZH] “不是什么大事，我妹妹身边有六名丫鬟担心自己嫁不出去，托她找个好人家。她想来想去还是林统制手下的士兵最适合。她一个妇道人家不好主动前来拜访，便让她嫂嫂向我转达。你也知道我疼妹妹，因此替她专程来找林统制。”
[ZH] 林觉算是听明白，朱淑真想给丫鬟找夫婿。
[ZH] 什么样的丫鬟她着急出手？林觉稍加思忖，便了然于胸。
[ZH] “恐怕是陈浩宇的丫鬟吧？该不会被陈浩宇糟蹋后一脚踢开，朱淑真不得不为他擦屁股，才想到我？”
[ZH] 被对方猜中，朱景元也没必要再隐晦表达。
[EN] After posting guards at Lazybones Village and guiding the County Magistrate of Linshui to survey the surrounding soil, Lin Jue rode back into Lin’an after dusk, planning to report to the Prefect and Prince Yi the next day.
[ZH] “林统制厉害，一猜就中。浩宇以前的确做过许多荒唐事，搞得淑真还得替他出面解决。我听文心说，六个丫鬟长得挺水灵，士兵们肯定满意，再说我妹妹多少也会给她们准备些嫁妆，你手下的士兵肯定不会吃亏。”
[EN] As he reined in outside Lin Manor, a familiar voice hailed him. “Commander Lin, back at last! You kept me waiting!”
[ZH] 林觉心中不免冷笑：“朱淑真啊朱淑真，你瞧不上我，费尽心思跟我和离，又能如何？嫁给陈浩宇还得替人家擦屁股，日子不好过啊！该不会现在觉出前夫的好来？才派你的哥哥来找我？既如此，我就帮你，让你知道我比陈浩宇强多了！”
[EN] It was Zhu Jingyuan. Lin Jue dismounted, handed his reins to a servant, and looked toward a parked Zhu family carriage where Zhu Jingyuan was poking his head out.
[ZH] “请朱兄转告淑真，让她把嫁妆准备好。”
[EN] Through the Prefect’s pull, Zhu Jingyuan held a low-ranking clerical post at the prefectural yamen—no official grade, just an errand-runner—yet everyone showed him face out of respect for his father, and Lin Jue was no exception.
[ZH] 林觉爽快答应，朱景元开心不已：“我替妹妹谢过林统制，明日在聚香楼设宴，以表谢意。”
[EN] He stepped over. “Has Brother Zhu been waiting out here long?” Having no official title, Jingyuan could not be called “Lord,” and since he had once been his brother-in-law, “Brother Zhu” came out easy enough.
[ZH] “朱兄跟我不必客气，再说明日一大堆事，根本没有心情去聚香楼，先记在账上，等闲了我们再一起畅饮。”
[EN] “Not too long—about half an hour. You’re a busy man these days handling big projects for Prince Yi and the Prefect, impossible to track down, so I figured I’d stake out your front gate. Worked like a charm!”
[ZH] “好！”
[EN] “Since you’re here, Brother Zhu, come inside for a cup of tea.”
[ZH] 第二日，林觉在府衙向朱晞颜汇报完懒人村的情况后，二人一起来到司理院。
[EN] “I won’t impose. Why not hop into the carriage and chat a minute?”
[ZH] 仪王在司理院的堂内接见他们。
[EN] Wondering what warranted ambushing him at his gate, Lin Jue climbed aboard and sat across from him. “Must be something urgent, Brother Zhu. Speak straight.”
[ZH] 有朱晞颜在，林觉不能主动说话，只需跟在身后行礼。
[EN] “Nothing to do with the yamen—a personal favor, and only Commander Lin can pull it off.”
[ZH] 朱晞颜开口说：“殿下，昨日林统制带人已将懒人村的村口把守，无人再敢往出跑。临水县县令也详细勘察过村中的土质，发现那里的土质只适合长杂草和野菜，确实不适合种植粮食。”
[EN] His curiosity piqued, Lin Jue said, “I’m listening.”
[ZH] 申简辰想想说：“既如此，不如顺其自然，发动懒人村的人清除杂草，开垦土地，大量种植野菜，还请知府大人帮着寻找销路。”
[EN] “As Commander, you oversee Lin’an’s garrison. Your word carries far more weight now than back when you were a drill officer. Besides, it’s a good thing—your soldiers won’t say no.”
[ZH] 朱晞颜再不乐意，也得答应：“下官记下。关于在懒人村建造土屋和窑洞一事，下官也已安排下去，交由林统制负责，大概需要半年的时间方可竣工。”
[EN] “Come out with it, Brother Zhu.”
[ZH] “有劳知府大人与林统制，此事办好利国利民，也不枉二位为官一场。说不定此事之后，临安官府会得到百姓夸赞。”
[EN] “Nothing huge. Six maids around my sister are worried they’ll never get married and begged her to find them good matches. After thinking it over, she figured the soldiers under your command are the best fit. As a married woman, she couldn’t come calling in person, so she had her sister-in-law pass the request to me. You know how I dote on my sister, so I came straight to you.”
[ZH] 朱晞颜对百姓夸赞并不奢求，只求多一事不如少一事。
[EN] Lin Jue caught on in a heartbeat. Zhu Shuzhen wanted husbands for maids—and which maids would she be in such a hurry to marry off? A second’s thought gave him the answer.
[ZH] 仪王刚来时什么都不理会，令他舒心不已；成完婚再次回到临安，却要做事。
[EN] “Chen Haoyu’s maids, I bet? He had his way with them and tossed them aside, leaving Zhu Shuzhen to clean up his mess—and she thought of me?”
[ZH] 本以为他担心没事做会憋出病来，选择司理参军一职不过是为怡情悦性，没想到竟认真起来，给府衙添了这么大的麻烦，不仅要动用人力还要花银子，以后还不知会搞出多少名堂来。
[EN] Guessed dead-on, Zhu Jingyuan dropped the tact. “Sharp as ever, Commander Lin. Haoyu did plenty of foolish things in the past, and now Shuzhen has to sort them out. Wenxin tells me all six girls are fresh and pretty—your men won’t be disappointed. Plus my sister will put together dowries for them, so your soldiers come out ahead.”
[ZH] “下官不敢居功，都是仪王的功劳。”
[EN] Lin Jue sneered inside. *Zhu Shuzhen, oh Zhu Shuzhen! You looked down on me and bent over backward for a mutual divorce, and for what? Marrying Chen Haoyu only to wipe up his messes—life isn’t so sweet now, is it? Starting to see how much better your ex-husband was? Fine, I’ll help you—just to show you I’m ten times the man Chen Haoyu is!*
[ZH] “辛苦知府大人和林统制，此事若成，我定向官家替二位请功。”
[EN] “Tell Shuzhen to get their dowries ready,” he said.
[ZH] “为朝廷做事，实乃下官的本分。”
[EN] Delighted by his prompt agreement, Zhu Jingyuan beamed. “Thank you on my sister’s behalf, Commander Lin! Tomorrow I’ll host a banquet at Juxiang Tower to repay you.”
[ZH] “好了，去忙吧。”
[EN] “No need for ceremony, Brother Zhu—tomorrow I’m swamped and in no mood for banquets. Put it on a tab, and we’ll drink our fill once things quiet down.”
[ZH] 二人离开后，在司理院门口分别。
[EN] “Deal!”
[ZH] 林觉装作去忙懒人村的事，估摸着知府大人走远，扭转马头又来到司理院。
[EN] The next morning, after briefing Zhu Xiyan at the prefectural office on Lazybones Village, Lin Jue accompanied him to the Judicial Office.
[ZH] 他将马拴好，又径直走入堂内。
[EN] Prince Yi received them in the main hall. With Zhu Xiyan present, Lin Jue stayed silent behind him after bowing.
[ZH] “仪王殿下，下官还有一事禀明。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan spoke first. “Your Highness, yesterday Commander Lin posted guards at the entrance to Lazybones Village so no one slips out. The County Magistrate of Linshui also inspected the soil in detail and confirmed it can only grow weeds and wild vegetables, not grain.”
[ZH] 申简辰放下手中的卷宗，露出疑惑的表情，林觉要说的事八成不能当着朱晞颜的面讲，要不怎么去而又返？
[EN] Shen Jianchen thought it over. “Then work with nature instead of against it. Have the villagers clear the weeds, till the plots, and cultivate wild vegetables on a large scale—and I’ll trouble the Prefect to help secure buyers.”
[ZH] “林统制有什么话尽管说。”
[EN] Reluctant as he was, Zhu Xiyan had to agree. “Understood. As for building earthen cottages and cave dwellings in the village, I’ve already put Commander Lin in charge. Construction should take about half a year.”
[ZH] “事关朱淑真。”
[EN] “Thank you both for your trouble,” Shen Jianchen said. “Pull this off and it will benefit both court and commoner—worthy of your offices. The people of Lin’an might even sing the yamen’s praises.”
[ZH] 林觉本想自己将昨日朱景元说的事解决即可，今日见到仪王，又想告诉他。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan cared nothing for popular praise; he just wanted a quiet life. When Prince Yi first arrived and ignored everything, Zhu Xiyan had been ecstatic. Yet since returning from his wedding, the prince had turned dead serious—piling work on the yamen, burning manpower and silver, with no telling what else he would cook up next.
[ZH] 仪王心中放不下朱淑真，林觉最清楚不过。将朱淑真的事告诉仪王，方便他通过此事了解到对方的境况，才会认为他林觉是为仪王心中之人办事，自然对他的印象会更好，也才会从心底把他当成自己人。
[EN] “I dare not claim credit,” Zhu Xiyan said. “All credit belongs to Your Highness.”
[ZH] 仪王果然急切地问：“什么事？”
[EN] “Both of you have worked hard. Once this succeeds, I will petition His Majesty to commend you.”
[ZH] “也不是什么大事，陈浩宇以前喜欢抢些黄花大姑娘放在陈家其他宅子里当丫鬟，有些已失身。现在朱淑真想给失了身的丫鬟找婆家，偏偏想到临安士兵，求到下官名下，下官正犹豫要不要答应，想听听殿下的意思。”
[EN] “Serving the court is merely our duty.”
[ZH] 千里寻开始为陈浩宇解决麻烦事，看来她已接纳他。申简辰在心中为自己和千里寻叹口气，他唯一能做的便是在背后默默帮她。
[EN] “Good. Go see to your duties.”
[ZH] “此事对林统制来讲十分容易，答应便是。”
[EN] Outside the Judicial Office, the two parted ways. Pretending to ride off to Lazybones Village, Lin Jue waited until the Prefect was well down the road, then wheeled his horse back to the Judicial Office, tied it up, and walked straight back into the hall.
[ZH] “是。”
[EN] “Your Highness, I have one more matter to report.”
