[ZH] “她不是帮你，是帮她自己，她虽嫁给蒋爷，心里还惦记着陈家二公子。母老虎当着她的面把人绑走，自此二公子再也没来，她心中又恨又憋屈，才想借着春金兰到陈家闹上一闹。”
[EN] “Not tonight—tomorrow, same time, same spot.”
[ZH] “原来如此。”陈浩东叮嘱道，“你快回吧，省得春香楼的妈妈到处找你发现我的马车。”
[EN] She leaned against his chest. “Everything about you is great except you’re too sneaky. When can you stroll right into Chunxiang Brothel openly like your cousin used to?”
[ZH] 春金蝉向陈浩东抛了个媚眼才走下马车。
[EN] “Give me one more year.” He wrapped an arm around her. “If only my wife were half like you.”
[ZH] 那媚眼令陈浩东春心荡漾了一会儿，想起还要找疤子，便没有心情继续荡漾下去。
[EN] “Don’t mention her when we’re together.”
[ZH] 既然疤子只能在街上瞎混，不如沿街碰碰运气。陈浩东后悔为何不早些搞清楚疤子的住处，既然大体方位在城东，不如去城东的街上兜一圈，还好春香楼离城东不远。
[EN] After holding her a moment, he let go. “Head back inside. I still have to hit the Judicial Office.”
[ZH] 夜色下，陈浩东独自一人驾马车在街上转悠，他告诉自己今晚无论如何必须找到疤子。他仔细瞧着街上的行人和街道两边的小商贩，以及大的商铺，希望疤子的身影能快些进入他的视线之内。
[EN] Chun Jinchan clearly knew what was up. “Jinlan’s little stunt?”
[ZH] 功夫不负有心人，他瞧见一名常跟着浩宇一起的小弟正在街边吃小笼包。
[EN] “Who else?”
[ZH] 他停好马车，匆忙跳下，来到那位小弟跟前，急切地问：“疤子在哪里？”
[EN] “She really packs a punch.”
[ZH] 那位小弟刚一口咽下一个包子，瞧向陈浩东：“原来是大公子，好久不见，疤子在菜市场，您去那边找他。”
[EN] “Got you to thank too—without you egging her on, she wouldn’t have raised half that storm at Chen Manor.”
[ZH] 陈浩东迅速撇下那位小弟，朝一旁的巷子拐去。
[EN] “Not just me,” Chun Jinchan said with a smirk. “Madam Jiang did the heavy lifting.”
[ZH] 虽是晚上，巷子里还有许多卖菜的，菜摊旁的竹竿上挂着灯笼，买菜的人也不少。
[EN] “Madam Jiang?”
[ZH] 疤子带着两个小弟到处拿菜，两个小弟各背一个竹筐，见菜就往里面放。疤子手拿棍子，到菜摊旁，将棍子举在卖家面前。
[EN] “Chun Jinmo.”
[ZH] “老子就喜欢白拿，你能把我怎样？下次见我来，主动把菜放进竹筐里！听到没？”
[EN] “Oh, her surname’s Jiang. Why’d she help me?”
[ZH] 卖菜的人敢怒不敢言。
[EN] “She wasn’t helping you—she was helping herself. Married Master Jiang, sure, yet she still pines for Second Young Master. That tigress marched in and hauled him off right under her nose, and he never came back. Bitter as gall, she jumped at using Chun Jinlan to stir up Chen Manor.”
[ZH] 陈浩东喊道：“疤子！何必抢他们的？你要多少菜？我买给你！”
[EN] “Makes sense. Slip back in before the madam comes looking and spots my carriage.”
[ZH] 疤子回过头，金主前来，他的两只眼睛立刻笑成一条缝，收起棍子，走上前：“天儿都黑了，大公子怎么跑这里来？难道专程来找我？”
[EN] Chun Jinchan threw him a sultry wink and hopped down. For a moment his pulse stirred, until the thought of Scarface doused it cold.
[ZH] “可不是？我的马车停在外面，去车上说话。”
[EN] With Scarface drifting around the east quarter—not far from Chunxiang Brothel—he had to comb the streets tonight no matter what. Driving slow through the dark, scanning pedestrians, stalls, and storefronts, he finally struck gold: one of Haoyu’s old sidekicks stood scarfing soup dumplings by a street stand.
[ZH] 疤子已无心欺负卖菜人，跟着陈大公子往巷子外走。两名小弟懒得跟着疤子，也跑去吃小笼包。
[EN] Pulling up, Chen Haodong hopped down and hurried over. “Where’s Scarface?”
[ZH] 马车里，没等陈大公子开口，疤子先笑嘻嘻地说：“好久没去永泰茶楼找大公子，您千万别怪我，我也想跟您混些银子，可也得有料才行，您也知道，您的堂弟不再带着哥几个，实在搞不来他的消息。”
[EN] Swallowing a dumpling whole, the lackey recognized him. “Oh, Eldest Young Master! Long time no see. Scarface is over at the vegetable market.”
[ZH] “他现在整日待在绸缎庄，我对他再清楚不过，哪需要你的消息？”
[EN] Ditching him on the spot, Chen Haodong ducked into the side alley. Lanterns hung on bamboo poles over night vegetable stalls, crowds still shopping.
[ZH] 疤子露出疑惑的笑容：“大晚上的，大公子有什么急事找我？”
[EN] Scarface swaggered past with two lackeys lugging bamboo baskets, stuffing produce in free of charge while Scarface waved a club in a vendor’s face. “I like taking stuff for free—what’re you gonna do about it? Next time I walk up, load the baskets yourselves, got it?”
[ZH] “还记得被你卖到扬州青楼的女子吗？”
[EN] The vendors seethed in silence.
[ZH] 疤子没了笑容，更加疑惑：“大公子为何提她？”
[EN] “Scarface!” Chen Haodong called. “Why rob them? Whatever vegetables you want, I’ll buy!”
[ZH] “她现在已入春香楼，名叫春金兰。”
[EN] Seeing his meal ticket, Scarface’s eyes crinkled into slits. Lowering his club, he jogged over. “Out after dark looking for me, Eldest Young Master?”
[ZH] 自从陈浩宇改了性情后，疤子没怎么去过春香楼。此刻想来，多亏没再去，否则碰到冤家只怕没好果子吃。
[EN] “Sure am. My carriage is right outside—let’s talk there.”
[ZH] “大公子为何跟我提她？”
[EN] Dropping the shakedown, Scarface followed him out while his two sidekicks wandered off for dumplings.
[ZH] “她闹到陈家，逼着浩宇纳她为妾，否则就要闹出人命来。我堂弟根本不承认是他做下的事，此事便由司理院出面解决，他们两个现在被关在司理院。你瞧，马车上的东西是我准备去司理院送给浩宇的。”
[EN] Inside the carriage, Scarface grinned right away. “Don’t blame me for not stopping by Yongtai Teahouse, Eldest Young Master! I’d love to earn your silver, except you need fresh dirt for that, and since your cousin dropped the boys, I’ve got no news on him.”
[ZH] 疤子紧张不已：“这种事怎么能让司理院插手？”
[EN] “He’s stuck at the silk shop all day—I know his schedule better than you. No need for news.”
[ZH] “春金兰已供出你，恐怕明日司理院会找你前去问话。”
[EN] Scarface’s grin turned puzzled. “Then what’s the late-night rush?”
[ZH] 陈浩东说得轻松，却将疤子搞得紧张。疤子心想，不对啊？我只是把人卖到扬州青楼，也没干别的，为何要紧张？
[EN] “Remember the girl you sold to that Yangzhou brothel?”
[ZH] 他故作轻松的样子：“大公子为何不紧张？人是你糟蹋的，也是你让我把她卖到青楼，司理院要是找我，只怕我忍受不了板子供出实情来。”
[EN] Scarface’s smile vanished. “Why bring her up?”
[ZH] 陈浩东撺掇春金兰出来闹事，也想过会将以前的事翻出来，思来想去再怎么翻也不会翻到他的头上，毕竟那时将一切处理得毫无痕迹，再说已过去一年，即使当初留下痕迹，也被时间销毁。
[EN] “She’s at Chunxiang Brothel now, calling herself Chun Jinlan.”
[ZH] 没想到司理院会插手，只怕真的会把自己给查出来，到时搬起石头砸自己的脚。关键还在疤子，只要将他搞定，便可高枕无忧。
[EN] Scarface hadn’t set foot in Chunxiang Brothel much since Chen Haoyu reformed—lucky break, or he’d have bumped straight into a hornet’s nest. “Why tell me about her?”
[ZH] “卖她的银子可都归你，你答应过我，绝不会告诉第三个人。”
[EN] “She stormed Chen Manor demanding Haoyu take her as a concubine or she’d make someone die. My cousin denied everything, so the Judicial Office stepped in and locked them both up. See these bundles? I’m on my way to deliver bedding to Haoyu.”
[ZH] “我是答应过，也做到，可我怕到司理院吃板子。”
[EN] Scarface tensed. “How’d the Judicial Office get dragged in?”
[ZH] “只要你按照我说的做，绝对不会吃板子。”
[EN] “Chun Jinlan already named you. Tomorrow they’ll haul you in for questioning.”
[ZH] “大公子请讲。”
[EN] Chen Haodong sounded breezy, yet Scarface’s stomach dropped—wait a minute, all he did was sell her! Putting on a casual air, Scarface countered, “How come Eldest Young Master isn’t nervous? You’re the one who defiled her and ordered me to sell her! If they start swinging bamboo paddles at the Judicial Office, I might not hold out and spill the truth!”
[ZH] 陈浩东靠近疤子，用阴沉的语气说：“你把所有事揽下，主动向参军大人认罪，他怎会用板子打你？”
[EN] Chen Haodong had figured the old affair might resurface, yet he had scrubbed every trace a year ago—never expecting the Judicial Office to wade in and risk exposing him. Everything hinged on locking Scarface down.
[ZH] 原来是让他顶替所有罪过，疤子很不情愿：“大公子不能只顾自己，害我去牢里受罪，我听说强奸罪可是死刑，你让我承担，不是要了我的命吗？不行不行，给再多银子都不行。”
[EN] “You pocketed every copper from selling her, and you swore not a third soul would ever hear.”
[ZH] 疤子的态度陈浩东早料到，已有应对之策：“怎么能说是强奸？你就说是你情我愿，这种事情很难查到证据，只要你一口咬定，春金兰也拿你没办法。”
[EN] “I kept my word! I’m just scared of taking a beating at the Judicial Office!”
[ZH] 疤子想想说：“大公子打算出多少银子？”
[EN] “Do as I say, and not one paddle will touch you.”
[ZH] 对方开始要银子，证明他已松口：“你出个价。”
[EN] “I’m all ears, Eldest Young Master.”
[ZH] 疤子想了想说出一个数字：“二十两。”
[EN] Leaning close, Chen Haodong lowered his voice. “Take the whole rap yourself and confess upfront to the Judicial Investigator. Why would he beat a man who confesses?”
[ZH] 陈浩东心想，穷鬼就是穷鬼，让他自己出价才说二十两，真是没见过大钱，既是让他出去顶罪，还需大方些：“我给你一百两。”
[EN] Realizing he was being cast as the fall guy, Scarface balked. “Not a chance! You stay clean while I rot in jail? Rape gets you executed! Asking me to take that rap is asking for my neck! No amount of silver buys my head!”
[ZH] 疤子惊得从座位上溜下：“大公子没骗我？一百两？”
[EN] Expecting that pushback, Chen Haodong had his angle ready. “Who said anything about rape? Claim it was consensual! Hardest thing on earth to disprove—stick to that story and Chun Jinlan can’t touch you.”
[ZH] “红口白牙说出，岂可骗你？”
[EN] Scarface chewed on it. “How much silver are we talking?”
[ZH] 疤子爬起来继续坐好：“我若作假证，诬陷二公子，你也得给我一百两？”
[EN] Asking a price meant he was hooked. “Name it.”
[ZH] 陈浩东点点头：“自然。这便是我接下来要说的第二招。明日去司理院，先一口咬定是浩宇糟蹋了春金兰，吩咐你把人卖到扬州青楼。实在不行再使第一招，我保证你可全身而退。”
[EN] “Twenty taels,” Scarface ventured.
[ZH] 疤子埋怨道：“大公子若早这么说，我又何必拒绝？说不定第二招就搞定。您应该先说第二招再说第一招，搞得我到现在都心惊胆战。”
[EN] *Cheap pauper—never seen real money.* If the man was taking the fall, pay him right. “I’ll give you a hundred taels.”
[ZH] “我向来喜欢先说最严重的状况。”
[EN] Scarface slid halfway off the bench. “No kidding?! A hundred taels?!”
[ZH] “大公子放心，明日交给我，您只当与此事无关。”
[EN] “Have I ever gone back on my word?”
[ZH] 陈浩东从怀里掏出一袋银子，递给疤子：“额外给你的，先花着。”
[EN] Scrambling back up, Scarface asked, “And if I lie in court to pin it on Second Young Master, I still get the hundred?”
[ZH] 疤子满脸堆笑地接过：“好多日没见到这么多银子，我先替兄弟们谢过大公子。”
[EN] “Of course—that’s Plan B, which you try first! Tomorrow in court, swear up and down Haoyu defiled Chun Jinlan and told you to sell her to Yangzhou. Only fall back on Plan A if Plan B fails. I guarantee you walk out whole.”
[ZH] 陈浩东警惕地问：“你那几个兄弟可知道春金兰的事？”
[EN] “Wish you’d led with Plan B instead of scaring me half to death with Plan A!”
[ZH] “您忘了？那晚他们都被我支走，什么都不知道。大公子今日不提，我都快忘记。”
[EN] “I prefer laying out the worst case first.”
[ZH] “好，谁都不能说。去吧。”
[EN] “Leave tomorrow to me, Eldest Young Master—you’re clean as snow.”
[ZH] 疤子揣好银子离开后，陈浩东松口气，继续赶马车，现在终于可以放心去司理院扮演一位疼弟弟的堂兄。
[EN] Chen Haodong tossed him a pouch of silver. “Bonus spending money upfront.”
[ZH] 马车到达司理院门口，陈浩东瞧见青瓜站在门外，那样子显然正在犹豫要不要上前敲开司理院的大门。想到车上准备的东西需要有人搬，陈浩东停好车，喊道：“青瓜，你怎么也来了？”
[EN] Grinning ear to ear, Scarface tucked it away. “Haven’t seen this much silver in ages—thanks on behalf of the boys!”
[ZH] 听到大公子的声音，青瓜迎上去：“我来司理院陪我家公子，可我不敢进去。”
[EN] Chen Haodong turned sharp. “Do your buddies know anything about Chun Jinlan?”
[ZH] 陈浩东跳下马车：“既然来了，帮着拿东西，车上用的吃的，还有药都拿上，我去敲门。”
[EN] “Remember? I sent them all away that night—they don’t know squat. I’d almost forgotten myself till you brought it up.”
[ZH] “还是大公子想得周到。”青瓜此时认为自家公子有个好堂兄真是幸运，他把车上的东西抱在怀里，还好不多，他一个人刚好拿得下。
[EN] “Good. Not a word to anyone. Off you go.”
[ZH] 陈浩东上前在大门上敲了三下。
[EN] Watching Scarface vanish into the dark, Chen Haodong exhaled and drove on to the Judicial Office to play the doting elder cousin.
[ZH] 门从里面打开，一位差役探出脑袋：“大晚上的跑司理院做什么？”
[EN] At the Judicial Office gate, he spotted Qinggua pacing outside, torn over whether to knock. Needing hands to haul the bundles anyway, he reined in. “Qinggua? What are you doing here?”
[ZH] 陈浩东掏出一锭银子塞给对方：“我是陈浩宇的堂兄，他今日被带进司理院，家人担心，让我给他送铺盖来。”
[EN] Qinggua brightened. “Came to keep Young Master company, only I’m scared to go in.”
[ZH] 差役接过银子，脸上立刻露出笑容：“原来是陈家人，仪王交代过，只要是陈家人，一律放进来。”
[EN] Hopping down, Chen Haodong said, “Grab the bedding, snacks, and salve from the carriage while I knock.”
[ZH] 差役将门开大：“里边请。”
[EN] “Eldest Young Master thinks of everything!” Grateful his master had such a decent cousin, Qinggua hoisted the bundles as Chen Haodong rapped three times.
[ZH] 青瓜怀抱着铺盖，跟着大公子进入。
[EN] A constable poked his head out. “What’s your business here this late?”
[ZH] 走进后，陈浩东问：“仪王可在？”
[EN] Chen Haodong pressed a silver ingot into his palm. “I’m Chen Haoyu’s elder cousin. The family’s worried about him and sent bedding.”
[ZH] 差役回道：“仪王妃差人来唤，王爷回幽林别居了。”
[EN] The constable broke into a beam. “A Chen! Prince Yi left orders to let any Chen family member right in. Step inside!”
[ZH] “都头与师爷可在？”
[EN] Following him in with Qinggua loaded behind, Chen Haodong fished for details. “Is Prince Yi here?”
[ZH] “都已睡下。”
[EN] “Princess Consort Yi sent for him—His Highness went back to Youlin Retreat.”
[ZH] “我送东西，你可做得了主？”
[EN] “Chief Constable and Clerk Han?”
[ZH] “做得了主。司理院的牢狱是临时用两所旧房子改造的，重要的犯人不会被关在这里，每个当值的差役都能做主。”
[EN] “Both asleep.”
[ZH] 陈浩东问得详细，无非是想确认今晚司理院到底都住些什么人。反正仪王不在，他轻松许多。
[EN] “Can you authorize my drop-off?”
[ZH] 在差役的带领下，两人跟着来到司理院最后面，确实有两个房间，门上分别上着大锁，只有一面墙最高处开着小窗户，可以理解为用来透气而已。
[EN] “Sure can. Our holding cells are just two converted back rooms—serious felons aren’t kept here, so any constable on duty has the keys.”
[ZH] 差役从腰间取下钥匙，边开锁边说：“今晚我当差，钥匙随身携带。陈公子被关在这间屋里，我打开锁，您进去把东西放了，最多说一刻钟的话。”
[EN] Prince Yi wasn’t here—better and better.
[ZH] 陈浩东说：“多谢。”
[EN] Guided to the rear courtyard, they found two padlocked rooms with tiny air vents cut high up on one wall. Unhooking a key from his belt, the constable unlocked one door. “I’m on watch tonight. Young Master Chen’s in here. Drop the stuff off—fifteen minutes tops.”
[ZH] 门已打开，陈浩东先走进去，青瓜跟着进去，差役又将门从外面关上。
[EN] “Much obliged.”
[ZH] 屋里只有一个炕和一张桌子，连椅子都没有，桌上点着一盏油灯。借着昏暗的灯光，两人看清炕上躺着的人正是陈浩宇。
[EN] Chen Haodong stepped in with Qinggua right behind, and the constable shut the door from outside.
[ZH] 青瓜忍不住唤声：“公子！”
[EN] Inside stood just a brick kang bed, a table with a flickering oil lamp, and not even a chair. Lying on the kang was Chen Haoyu.
[ZH] 听到青瓜的声音，陈浩宇立刻坐起来：“东哥，你怎么来这里？”
[EN] “Young Master!” Qinggua blurted.
[ZH] “大伯和大婶子担心你，派我给你送铺盖来。”陈浩东向四周瞧一遍，“可住得习惯？”
[EN] Sitting right up, Chen Haoyu blinked. “Brother Dong? What brings you here?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“我啊，在哪里都住得习惯，这叫随遇而安。”
[EN] “Uncle and Aunt were worried sick and sent me with bedding.” Chen Haodong glanced around. “Managing all right in here?”
[ZH] 青瓜一心替公子难过，怀里抱着东西都忘记放下。
[EN] “I’m fine anywhere—take life as it comes.”
[ZH] 陈浩东批评道：“还不赶紧把东西放下？难道想让你家公子今晚睡在硬邦邦的炕上不成？”
[EN] Staring in misery, Qinggua forgot to put the bundles down. Chen Haodong chided him, “Put that stuff down already! Want your master sleeping on bare brick?”
[ZH] 青瓜这才把东西放在炕上，凑到公子跟前，瞅着他的手臂：“公子，疼吗？”
[EN] Setting the bedding on the kang, Qinggua peered at his arm. “Does it hurt, Young Master?”
[ZH] “一点儿都不疼，少夫人可好？”
[EN] “Not a bit. How’s Young Madam?”
[ZH] “少夫人好不好我不知道，反正我看公子一点儿都不好。大公子，能不能给外面的人说说，让我陪公子住在这里？”
[EN] “No idea about Young Madam, I just see you looking rough! Eldest Young Master, could you talk to the guard outside and let me stay here with Young Master?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇拒绝道：“我不需要人陪，你还是回去吧。”
[EN] “I don’t need a babysitter—go home,” Chen Haoyu refused.
[ZH] 青瓜继续要求道：“还是让我留下来，陪公子说说话也行。”
[EN] “Let me stay! At least you’ll have someone to talk to!”

#CH 280
[ZH] 陈浩宇被青瓜感动，他住在简陋的环境，青瓜还主动跑来陪伴，足以说明他是位义仆。以前他身边跟的那些人只因钱财围着他转，如果让他们来陪他，那些人估计跑得比兔子还快。
[ZH] 他为以前忽略青瓜感到惭愧，越是感动和惭愧，越说不出感激的话来，所有感情只能汇聚在平常说习惯的话中：“说你贱，你还真贱。”
[ZH] 同样骂人的话比往常任何时候听起来都顺耳，青瓜肯定地回应道：“我喜欢对公子贱。”
[ZH] 主仆二人腻歪的样子，陈浩东听不下去，何况他还要去瞧瞧隔壁那位，若青瓜跟着，实在不方便。
[ZH] “行了，你们两个别再腻歪，我给差役说一声，大不了再给他些银子，这种小事他必然答应。”
[ZH] 青瓜高兴地说：“谢大公子！”
[ZH] 陈浩东不打算继续待下去：“东西和人都送到，我该走了，要不门外的差役该不高兴。”
[ZH] 陈浩东推门而出，顺手把门从外面关好，又从怀里掏出两锭银子塞给差役：“刚才的下人一直跟着浩宇，能否让他留下陪着？”
[ZH] 一下子跳进怀里两锭银子，差役更高兴得合不拢嘴：“又不是什么大事，尽管留。”
[EN] Touchiness softened into gratitude inside Chen Haoyu. Locked in this bare room, Qinggua still came running to keep him company—a truly loyal servant, unlike the fair-weather crowd who would have bolted faster than rabbits once the silver stopped flowing. Ashamed of how he had treated Qinggua before, he couldn’t bring himself to say anything sappy and fell back on his old teasing. “Call you shameless, and you prove me right.”
[ZH] 差役揣了银子，上前把门锁好，一转身见陈浩东还站在原地，心想人家一次给了两锭银子，应该还有事相求，便问道：“还有事？”
[EN] The familiar insult sounded sweeter than praise. Qinggua beamed. “I love being shameless for Young Master!”
[ZH] “差役大哥好眼力。”陈浩东先夸赞一番，继续说，“隔壁关着的姑娘把浩宇的手臂给刺伤，狠着呢，我想进去劝她两句，不知可否方便？”
[EN] Sick of their sappy banter—and eager to visit the cell next door without Qinggua tagging along—Chen Haodong cut in. “All right, knock it off. I’ll talk to the constable and toss him extra silver if needed. Easy fix.”
[ZH] 差役犹豫片刻：“按说你不是她的家人，不能放你进去，看在银子的份上我偷偷给你行个方便，不能超过一刻钟。”
[EN] “Thank you, Eldest Young Master!” Qinggua cheered.
[ZH] “多谢差役大哥。”
[EN] “Supplies and company delivered—time I got going before the guard loses patience.” Stepping out and shutting the door behind him, Chen Haodong slipped two more silver ingots into the constable’s hand. “That servant always waits on Haoyu. Mind letting him stay the night?”
[ZH] 差役打开大锁，推开门：“公子，请。”
[EN] Two ingots landing straight in his palm stretched the constable’s grin ear to ear. “No big deal—let him stay.” Locking the door, he turned to find Chen Haodong still lingering. Two ingots at once meant another favor coming. “Something else?”
[ZH] 陈浩东走进后，差役依旧将门关上。
[EN] “Sharp eyes, Brother!” Chen Haodong flattered. “That girl locked next door is a fierce one—she stabbed Haoyu’s arm. Think I could slip in a minute to talk some sense into her?”
[ZH] 还未来得及寻找春金兰，她已迫不及待站在他面前：“大公子可来了，刚才隔着门听到外面的动静，猜到大公子一准儿会来看我。”
[EN] The constable hesitated. “You’re not her kin, so it’s against the rules... tell you what, for the silver, I’ll look the other way. Fifteen minutes max.”
[ZH] 陈浩东装作一本正经的样子，他一直在春金兰面前都是这么装的。
[EN] “Much obliged, Brother.”
[ZH] 春金兰对男人恨之入骨，在青楼不愿接客，要不是春金沫罩着，估计在春香楼也待不下去。对付她只需装成正人君子，才能获得她的信任。
[EN] The padlock clicked open, the door swung wide, and Chen Haodong stepped inside before the constable shut it behind him.
[ZH] 陈浩东故意向后退出一步：“我一向见不得他人受苦，来看我二弟，也顺便看看金兰姑娘。”
[EN] Chun Jinlan was already waiting right inside the doorway. “You came, Eldest Young Master! Heard the noise next door and knew you’d drop in to see me.”
[ZH] 对方特意保持距离，春金兰更是从心底生出敬意：“金兰谢过大公子。”
[EN] Chen Haodong put on his straitlaced gentleman act—the only mask that worked on a woman who hated men so fiercely she refused clients at the brothel and only survived under Chun Jinmo’s wing. Stepping back a polite pace, he said, “I can’t stand seeing anyone suffer. Came to check on Second Brother, so I stopped in to see you too, Miss Jinlan.”
[ZH] 陈浩东继续美化自己：“我二弟做下错事，我当大哥的痛心不已，希望帮他找到一个改过自新的机会，才到处打听你的下落，苍天有眼，终于让我找到。我找到你，就是想让浩宇把你放在他身边，你既有个好去处，他也可弥补过错。”
[EN] That deliberate distance filled her with respect. “Thank you, Eldest Young Master.”
[ZH] 春金兰说：“大公子的好心我都明白。”
[EN] He piled on the noble act. “My brother’s wrongdoing broke my heart. Hoping to give him a chance to make amends, I tracked you down everywhere until Heaven let me find you—all so Haoyu would take you in, giving you a secure home and letting him right his wrong.”
[ZH] “你不明白，今日我才意识到我错了。本以为你来闹上一闹，家人便可大事化小，劝说浩宇纳了你，谁知你和浩宇被关起来，都是我害得你们受罪。”
[EN] “I know your good intentions, Eldest Young Master.”
[ZH] “大公子不必自责，不是你的错，你也是好心。”
[EN] “No—today I realized I blundered. I thought making a scene would get the elders to smooth things over and make Haoyu take you in. Instead both of you got locked up here. It’s my fault you’re suffering.”
[ZH] 陈浩东扫视一眼屋子，跟隔壁布置得一模一样，炕上只有草席，连个盖的都没有，他继续以情动人：“本来我是一个人前来，偷偷给你带了床薄被，可在司理院门口碰到浩宇身边的下人，被他一股脑搬到隔壁……”
[EN] “Don’t blame yourself—you meant well.”
[ZH] 陈浩东脱下身上的外衫，走到炕边放下：“还好天儿越来越热，晚上无需盖得太厚，我的衫子送你，睡觉时盖上肚子，省得着凉。”
[EN] Glancing around the twin cell—bare straw mat on the kang, not even a thin blanket—he cranked up the sympathy. “I came alone and smuggled a thin quilt for you, only Haoyu’s servant caught me at the gate and hauled everything into his room...” Shrugging off his own outer robe, he laid it on the kang. “Good thing the weather’s warming up. Take my robe to cover your stomach tonight so you don’t catch a chill.”
[ZH] 炕上的衫子令春金兰感动不已，她自从被卖到青楼，碰到的男人除了欺负她还是欺负她，第一次有男人见她毫不动色，还如此关切，她感动地跪下：“大公子是好人，既怜惜我，不如让我跟了你？”
[EN] Staring at the robe, Chun Jinlan’s eyes welled up. Ever since being sold to a brothel, every man she met had only used and abused her; never once had a man looked at her without lust and treated her with such kindness. Dropping to her knees, she pleaded, “You’re such a good man, Eldest Young Master! Since you pity me, why not let me belong to you instead?”
[ZH] 陈浩东这才意识到他的戏演得有些过，赶紧拒绝道：“万万不可！不是我嫌弃你，说到底是浩宇害了你，错误需他承担，再说我娘子也不允许我纳妾。”
[EN] Realizing he had laid the act on too thick, Chen Haodong recoiled. “Out of the question! Not that I look down on you, yet Haoyu’s the one who wronged you and must answer for it—besides, my wife would never allow a concubine.”
[ZH] 春金兰流下伤心的眼泪：“为何我总是遇不到好男人？好不容易遇见还成了亲，还对家里的娘子死心塌地。”
[EN] Tears spilled down her cheeks. “Why can’t I ever meet a good man? The one time I do, he’s already married and devoted to his wife!”
[ZH] 一刻钟的时间太短，情已扇够，是该说重点的时候：“你还是起来吧，我有要事与你说。”
[EN] With fifteen minutes ticking down and her emotions primed, it was time to plant the hook. “Get up—I’ve got something crucial to tell you.”
[ZH] 春金兰站起来，擦着眼角的泪水：“大公子请讲。”
[EN] Wiping her eyes, she rose. “Go ahead, Eldest Young Master.”
[ZH] “浩宇的娘子心胸狭窄，容不下他身边有妾室，我估计她今晚会找到疤子和浩宇的狐朋狗友，用钱财买通他们，让他们明日做假证。”
[EN] “Haoyu’s wife is jealous and won’t tolerate a concubine. My bet is she’s out tonight buying off Scarface and Haoyu’s cronies to give false testimony tomorrow.”
[ZH] “我该如何是好？”
[EN] “What do I do?”
[ZH] “实在不行，只能以性命威胁。”
[EN] “If all else fails, threaten to take your own life.”
[ZH] “我还不想死。”
[EN] “I don’t want to die!”
[ZH] “没有让你真死，吓唬吓唬他们而已。仪王心善，见你连性命都不顾，自然不会轻易相信证人的话，必然会彻查此案，还你一个公道，然后让浩宇纳了你。有仪王出面为你做主，没有人敢反对。”
[EN] “Nobody’s asking you to die—just scare them! Prince Yi is soft-hearted. Seeing you put your life on the line, he won’t swallow those witnesses’ lies; he’ll dig to the bottom, get you justice, and force Haoyu to take you in. Once Prince Yi backs you, nobody dares object.”
[ZH] 春金兰疑惑道：“仪王真的会替我做主？”
[EN] Doubt flickered in her eyes. “Will Prince Yi really stand up for me?”
[ZH] “仪王最是体察民情，善于为百姓考虑。懒人村的事你可听说过？”
[EN] “He cares about commoners more than anyone—heard about Lazybones Village?”
[ZH] “听说了。”
[EN] “I did.”
[ZH] “那还有什么可怀疑的？人家是贵人，屈尊来陈家办案，一点儿怨言都没有，你也是亲眼见过。再说温都头武功了得，绝对会在关键时刻救下你，你尽管假戏真做，才能让人信以为真。”
[EN] “Then what’s to doubt? A royal lowering himself to visit Chen Manor without a single complaint—you saw it with your own eyes! Plus Chief Constable Wen is a top martial artist; he’ll stop you at the last second for sure. You just have to make the act look real.”
[ZH] 春金兰狠狠心说：“好，我听大公子的。只是我的匕首被陈浩宇的娘子抢走……”
[EN] Hardening her resolve, Chun Jinlan nodded. “All right, I’ll do it. Only Chen Haoyu’s wife took my dagger...”
[ZH] 陈浩东取出提前藏在身上的一把匕首：“我已为你准备好，希望明日之后你能成为陈家的一员，为自己谋个好去处，也算给浩宇一次改邪归正的机会。”
[EN] Chen Haodong pulled a hidden blade from his clothes. “Already brought you a spare. Here’s hoping tomorrow makes you one of the Chens—a good home for you, and a chance for Haoyu to straighten up.”
[ZH] 春金兰接过匕首，跟之前的那把一模一样，她像上次一样藏在袖中。
[EN] Taking the twin dagger, she tucked it into her sleeve just like the first one.
[ZH] 蛊惑成功，陈浩东甚为满意，是该离开的时候，还需叮嘱几句：“若有人问起我今日见你说些什么，你只说我是劝你放过浩宇和陈家，其他一概不能说，我本为你好，只怕别人多心。”
[EN] Satisfied she was hooked, he added one last warning before leaving. “If anyone asks why I saw you tonight, just say I came urging you to drop the charges against Haoyu and the Chens. Not another word—I mean well, yet people love to gossip.”
[ZH] 春金兰点点头：“大公子放心，你是好人，我绝不会连累你。”
[EN] “Don’t worry, Eldest Young Master. You’re a good man—I’d never drag you down.”
[ZH] 陈浩东瞅向炕上的衫子，他再傻也知不能留下把柄，边靠近土坑，边说：“我突然觉得把我的衫子放在这里十分不妥，司理院的人个个精明，怕他们怀疑我们两个之间不清不楚，反而对你不利，我看我还是把它拿回去。”
[EN] His eyes fell on the outer robe on the kang. Foolish to leave physical proof behind. Walking over, he scooped it up. “Actually, leaving my robe here is a bad idea—these Judicial Office men are sharp, and if they suspect anything improper between us, it’ll hurt your case. Better I take it back.”
[ZH] 陈浩东已将外衫披在身上，春金兰失望之余只是认为他担心个人名节而已，叹口气：“唉，大公子想得周到，还是拿回去得好，你有这份心意我已心满意足。”
[EN] Slipping it back on, he left Chun Jinlan disappointed yet admiring his caution. “You think of everything, Eldest Young Master. Knowing you care is enough for me.”
[ZH] 陈浩东整理好衣装，心中嘲笑春金兰实在好哄。一切已安排妥当，他可高枕无忧。
[EN] Smoothing his sleeves, he sneered inside at how easy she was to play. Everything was in place.
[ZH] 陈浩东推开门，离开简陋阴暗之地。待差役将门锁好后，他又拿出一锭银子，塞到差役手中：“差役大哥，那女的实在不好劝说，我白白进去一趟，您就当今晚的事没发生过，别在他人面前提起，省得给仪王平添烦恼。”
[EN] Stepping back out into the yard while the constable relocked the door, Chen Haodong handed over one final silver ingot. “Stubborn woman—wasted my breath trying to talk her round. Pretend tonight never happened, Brother, and don’t breathe a word to anyone lest it bother Prince Yi.”
[ZH] 差役摸着银子，满口应下：“不是什么大事，提它作甚？您尽管把心放在肚子里。”
[EN] Pocketing the silver, the constable beamed. “Not worth mentioning—set your mind at ease!”
[ZH] 陈浩东这才大步流星离开司理院。
[EN] Chen Haodong strode out of the Judicial Office without a care in the world.

#CH 281
[ZH] 第二天，温都头差人将证人找来带到司理院。一切就绪后，申简辰继昨日之后再次审理陈浩宇的案子。
[ZH] 陈家来了陈江林和陈浩东二人，他们坐在差役身后专为旁听之人准备的位置。
[ZH] 千里寻未出现，申简辰觉得甚好，省得她听到更多细节心里难过。
[ZH] 陈浩宇与春金兰被差役带进来，乖乖跪在地上。
[ZH] 温信大声说：“将证人带进来！”
[ZH] 只见两名差役带着三名证人走进来，有疤子，还有两名与陈浩宇一起在捶丸场和西湖烤过肉的公子。
[ZH] 证人都跪下，一起说：“拜见大人！”
[ZH] 申简辰先问两位公子：“去年春天可有一日你们将陈浩宇从乡下叫回城里？”
[ZH] 曾在捶丸场调戏过红豆的炙肉男先开口：“记得记得，那天我生日，三月初三，想把朋友们聚到一起。听说陈公子去了乡下的宅子，没他不热闹，便派人将他从乡下叫回来。”
[ZH] 申简辰继续问：“他跟你们待了一晚上？”
[ZH] 另一位公子说：“回大人，那晚我们在勾栏瓦肆玩了一个通宵，陈公子一直与我们在一起玩儿到天亮，还在瓦子里睡了一会儿才离开。”
[ZH] 两位公子的证词显然对陈浩宇有利，春金兰一心认为是女词人买通证人，急切地说：“大人，您不能听他们一面之词，他们一定是拿了陈浩宇的银子，才向着他说话！”
[EN] The next morning, Chief Constable Wen brought in the witnesses, and Shen Jianchen reopened Chen Haoyu’s hearing.
[ZH] 陈浩宇强辩道：“我昨晚被关着，哪有机会去找他们？”
[EN] Chen Jianglin and Chen Haodong attended from the spectators’ seats behind the constables. Qianli Xun stayed away—just as well, Shen Jianchen thought, sparing her the ugly details.
[ZH] 春金兰说：“你娘子有的是时间和机会！难道她不会帮你收买人心？”
[EN] Constables escorted Chen Haoyu and Chun Jinlan in to kneel.
[ZH] 有人诋毁千里寻，申简辰决不允许，他将惊堂木一拍“啪！”：“若再吵闹下去，一人二十大板！”
[EN] “Bring in the witnesses!” Wen Xin called.
[ZH] 春金兰吓得不敢再言语。
[EN] Two constables led three men forward: Scarface, plus two young masters who had grilled meat by West Lake and played chuiwan with Chen Haoyu. All three knelt. “Greetings, Your Lordship!”
[ZH] 申简辰又问疤子：“你是疤子？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen questioned the two young masters first. “One day last spring, did you send for Chen Haoyu to return from the countryside?”
[ZH] 疤子回道：“小的是。”
[EN] The barbecue enthusiast who had once flirted with Hongdou spoke up. “Remember it clear as day! My birthday, the third day of the third month. Wanted the gang together, heard Young Master Chen was out at his country house, and figured it wouldn’t be lively without him—so I sent someone to fetch him back.”
[ZH] “经常跟着陈浩宇混？”
[EN] “Did he stay with you all night?”
[ZH] “以前是，现在陈公子不愿带我们，没再跟他混。”
[EN] The second young master answered. “Yes, Your Lordship! We partied all night in the entertainment quarters—Young Master Chen stayed with us till dawn and even napped there before heading off.”
[ZH] “可认得跪着的女子？”
[EN] With their testimony favoring Chen Haoyu, Chun Jinlan was sure the poetess had bought them off and burst out, “Don’t believe them, Your Lordship! They must’ve taken Chen Haoyu’s silver to lie for him!”
[ZH] 疤子早已看到春金兰，老实地点点头：“认得，还是我把她卖到扬州青楼。”
[EN] “I was locked up all night!” Chen Haoyu shot back. “When did I get a chance to see them?”
[ZH] 疤子进入堂内的一刻，春金兰就充满仇恨，因大老爷在，暂时忍了。此时疤子承认，她再也忍不下去，正好疤子跪在她身旁，冲过去用拳头砸在疤子的身上。
[EN] “Your wife had plenty of time and chances! Wouldn’t she buy them off for you?”
[ZH] “你害我好惨！我要杀了你！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen would not let anyone smear Qianli Xun. Crack! His gavel slammed the desk. “Another outburst and you each get twenty strokes of the paddle!”
[ZH] 疤子不敢动手，直往一旁躲，还是被她打在身上。那女人劲儿不大，不是很疼，可春金兰跟疯了一样，竟然把头伸过来要咬他，还好上来两名差役将她拉开。
[EN] Chun Jinlan clammed up in fright.
[ZH] 差役把春金兰按在地上。
[EN] Shen Jianchen turned to Scarface. “You’re Scarface?”
[ZH] 申简辰说：“春金兰，你再胡闹，我就让人把你绑起来。”
[EN] “Yes, Your Lordship.”
[ZH] 春金兰忍着怒气不敢再闹腾，两名差役才又回到原位站好。
[EN] “Used to run with Chen Haoyu?”
[ZH] 申简辰问道：“疤子，你为何要把她卖到扬州青楼？”
[EN] “Used to. Young Master Chen won’t hang with us anymore.”
[ZH] 疤子开始胡说：“陈公子见她美貌，没忍住把她给糟蹋了，可她脾气不好，实在不好玩儿，陈公子极其嫌弃，将她打晕，让我拉到扬州给卖了。”
[EN] “Recognize the woman kneeling here?”
[ZH] 申简辰又问：“可是去年三月初三那晚？”
[EN] Scarface nodded straightaway. “Sure do—I’m the one who sold her to the Yangzhou brothel.”
[ZH] 疤子肯定道：“正是，我清楚地记得，陈公子说三月初三带我们去乡下放风筝。我们当时正在放风筝，发现她在河边洗衣裳，陈公子吩咐哥几个悄悄把她抓进宅子里。”
[EN] Seething since Scarface walked in, Chun Jinlan snapped at his admission. Since he knelt right beside her, she threw herself sideways and rained fists on him. “You ruined my life! I’ll kill you!”
[ZH] 春金兰虽恨疤子，可他的证词显然对她有利，怒气便消了许多。
[EN] Not daring to hit back, Scarface dodged and took the weak blows—until she lunged to sink her teeth into him and two constables hauled her off and pinned her down.
[ZH] 又换陈浩宇着急，他冲着疤子说：“本公子平日待你不薄，你怎么满嘴谎言？明明那日我回城里，叮嘱你们几个帮我看着她，等我第二日回来再……，该不会是你做下龌龊事，又把人给卖了，赖到我身上？”
[EN] “One more stunt, Chun Jinlan, and you go back in ropes,” Shen Jianchen warned. She bit back her rage, and the constables stepped back.
[ZH] 疤子继续胡诌：“陈公子是待我不薄，我也记着您的好，可一码归一码，公堂之上我不能欺瞒大人，需照实说。再说陈公子您抢来的人，我怎么敢动？”
[EN] “Scarface, why did you sell her to a Yangzhou brothel?”
[ZH] 春金兰趁机说：“大人，疤子亲自将我卖到扬州青楼，他的证词最可信。”
[EN] Scarface launched right into his lie. “Young Master Chen saw how pretty she was, couldn’t hold back, and forced himself on her. Only she had a nasty temper and wasn’t any fun, so he got disgusted, knocked her out, and told me to haul her to Yangzhou and sell her.”
[ZH] 炙肉男疑惑道：“不对啊，明明那晚陈公子跟我们在一起，难道他有分身术？”
[EN] “Was that the night of the third day of the third month last year?”
[ZH] 三个证人，两种说辞，其中肯定有人做了假证。
[EN] “Sure was! Remember clear as day—Young Master Chen took us out to fly kites that day, spotted her washing clothes by the creek, and had the boys snatch her into the country house.”
[ZH] 申简辰仔细观察，认真思索，一个人说谎往往是为了对自己有利的目的。
[EN] Much as she despised Scarface, his words backed her up, cooling her anger.
[ZH] 他认为两位公虽不着调，证词却十分可信，只因此事与二人没有利益牵扯，他们没有必要编谎言欺骗人。而疤子与此事息息相关，还是他亲自赶马车将春金兰卖到扬州，说不定真如陈浩宇所说，那晚是疤子做下龌龊事赖到陈浩宇身上。
[EN] Now Chen Haoyu panicked and snapped at him, “I never treated you shabby—why spout lies?! That day when I went back to town, I told you boys to watch her till I returned the next day! Don’t tell me you did the dirty deed yourself, sold her off, and pinned it on me?!”
[ZH] “疤子，你刚才的证词中提到‘我们’看来跟着陈浩宇的还有其他人？”
[EN] Scarface kept spinning. “You treated me decent, Young Master Chen, and I haven’t forgotten—yet facts are facts, and I can’t lie in court! Besides, how would I dare touch a girl you snatched?”
[ZH] 司理参军心思细腻，疤子提醒自己说话需谨慎：“是有其他人，不过陈公子回城后，其他人都回家了，只留我一人在宅子里。”
[EN] Chun Jinlan jumped in. “He sold me to Yangzhou with his own hands, Your Lordship—his word counts most!”
[ZH] “这么说，你也承认陈浩宇被人叫回城里？”
[EN] The barbecue young master scratched his head. “Hold on—Young Master Chen was with us all night! Can he split himself in two?”
[ZH] “承认，当然承认，只是陈公子惦记被抓的姑娘，大半夜又回来，办完事，才吩咐我把人给卖了。”
[EN] Three witnesses, two clashing stories—somebody was lying.
[ZH] 申简辰又转向陈浩宇：“陈浩宇，乡下的宅子里可还有下人？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen weighed their motives. Flighty as the two young masters were, they had no stake in the case and zero reason to perjure themselves. Scarface, on the other hand, was neck-deep in it and had driven the girl to Yangzhou himself—maybe he really had done the deed and blamed Haoyu.
[ZH] 仪王的问题吓得陈浩东手中的茶盏晃了一下，茶水溅到衣衫上。他赶紧将茶盏放好，抖了抖衣衫，掩饰好情绪，尴尬地向一旁的大伯笑笑。心想，那日天够晚，宅子里的下人也都被调走，唯一留下的管家也睡得跟个死猪一样，他做下的事除了疤子，应该无人知晓。
[EN] “Scarface, you said ‘we’ earlier. So other men came with Chen Haoyu?”
[ZH] 陈江林一直认真听证词，关键还在疤子，若有人能证明疤子的话是假，儿子便可无事，仪王的问题也正问到他的心里。
[EN] Wary of the sharp investigator, Scarface chose his words with care. “Yes, others came, yet after Young Master Chen returned to town, the rest went home—leaving just me at the house.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇回道：“陈家所有在外的宅子都有下人，乡下的宅子也不例外。”
[EN] “So you admit Chen Haoyu got called back to town?”
[ZH] 申简辰对众人说：“三位证人的证词出现矛盾，今日还无法定案，先审到这里，待司理院找到相关证据后再继续审理。”
[EN] “Sure do—only Young Master Chen couldn’t stop thinking about the girl, came back in the dead of night, finished his business, and ordered me to sell her.”
[ZH] 春金兰摸摸藏在袖子里的匕首，今日状况不明，还不到启用它的时候。
[EN] Shen Jianchen shifted to Chen Haoyu. “Were there any servants at that country house?”
[ZH] 差役将陈浩宇和春金兰带下去后，三位证人也都离去。
[EN] The question jolted Chen Haodong’s hand; tea sloshed from his bowl onto his sleeve. Setting the bowl down quick, he brushed his robe and flashed his uncle an awkward smile. *Relax—the servants had all been pulled away that night, and the one caretaker left slept like a dead pig. Nobody knows except Scarface.*
[ZH] 陈江林与陈浩东起身拜别仪王。
[EN] Chen Jianglin listened with bated breath; if someone could punch a hole in Scarface’s story, his son walked free.
[ZH] 陈江林说：“辛苦仪王殿下为我这不成器的儿子操心，若他真的做下错事，任凭仪王处置，我绝不姑息。”
[EN] “Every outside Chen residence has servants,” Chen Haoyu replied. “That country house included.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇如何申简辰不会放在心上，他只为千里寻担忧，若陈浩宇真的有事，她岂不是刚嫁人就得守寡？为了她好，他也希望陈浩宇无事。
[EN] Shen Jianchen addressed the hall. “With conflicting witness accounts, no verdict can be reached today. Adjourned until the Judicial Office gathers further proof.”
[ZH] “常言道知子莫若父，我相信陈老爷心中对此事已有定论，只是还需寻找有力的证据。”
[EN] Chun Jinlan felt the hidden dagger in her sleeve—still too unclear to play her trump card.
[ZH] 陈江林佩服仪王，年纪轻轻竟然能看透他的心思：“殿下所言极是。”
[EN] Once constables led Chen Haoyu and Chun Jinlan away and the witnesses left, Chen Jianglin and Chen Haodong rose to bow.
[ZH] 申简辰瞧向身边的陈浩东，他恭恭敬敬，一言不发，眼神中却透出紧张的情绪来，不如探探虚实：“陈大公子可了解陈浩宇？”
[EN] “Thank you for taking trouble over my wayward son, Your Highness,” Chen Jianglin said. “If he truly committed this crime, punish him by law—I will not shield him.”
[ZH] 仪王冷不丁发问，搞得陈浩东紧张不已：“浩宇是我堂弟，我自然对他十分了解。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen cared nothing for Chen Haoyu himself, only for Qianli Xun—if Haoyu swung for this, she’d be widowed right after her wedding. For her sake, he hoped Haoyu was clean.
[ZH] 申简辰继续追问：“既然了解，你认为哪个证词是假？”
[EN] “No one knows a son like his father,” Shen Jianchen said. “I believe Master Chen already knows the truth in his heart; we just need solid proof.”
[ZH] 陈浩东装作毫不犹豫的样子：“仪王既然给小人机会，小人斗胆说一说，我与浩宇手足情深，自然向着他，满心希望是疤子作假证，害我二弟。”
[EN] Impressed by the young prince’s insight, Chen Jianglin bowed. “Your Highness speaks true.”
[ZH] 申简辰故意顺着对方：“很好，既然陈大公子如此希望，我会差人好好找出证据证明疤子是作假证。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen turned his eyes on Chen Haodong—standing there pious and silent, yet tension leaked from his gaze. Time to poke him. “Does Eldest Young Master Chen know Chen Haoyu well?”
[ZH] 陈浩东更加紧张：“仪王不必将小人的话当回事，小人所说只是亲情使然。”
[EN] Caught off guard, Chen Haodong stiffened. “Haoyu is my cousin—of course I know him well.”
[ZH] “怎么？你不希望我找到证据证明疤子的证词是假？”
[EN] “Then whose testimony do you think is false?”
[ZH] 仪王的反问更让陈浩东不知所措：“小人不是这个意思……”
[EN] Chen Haodong put on an earnest front. “Since Your Highness asks, I’ll speak bold: Haoyu and I are like brothers, so of course I side with him and hope with all my heart that Scarface is lying to frame him.”
[ZH] “你不必紧张，我只是随便问问。”申简辰瞧向陈江林，“陈老爷可听过一个词：祸起萧墙？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen played right along. “Splendid! Since you hope for that so much, I’ll have my men dig up proof that Scarface perjured himself.”
[ZH] 陈江林一时不明白，还是回道：“听过。”
[EN] Panic tightened Chen Haodong’s throat. “Pay no mind to my words, Your Highness—I only spoke out of family feeling.”
[ZH] “这个词不仅适用于皇家，普通百姓家也适用，尤其像陈家这种大户人家。行了，你们回去吧，本王还要继续寻找证据。”
[EN] “What, don’t you want me to prove Scarface lied?”
[ZH] 仪王明确发话，二人躬起身，后退几步后才离开。
[EN] Flustered, Chen Haodong stammered, “That isn’t what I meant...”
[ZH] 走出司理院的途中，陈江林一直思忖，仪王为何要提“祸起萧墙”四个字？还说也适用陈家？难道他想提醒什么？
[EN] “Relax—just asking.” Shen Jianchen looked back at Chen Jianglin. “Master Chen, have you heard the saying ‘calamity starts inside one’s own walls’?”
[ZH] 直到与浩东一起坐上马车，看到这位大侄子忧心忡忡，心中自问：“难道仪王的意思浩宇之事与浩东有关？”
[EN] Baffled, Chen Jianglin answered, “I have.”
[ZH] 很快陈江林否认了他的看法，怎么可能？八竿子打不着的事。他们兄弟平日看着十分和谐，况且浩东没有任何理由给浩宇制造麻烦。
[EN] “It doesn’t apply just to imperial houses—ordinary households too, most of all wealthy clans like the Chens. Run along now; I still have evidence to hunt down.”
[ZH] 接着又觉不对，还是有理由，陈家产业的继承人要在他们兄弟中产生。
[EN] Dismissed, both men bowed and backed out of the hall.
[ZH] 又一想，浩飞一心考功名，对继承产业不感兴趣。浩宇破事一大堆，以前吊儿郎当，族中长辈也瞧不上。前日陈陆来府中，当着他的面将浩宇和他娘子数落了一番，如此看来大家根本不可能选浩宇。
[EN] All the way out, Chen Jianglin chewed over those words. *Calamity inside one’s own walls—applicable to the Chens?* Climbing into the carriage beside his anxious nephew, a wild thought flashed across his mind: *Was Prince Yi hinting Haodong had a hand in Haoyu’s mess?*
[ZH] 陈家产业的继承人只能是浩东，连陈江林自己也这么认为，浩东应该能感受到，如此好的形势，他怎么可能做出伤害浩宇之事？
[EN] He brushed it off almost at once. Absurd! The cousins got along fine, and why on earth would Haodong sabotage Haoyu? Well—for the business inheritance, maybe. Yet Haofei cared only for imperial exams, and Haoyu’s wild past had already sunk him with the elders—just look at how Chen Lu chewed out Haoyu and his wife the other day. Haodong already had the inheritance locked up, and he knew it. Why risk hurting Haoyu?
[ZH] 最终陈江林得出结论：贵人有时说话也没多大意思，听听得了，千万不能太当真。
[EN] In the end Chen Jianglin concluded royals just tossed out cryptic remarks for show—best not to overthink it.

#CH 282
[ZH] 司理院内，申简辰向温信与韩谷安排任务。
[ZH] “温都头带人去趟陈家在乡下的宅子，到关押春金兰的屋子仔细查探，也许能找出新的证据。韩师爷带几名差役找一找与疤子在一起混的人，问问他们当日的情况。”
[ZH] 二人齐声回道：“ 谨遵仪王吩咐！”便各自带几名差役前去忙活。
[ZH] 昨晚帮陈浩东开门的差役进来，手里拿个小布包，小心翼翼放在仪王面前的桌上。
[ZH] “王爷，您真神了，陈家大公子果真昨晚来司理院，这是他给我的银子，共四两，小的一两都没贪。”
[ZH] 申简辰拿起布包，走到差役跟前，塞进他怀里：“既然是陈家大公子送你的，都拿着吧。”
[ZH] 差役欣喜之余又不大相信，认为仪王试探他：“不不不！我是司理院的差役，怎能随意被人贿赂？”
[ZH] 申简辰笑道：“放心拿着，算是我奖赏你的。”
[ZH] 差役终于放心，将银子接在手里，继续赞美：“王爷，您该不会有未卜先知的能力？昨晚，陈家大公子果真见了春金兰。”
[ZH] “他对春金兰说了什么？”
[ZH] “说话声音小，门又厚，听得不大清楚，大略听出春金兰应该是陈家大公子找来的，她还跪下要求给陈家大公子做妾室。”
[ZH] 申简辰迅速做出判断，春金兰的事既与陈浩东有关，疤子多半也被他收买，要不怎么一直跟着陈浩宇混的人说出的证词一点儿没向着他？看来关键人物还在陈浩东，需派人悄悄跟踪他，希望能找到些线索。
[ZH] 为了避免被对方发现，盯梢的人必须轻功好。温都头功夫不错，轻功差了许多。白风最合适，可已安排他去竹里村忙活建竹屋的事，申简辰决定亲自出马。
[ZH] “你去忙吧。”
[ZH] 差役揣好银子行完礼离开。
[ZH] 申简辰走出大堂，回到居住的院子，换上便装，总觉得衣裳太过显眼，决定找家裁缝铺临时买件普通衣衫。
[ZH] 他独自一人出了司理院，步行来到热闹的街市，随便找一家普通的裁缝铺，选来选去挑一件普通的玄色衣衫，在更衣室换了。布料虽普通，穿在身上挺舒服，申简辰十分满意。
[ZH] 当他从更衣室走出时，裁缝铺的伙计夸道：“公子是天生的衣架子，普通的衣衫穿在您身上一下子高档许多。”
[ZH] 申简辰笑了笑，问道：“能不能把做这件衣衫的料子给我裁上一块？”
[ZH] “巧了，做衣衫时多出许多边角料，我给您挑一块合适的。”伙计进到里屋，很快出来，“客官，您瞧，这块儿怎么样？”
[ZH] 申简辰很满意，接过塞进怀里，顺便拿出银两：“再麻烦裁缝铺帮我把换下的衣衫送到司理院。”
[ZH] 伙计接过银两，既疑惑又不敢相信：“您该不会是参军大人吧？”
[EN] Back inside the Judicial Office, Shen Jianchen issued his orders. “Chief Constable Wen, take men out to the Chens’ country residence and comb the room where Chun Jinlan was held—we might turn up fresh evidence. Clerk Han, take constables and track down Scarface’s buddies to ask what happened that day.”
[ZH] 申简辰没有回答，转身离开裁缝铺。
[EN] “At your command, Your Highness!” Both bowed and led their squads out.
[ZH] 伙计手拿银两自语道：“气质很像，只是太年轻。买这么普通的衣衫，应该不是参军大人。”
[EN] Just then the constable who had let Chen Haodong in the night before stepped up and laid a small cloth packet on the prince’s desk. “You’re a prophet, Your Highness! Eldest Young Master Chen really did show up last night—here are the four taels of silver he slipped me, didn’t pocket a single coin.”
[ZH] 申简辰租辆马车，先来到陈家绸缎庄外躲着瞧了一会儿，没见到陈浩东的影子。
[EN] Shen Jianchen picked up the packet and pressed it straight back into the constable’s chest. “Since Eldest Young Master Chen gave it to you, keep it.”
[ZH] 一位伙计刚好从里面出来，他迎上去打探道：“小哥，不知陈家大公子今日可在绸缎庄？”
[EN] Startled, thinking it a trap, the constable waved both hands. “No, no, no! A Judicial Office constable can’t take bribes!”
[ZH] 伙计将申简辰上下瞧了瞧：“你是谁？为何问大公子？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen smiled. “Take it—consider it my reward.”
[ZH] 申简辰瞎编道：“我是司理院的差役，参军大人让我来请陈大公子前去问话。”
[EN] Relieved, the constable tucked the silver away, beaming. “Can Your Highness really tell the future? Eldest Young Master Chen actually went in to see Chun Jinlan too!”
[ZH] 伙计立刻变得恭敬起来：“大公子一早没来，听说是去了司理院。”
[EN] “What did he say to her?”
[ZH] 申简辰说：“司理院审完案子他便离开了，难道没来这里？”
[EN] “Voices were low and the door’s thick, so I only caught bits—sounded like Eldest Young Master Chen was the one who fetched her to Lin’an, and she even knelt begging to be his concubine.”
[ZH] 伙计回道：“没来，不过我听说大公子最近常在茶庄，您要不去那里找他？”
[EN] Everything clicked into place. If Chen Haodong brought Chun Jinlan in, ten to one he bought Scarface too—why else would Haoyu’s own lackey knife him in court? Chen Haodong was the key; somebody had to tail him.
[ZH] 申简辰道声“多谢”，又乘坐租来的马车赶去陈家茶庄。
[EN] Tailing him undetected took real light-foot skill. Wen Xin could fight yet lacked agility, and Bai Feng was out in Zhuli Village building the cottage. Shen Jianchen decided to go himself.
[ZH] 在茶庄外守候多时，终于见陈浩东从茶庄走出，上了一辆陈家的马车。申简辰正要上车尾随，瞧见从陈家茶肆里走出一位公子，身形十分熟悉。
[EN] Dismissing the constable, he changed into plainclothes in his quarters, still found them too fine, and walked into the bustling market to pick out an ordinary dark-colored robe at a modest tailor shop. Changing inside the fitting room, he liked the simple, comfortable fit.
[ZH] “原来你也怀疑陈浩东，我们两个竟然想到一处。”
[EN] Stepping out, the shop clerk beamed. “A natural clothes-hanger, Young Master—even plain cloth looks high-class on you!”
[ZH] 他迅速上前堵在那位公子面前，小声说：“打扮得如此招摇，我一眼认出，还想瞒过陈浩东？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen smiled. “Got a scrap left over from this same fabric?”
[ZH] 昨晚听了韦嬷嬷所说，千里寻开始怀疑陈浩东，也想出跟踪的法子。她做过两身男装，装扮成公子的模样跟踪对方，应该不会被人轻易认出。
[EN] “Lucky break—plenty of trimmings! Let me grab a piece.” Fetching a black strip from the back room, the clerk handed it over. Pleased, Shen Jianchen tucked it inside his robe, paid in silver, and added, “Have your shop deliver my changed robes to the Judicial Office.”
[ZH] 申简辰却一眼瞧出，千里寻不满的同时想打击对方两句：“你以为穿上一身普通衣衫，别人就认不出你是仪王？”
[EN] Staring at the silver, the clerk gaped. “Wait—aren’t you the Judicial Investigator?”
[ZH] 时间不允许二人继续打口水仗，申简辰建议：“既然目的相同，不如跟我一起上马车？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen walked out without answering. The clerk muttered to himself, “Same bearing, just way too young—and buying cheap cloth like that? Couldn’t be him.”
[ZH] 千里寻顾不上许多，很快答应：“还愣着干什么？再不走可就追不上。”
[EN] Hiring a carriage, Shen Jianchen staked out the Chen silk shop first, saw no sign of Chen Haodong, and collared a clerk stepping out. “Excuse me, is Eldest Young Master Chen in today?”
[ZH] 她走过去，第一个上了马车。
[EN] The clerk eyed him up and down. “Who’s asking?”
[ZH] 申简辰会心一笑，跟在千里寻后面也上了马车，向车夫叮嘱道：“跟着前面那辆陈家的马车。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen bluffed on the spot. “Judicial Office runner—the Judicial Investigator wants Eldest Young Master for questioning.”
[ZH] 车夫拿人钱财，替人办事，自然不会多问一个字，尽心尽力跟上，同时保持安全的距离。
[EN] The clerk turned polite at once. “He didn’t come in this morning—heard he went to the Judicial Office.”
[ZH] 自穿越而来，与千里寻第一次乘坐马车，还离得这么近。要搁以前，申简辰绝对会抓起千里寻的手对她说情话。
[EN] “He left after the hearing. Didn’t come here?”
[ZH] 现在两人各自步入婚姻，他还没有遵守“守身如玉”的承诺，已没资格再对她说任何情话。况且他一进马车，她有意调整身姿，与他保持安全的距离。
[EN] “No, sir, though he’s been spending most days at the tea shop lately. Try there.”
[ZH] 申简辰只能礼貌地问：“你为何会跟踪陈浩东？”
[EN] Thanking him, Shen Jianchen rode over to the Chen tea shop. After a long wait, Chen Haodong finally stepped out and climbed into a Chen carriage. Just as Shen Jianchen moved to board his hired rig, a familiar figure in young-master attire strolled out of the adjacent tea stall.
[ZH] 两人目的一致，仪王又负责审理案子，根本没有必要隐瞒。
[EN] *So you suspect Chen Haodong too—great minds think alike.*
[ZH] “韦嬷嬷在永泰茶楼碰见他与春金兰一起喝茶，猜他们之间有关系，我便想到跟踪的法子，希望能找到些蛛丝马迹。”
[EN] Stepping right into her path, he murmured, “Dressed loud as a peacock—I spotted you in one glance, and you expect to fool Chen Haodong?”
[ZH] 申简辰停了一会儿，问道：“为了陈浩宇？”
[EN] After Matron Wei’s tip last night, Qianli Xun had suspected Chen Haodong too and slipped into one of her two sets of men’s robes to tail him. Stung that he blew her cover on sight, she fired back, “And you think cheap plainclothes hide the fact that you’re Prince Yi?”
[ZH] 千里寻毫不犹豫地承认：“他是我夫君，我自然是为他，为他便是为我自己。”
[EN] No time for bickering. “Same target—hop in my carriage?”
[ZH] 申简辰还是忍不住说出心里话：“我会帮你，只要你好，我才会更好。”
[EN] “What are you standing around for? We’ll lose him!” Qianli Xun marched straight over and climbed aboard first.
[ZH] 千里寻被感动，梦容恨她的话语再次回荡在耳边，她立刻让自己变得清醒：“不！你不必有意帮我，还是多想想如何好好对待梦容。”
[EN] Smiling to himself, Shen Jianchen climbed in after her and told the driver, “Tail that Chen carriage ahead.” Paid handsomely, the driver asked zero questions and kept a safe distance behind.
[ZH] 提到乔梦容，申简辰想起书卷一事：“书卷的事，我替梦容向你道歉，你别怪她。”
[EN] It was their first time riding together in a carriage since crossing over—so close. In the old days Shen Jianchen would have grabbed her hand and poured out sweet words. Now both were married, and he hadn’t even kept his vow of chastity; he had no right left. Besides, the second he climbed in, she shifted over to put polite space between them.
[ZH] “我从来没有真正责怪过她，只是时常惋惜，我与她的友情已回不到从前。”
[EN] He kept it civil. “Why are you tailing Chen Haodong?”
[ZH] “都是我的错。”
[EN] Same goal, and he was the judge on the case—no point hiding anything. “Matron Wei spotted him drinking tea with Chun Jinlan in a private booth at Yongtai Teahouse. Figured they’re in cahoots, so I came to dig up clues.”
[ZH] “不能怪你，不过是造化弄人罢了。”千里寻体会了一会儿造化弄人带来的无奈之感，唤了声，“仪王。”
[EN] After a pause, he asked, “For Chen Haoyu?”
[ZH] “为何不唤我名字？”
[EN] “He’s my husband,” she answered without blinking. “Helping him is helping myself.”
[ZH] “赵朴。”
[EN] Words slipped out before he could stop them. “I’ll help you. So long as you’re happy, I’m happy.”
[ZH] “你知道我说的不是这个名字。”
[EN] A tug of warmth hit her—then Mengrong’s bitter words echoed back, snapping her awake. “No! Don’t go out of your way for me. Put your energy into treating Mengrong right.”
[ZH] “以后我只会把你当仪王赵朴看待，那个名字我会将它藏在心底，偶尔拿出来回味一番。”
[EN] Mention of Qiao Mengrong reminded him of the pamphlets. “About those pamphlets—I apologize on Mengrong’s behalf. Don’t hold it against her.”
[ZH] 沉默片刻后，申简辰说道：“我听你的，也把‘千里寻’三个字藏在心底，偶尔拿出来品味一番。”
[EN] “I never really blamed her. I just miss what we used to have—knowing our friendship can never go back.”
[ZH] 两人会心一笑。
[EN] “My fault.”
[ZH] 千里寻轻松地问：“你怎么会怀疑陈浩东？”
[EN] “Not yours—just fate playing tricks.” Sitting with that helplessness a beat, she called out, “Prince Yi.”
[ZH] 申简辰开始自夸，不过是想用自夸活跃两人之间的气氛。
[EN] “Why not use my name?”
[ZH] “我聪明绝顶，观察能力极强，透过现象看本质，一眼看出陈浩东有问题。”
[EN] “Zhao Pu.”
[ZH] 申简辰只顾自夸，答非所问，千里寻懒得与他辩解：“不说算了！”
[EN] “You know that’s not the name I mean.”
[ZH] “告诉你也无妨，我去陈家审案时发现陈浩东与春金兰互看对方的眼神不对劲，昨晚他借口给陈浩宇送东西，还去了关春金兰的屋子，由此断定陈浩东很有问题。”
[EN] “From now on, you’re Prince Yi, Zhao Pu, to me. That other name—I’ll lock it away in my heart and look back on it now and then.”
[ZH] “陈浩东也够傻的，节骨眼儿上还敢见春金兰？难道不担心暴露？”
[EN] Silence hung a moment before he nodded. “Deal. I’ll lock ‘Qianli Xun’ away in my heart too, and savor it now and then.”
[ZH] “他一点儿都不傻，是我太聪明，才显得他傻而已。”
[EN] They shared a quiet smile.
[ZH] 对方又开始自夸，千里寻露出不屑的表情，也为二人能像朋友一样讲话而欣慰。
[EN] Lightening the air, she asked, “How did you start suspecting Chen Haodong?”
[ZH] 申简辰撩开马车的帘子，向外瞧，已是黄昏时分，夕阳西下。再瞧瞧周围，显然马车已出城。
[EN] Shen Jianchen puffed up to lift the mood. “Pure genius—razor-sharp observation, seeing right through the surface to spot Chen Haodong’s guilt at one glance.”
[ZH] “这个点儿也不知陈浩东为何会往城外跑？”
[EN] She rolled her eyes. “Keep bragging, then—forget I asked!”
[ZH] “他表面装得跟正人君子似的，谁知背地里做些什么？”
[EN] “All right, all right. At Chen Manor I caught Chen Haodong and Chun Jinlan trading shady looks. Then last night he used delivering bedding to Haoyu as cover to sneak into Chun Jinlan’s cell. Dead giveaway.”
[ZH] “你说他为什么要给陈浩宇捣鼓出这等事来？”
[EN] “Chen Haodong can’t be that dumb—meeting Chun Jinlan right in the middle of an investigation? Not scared of getting caught?”
[ZH] “应该是被陈家产业继承人的事给闹的。”
[EN] “He’s not dumb—I’m just so brilliant I make him look dumb.”
[ZH] “你怎么知道？”
[EN] She scoffed, secretly relieved they could still banter like old friends.
[ZH] “我又不是傻子，观察观察也就明白。”
[EN] Lifting the window curtain, Shen Jianchen saw dusk settling and open countryside rolling past. “Why’s Chen Haodong heading outside the city at this hour?”
[ZH] “陈家大老爷好像还不明白，不如你回去提点提点他？”
[EN] “Plays the saint in public—who knows what dirt he gets up to in the dark.”
[ZH] “陈家产业上的事，我才懒得掺和，跟我没什么关系，陈浩宇也志不在此，我只求尽快解决麻烦，生活回归平静。”
[EN] “Why do you think he’s setting Chen Haoyu up?”
[ZH] 申简辰担忧而又关切地问：“陈浩宇惹下麻烦，你不难过？”
[EN] “The Chen family business inheritance, obviously.”
[ZH] “有什么好难过的？嫁给他时就知道他是什么德行，我早已做好应对一切的准备。”千里寻不想让申简辰觉得她对陈浩宇失望，从而对她继续抱有幻想。
[EN] “How’d you figure that?”
[ZH] 申简辰听出些苗头来，心想：“你又何必在我面前装？我不会再做事莽撞，也不再特意寻找会爬树的猪。可若有一日被我撞见，我还是不会放弃。也许……也许我永远也遇不到一头会爬树的猪。”
[EN] “I’m not blind—takes two minutes of watching to see it.”
[ZH] 马车突然停下，车夫向里说：“公子，刚才的马车在一所宅子前停下，车里的人进了宅子。”
[EN] “Eldest Master Chen doesn’t seem to see it. Why not tip him off when you get home?”
[ZH] 两人跳下马车，申简辰将银子递给车夫：“你把车赶到路边，在那里等着，待会儿我们办完事，还需载我们回去。”
[EN] “Not touching Chen business politics with a ten-foot pole. Doesn’t concern me, and Chen Haoyu has zero ambition for it anyway. All I want is this mess over so life goes back to normal.”
[ZH] “公子放心，来的路上我都观察过，有棵大树，不高，枝叶繁茂，我把马车赶到树下，天色暗下后，不容易被人发现。”
[EN] Concern shaded his voice. “Doesn’t Chen Haoyu’s mess upset you?”
[ZH] 车夫收好银子，轻手轻脚将马车赶走。
[EN] “What’s to be upset about? I knew his track record the day I married him and braced for everything.” She wasn’t about to let Shen Jianchen think she was disillusioned with Haoyu and nurse false hopes.
[ZH] 千里寻夸道：“你找的车夫不错，挺有安全意识。”
[EN] Reading between the lines, he thought, *No need to put on a front with me. I won’t act reckless anymore, and I’ve stopped hunting for a tree-climbing pig. Still, if I ever stumble across one, I’m not giving up—though odds are I’ll never see a pig climb a tree.*
[ZH] “也不瞧瞧我是谁？”
[EN] The carriage halted. The driver called in, “Young Master, that carriage stopped at a house ahead, and the passenger went inside.”
[ZH] “又开始自夸？”
[EN] Both hopped down, and Shen Jianchen handed over silver. “Pull off the road and wait—we’ll need a ride back once we’re done.”
[ZH] 申简辰从怀里掏出黑布绑在自己脸上：“夜晚偷窥他人，需装扮一下，免得被认出来。仅此一条，你怎么办？”
[EN] “Don’t worry, Young Master—I spotted a thick, low-branched tree on the way in. I’ll park underneath; once dark hits, nobody’ll see us.” The driver eased the rig away on quiet wheels.
[ZH] 千里寻掏出丝帕：“我也有。”
[EN] “Not bad,” Qianli Xun praised. “Your driver’s got solid security instincts.”
[ZH] “颜色太过鲜艳，容易被发现，还有你这身衣裳，不如你在外守着，我进去。”
[EN] “Look who hired him.”
[ZH] “我也想进去，天快黑了，谁会注意到？”
[EN] “Bragging again?”
[ZH] 申简辰并未过分强求，只因他喜欢顺着千里寻：“好吧，那你跟紧我。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen pulled the scrap of black cloth from his robe and tied it over his face. “Spying at night takes a mask so nobody spots us. Only got one—what about you?”
[ZH] “何须跟你？难道我自己不会？”话音刚落，千里寻已用轻功跳上院墙。
[EN] Qianli Xun fished out a silk handkerchief. “Covered.”
[ZH] 申简辰将脚在地上一蹬，很快落在千里寻身边。
[EN] “Way too bright—and look at your loud outfit! Wait out here while I go in.”
[ZH] 千里寻小声说：“宅子不大，我刚瞧过一遍，没有下人，也不知他来此处做什么？该不会这里就是陈家在乡下的宅子？”
[EN] “I’m coming too. Almost pitch dark—who’s going to notice?”
[ZH] 申简辰轻声回应：“应该不是，陈家在乡下的宅子肯定比这儿大，而且还有下人。”
[EN] Never one to fight her stubborn streak, he gave in. “Fine—stick close.”
[ZH] “你怎么知道？”
[EN] “Why stick close? Think I can’t climb a wall myself?” Before the words left her mouth, she sprang cleanly atop the courtyard wall with her light-foot technique. Shen Jianchen kicked off and landed right beside her.
[ZH] “我审理此案，当然比你清楚。”
[EN] She whispered, “Small place—just scanned it, zero servants. Think this is the Chens’ country house?”
[ZH] “你没有亲眼见过，怎么知道？”
[EN] “Doubt it,” he murmured back. “The Chens’ country house would be way bigger and staffed with servants.”
[ZH] “陈浩宇的说辞中提到陈家在乡下的宅子里有下人，仅凭这点便可断定。”
[EN] “How would you know? You’ve never seen it.”
[ZH] 千里寻只能认可，还想继续问时，听到一阵马车的声音，两人不约而同用轻功跳上屋顶，轻轻蹲下，向院外瞧去。
[EN] “Chen Haoyu testified there are servants at the country house—simple deduction.”
[ZH] 天还没有完全黑，多少能看清一些，那马车与春金沫带春金兰到陈府闹事时乘坐的一模一样。
[EN] Before she could argue, carriage wheels rumbled up outside. In sync, both vaulted onto the roof and crouched low to peek out.
[ZH] “春香楼的人。原来陈浩东到乡下私会商妓，难怪大嫂讨厌他。”
[EN] Twilight still lingered enough to spot the flashy carriage—identical to the Chunxiang Brothel rig Chun Jinmo had arrived in.
[ZH] 申简辰没有回应，一直观察那辆马车，见一女子从马车里下来，推开门，走进院中，女子正是春金蝉。
[EN] “Chunxiang Brothel,” Qianli Xun muttered. “Chen Haodong’s sneaking out to the sticks to meet a courtesan—no wonder Eldest Sister-in-law despises him.”
[ZH] 陈浩东从屋里出来，将春金蝉拥入怀中，以为宅子里只有他们二人，说话毫不忌讳，声音也大。
[EN] Shen Jianchen watched in silence as a woman stepped down, pushed open the gate, and entered: Chun Jinchan.
[ZH] “白天不管是在司理院还是在茶庄一直念着你的骚劲儿，快随我进屋，让我快活快活。”
[EN] Chen Haodong stepped out of the house and swept her into his arms, speaking loud and loose since they had the place to themselves. “At the Judicial Office and the tea shop all day, all I could think about was how wild you are—come inside and show me a good time!”
[ZH] 春金蝉也说的直白：“我连着七日没接客，把这身子洗了不知多少回，就等陪你，你可得把七日的银子都给了，否则妈妈会不高兴。”
[EN] Chun Jinchan spoke just as blunt. “I turned down clients seven straight days and scrubbed myself spotless waiting for you—you’d better pay for all seven days, or the madam will pitch a fit.”
[ZH] “银子上我何时含糊过？”陈浩东说着朝春金蝉脸上亲去，亲够后，两人相拥进入屋内。
[EN] “When have I ever been stingy with silver?” Covering her face with kisses, he hauled her indoors.
[ZH] 千里寻不好意思起来：“他们是来偷情，估计什么都探不到，我先回……”
[EN] Cheeks heating, Qianli Xun shifted back. “Just a sleazy tryst—we’re not learning anything useful. I’m heading back—”
[ZH] 申简辰下意识抓住千里寻的手臂，见她露出为难的表情，很快放开手解释道：“他们在一起必然要聊天儿，说不定会提到重要信息，还是留下来一起听。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen caught her arm on reflex, then let go at her awkward look. “People always talk after—he might spill something vital. Stay and listen.”
[ZH] 千里寻没有拒绝：“好吧，那就留下来听听。”
[EN] “Fine. We’ll listen.”
[ZH] 申简辰估摸着位置，从屋顶去掉瓦片，再捣鼓捣鼓，终于看到屋内的情景：陈浩东衣衫不整，正与春金兰在床上忙碌着……
[EN] Picking a spot above the bedchamber, Shen Jianchen lifted a roof tile and peered through—right onto Chen Haodong half-undressed and tumbling across the bed with Chun Jinchan.

#CH 283
[ZH] 千里寻并不去看，从传出的声音足以判断二人正在做着什么。她听得脸红心跳，后悔不该答应留下来。想走，身体不听使唤，屋里的声音仿佛有种魔力，将她的身体吸住，无法挪动。
[ZH] 她看向申简辰，他旁若无人，透过屋顶的小洞向下瞧着，既不脸红，心也不跳，仿佛看热闹似的。
[ZH] 千里寻红着脸，还好被丝帕遮住，不容易被人发现。她小声奚落：“哼！你如此专注，真不愧是圆过房的人。”
[ZH] 申简辰将目光移开，不好意思起来：“我是担心遗漏掉重要的话。”
[ZH] 千里寻继续小声奚落：“哼！也不用脑子想想，他们两个只顾亲热，哪有心情聊重要的话？你自己想看，不如老实承认，还显得你是真诚之人。”
[ZH] 申简辰更不好意思起来：“好了好了，不看就是。”
[ZH] 他坐正后，抬头看天上的一轮明月。刚才千里寻提到他圆过房，应该是白风说给苦荞，她从苦荞那里得知。
[ZH] 为了不被屋里的人听到，申简辰尽量将声音压低：“你会不会怪我？”
[ZH] 对方突如其来的问题，千里寻搞不明白，露出疑惑的表情。
[ZH] 申简辰将目光移到千里寻的脸上，借着月光清楚地接触到她不解的目光：“我食言了，没有守身如玉。”
[ZH] 原来是为此事，他回汴梁前说出这四个字，千里寻多少感动过，偶尔也会回想一番。可因并不现实，她从未真正放在心上。
[ZH] “我并未当回事，你不算食言。”
[ZH] 申简辰不知自己是该失落还是该轻松，他苦笑一下：“你和陈浩宇呢？是不是也已圆房？”
[ZH] 不愿对方再抱任何希望，千里寻哄骗道：“当……当然。”因说谎，心中稍有紧张，手下意识从腿上挪开，一不小心碰到揭开的瓦片。
[ZH] 那瓦片顺着屋顶掉落在院内，只听“咣当”一声，瓦片掉在地上被摔成几瓣。
[ZH] 屋里传出陈浩东的声音：“谁？”
[EN] Qianli Xun refused to look—the sounds rising through the gap left zero doubt what was happening below. Heart hammering and face burning, she kicked herself for staying, yet her feet felt nailed to the tiles.
[ZH] 申简辰急中生智，学了声猫叫：“喵！”
[EN] Glancing over, she saw Shen Jianchen peering right through the hole without blinking, neither blushing nor flustered, cool as a spectator at a fair.
[ZH] 接着传来春金蝉的声音：“原来是只野猫，大公子别理会，我们继续。”
[EN] Thank goodness the handkerchief hid her red face. She whispered with a scoff, “Hmph! Staring so intent—spoken like a man who’s already consummated his marriage.”
[ZH] 屋里又传出一阵春波荡漾的笑声。
[EN] Shen Jianchen jerked his gaze away, sheepish. “I didn’t want to miss anything important they might say.”
[ZH] 两人不再说话，默默坐在屋顶，看似欣赏月光，其实各怀心思。
[EN] “Hmph! Use your head—they’re busy rolling around, not sharing secrets! If you want to watch, own up to it like an honest man.”
[ZH] 千里寻埋怨屋里的二人闹得太火热，搞得她坐在屋顶听到不该听的，同时为大嫂叶安雅感到不值。
[EN] Flustered, he gave in. “Fine, fine—I won’t look.”
[ZH] 叶安雅刚给她说想要和离时，她替她担忧过，现在却认为叶安雅离开陈浩东才是正确的选择。
[EN] Sitting straight, he tilted his face up at the bright moon. She knew about his consummation—Bai Feng must have told Kuqiao, who told her.
[ZH] 申简辰则为二人都已圆房而伤感，世间之事不如意者十有八九，他很想用感情去换其他如意之事，可生活偏偏不让他如意。
[EN] Keeping his voice barely a breath so nobody below heard, he asked, “Do you blame me?”
[ZH] 屋里的躁动终于告一段落，陈浩东一脸享受的样子，闭着眼躺在床上；春金蝉则靠在他的身上。
[EN] Caught off guard, she shot him a puzzled look.
[ZH] “大公子雄风十足，会令不少女人神魂颠倒，也不知你家娘子是怎么想的，竟然一点儿都不珍惜？”
[EN] Meeting her eyes in the moonlight, he murmured, “I broke my word. I didn’t keep myself chaste.”
[ZH] “你不是不喜欢提她吗？”
[EN] Oh—that vow he had made before returning to Bianliang. It had touched her at the time, even lingered in her thoughts now and then, yet it had never been realistic, so she hadn’t held him to it. “I never took it seriously—so you didn’t break your word.”
[ZH] “偶尔提提也无妨。”
[EN] Unsure whether to feel crushed or relieved, he gave a wry smile. “What about you and Chen Haoyu? Have you consummated too?”
[ZH] “等我成了陈家产业的继承人，到时找机会让你好好教教她如何伺候男人。”
[EN] Determined to snuff out any lingering hope in him, she fibbed. “Of—of course.” Nerves over the lie made her shift her hand off her knee—bumping the loosened roof tile.
[ZH] “我可不教！省得她学会后大公子不再来找我。”
[EN] Clatter! The tile slid down the eaves and shattered into shards in the yard.
[ZH] “怎么会？你这股子骚劲儿无人能比，我可舍不得。”
[EN] “Who’s out there?!” Chen Haodong barked from inside.
[ZH] 春金蝉开心地笑了一会儿，问道：“金兰给陈浩宇当妾室可有希望？”
[EN] Quick as lightning, Shen Jianchen let out a crisp “Meow!”
[ZH] 终于扯到重要话题，两人不约而同将头凑到小洞前，结果他们的头不小心撞到一起。两人各自捂住撞到的额头，忍了一会儿。
[EN] Chun Jinchan’s voice followed. “Just a stray cat, Eldest Young Master—ignore it and keep going.”
[ZH] 申简辰不再去抢，把地方让给千里寻。屋里的二人说话声音够大，不凑近，也能听到七八分。
[EN] Lush, giggling laughter floated back up from inside.
[ZH] 千里寻毫不客气，将耳朵凑到小洞跟前……
[EN] Neither spoke after that, sitting side by side on the roof beneath the moon, lost in private thoughts. Qianli Xun fumed at the racket below and felt awful for Ye Anya—earlier she had worried over her sister-in-law wanting a divorce, yet walking away from Chen Haodong was clearly the smartest move possible. Meanwhile, Shen Jianchen ached over thinking both of them had moved on; nine times out of ten life denied you what you wanted most.
[ZH] 只听陈浩东回道：“还很难说，今日审案不算顺利。疤子什么都承认，浩宇的两个朋友却说他那天晚上与他们在一起。证词不一致，参军大人还需寻找新的证据，只怕结果不如人意。”
[EN] At last the commotion below quieted. Chen Haodong lay back with his eyes shut in smug bliss while Chun Jinchan draped herself over him.
[ZH] “大公子希望谁说的是假话？”
[EN] “Eldest Young Master’s vigor could sweep half the women in town off their feet,” she purred. “What’s wrong with your wife, not appreciating what she’s got?”
[ZH] “你明明知道，还来问我？”
[EN] “Thought you didn’t like bringing her up?”
[ZH] “你昨晚找疤子就是为此事？”
[EN] “Now and then doesn’t hurt.”
[ZH] “当然，我担心疤子向着我堂弟，到公堂上作假证，便找他将利害关系说了，然后晓之以理动之以情，他终于答应照实说。”
[EN] “Once I inherit the Chen family business, I’ll find a chance for you to teach her how to serve a man properly.”
[ZH] “这么说来那两位公子说的是假话？”
[EN] “No way! What if she learns and you stop coming to see me?”
[ZH] “也能理解，浩宇常常与他们玩儿在一起，感情自然深些，帮他做假证再正常不过。”
[EN] “Never—nobody matches your fire. I couldn’t give you up.”
[ZH] “不如大公子向司理参军说说，就说那两名公子作假证。”
[EN] Giggling in delight, Chun Jinchan asked, “Any chance Jinlan becomes Chen Haoyu’s concubine?”
[ZH] “现在的司理参军是仪王，我哪有那么大的面子？再说什么事都得讲个证据，凭我空口白牙没人信。”
[EN] Finally—the real goods! Both leaned over the hole at the same instant and knocked foreheads with a dull thud. Wincing, they clutched their brows until the sting faded, and Shen Jianchen yielded the spot to her—the voices below carried loud enough anyway.
[ZH] 听了半天，可以确定春金兰闹事的确与陈浩东有关，还是听不出与疤子做假证相关之事。
[EN] Pressing her ear close, Qianli Xun caught Chen Haodong’s answer. “Hard to say. Today’s hearing didn’t go smooth. Scarface confessed everything, yet two of Haoyu’s friends swore he was with them all night. With clashing testimonies, the Judicial Investigator is hunting more proof—might not go my way.”
[ZH] 屋里的二人又开始闹腾，千里寻实在听不下去。她偷偷溜出来，还需尽快回去，只怕长辈无意中去墨香苑发现她不在，不好交代。
[EN] “Who do you hope is lying, Eldest Young Master?”
[ZH] “没什么好听的，剩下的交给你，我先行一步。”
[EN] “You already know—why ask?”
[ZH] “马车归你。”
[EN] “That’s why you went looking for Scarface last night?”
[ZH] 千里寻并未拒绝，立刻用轻功离开。
[EN] “Of course. Scared Scarface would side with my cousin and perjure himself, so I laid out the stakes, reasoned with him, and got him to swear he’d tell the truth.”
[ZH] 剩下申简辰一人，他决定给陈浩东来个措手不及，逼他说出实情。
[EN] “So those two young masters lied?”
[ZH] 他用轻功跳入院内，将脸上的黑布取下，塞进怀里，靠近闭着的屋门。
[EN] “Makes sense—they party with Haoyu all the time. Covering for a buddy is normal.”
[ZH] 里边的二人应该以为此处只有他们，并未将门关紧，还露出一条清晰的缝隙。申简辰上前轻轻将门推开，木门发出“咯吱”的声音。
[EN] “Why not tell the Judicial Investigator those two are lying?”
[ZH] “谁？”陈浩东已无心闹腾，惊恐地问道。
[EN] “Prince Yi is the Judicial Investigator—think I carry that kind of pull? Besides, courts run on proof, not hot air.”
[ZH] 申简辰不想再往里走，站在外厅大声说：“陈大公子外出偷 情，真是快活！”
[EN] So Chen Haodong was definitely behind Chun Jinlan’s outburst, yet nothing proved he paid Scarface to lie.
[ZH] 声音如此熟悉，沉稳中颇具力度，陈浩东反应了一会儿，吓出一身冷汗。迅速从春金禅的身上爬起来，抓起衣衫，胡乱穿着。他只觉双腿无力，脑袋一片空白，踉跄着来到外厅。
[EN] Below, the pair started getting rowdy all over again. Too embarrassed to keep listening—and needing to get back before any Chen elder noticed her missing from Moxiang Courtyard—Qianli Xun stood. “Nothing left worth hearing. Leave the rest to you—I’m out.”
[ZH] 果然是仪王，他吓得跪在地上，全身颤抖：“殿……殿下……怎会……来此？”
[EN] “Take the carriage.”
[ZH] 申简辰半真半假地说：“我闲来无事，出来转转，没想到碰见陈大公子在外偷 情，还听到些不该听的。”
[EN] She accepted without fuss and vaulted off into the night.
[ZH] 陈浩东头上直冒冷汗：“我娘子……对我十分冷淡，不得已才……才把春香楼的商妓叫到这里，还……还请殿下替我保密。”
[EN] Left alone, Shen Jianchen decided to catch Chen Haodong off guard and shake the truth out of him. Dropping into the yard, he pocketed the black mask and stepped up to the door. Assuming they had the house to themselves, the pair hadn’t even latched it—a clear crack showed light inside.
[ZH] “我才懒得管你们夫妻之事，之所以打断陈大公子的雅兴，是想问你几个问题。”
[EN] He pushed the wooden door open with a slow creak.
[ZH] “殿下尽管问。”
[EN] “Who’s there?!” Chen Haodong yelped in panic.
[ZH] “春金兰可否是你找来？然后撺掇她到陈家闹事？”
[EN] Stopping in the outer hall, Shen Jianchen spoke loud and cool. “Sneaking out for a tryst—having a grand time, Eldest Young Master Chen?”
[ZH] 看来与春金蝉一番对话已被仪王听去，没法再瞒下去，陈浩东老实交代：“是……是我撺掇的。”
[EN] That steady, unmistakable voice sent cold sweat pouring down Chen Haodong’s back. Scrambling off Chun Jinchan, he yanked his clothes on crooked, mind blank and knees like water, and stumbled into the outer room.
[ZH] “为了陈家产业？”
[EN] Prince Yi stood right there. Dropping to his knees, Chen Haodong shook head to toe. “Your—Your Highness... what brings you here?”
[ZH] “正是。不过，我也是一片好心，既帮浩宇改过自新，也能让金兰姑娘有个好去处，她实在太苦，我看着心中难安。当然，对我也有好处。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen gave a half-truth. “Out for an evening stroll when I stumbled right onto your tryst—and overheard a few things I shouldn’t have.”
[ZH] “哼！你倒挺会找理由。”
[EN] Cold sweat beaded Chen Haodong’s brow. “My wife... treats me like ice, so I had no choice but to call a courtesan from Chunxiang Brothel here... Please keep this quiet, Your Highness!”
[ZH] “不是理由，是发自肺腑之言。”
[EN] “I couldn’t care less about your bedroom affairs. I interrupted your fun to ask a few questions.”
[ZH] 陈浩东已没刚才紧张，被仪王发现的不过是私情和想要继承陈家产业的野心，还不至于丢掉小命。
[EN] “Ask anything, Your Highness.”
[ZH] “昨晚你找过疤子？”
[EN] “Did you dig up Chun Jinlan and put her up to making a scene at Chen Manor?”
[ZH] “找过。”
[EN] No hiding it after what the prince overheard. “Yes... I egged her on.”
[ZH] “你找他都说些什么？”
[EN] “Over the Chen family business?”
[ZH] 陈浩东思考片刻说道：“我担心他在公堂上做假证，告诉他作假证迟早会被查出，到时罪过可就大了，不如照实说，陈家为保住浩宇性命，必然同意把春金兰放他在身边，两全其美。”
[EN] “Yes—though I meant well too! Giving Haoyu a chance to reform and finding poor Miss Jinlan a home—her suffering tore at my heart. Plus, sure, it helped me out.”
[ZH] “你认为他今日在公堂上所言是真是假？”
[EN] “Hmph! Slick excuses.”
[ZH] “真实不虚。”
[EN] “Not excuses—straight from the heart!” Catching his breath, Chen Haodong calmed down; being caught cheating and scheming for the family business was embarrassing, not fatal.
[ZH] “难道你想要了陈浩宇的命？奸淫妇女可是死罪，宋律上写得明明白白。”
[EN] “Did you see Scarface last night?”
[ZH] 陈浩东吓得头快要碰到地上：“我们是一家人，我怎会有这心思？找春金兰到陈家闹事，只想逼浩宇纳商妓为妾，未曾想会闹到司理院，还请殿下劝说陈家长辈将此事私了。”
[EN] “I did.”
[ZH] “陈家长辈愿意交给司理院，一来是信任我，二来也是不愿纵容陈家子弟。这份魄力，本王相当佩服，又岂会反过来劝陈家长辈私了？”
[EN] “What did you say to him?”
[ZH] 陈浩东假装哭道：“呜呜……，这么说来，浩宇必然要被判死刑，岂不是我的罪过？还请殿下饶他一命，呜呜……”
[EN] Thinking a beat, Chen Haodong answered smooth. “I feared he’d perjure himself in court and warned him lying would get him harsher punishment once exposed. Told him to tell the truth—that way the Chens would let Haoyu take Chun Jinlan in to save his neck. Best outcome for all.”
[ZH] 通过一番问话，申简辰能够确定，陈浩东为了继承陈家产业费尽心机，连一点兄弟之情都不顾，这种人若继承陈家产业，陈家的未来十分堪忧。
[EN] “And do you believe his testimony today was true?”
[ZH] 申简辰不想再问下去，对方说话滴水不漏，也问不出什么：“陈大公子不当戏子实在屈才，今日就聊到这里。”
[EN] “Every word.”
[ZH] 仪王要离开，陈浩东松口气，面向仪王出去的背影：“今日之事，还请殿下替草民保密！”
[EN] “Are you trying to get Chen Haoyu executed? Rape is a capital crime under Song law.”
[ZH] 申简辰很是不屑，心想，自己做下丑事，还想让别人替你保密，实在可笑！
[EN] Chen Haodong’s forehead nearly grazed the floor. “We’re family—how could I wish him dead?! I only pushed Chun Jinlan to force him to take a courtesan as concubine, never dreamed it would land at the Judicial Office! Please urge the Chen elders to settle this privately, Your Highness!”
[ZH] 走出宅子，申简辰利用轻功跳上一棵高树。
[EN] “The Chen elders came to the Judicial Office because they trust me and refuse to coddle a guilty kinsman. I admire their integrity—why would I urge them to sweep it under the rug?”
[ZH] 千里寻应该已乘坐马车回城，他打算踩着乡下的高树，边欣赏夜色，边朝幽林别居的方向而去。
[EN] Chen Haodong broke into crocodile tears. “Waaah... if Haoyu gets sentenced to death, it’ll be on my head! Spare his life, Your Highness!”
[ZH] 刚踩了十来棵树，竟然看到温信带着司理院的几名差役从树下经过。
[EN] Every word confirmed Chen Haodong would stab his own cousin in the back for the family fortune—handing the Chen empire to a snake like him spelled disaster. Still, his answers were airtight for now. “Shame you didn’t become an actor, Eldest Young Master Chen. We’re done here.”
[ZH] 于是用轻功落在他们面前：“这么晚温都头还在办差？”
[EN] Relieved, Chen Haodong called after his retreating back, “Please keep tonight’s matter confidential, Your Highness!”
[ZH] 仪王突然从天而降，大家吃惊的同时，赶紧行礼：“拜见王爷！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen scoffed to himself—doing dirty deeds and begging witnesses to keep quiet!
[ZH] 申简辰礼貌性地说道：“免礼，你们这是要去哪里？”
[EN] Vaulting up into a tall tree outside, knowing Qianli Xun had taken the carriage back to town, he leaped treetop to treetop toward Youlin Retreat under the moonlight—only to spot Wen Xin marching beneath him with a squad of constables after ten trees.
[ZH] 温信回道：“陈家在乡下的宅子就在附近，白天来过一回，没查出什么。属下想，既然事情是在晚上发生，不如趁天黑过再查探一番，也许会有新的发现。”
[EN] Dropping right in front of them, he asked, “Still on duty this late, Chief Constable Wen?”
[ZH] 既然碰到，申简辰决定亲自前往：“本王随你们去看看。”
[EN] Startled by the prince dropping from the sky, the men bowed. “Greetings, Your Highness!”
[ZH] ……
[EN] “At ease. Where are you headed?”
[ZH] 仪王离开后，春金蝉整理好衣装，出来扶起陈浩东：“你我刚才亲热时不会被他瞧见？”
[EN] “The Chens’ country house is right nearby,” Wen Xin explained. “Turned up nothing in daylight, so since the crime happened at night, I figured we’d inspect it after dark—might catch something fresh.”
[ZH] 陈浩东走到椅子跟前坐下：“八成是。”
[EN] “I’ll tag along and take a look.”
[ZH] 春金蝉笑道：“呵呵，我倒没什么，就当伺候大公子的同时，顺带伺候了一位王爷。”
[EN] Back at the tryst house, Chun Jinchan straightened her clothes and helped Chen Haodong up. “Think he saw us going at it?”
[ZH] “亏你还笑得出！”陈浩东将屋子瞧一遍，“此处已被人发现，明儿我找人把这宅子给卖了。”
[EN] Collapsing into a chair, Chen Haodong muttered, “Odds are yes.”
[ZH] 春金蝉不满道：“是该卖了，总没人打扫，都是灰尘，老跟你这么偷偷摸摸的，什么时候是个头？”
[EN] Chun Jinchan giggled. “Doesn’t bother me—serving a prince along with Eldest Young Master is a bonus!”
[ZH] 陈浩东并未回答，掏出几张银票：“拿着，你先回城里，我再待会儿。”
[EN] “How can you still laugh?!” Glancing around, he snapped, “This place is burned—I’ll have someone sell it tomorrow.”
[ZH] 见到银票，春金兰喜笑颜开，接过来塞进怀里：“大公子出手就是阔绰，找好新地儿，差人告诉我。别再找乡下的地儿，太远。”然后在陈浩东的额头亲了一口，扭着腰肢离开。
[EN] “Good riddance—dusty, never cleaned, and sneaking around like rats gets old fast.”
[ZH] 陈浩东坐在椅子上，将自己与春金蝉亲热完说过的每一句话回想一遍，又把刚才与仪王的对话也回顾一回，确认没有说错一句，才深深吐口气，自语道：“仪王突然出现，说明他已盯上我。没想到这位王爷如此厉害，这么快怀疑到我头上。多亏我掩饰得好，加之一切处理得干净，他肯定找不出任何线索。”
[EN] Ignoring her gripe, he tossed out several bank notes. “Take these and head back to town. I’ll sit a while longer.”
[ZH] ……
[EN] Eyes lighting up at the silver notes, Chun Jinchan tucked them inside her bodice. “Generous as ever! Send word once you find a new spot—somewhere closer to town, not out in the sticks.” Planting a kiss on his forehead, she swayed out.
[ZH] 陈家在乡下的宅子虽比不上城里的陈府，却也有幽林别居那么大，不愧是有钱人家，乡下的宅子不住人，也收拾得整洁干净。
[EN] Alone in the chair, Chen Haodong replayed every word he had spoken to Chun Jinchan and Prince Yi—not a single slip. Exhaling long, he muttered, “Prince Yi showing up proves he’s onto me. Sharp prince—locked on this fast! Good thing I covered my tracks clean; he won’t dig up a shred of proof.”
[ZH] 司理院的人大晚上又来宅子，管家亲自迎接，见多了位年轻人，衣着虽普通，气度却不凡，温都头对他又恭敬，猜想应是参军大人，行礼道：“拜见大人。”
[EN] Meanwhile, the Chens’ country residence loomed ahead—smaller than the main manor in town, yet rivaling Youlin Retreat in size and kept spotless despite sitting empty.
[ZH] 温信发话道：“带我和大人再去陈公子关人的屋子走一趟。”
[EN] Greeting the constables at the gate, the caretaker noticed the plainly dressed young man whose presence commanded Wen Xin’s deference, guessed it was the Judicial Investigator, and bowed low. “Greetings, Your Lordship.”
[ZH] 管家说：“大人请。”
[EN] “Take His Lordship and me back to the room where Young Master Chen locked people up,” Wen Xin ordered.
[ZH] 申简辰边走边问：“宅子里有多少下人？”
[EN] “Right this way, Your Lordship.”
[ZH] 管家回道：“不多，五六十个。”
[EN] Walking along, Shen Jianchen asked, “How many servants work here?”
[ZH] “温都头白天来过，你应该知道是为何事？”
[EN] “Not many—fifty or sixty.”
[ZH] “小人知道，还不是二公子惹下的事？”
[EN] “Chief Constable Wen came by today, so you know why we’re here?”
[ZH] “他常带人来此？”
[EN] “Yes, Your Lordship—Second Young Master’s old mess.”
[ZH] “以前常来，不知道从哪里搞来些姑娘家，放在宅子里当丫鬟，听说陈家别的宅子也有。两日前，有几个丫鬟还跑去城里的陈府闹事，逼着二少夫人帮着找婆家。二少夫人心善，都给找了，也算圆满。”
[EN] “Did he bring girls here often?”
[ZH] 申简辰心想，被陈浩宇抢来的姑娘还真不少，还好那些他欺负过的姑娘没有告到官府，若一个个告，他不知得死多少回。
[EN] “Used to—no idea where he found them, turning them into maids here and at other outside properties. Two days ago a bunch marched over to the main manor demanding Second Young Madam find them husbands. Good-hearted lady that she is, she sorted every one out.”
[ZH] 想到这里，替千里寻感到不值。埋怨命运实在无理，为何要给二人设置两条难以相交的道路。
[EN] Shen Jianchen felt a twinge for Qianli Xun—Chen Haoyu had snatched so many girls he’d have lost his head ten times over if they had sued. How unfair of fate to lay such mismatched paths for the two of them.

#CH 284
[ZH] 申简辰见时不时有下人从身边经过，随口问道：“陈家的主人又没住在宅子，为何下人如此忙碌？”
[ZH] “主人家虽不在，该忙活的还得忙活。”管家回完话，在一间屋子前停下，“大人，就是这间屋子，陈公子每次抓来的姑娘都关在这里。”
[ZH] 申简辰命令道：“打开。”
[ZH] 管家推开门，恭敬行礼：“大人，小人还有事要忙，就陪您到这里。”
[ZH] “且慢！我还有话问你。”申简辰径直走进屋内。
[ZH] 温都头等人也都跟着进入。
[ZH] 管家不敢不听，站在门外耐心等候。
[ZH] 屋内布置精美，虽是晚上，一眼瞧去也有种窗明几净的感觉，还时不时传来一股熏香的味道。
[ZH] 申简辰进入里屋，瞧到一张软床，猜想去年春金兰被抓后，应该是被绑在那张床上。
[ZH] 他用手指在身边的木制家具上摸了摸，没有丝毫灰尘，如此整洁干净，恐怕一年前留下的证据早已被勤劳的下人当垃圾一样丢掉。
[ZH] 温信在一旁说：“白天已将屋内搜遍，找不到任何相关的痕迹。”
[ZH] “可问过收拾屋子的下人？我指的是去年三月初四一早打扫屋子的丫鬟。”
[ZH] “问过，丫鬟说时间太长，想不起来，既然想不起来说明印象不深刻，印象不深刻证明没有什么特别的发现。”
[ZH] 申简辰又将屋子扫视一遍：“其他人可查问过？”
[ZH] “本想查问，管家说去年三月初三陈浩宇离开不久宅子里的下人都被临时调走。”
[ZH] “临时调走？如此凑巧？”
[ZH] “我也觉得太过凑巧。”
[ZH] “把管家叫进来。”
[ZH] 温信到门口将管家唤进来，管家乖乖进入，拜道：“大人有何吩咐？”
[ZH] 申简辰转身面向管家：“去年三月初三为何宅子里的下人都被调走？”
[ZH] 管家老实回道：“主人家吩咐，小人也不敢多问，只管照做。听回来的下人说陈府要把城里的所有宅子打扫一遍，人手不够，才从乡下的宅子调人。”
[ZH] “主人？陈家大老爷？”
[ZH] “不是，是大公子。”
[ZH] 申简辰警觉起来，心想：“又是他，看来他的确十分可疑。”
[ZH] “陈浩东？”
[ZH] “是。”
[ZH] “你呢？可留在宅子？”
[ZH] “我倒是留着。”
[ZH] 申简辰走到椅子跟前坐下：“你既留着，应该知道那晚陈浩宇有没有回来？”
[ZH] 管家解释道：“我虽留着，多贪了几杯，睡过去，醒过来已是第二日午时，确实见到二公子，可头天晚上的状况我一点儿都不清楚。”
[EN] Watching servants hurry past even at night, Shen Jianchen asked, “No masters live here—why are the servants so busy?”
[ZH] 申简辰继续问：“陈浩东可亲自前来将下人调走？”
[EN] “Masters away or not, chores still have to get done.” Stopping outside a door, the caretaker said, “Here’s the room, Your Lordship—every girl Second Young Master snatched got locked in here.”
[ZH] “那倒没有，是陈府的王管家过来将下人们带走，三日后才将所有下人送回来。”
[EN] “Open it.”
[ZH] 申简辰总觉得此事蹊跷，陈浩东偷情的宅子离此处不远，把人调走也是他所为，一切迹象都指向他，只是找不到证据。
[EN] Pushing the door wide, the caretaker bowed. “If you’ll excuse me, I’ve got duties to see to—”
[ZH] “我已问完话，你去忙吧。”
[EN] “Hold on. I’ve got questions for you.” Shen Jianchen stepped inside, Wen Xin and the constables trailing while the caretaker waited outside.
[ZH] 管家退去，申简辰对温信说：“屋子里没什么可查的，趁夜色重点观察屋子周围，再把所有下人查一遍，问问有没有人三月初三那天没有被调走。明日再到陈府找管家确认下人被调走之事。”
[EN] Even at night, the room looked plush, bright, and scented with incense. Stepping into the inner chamber, Shen Jianchen eyed the soft bed where Chun Jinlan had been bound, then ran a finger along a wooden chest—zero dust. Any clue from a year ago had long since been scrubbed away by diligent maids.
[ZH] 温信回道：“是。”
[EN] “Combed every inch in daylight,” Wen Xin said. “Not a single trace.”
[ZH] 申简辰留下温信与差役，只身离开陈家的宅子，继续回幽林别居。
[EN] “Questioned the maid who cleaned up the morning of the fourth day of the third month last year?”
[ZH] 梦容对他越来越依赖，还需及时赶回去，否则她心中不安。
[EN] “Did. She said it’s too long ago to recall—which means nothing stood out.”
[ZH] 话说乔梦容眼巴巴等到天色逐渐暗下，却不见哥哥的身影。她担心不已，差幽林别居的下人前去司理院打探。
[EN] “What about the rest of the staff?”
[ZH] 下人回来后说仪王白天一个人出去，一直没有回司理院。
[EN] “Tried—caretaker says all the servants got pulled away right after Chen Haoyu left on the third day of the third month.”
[ZH] 乔梦容更是坐立难安，哥哥一个人出去，到底要做什么？难道背着她与人私会？
[EN] “Pulled away on that exact day? Awfully convenient.”
[ZH] 她越想心越不安：“喜兰，备马车，随我去趟陈府。”
[EN] “Thought so too.”
[ZH] 喜兰明白王妃的心思，乖乖备好马车，跟随王妃来到陈府。
[EN] “Call the caretaker in.”
[ZH] 乔梦容等不及喜兰先下马车扶她，第一个跳下车，冲向陈府的大门。
[EN] Summoned back, the caretaker bowed. “Your orders, Your Lordship?”
[ZH] 喜兰赶紧跟上，快走几步，上前敲门：咚咚咚！
[EN] “Why were all the servants transferred out on the third day of the third month last year?”
[ZH] 陈府的大门被打开，下人问道：“您是？”
[EN] “Master’s orders—not my place to ask. When the staff got back, they said Chen Manor was deep-cleaning all its city residences and ran short on hands.”
[ZH] 喜兰厉声说：“你面前这位是仪王妃，识相的把门开大些，让王妃进去！”
[EN] “Which master? Eldest Master Chen?”
[ZH] 下人一听是仪王妃，吓得把门开大跪在地上：“拜见仪王妃，小人这就去通报主人前来迎接贵人……”
[EN] “No, Your Lordship—Eldest Young Master.”
[ZH] 乔梦容尽量将语速放慢：“不必打扰他人，我来是找幽栖居士，也就是陈家二少夫人，你负责在前面带路。”
[EN] Alarm bells rang in Shen Jianchen’s head. *Him again!* “Chen Haodong?”
[ZH] 下人从地上爬起来：“二少夫人住在墨香苑，我这就带贵人过去。”
[EN] “Yes.”
[ZH] 乔梦容没有心情欣赏陈府的夜景，她来不过是想搞清楚朱淑真到底在不在？如果她不在，多半是出去与哥哥私会。
[EN] “And you stayed behind?”
[ZH] 她在心中祈祷朱淑真一定要在，否则她会疯掉。
[EN] “I did.”
[ZH] 乔梦容心中慨叹陈府挺大，比幽林别居还大，心中生出不满来，凭什么朱淑真既得到哥哥的心，还能嫁到这么有钱的人家？像她这种勾三搭四之人就该千夫所指，万人唾骂。
[EN] Taking a seat, Shen Jianchen pressed, “Then you’d know whether Chen Haoyu came back that night.”
[ZH] 在下人的引领下，终于来到墨香苑。
[EN] “I stayed, yes, only I drank a few cups too many and passed out cold—didn’t wake till noon the next day. Saw Second Young Master then, sure, yet I know nothing about the night before.”
[ZH] 借着月色看清墨香苑三个字时，乔梦容对下人说：“你去吧，不必惊动陈府其他人，我来找幽栖居士只为私事。”
[EN] “Did Chen Haodong come in person to transfer the staff?”
[ZH] 下人犹豫片刻后答应着退去。贵人前来，不告诉主人会被批评，还需通报一声。
[EN] “No—Butler Wang came from the main manor to fetch them and brought them back three days later.”
[ZH] 喜兰上前敲墨香苑的院门，开门的是丑丫，今晚她当值。
[EN] Too many coincidences—Chen Haodong’s secret tryst house sat right down the road, and he pulled the staff away that very day. Every arrow pointed straight at him, yet hard proof remained missing.
[ZH] 丑丫认得仪王妃，激动道：“郡主，不！仪王妃来找我家小姐？”
[EN] Dismissing the caretaker, Shen Jianchen told Wen Xin, “Nothing left inside. Comb the grounds outside under night cover, then question every servant to see if anyone missed that transfer on the third day of the third month. Tomorrow verify the transfer with Butler Wang at Chen Manor.”
[ZH] 喜兰帮着问：“幽栖居士可在？”
[EN] “Yes, Your Highness.”
[ZH] 丑丫回道：“在，当然在，这会儿应该刚睡下。仪王妃，里边请，我这就去给苦荞姐姐通报。”
[EN] Leaving Wen Xin and his squad at work, Shen Jianchen headed back to Youlin Retreat—Mengrong leaned on him more every day, and staying out too late would set her off.
[ZH] 乔梦容松口气，人在便好，不过还需亲眼看到方可安心。
[EN] Sure enough, back at Youlin Retreat, Qiao Mengrong had watched night fall with no sign of her brother. Sending a servant to check the Judicial Office only brought back word that Prince Yi had walked out alone during the day and never returned.
[ZH] 她走进墨香苑，院子不小，到处种着花，还有小池塘，里面长满荷叶与花骨朵，不远处还有一棵树，月色下判断不出是什么树。
[EN] Dread chewed at her nerves. Where had he gone alone—a secret rendezvous?
[ZH] 乔梦容无心多想，跟着丑丫来到素心草堂，看样子朱淑真应该住在这间屋子里。
[EN] “Xilan! Hitch the carriage—we’re going to Chen Manor!”
[ZH] 丑丫在门外向里说：“苦荞姐姐，仪王妃前来拜访。”
[EN] Reading her mistress’s mind, Xilan had the carriage ready in minutes. Pulling up outside Chen Manor, Qiao Mengrong didn’t even wait for Xilan’s arm—she vaulted down and marched straight for the main gate while Xilan sprinted ahead to pound the knocker.
[ZH] 乔梦容期待朱淑真能迅速推门而出，给她吃一颗定心丸。
[EN] A gatekeeper cracked the door. “Who is it?”
[ZH] 门是被打开，出来的却是苦荞，她看上去心神不安，还顺便把门带上。
[EN] “Princess Consort Yi stands before you!” Xilan snapped. “Open wide and let Her Highness in!”
[ZH] 乔梦容的心不由悬起来，难道她不在？
[EN] Terrified, the servant flung the gate wide and dropped to his knees. “Greetings, Your Highness! Let me summon my masters to welcome you—”
[ZH] 只见苦荞跪在地上，丑丫也跟着跪下。
[EN] “Don’t disturb anyone,” Qiao Mengrong said, forcing her voice slow and steady. “I’m here to see Youqi Jushi—Second Young Madam. Lead the way.”
[ZH] 苦荞说：“郡主前来，小姐本应出来迎接，可这几日不断有人找上门来要给姑爷当妾室，此事又惊动司理院，姑爷现在人还在司理院关着，小姐心中难过，刚刚睡下，恐怕……恐怕不能出来面见贵人。”
[EN] Scrambling up, the gatekeeper said, “Second Young Madam lives in Moxiang Courtyard. Right this way, Your Highness.”
[ZH] 乔梦容猜测定是陈浩东捣鼓出来的麻烦事，朱淑真不开心，她便开心。可苦荞的话疑点重重，乔梦容无法信服。再说司理院出面审理，哥哥更有机会与朱淑真接触，说不定哥哥没按时回幽林别居正是与她私会。
[EN] Blind to the manor’s nocturnal scenery, Qiao Mengrong cared about one thing alone: was Zhu Shuzhen home? If not, she had to be out meeting her brother—and Qiao Mengrong prayed she was here, or she’d lose her mind. Still, noticing Chen Manor dwarfed even Youlin Retreat sparked fresh venom: why did a shameless flirt like Zhu Shuzhen get both her brother’s heart and the richest family in Lin’an?
[ZH] 想到这里，乔梦容怒色道：“她既难过，我更应该见见她，说些安慰的话，你不过是个下人，哪有权利替幽栖居士做主？”
[EN] At the moonlit plaque of Moxiang Courtyard, Qiao Mengrong dismissed the guide. “Leave us—no need to alert anyone else. Private visit.” Hesitating, the servant bowed off—and went straight to alert his master anyway, knowing better than to hide a royal visit.
[ZH] 苦荞继续跪着不敢起来。
[EN] Xilan knocked on the courtyard gate. On night watch, Chouya opened it and gasped. “Princess—I mean, Princess Consort Yi! Here to see my miss?”
[ZH] 小姐白天女扮男装偷偷溜出去，天黑了还未归来。仪王妃此时跑来要见人，实在想不通她要做什么？凭上次诗会时她对小姐的态度，苦荞绝对不会认为她来是安慰小姐。难道是听到什么？来找小姐算账？
[EN] “Is Youqi Jushi in?” Xilan demanded.
[ZH] 苦荞十分紧张，不知说些什么话来应对。
[EN] “Yes, of course—she should’ve just gone to bed. Please come in, Your Highness, I’ll go tell Sister Kuqiao!”
[ZH] 丑丫猜到小姐应该不在，否则怎么敢慢待贵人？也帮着圆谎：“贵人不要怪罪，不是小姐不愿见您，实在是她今日身体不适，怕冲撞了您。”
[EN] Relief washed over Qiao Mengrong, though she wouldn’t trust it until she laid eyes on her. Crossing the flower-filled courtyard past the lotus pond and a shadowy tree, she followed Chouya to Suxin Thatched Hall.
[ZH] 苦荞也临时找到理由：“对，是怕冲撞了贵人，小姐心情不好，精神不济，出来见贵人，只怕不敬。”
[EN] Chouya called outside the door, “Sister Kuqiao, Princess Consort Yi is here to visit!”
[ZH] 喜兰替仪王妃说道：“幽栖居士躲着不出来才是对王妃大不敬！王妃好不容易来趟陈家找个人，你们都推三阻四，难道陈家人眼中容不下贵人？”
[EN] Expecting Zhu Shuzhen to step out right away, Qiao Mengrong watched the door crack open—only for Kuqiao to slip out looking rattled and pull it shut behind her.
[ZH] 传来陈江林的声音：“仪王妃驾到，有失远迎，陈家人的心中时刻放着贵人。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong’s heart leaped into her throat. *She’s not here!*
[ZH] 原来下人只将此事告知大老爷，陈江林不想惊动老太太与两个弟弟，亲自带着姚轻琰前来，一路上他们十分纳闷，仪王妃为何大晚上跑到陈家？
[EN] Kuqiao dropped to her knees, Chouya kneeling beside her. “My miss ought to greet Your Highness in person, only women have been banging on the door these past days demanding to be Young Master’s concubines, and now Young Master is locked up at the Judicial Office. My miss was heartbroken and just fell asleep—I’m afraid she can’t come out to receive Your Highness.”
[ZH] 乔梦容转过身，不满道：“陈老爷嘴上说得好，可我来了一会儿，这两个丫鬟推三阻四，不想让我见幽栖居士，难道人根本不在房中？而是大晚上偷偷跑出去与人私会？”
[EN] Sure Chen Haodong’s scheme had made Zhu Shuzhen miserable, Qiao Mengrong still smelled a lie—what if the Judicial Office case had thrown her brother and Zhu Shuzhen together right now?
[ZH] 陈江林与姚轻琰不明所以，贵人生气，他们也都跪下。
[EN] Her face darkened. “If she’s heartbroken, all the more reason I should comfort her! Who gave a servant the right to speak for Youqi Jushi?”
[ZH] 姚轻琰解释道：“贵人息怒，千万不要误会，淑真肯定在，她没立刻出来，定是有原因，我这就让下人把门打开，亲自将淑真带出来面见贵人。”
[EN] Kuqiao stayed glued to her knees in cold sweat. Her miss had snuck out in men’s robes and still wasn’t back! Judging by how hostile the Princess Consort had been at the poetry salon, she sure hadn’t come to offer sympathy.
[ZH] 乔梦容又舒心一些：“都起来吧，搞得好像我欺负你们似的。”
[EN] Guessing her miss was out, Chouya chimed in to cover. “Please don’t be angry, Your Highness—Miss is feeling unwell today and fears offending you.”
[ZH] 众人起身，姚轻琰上前对苦荞说：“把门打开。”
[EN] Kuqiao grabbed the lifeline. “Yes! Low spirits and unwell—she’d hate to show disrespect.”
[ZH] 苦荞心想，这下完了，仪王妃见不到自家小姐，不知会如何编排？眼下情形，只能将门打开。
[EN] Xilan snapped, “Hiding indoors is real disrespect! Her Highness makes a rare trip to Chen Manor and you stall at every turn—do the Chens have zero regard for royalty?!”
[ZH] 苦荞小心推开门，姚轻琰走进去。
[EN] “Forgive our late welcome, Princess Consort Yi! The Chens hold royalty in utmost esteem.”
[ZH] 乔梦容已等不及，跟着进入，屋里的布置与望月轩的闺房很相似，亲切感袭上心头，她与淑真一起围坐火盆旁聊天儿的情景浮现在眼前……
[EN] Informed by the gatekeeper, Chen Jianglin had avoided waking Old Madam Xia or his brothers and hurried over with Yao Qingyan, baffled why the Princess Consort had come calling at night.
[ZH] 以前美好的回忆更让她恨朱淑真，都是他不顾闺中密友的情谊，偷走哥哥的心！
[EN] Qiao Mengrong wheeled on him. “Fine words, Master Chen—yet these two maids have stalled me at the door since I arrived! Is Youqi Jushi even inside, or did she sneak out after dark for a secret tryst?!”
[ZH] 苦荞越发紧张，大夫人正往里屋走，若看到床上空无一人，如何向仪王妃交代？
[EN] Alarmed by her fury, Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan dropped to their knees.
[ZH] 苦荞心惊胆战跟在大夫人身后，竟然意外地发现闺床上的被子不是她出去前叠得整整齐齐的样子，小姐竟然躺在床上，只是被子盖得乱些，刚脱下的男装压在被子下面露出一角。
