[EN] “Calm your anger, Your Highness—don’t misunderstand!” Yao Qingyan pleaded. “Shuzhen is definitely inside! There must be a reason she hasn’t come out. I’ll open the door and fetch her myself right now!”
[ZH] 回来就好，苦荞松口气，迅速上前为小姐盖好被子的同时，偷偷把男装向被子下面塞了塞。
[EN] Mollified, Qiao Mengrong waved them up. “Rise—making it look like I’m bullying you.”
[ZH] 姚轻琰上前轻声说：“淑真，贵人来看你，还是起来见一见。”
[EN] Getting to her feet, Yao Qingyan ordered Kuqiao, “Open the door.”
[ZH] 千里寻睁开紧闭的双眼，装作熟睡后刚醒来的样子：“谁？谁来看我？”
[EN] *We’re doomed,* Kuqiao thought, heart pounding as she pushed the door open.
[ZH] 姚轻琰强调道：“仪王妃。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan stepped inside with Qiao Mengrong right on her heels. The layout echoed Wangyue Pavilion’s bedchamber so much that a stab of nostalgia hit Qiao Mengrong—remembering nights huddled beside a brazier chatting with Shuzhen—only to curdle straight back into hatred over her betraying their friendship and stealing her brother’s heart.
[ZH] 千里寻立刻坐起来：“苦荞，为何不早些唤我？差点儿怠慢贵人！”
[EN] Trembling behind Madam as they entered the inner chamber, Kuqiao braced for an empty bed—and nearly collapsed in relief! Instead of the neat quilt she had folded, her miss lay tucked underneath, just one corner of her discarded men’s robe peeking out from beneath the covers.
[ZH] 苦荞装道：“小姐心里难过，好不容易睡个好觉，我不想打扰。”
[EN] Darting ahead, Kuqiao pulled the quilt smooth while jamming the men’s robe out of sight underneath.
[ZH] 千里寻故意批评道：“我没事，贵人重要，记着，以后只要是贵人来，哪怕我病着，也要立刻爬起来见，否则是对贵人大大的不敬。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan leaned over with a gentle call. “Shuzhen, Her Highness came to see you—best get up.”
[ZH] 姚轻琰露出满意的笑容。
[EN] Qianli Xun blinked open groggy eyes, playing freshly woken to perfection. “Who...? Who came to see me?”
[ZH] 苦荞假装做错事的样子：“记下了。”
[EN] “Princess Consort Yi,” Yao Qingyan stressed.
[ZH] “我得赶紧穿了衣裳去见贵人。”
[EN] Qianli Xun shot upright. “Kuqiao! Why didn’t you wake me sooner? We nearly slighted Her Highness!”
[ZH] 千里寻走下床，在苦荞的伺候下穿好衣裳，与姚轻琰一起来到外厅。
[EN] Kuqiao played right along. “You were so upset, Miss, and had just fallen asleep—I hated to wake you.”
[ZH] 昔日的闺中密友出现在眼前，乔梦容本有的一脸担忧即刻消失得无影无踪。
[EN] “I’m fine—Her Highness comes first!” Qianli Xun scolded for show. “Remember: whenever Her Highness visits, even if I’m sick in bed, get me up right away!”
[ZH] 千里寻与婆婆一起拜见贵人
[EN] Yao Qingyan smiled in approval while Kuqiao bowed her head. “Yes, Miss.”
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“让仪王妃久等。”
[EN] “Let me dress quick and greet Her Highness.”
[ZH] 千里寻则说：“仪王妃大驾光临，淑真受宠若惊，若有怠慢，还请见谅。”
[EN] Slipping out of bed with Kuqiao’s help, Qianli Xun came out to the main room with Yao Qingyan and curtsied beside her mother-in-law.
[ZH] 乔梦容轻松之后，很是不屑：“我来见你没什么事，你既然在，我待着也无趣。”
[EN] The sight of her former best friend standing right there wiped every trace of panic from Qiao Mengrong’s face.
[ZH] 乔梦容说完，拂袖而去。
[EN] “Forgive the wait, Your Highness,” Yao Qingyan said.
[ZH] 经过外面时，陈江林对着仪王妃的背影大声说：“恭送贵人！”
[EN] “Your Highness honors my humble room,” Qianli Xun added. “Forgive any slight.”
[ZH] 屋内，姚轻琰甚是疑惑：“这就走了？贵人闹这一出是何意？”
[EN] Relieved and back to frosty disdain, Qiao Mengrong rose. “Nothing important—now that I see you’re here, staying is boring.” With a flick of her sleeve, she swept out as Chen Jianglin called out from the yard, “Safe journey, Your Highness!”
[ZH] 千里寻多少已猜出，估计是找不到仪王给闹的。多亏她回来得及时，否则梦容绝对会怀疑她跟仪王在一起。她确实与仪王在一起，只不过不是她想的那样。
[EN] Inside, Yao Qingyan frowned in bafflement. “Leaving just like that? What was that whole stunt about?”
[ZH] 还需向婆婆解释：“贵人吗？仗着身份尊贵，喜欢瞎闹，只要我们没得罪她便好。”
[EN] Qianli Xun knew the score—she had lost track of Prince Yi and freaked out. Good thing she got home in time, or Mengrong would’ve sworn they were together—which, well, they had been, just not like that. “Royals throw whims around because they can, Mother-in-law. So long as we didn’t offend her, no harm done.”
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“淑真啊，我知道因仪王抢亲的事，仪王妃对你有看法，以后你要多加注意，不要被人抓住把柄对你的名节说三道四。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan sighed. “Shuzhen, I know the Princess Consort holds a grudge over Prince Yi’s wedding raid. Be extra careful going forward so nobody finds an excuse to smear your reputation.”
[ZH] 抢亲之事陈家不计较，长辈还对她这么好，千里寻自是感动：“淑真记下，以后定会多加注意，不给公婆添麻烦。”
[EN] Touched that the Chens never blamed her for the wedding raid and still watched out for her, Qianli Xun nodded. “I’ll remember, Mother-in-law—I won’t bring trouble on you and Father-in-law.”
[ZH] “说什么麻烦不麻烦的？都是一家人。浩宇的事……”姚轻琰担心因为商妓上门闹事，淑真不愿再接受儿子。
[EN] “Don’t talk about trouble—we’re family. As for Haoyu...” Yao Qingyan hesitated, worried the courtesan scandal had soured Shuzhen on her son.
[ZH] 千里寻打算给婆婆吃颗定心丸：“婆婆放心，陈浩宇再不济，也是我夫君，事情如果不是他做下，我会陪他一起渡过难关；若是他做下，我情愿为他守寡，跟着公婆还有奶奶生活一辈子。”
[EN] Deciding to set her mother-in-law’s heart at rest, Qianli Xun said, “Don’t worry, Mother-in-law. Flawed as he is, Chen Haoyu is my husband. If he’s innocent, I’ll stand by him through this storm; if he’s guilty, I’ll live out my life as his widow right beside Grandmother and you and Father-in-law.”
[ZH] 姚轻琰眼眶变红，握住淑真的手：“浩宇能娶到你，是他的福气。”
[EN] Eyes reddening, Yao Qingyan clasped her hand. “Marrying you was the greatest blessing of Haoyu’s life.”
[ZH] “我能遇到奶奶和公婆这样的家人也是我的福气。”
[EN] “Having Grandmother and parents-in-law like you is my blessing too.”
[ZH] 婆媳二人说些表达感情的话，融洽的气氛令苦荞感动。
[EN] Watching the warm bond between mother-in-law and daughter-in-law brought tears to Kuqiao’s eyes.

#CH 285
[ZH] 大老爷和夫人走后，丑丫也回了西院。
[ZH] 苦荞把素心草堂的门关好，进到里屋，掀开被子，拿出男装，边整理边说：“可吓死我，差点儿穿帮，还好小姐回来得及时。不过，您到底是怎么进来的？”
[ZH] 千里寻从桌上放的茶壶里给自己倒杯水，喝完说道：“你家小姐我有飞檐走壁的本领，公婆出现时，我已在屋顶，迅速从后面的窗户进来，还好及时，没让你这丫头被主人打板子。”
[ZH] 苦荞已将男装整理好，放进收纳脏衣裳的竹筐：“老爷和夫人人好，绝对不会用板子打我。只是仪王妃来得突然，也不知她来做什么？”
[ZH] 千里寻坐到床边：“还能来干什么？肯定是找不到仪王怀疑到我头上。以前教她的话一句都没记在心上，就她那个紧张劲儿，有几个男人吓跑几个。”
[ZH] 苦荞走到小姐跟前：“仪王妃再怎么怀疑也不该怀疑到您头上。”
[ZH] “她还真怀疑对了，今晚我的确跟仪王在一起。”
[ZH] “啊！小姐，您可千万别做傻事！”
[ZH] “不是你想的那样，我与仪王为陈浩宇的案子同时盯上一个人，结果给碰上，也是凑巧。”
[ZH] “原来如此，恐怕仪王妃知道会瞎想。”
[EN] Once Eldest Master and Madam left and Chouya went back to the west wing, Kuqiao bolted the door, hurried into the inner room, and fished the men’s robe from beneath the quilt. “Nearly scared the soul out of me! One second later and we’d have been busted! How on earth did you get in?”
[ZH] “仪王不说，我不说，你也不说，她怎么会知道？”
[EN] Pouring herself water from the teapot and gulping it down, Qianli Xun grinned. “Your miss walks roofs and vaults walls, remember? I was already on the roof when my in-laws showed up, slipped straight through the back window—just in time to save your hide from a paddling.”
[ZH] “我会替小姐保密。”
[EN] Tossing the robe into the laundry basket, Kuqiao protested, “Master and Madam are kind—they’d never paddle me! Still, what got into Princess Consort Yi, barging in out of nowhere?”
[ZH] “不能告诉白风！”
[EN] Sitting on the bed’s edge, Qianli Xun sighed. “What else? Lost track of Prince Yi and jumped straight to suspecting me. Not one word I taught her before stuck—clutching that tight will scare off any man.”
[ZH] “坚决不会！”
[EN] “Suspect all she likes, she shouldn’t pin it on you!”
[ZH] ……
[EN] “Actually... she guessed right. I really was with Prince Yi tonight.”
[ZH] 离开陈府的路上，乔梦容的心情已经没有去陈府时那么紧张，可依然免不了胡乱猜测。
[EN] Kuqiao gasped. “Miss! Don’t do anything reckless!”
[ZH] 哥哥既然没有与朱淑真私会，他到底去了哪里？难道会去青楼？青楼的女子主动热情，什么不要脸的事都做得出来，没几个男人能招架得住，只怕哥哥也像其他男人一样。
[EN] “Not what you’re thinking! Both of us tailed the exact same suspect for Chen Haoyu’s case and bumped into each other—pure coincidence.”
[ZH] “喜兰，你说哥哥会不会去青楼？”
[EN] “Oh! Still, if Princess Consort Yi found out, she’d lose her mind.”
[ZH] 喜兰果断否认：“肯定不会！王爷是何等人？身份尊贵，岂能去那种地方？”
[EN] “Prince Yi won’t talk, I won’t talk, you won’t talk—how could she ever know?”
[ZH] 乔梦容点点头：“你说得对，哥哥不是那样的人，是我多虑。”
[EN] “My lips are sealed!”
[ZH] 喜兰继续安慰：“郡主别瞎想，幽栖居士既然老老实实在家，王爷必然是去忙公务，忙完一准儿回幽林别居。”
[EN] “Not even to Bai Feng!”
[ZH] 乔梦容想起以前的朱淑真在幽林别居对她说过的话。
[EN] “Not a single syllable!”
[ZH] 那时她正与乔公子热恋，还未对哥哥动心，真心诚意劝她把心态放好，不要把哥哥看得太重，否则会受伤。
[EN] Meanwhile, riding home from Chen Manor, Qiao Mengrong felt the knot in her stomach loosen, yet fresh doubts crept in. If her brother wasn’t with Zhu Shuzhen, where was he? A brothel? Courtesans were brazen and shameless—few men resisted them.
[ZH] 谁知抢走哥哥心的人竟然是她，乔梦容便不再把她说过的话当回事。与哥哥圆房后，她将哥哥看得更加重要，越是重视，越怕失去，越想除去让她无法心安之人。
[EN] “Xilan, think Brother went to a brothel?”
[ZH] 陈浩东正在实施计划，按照乔梦容的意思，应该直接让蒋爷的人杀了朱淑真。可陈家大公子偏偏要先实施他准备好的两件麻烦事。以后还需依赖他跑腿，乔梦容不想计较太多，那就让朱淑真多活几日。
[EN] “Not a chance!” Xilan denied flat-out. “Who is His Highness? A man of his rank would never set foot in a place like that!”
[ZH] 回到幽林别居，乔梦容第一件事便是问守在门口的下人：“哥哥可回来？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong nodded. “You’re right—Brother isn’t that type. I’m overthinking.”
[ZH] 下人回道：“王爷前脚刚回来，王妃您就回来了。”
[EN] “Don’t torture yourself, Princess,” Xilan soothed. “Since Youqi Jushi is sitting safe at home, His Highness must be tied up on official business and heading back to Youlin Retreat right now.”
[ZH] 乔梦容的脸上露出笑容，她已等不及，飞快地跑着，希望能追上哥哥。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong thought back to the old days when Zhu Shuzhen had urged her at Youlin Retreat not to cling so hard to her brother lest she get hurt. Back then she was head over heels for Young Master Qiao and hadn’t fallen for Brother yet. Once Zhu Shuzhen stole Brother’s heart, every word she ever said rang hollow. Consummating the marriage had only made Qiao Mengrong prize him more—and terrified of losing him, she wanted the threat erased for good.
[ZH] 幽林别居到处挂着灯笼，夜晚的道路看得清楚，下人也将道路清理干净，没有任何阻挡之物。喜兰不用担心王妃被绊倒，也跟着跑起来。
[EN] Chen Haodong was working his angle. Left to her, she’d have had Master Jiang’s men kill Zhu Shuzhen straightaway, yet Eldest Young Master Chen insisted on setting off his two traps first. Needing him as her errand boy for now, she let Zhu Shuzhen breathe a few extra days.
[ZH] 当乔梦容跑回院子，看到哥哥即将进屋的背影，她喘着气兴奋地喊声：“哥哥！”
[EN] Arriving at Youlin Retreat, Qiao Mengrong’s first question to the gatekeeper was, “Is Brother back?”
[ZH] 申简辰停下脚步，转过身，他感到纳闷，为何梦容一脸急切的样子？只见梦容飞快地扑进他的怀里，将他紧紧抱住。
[EN] “His Highness stepped through the gate the moment before you arrived, Your Highness!”
[ZH] “哥哥为何回来这么晚？我以为要失去哥哥。”
[EN] Her face lit up. Unable to wait, she hiked her skirts and sprinted along the lantern-lit, freshly swept walkways toward his courtyard, Xilan jogging right behind.
[ZH] 梦容的样子显得十分可怜，申简辰用手在她的头上摸摸：“怎么会？再晚我都会回来。”
[EN] Spotting her brother’s back just about to cross his threshold, she cried out breathless, “Brother!”
[ZH] “哥哥身上的衣衫料子实在普通。”
[EN] Turning in surprise at her urgency, Shen Jianchen barely caught his footing before she threw herself into his arms and clung tight. “Why are you back so late, Brother? I thought I’d lost you!”
[ZH] “临时买的，装扮起来跟踪可疑之人才不容易被发现。”
[EN] Pity softening him, Shen Jianchen stroked her hair. “How could that happen? No matter how late, I’ll always come home.”
[ZH] “哥哥是去办案？”
[EN] “The cloth on your robe is so coarse.”
[ZH] “是。”
[EN] “Bought it on the fly—easier to tail a suspect without standing out.”
[ZH] “可是陈浩宇的案子？”
[EN] “Working a case?”
[ZH] 因担心乔梦容胡思乱想，或者问东问西，申简辰开始编起瞎话：“不是，是其他案子。”
[EN] “Yes.”
[ZH] 乔梦容并不关心案子的事，陈浩东为了个人利益捣鼓出的事，又不能置朱淑真于死地，她才懒得理会。她放开哥哥，牵起他的手：“进屋，我帮哥哥换衣裳。”
[EN] “Chen Haoyu’s case?”
[ZH] 两位主人进屋之后，喜兰将门从外面关好。有王爷在，王妃心情好，他们会互相照顾对方，无须她在一旁添乱。她只需站在门外，若里面有需要，她再进屋伺候。
[EN] Not wanting to set off a storm of questions and suspicions, he fibbed. “No—a different one.”
[ZH] 卧房内，乔梦容为哥哥褪去衣衫：“为何没见白风？”
[EN] Uninterested in Chen Haodong’s minor harassment anyway since it wouldn’t kill Zhu Shuzhen, Qiao Mengrong let go and took his hand. “Come inside and let me help you change.”
[ZH] 白风去竹林之事绝对不能让梦容知道。竹林在幽栖居士的名下，建竹屋是因听苦蕨说竹林的东家想建，申简辰才有此想法，若照实说，恐怕梦容多疑，问来问去十分麻烦，干脆编个瞎话。
[EN] Once they stepped in, Xilan pulled the door shut from outside. When His Highness was home and Her Highness was happy, they didn’t need a third wheel hovering indoors; she’d wait outside in case they called.
[ZH] “最近司理院忙，我不想让他来回跑，让他在司理院住上一段时日。”
[EN] Helping him out of his robe inside the bedchamber, Qiao Mengrong asked, “Where’s Bai Feng?”
[ZH] “哥哥身边不能没人伺候。”
[EN] She could never know Bai Feng was out in the bamboo grove—that grove belonged to Youqi Jushi, and he had only started building the bamboo cottage after hearing Kujue mention the grove’s owner wanted one. Telling the truth would spark endless interrogation, so he lied smooth. “Judicial Office is swamped—I had him stay over there a while to save the commute.”
[ZH] 申简辰主动抱住梦容：“要不是你在幽林别居，我也不会回来，有你伺候我，根本不需要白风。”
[EN] “You can’t go without someone waiting on you.”
[ZH] 这招果然管用，梦容不再问东问西，脸贴在哥哥的胸前：“我想给哥哥生个孩子。”
[EN] Pulling her into his arms, Shen Jianchen murmured, “If not for you waiting at Youlin Retreat, I wouldn’t have come back tonight either. With you taking care of me, who needs Bai Feng?”
[ZH] “你想怎样都行，我听你的。”申简辰只想让梦容开心，用他的爱化去她对千里寻的仇恨，也许有一日，她们的关系会恢复如初。
[EN] It worked like magic. Dropping the questions, she pressed her cheek against his chest. “I want to give you a child, Brother.”
[ZH] 喜兰见屋内的灯被熄灭，露出会心的笑容，她没必要再站着，安心回屋睡觉。
[EN] “Whatever you want, I’m yours.” All he hoped was to make her happy and melt away her hatred for Qianli Xun—maybe one day the two could even mend their bond.
[ZH] ……
[EN] Outside, seeing the bedchamber lamp go out, Xilan smiled to herself and went off to sleep.
[ZH] 天亮后，申简辰刚回到司理院，屁股还没坐热，韩谷走进来拜道：“王爷，昨日我办完事回来没见到您，今日一早来向您汇报查探的情况。”
[EN] At dawn, Shen Jianchen had barely sat down at the Judicial Office when Han Gu stepped in and bowed. “Your Highness, I missed you when I got back last night, so I came first thing to report.”
[ZH] 申简辰放下卷宗：“可查到重要线索？”
[EN] Setting down his dossier, Shen Jianchen asked, “Turn up any solid leads?”
[ZH] 韩谷摇摇头：“没有，不过那些人一口咬定是陈浩宇干的。”
[EN] Han Gu shook his head. “Nothing solid—Scarface’s cronies all swear Chen Haoyu did it.”
[ZH] “他们可曾亲眼看到？”
[EN] “Did they see it with their own eyes?”
[ZH] “那倒没有，他们说，陈浩宇回城里后，疤子给他们些银子，让他们去城里逛。”
[EN] “No. They said right after Chen Haoyu returned to town, Scarface handed them silver and sent them off to roam the city.”
[ZH] “疤子很可疑。他们有没有提到陈家乡下宅子里的下人被调走之事？”
[EN] “Scarface reeks of guilt. Did they mention the staff being pulled away from the country house?”
[ZH] “我正想向王爷汇报此事。跟着疤子的人说，他们还没离开时，陈家让把宅子里的下人全部调走，只留管家一人。此事与他们无关，也没多想，只想着陈公子回来见没有下人，肯定会生气。他们还提到一件事……”
[EN] “Right—before they left, orders came from Chen Manor pulling every servant out except the caretaker. They thought nothing of it except figuring Young Master Chen would be mad when he got back. One more detail...”
[ZH] “什么事？”
[EN] “Out with it.”
[ZH] “疤子准备了一壶好酒，他们几个本想尝尝，疤子不允许，说是给陈公子和春金兰预备的。”
[EN] “Scarface had a jug of fine wine ready. The boys wanted a taste, and Scarface snapped no—claimed it was saved for Young Master Chen and Chun Jinlan.”
[ZH] “很有可能这壶酒是给管家预备的。”
[EN] “Odds are that wine was meant for the caretaker.”
[ZH] 话音刚落，温信带人回来，也将昨晚查探的情况一一汇报。
[EN] Right on cue, Wen Xin returned with his squad to deliver his full report from the night before.

#CH 286
[ZH] 原来昨晚仪王走后，温信安排差役将宅子里的下人全部唤出来一一查问；他则趁着夜色仔细观察屋外的情景。
[ZH] 屋外景色甚好，除花花草草外，还栽种着一棵香樟树，正处于花期，时不时能嗅到芬芳的花香。
[ZH] 温信走到花树旁，仔细观察这棵树，树干较粗，躲个人应该没有问题。再瞧树根处，绕着树根堆着一圈凋零的花瓣，显然是下人专门把落下的花瓣扫到树下。
[ZH] 有一男仆从旁边经过，温信叫住他：“你过来一下。”
[ZH] 男仆走过去：“都头有何吩咐？”
[ZH] 温信指着树下堆的细碎花瓣：“为何要把凋谢的花瓣堆在树下？”
[ZH] 男仆瞧了瞧，回道：“回都头，陈府的花匠让这么做的，他说花瓣最终会化为泥土，是上好的肥料，用来滋养花树，来年花会开得更艳。”
[ZH] “这么说去年的花瓣也会留在树根处？”
[ZH] “按理说是这样。”
[ZH] 温信心想，去年三月初三香樟树还未到花期，应该没有花瓣落下，即使有人躲在树后瞧见什么，不小心掉落下东西，也容易被人发现，捡了去再正常不过。再说一切不过是他的猜测而已，也许根本没有人躲在树后。
[ZH] “你去忙吧。”
[EN] After Prince Yi had left the country house the night before, Wen Xin had his constables pull every servant out for individual questioning while he prowled the grounds outside the detention room by moonlight.
[ZH] 男仆行完礼离开。
[EN] Lush flowers and shrubs filled the yard, anchored by a mature camphor tree in full bloom, drifting sweet fragrance through the air. Stepping up to inspect it, Wen Xin noted the trunk was thick enough to hide a person—and around its roots lay a neat ring of swept-up fallen petals.
[ZH] 温信觉得自己关注一棵花树太过好笑，正打算观察其他地方，一位差役过来。
[EN] Spotting a male servant passing by, Wen Xin hailed him. “Come here a second.”
[ZH] “温都头，我等已将宅子里的所有下人问过一遍，发现三月初三那日有个丫鬟没有被调走。”
[EN] “Yes, Chief Constable?”
[ZH] 温信急切地问：“谁？把她给我叫来。”
[EN] Pointing at the heap around the trunk, Wen Xin asked, “Why pile dead petals under the tree?”
[ZH] “丫鬟名唤阿叶，当天告了假，回去看望家人。”
[EN] “Chen Manor’s gardener ordered it, sir. Says fallen petals rot into rich soil that feeds the roots so next year’s blooms grow brighter.”
[ZH] “告假？”温信又感到失望，“还是将她唤来，再将管家叫来。”
[EN] “So last year’s petals stayed piled right here too?”
[ZH] 差役退去，不一会儿管家与阿叶一起过来。
[EN] “Stands to reason, sir.”
[ZH] 管家说：“温都头有什么要问的尽管问。”
[EN] Last year on the third day of the third month, the camphor wouldn’t have bloomed yet—still, even if someone hid behind the trunk and dropped something, somebody could have swept it up later. Dismissing the servant, Wen Xin smirked at himself for obsessing over a tree—right as a constable jogged up.
[ZH] 温信说：“你叫阿叶？去年三月初三告了假？”
[EN] “Chief Constable Wen, we questioned every single servant. Turns out one maid wasn’t transferred away on the third day of the third month!”
[ZH] 阿叶显得十分紧张：“是，奴婢的娘生了病，不得已才告假回家，管家可以……可以作证。”
[EN] Wen Xin’s eyes lit up. “Who? Bring her here!”
[ZH] 管家说：“的确有此事，还是我同意她回家一趟。”
[EN] “Maid named Aye—she took leave that day to visit her family.”
[ZH] 温信问：“你是何时回到宅子？”
[EN] “Leave?” Disappointment prickled, yet he pressed on. “Bring her anyway—and the caretaker.”
[ZH] 阿叶更加紧张：“我……我……我……”
[EN] Moments later the caretaker walked up with Aye in tow.
[ZH] 管家说：“这孩子胆小，被官爷问得都说不出话来，这个问题我来回答，本来我只给她两个时辰的假，可她回来已是第三日，还被我狠狠批评一顿。”
[EN] “Ask whatever you like, Chief Constable,” the caretaker offered.
[ZH] 依然问不出什么，温信失落地说：“下去吧。”
[EN] “You’re Aye? You took leave on the third day of the third month last year?”
[ZH] “温都头若有事，尽管差人来问。”管家说完，带着阿叶离开。
[EN] Aye tensed up. “Yes, sir—my mother fell ill, so I had to go home. The caretaker can vouch for me.”
[ZH] 什么线索都找不出，温信又开始关注香樟树的树根处，他走过去绕着观察一遍，取下身上的剑，用剑鞘将枯萎的花瓣拨了一遍，什么都没发现。
[EN] “True enough,” the caretaker confirmed. “I approved her leave myself.”
[ZH] 他又用剑鞘将花瓣下面的泥土使劲拨开，几乎拨了两遍，毫无发现。正当想要放弃之时，剑鞘碰到一个硬硬的东西。
[EN] “When did you get back to the house?” Wen Xin asked.
[ZH] 温信蹲下，用手将硬硬的东西捡起来，剥去上面的泥土，原来是一个小巧玲珑的木刻桃心，有拇指第一个关节那么大，一头拴着一条细绳，因长期埋在泥土里，已看不出细绳的颜色。
[EN] Aye stammered, sweating. “I... I... I...”
[ZH] 温信把玩一番，心下思忖，不知这玩意儿是否与案情相关？不管相不相关，还是拿去问问。
[EN] “Timid girl—scared speechless in front of officials,” the caretaker jumped in. “I’ll tell you: I only gave her two shichen off, yet she didn’t show back up till the third day! Gave her a thorough chewing-out for it too.”
[ZH] 他吩咐管家将所有下人聚集而来，大家排好队。温信把木刻桃心拿出来，举在众人面前。因天黑，大家并未看清是什么东西。温信解释一番，许多人摇头称根本没见过。
[EN] Still a dead end. Discouraged, Wen Xin waved them off.
[ZH] 一位丫鬟说：“天太黑我看不清，官爷能不能拿近些让我瞧瞧？”
[EN] With nothing else to go on, his gaze drifted back to the camphor tree’s roots. Walking around the trunk, he unhooked his sword and poked through the withered petals with the scabbard—nothing. Digging deeper into the dirt beneath, turning the soil over twice, he was about to quit when the scabbard tip struck something hard.
[ZH] 温信走到丫鬟身边，将木刻桃心展示在她面前，丫鬟借着月色仔细瞧一遍：“我认得，是阿叶的，她不小心弄丢，没想到被官爷找到。”
[EN] Crouching down, he picked it out and brushed off the dirt: a tiny, carved wooden peach-heart charm about the size of a thumb joint, knotted with a thin cord so soil-stained its original color was gone.
[ZH] 阿叶再次被提到，温信走到阿叶身旁，站在她前面的丫鬟闪到一边，其他下人也都让出路来。
[EN] Rolling it in his palm, unsure if it mattered, he had the caretaker line up the entire staff and held the charm up. Too dark to see clearly, most shook their heads even after he described it.
[ZH] 温信将木刻桃心展示在阿叶眼前：“看清楚，可是你的？”
[EN] One maid spoke up. “Too dark to tell, sir—mind bringing it closer?”
[ZH] 阿叶老实承认：“是……是……是我的。”
[EN] Stepping over, Wen Xin held it in the moonlight. The maid peered close. “I know that! It’s Aye’s! She lost it ages ago—can’t believe you found it, sir!”
[ZH] “为何会在香樟树下？”
[EN] Every eye turned to Aye. Parting the crowd, Wen Xin walked straight up and held the wooden heart right before her face. “Look close—yours?”
[ZH] “我……我……我不知道，也许是不小心掉的。”
[EN] Aye folded at once. “Yes—yes, it’s mine.”
[ZH] 多年办案的经验，使得温信十分敏感，总觉得阿叶知道些什么，不如炸她一炸。
[EN] “How did it end up under the camphor tree?”
[ZH] 他收起木刻桃心，装作一副笃定的样子：“不小心？我看未必！我来帮你回答。去年三月初三，你请假回家，四个时辰后回来，天色已晚，宅子里没有一个下人，你到处找，来到这里，结果看到不该看的，你吓坏了躲到香樟树后，慌乱中将木刻桃心遗失在树下。木刻桃心过于小巧，加之木色在泥土不容易被发现。等到香樟树到了花期，渐渐被掉落下来的花瓣掩盖，就连你自己也找不到，是也不是？”
[EN] “I—I don’t know... must’ve dropped it by accident.”
[ZH] 阿叶被吓坏，跪在地上：“官爷饶命！不关我的事！我只是不小心看到了而已！”
[EN] Years of detective work told Wen Xin she was hiding something big. Time to bluff her wide open.
[ZH] 假想的情节与事实相符，温信深深吐口气，案情总算有进展，可以回去交差。
[EN] Pocketing the charm, he spoke dead-certain. “By accident? Don’t think so! Let me tell you what really happened. On the third day of the third month last year, you went home on leave and came back four shichen later after dark. Not a servant in sight. Looking around, you came right here, saw something you shouldn’t have, panicked, and ducked behind that camphor tree—dropping your wooden charm in the dark. Too tiny and dirt-colored to spot, and once the camphor blossomed, fallen petals buried it before you could find it. Am I right or am I right?”
[ZH] 温信连夜将阿叶带回司理院，找地方让她住一晚，第二天面见参军大人。
[EN] Terrified, Aye hit her knees. “Spare me, Your Lordship! Nothing to do with me—I just saw it by accident!”
[ZH] ……
[EN] Dead on target! Wen Xin let out a triumphant breath, hauled Aye back to the Judicial Office that night, put her up in a room, and brought her before Prince Yi at dawn.
[ZH] 司理院内，阿叶跪在地上，老实交代那天她看到的情景。
[EN] Kneeling in the Judicial Office hall, Aye spilled every word.
[ZH] ﻿“大人，奴婢那日回到宅子，天刚刚变黑，宅子的大门闭着，并未关，我便推门而入，发现一个下人都没有，就去找管家，可管家喝得不省人事。我打算先回丫鬟房，半路上想起宅子里还关着一位姑娘，是陈公子抓来的，便想去瞧瞧人是否还在。结果……结果……”
[EN] “Your Lordship, I got back right after dark. The main gate stood closed yet unlocked, so I pushed in. Not a soul around, and the caretaker was passed out dead drunk. Heading back to the maids’ quarters, I remembered the girl Young Master Chen had locked up and went to check if she was still there. And then...”
[ZH] 申简辰问：“结果怎样？”
[EN] “And then what?” Shen Jianchen urged.
[ZH] 阿叶犹豫片刻，坚决说道：“结果我发现常跟着陈公子的疤子趁陈公子不在，进屋把那姑娘给糟蹋了，我被吓坏，在树后躲起来，想趁疤子走后，把那姑娘给放了。当门再次打开，我瞧见疤子扛着个袋子，仔细看，那袋子里装的肯定是个人，我猜应该是那位姑娘。没有机会救她，我只好放弃，估摸着疤子走远后，我打算回丫鬟房，结果从屋里又走出一人……”
[EN] Hesitating one beat, Aye blurted, “I saw Scarface—the thug who always trails Young Master Chen—slip into her room and defile her while Young Master was away! Scared out of my wits, I hid behind the camphor tree, hoping to untie the girl once Scarface left. Next thing, the door opened and Scarface hauled out a bulging sack—plainly a person inside, had to be that girl! No way to save her, so I waited till Scarface cleared off—and just as I turned to go, someone else walked out of that room!”
[ZH] 终于出现关键信息，申简辰急切地问：“是谁？”
[EN] Paydirt! Shen Jianchen leaned forward. “Who?”
[ZH] 阿叶回道：“那人披着黑色披风，头和脸都被遮住，根本看不清，不过从身形判断应该是个男子。当时我又被吓坏，继续在树后躲好，我的木刻桃心可能就是那时不小心弄丢，后来到树下找过几回，都没找到。遇到此事，我很害怕，担心若出事会问到我头上，便连夜又跑回家中，结果还是被官爷发现。”
[EN] “Couldn’t tell—they wore a long black cloak hiding head and face completely, though the build looked like a man’s. Petrified all over again, I stayed crouched behind the tree—must’ve dropped my wooden heart right then, searched for it later and never found it. Scared sick I’d get dragged in if anything blew up, I ran straight back home that same night—and somehow your officer caught me anyway.”
[ZH] “穿黑色披风的人可是陈浩宇？”
[EN] “Was the man in the black cloak Chen Haoyu?”
[ZH] 阿叶摇摇头：“不是，陈公子绝对不会如此装扮，他抓过好几回姑娘，都关在那间屋里，每次都正大光明，从不避讳，我想应该是别人。”
[EN] Aye shook her head hard. “No! Young Master Chen would never skulk around in disguise—he’s locked plenty of girls in that room, always bold as brass in broad daylight. Had to be someone else.”
[ZH] “你为何会认为是疤子糟蹋了那姑娘而不是穿黑色披风的人？”
[EN] “Why did you assume Scarface defiled her rather than the cloaked man?”
[ZH] 阿叶为难道：“开始我没看到那人，只看到疤子进去，然后听到那姑娘喊叫的声音，才一直认为是疤子所为。”
[EN] Aye looked torn. “I didn’t see the cloaked man at first—only saw Scarface go in and heard the girl screaming, so I assumed it was Scarface.”
[ZH] “阿叶，你可敢在公堂之上替陈浩宇作证？”
[EN] “Aye, dare you testify in court on Chen Haoyu’s behalf?”
[ZH] 阿叶说：“我虽很讨厌陈公子的做法，可那姑娘的确不是被陈公子糟蹋，我愿意为他作证。”
[EN] “Much as I despise Young Master Chen’s ways,” Aye said firm, “he didn’t defile that girl. I’ll testify for him.”

#CH 287
[ZH] 陈浩宇的案子又开始重新审理，陈家来了陈江林、陈浩东和陈浩飞。
[ZH] 陈浩东今日来心情不同上次，他知道仪王必然会披露他的用心，他已做好万全准备。
[ZH] 差役押着陈浩宇和春金兰进入，待他们跪下后，才站立两侧。
[ZH] 申简辰开口说：“经过昨日查探，案情有所进展，将陈府的王管家带进来。”
[ZH] 王管家走进后，跪在地上：“大人，小的昨晚上，已将知道的全部告知温都头。”
[ZH] “当着众人的面再说一遍。”
[ZH] 王管家又把昨晚说的话重复一遍：“去年三月初三那日，的确是我亲自差人把乡下宅子里的下人调走，只因陈府在城里的其他宅子需要彻底打扫，人手不够。”
[ZH] “谁安排你做的？”
[ZH] 王管家回道：“大公子。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu’s hearing resumed, with Chen Jianglin, Chen Haodong, and Chen Haofei attending from the gallery.
[ZH] “陈家的老爷们可知道？”
[EN] Chen Haodong came in braced this time—knowing Prince Yi would try exposing his hand, he had his defenses locked tight.
[ZH] 王管家回道：“此事一直是大公子负责，无须告知老爷们。”
[EN] Constables brought in Chen Haoyu and Chun Jinlan to kneel, then flanked the hall.
[ZH] 陈浩东心想：“看来仪王对我还有怀疑，要不怎么问得仔细？还好王管家的证词只能说明我把人调走，并不能证明其他。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen opened. “Yesterday’s investigation turned up new leads. Bring in Butler Wang of Chen Manor.”
[ZH] 申简辰转向陈浩东：“大公子能否解释一下，为何偏偏在去年三月初三把宅子里的下人调走？”
[EN] Butler Wang entered and knelt. “Your Lordship, I told Chief Constable Wen everything I know last night.”
[ZH] 陈浩东起身行礼：“回禀大人，陈家在城里的宅子每年都会安排下人彻底清理一遍，至于为何是三月初三，实属偶然，我也没想到那日浩宇会把人关在宅子里。”
[EN] “Repeat it in front of everyone.”
[ZH] 申简辰轻蔑一笑：“大公子请坐，待会儿还会有问题请教。”
[EN] Butler Wang recited clear. “On the third day of the third month last year, I personally sent orders pulling every servant out of the country house because Chen Manor’s city residences needed a thorough cleaning and ran short on hands.”
[ZH] 陈浩东坐下，内心虽不安，倒也不害怕，他已安排好，自己绝不会被牵扯进去。
[EN] “Who gave you that order?”
[ZH] 申简辰对王管家说：“话已问完，你退下吧。”
[EN] “Eldest Young Master.”
[ZH] 王管家行完礼，起身离开公堂。
[EN] “Did the Chen Masters know?”
[ZH] “传阿叶与疤子！”
[EN] “Eldest Young Master always handles those chores—no need to inform the Masters.”
[ZH] 阿叶与疤子进入后也都跪下行礼。
[EN] *Still gunning for me,* Chen Haodong thought. *Good thing transferring servants proves nothing on its own.*
[ZH] 有新的证人来，陈浩东紧张起来，他仔细瞧阿叶，应该是乡下宅子里的丫鬟。难道她知道些什么？他仔细回想，那晚掩饰得极好，绝不会被轻易认出。
[EN] Shen Jianchen turned to him. “Care to explain why you pulled every servant out on that exact day, Eldest Young Master?”
[ZH] 申简辰先问疤子：“疤子，如果你想说出实情还有机会，否则一会儿有人证明你做假证，会罪加一等。”
[EN] Rising to bow, Chen Haodong answered smooth. “Chen Manor deep-cleans its town properties every year, Your Lordship. Landing on the third day of the third month was pure chance—how could I know Haoyu had locked a girl up there that day?”
[ZH] 疤子依然坚持昨日的证词：“大人在上，小的句句属实。一切都是陈公子做的，小人只是帮凶。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen gave a cool smirk. “Sit—I’ll have more questions shortly.”
[ZH] 申简辰故意问：“哪个陈公子？堂上可有三位陈家公子？仔细些，千万不要说错。”
[EN] Chen Haodong sat, uneasy yet unafraid—he had his firewall in place.
[ZH] 疤子被问得紧张起来：“陈……陈家二……二公子。”
[EN] Dismissing Butler Wang, Shen Jianchen called, “Bring in Aye and Scarface!”
[ZH] 申简辰又向阿叶问道：“阿叶，把你在去年三月初三晚上看到的再向大家说一遍。”
[EN] Both entered and knelt. Seeing a new witness—one of the country-house maids—tightened Chen Haodong’s nerves. *No way she recognized me in that cloak!*
[ZH] 阿叶回道：“是，大人。那晚，我从家里回来，宅子里没有下人，管家也喝醉，我想趁机把二公子抢来的人给放了，可却看到疤子进入屋内，我吓得躲在树后等待机会。里面传出那姑娘的喊叫声，我当时一心认为是疤子将那姑娘给糟蹋，可当疤子把那姑娘用麻袋扛走后，里面又走出一人，穿着黑色披风，头和脸被挡住，根本看不清。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen warned Scarface first. “Last chance to tell the truth, Scarface. Once proof shows you perjured yourself, your penalty doubles.”
[ZH] 陈浩东轻松一些，心想：“真是百密一疏，竟然没注意到还有一个请假回家的丫鬟，还好她没看到我的脸。”
[EN] Scarface stuck to his script. “Every word I spoke is true, Your Lordship! Young Master Chen did it all; I was just his lackey.”
[ZH] 申简辰继续问：“穿黑色披风的人可是陈浩宇？”
[EN] “Which Young Master Chen?” Shen Jianchen asked sharp. “Three of them sit in this hall—mind you don’t misspeak.”
[ZH] 阿叶否认道：“不是！二公子做这种事情从来不藏着掖着，再说那人的个头比二公子矮一些，跟……跟大公子的个头差不多。”
[EN] Scarface gulped. “Second... Second Young Master Chen.”
[ZH] 陈浩东被吓坏，很快掩饰道：“你这丫头怎么说话呢？这是公堂，可不能随便找人比对！”
[EN] Turning to Aye, Shen Jianchen said, “Tell everyone what you witnessed on the night of the third day of the third month last year.”
[ZH] 阿叶一脸歉意：“奴婢一时着急，说错话，还请大公子原谅。”
[EN] “Yes, Your Lordship. When I got back from home that night, every servant was gone and the caretaker was dead drunk. I went to let the girl Second Young Master snatched go—only to see Scarface enter the room, so I hid behind the tree to wait. Screams came from inside, so I figured Scarface was violating her. Then Scarface hauled her out in a sack—and right after, a second person walked out wrapped head to face in a black cloak!”
[ZH] 陈浩东立刻面向仪王：“大人，这丫鬟喜欢说错话，您千万不能把她的话太当真，说不定那晚根本没什么穿黑色披风的人，她只是一时眼花看错而已。”
[EN] Chen Haodong exhaled a little. *Missed one maid on leave—lucky she never saw my face.*
[ZH] 丫鬟强调道：“大人信我！我绝对不会看错！”
[EN] “Was that cloaked man Chen Haoyu?” Shen Jianchen asked.
[ZH] “陈大公子先别着急下定论，丫鬟说的是真是假我自有判断。”申简辰又转向陈浩宇，“陈浩宇，把你第二日回到宅子的情形说一遍。”
[EN] “No!” Aye said flat-out. “Second Young Master never sneaks around for this sort of thing—plus that man stood shorter than Second Young Master, right around... around Eldest Young Master’s height!”
[ZH] 丫鬟的证词对儿子有利，仪王的问题也显然认为浩宇是第二日回到宅子，陈江林悬着的心终于放下。可浩东的心思，让他猜不透。
[EN] Jolted cold, Chen Haodong barked to cover, “Watch your mouth, girl! This is a court of law—don’t go comparing random people!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇解释道：“我是第二日午时回到宅子，没见到下人，心中奇怪了一阵，便不再去关注。心里念着抓来的女子，快步进屋后，却发现人已不见，到处找疤子等人也找不到。我以为女子逃走，疤子等人担心无法向我交差，也躲起来。便没将此事放在心上，很快忘记，再见到疤子他们，也未提及此事。”
[EN] Aye flinched. “Forgive me, Eldest Young Master—spoke out of turn in my nerves.”
[ZH] 春金兰此时比谁都难过，难道那晚是疤子糟蹋了自己？
[EN] Chen Haodong appealed straight to the bench. “Don’t put stock in a jittery maid’s chatter, Your Lordship! Likely there was no cloaked man at all—just her eyes playing tricks in the dark!”
[ZH] 她只记得那晚屋子里很黑，她很害怕。进来一人，二话不说，将一个布子盖在她的脸上，然后将她身上绑的绳子解开后，把她的双脚捆住。
[EN] “Believe me, Your Lordship!” Aye insisted. “My eyes didn’t fool me!”
[ZH] 双手被绑着，她难以反抗，加之对方力气大，她怎么用身躯抵抗都毫无用处。她撕心裂肺地喊着，没有任何人前来帮忙，只能任凭对方将她的衣裳扒开一部分，以粗暴的手段强行占有。
[EN] “Hold your conclusions, Eldest Young Master—I’ll judge her credibility myself.” Turning to Chen Haoyu, Shen Jianchen asked, “Describe returning to the house the next day.”
[ZH] 占有她的人从头到尾没有说一句话，唯一的印象是那人有力的身体和留在她耳边粗犷的喘息声。
[EN] Seeing the tide turn in his son’s favor lifted the boulder off Chen Jianglin’s chest, though Haodong’s jumpy reaction left him baffled.
[ZH] 春金兰大喊道：“大人！丫鬟一定是胡说！陈浩宇把我抓来，不是他害我又会是谁？”
[EN] “I got back around noon the next day,” Chen Haoyu explained. “Wondered a second why no servants were around, then brushed it off and went straight to the room. The girl was gone, and Scarface and the boys were nowhere to be found. Figured she’d escaped and the boys hid out of fear I’d chew them out, so I dropped it and forgot—never even brought it up when I saw them again.”
[ZH] 申简辰厉声说：“是否胡说，本官自有定论，还轮不上你来断定。”
[EN] Chun Jinlan’s world spun—had Scarface been the one who ruined her? All she remembered from that pitch-black room was someone throwing a cloth over her face, untying her body only to lash her ankles together, pinning her down with brute strength while she screamed her lungs out, tearing her clothes open, and taking her in silence—not a single word, only heavy, coarse breathing in her ear.
[ZH] 春金兰不敢再言语，她摸摸藏在袖子里的匕首，事态如此发展，对自己大为不利，待会儿瞅准时机用死威胁陈家人，也许还会获得机会。
[EN] “Lies!” Chun Jinlan shrieked. “The maid’s lying, Your Lordship! Chen Haoyu snatched me—who else could it be?!”
[ZH] 申简辰面向疤子：“疤子，你作何解释？”
[EN] “I decide who’s lying, not you!” Shen Jianchen snapped.
[ZH] 疤子强辩道：“大人，丫鬟信口胡说，人根本不是我糟蹋的。”
[EN] Cowed, she felt for the dagger in her sleeve. Things were tilting bad—one threat on her own life might flip the board.
[ZH] “穿黑色披风的人是谁？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen bored into Scarface. “Well, Scarface? Your explanation?”
[ZH] “还能是谁？只能是陈公子。”
[EN] “The maid’s making it up, Your Lordship! I didn’t touch her!”
[ZH] 陈浩东心中一惊：“难道疤子要如实招供？”
[EN] “Who was the man in the black cloak?”
[ZH] “哪个陈公子？”
[EN] “Who else? Young Master Chen!”
[ZH] “陈家……二公子。”
[EN] Chen Haodong’s heart lurched. *Is Scarface folding?!*
[ZH] 陈浩东松口气，一百两银子还是很管用。
[EN] “Which Young Master Chen?”
[ZH] 差役又将陈家乡下宅子的管家带进来。管家进入后，也是一顿磕头行礼。
[EN] “Second... Second Young Master Chen.”
[ZH] 申简辰问：“去年三月初三那晚，你喝的酒从何而来？”
[EN] Chen Haodong exhaled—worth every tael of that hundred!
[ZH] 管家老实交代：“是疤子给的，他说自己经常跟着二公子来乡下的宅子，跟我也算是老相识，晚上宅子里没人，不如痛饮几杯。我本来就好两口，满口答应，谁知那酒甚烈，没喝几盅，给昏睡过去。”
[EN] Next, constables brought in the country-house caretaker, who kowtowed low.
[ZH] “我看不是酒烈，而是有人给酒中放了些作料，才致使你昏昏欲睡。”申简辰转向疤子，“还是老实交代，你跟管家喝酒，意欲何为？”
[EN] “Where did the wine you drank that night come from?” Shen Jianchen asked.
[ZH] 疤子吓得趴在地上：“大老爷！我的确是好心，单纯陪管家喝两口，哪知他不胜酒力……”
[EN] “Scarface brought it, Your Lordship. Said we were old acquaintances from his trips with Second Young Master and offered a drink since the place was empty. I love my wine, so I said yes—stuff must’ve been strong, knocked me cold after just a few cups.”
[ZH] 疤子不承认，申简辰只能用刑罚伺候：“你既不愿招供，先打你二十大板！”
[EN] “Not strong wine—drugged wine.” Shen Jianchen snapped his eyes back to Scarface. “Confess! Why did you drug the caretaker?”
[ZH] 一旁的差役将疤子按倒在地，取来板子，当着众人的面开始打。
[EN] Scarface flopped flat. “I swear I meant well, Your Lordship—just sharing a drink! How could I know he couldn’t hold his liquor?!”
[ZH] 疤子疼得吱哇乱叫，打到十下时，实在忍受不了，大声求饶：“大人！饶命！我……我……我说！”
[EN] “Stubborn, eh? Twenty strokes of the paddle!”
[ZH] 申简辰抬起手，差役收起板子。
[EN] Constables pinned Scarface down and brought the heavy bamboo down right across the court. Screaming bloody murder, Scarface broke on the tenth stroke. “Mercy, Your Lordship! I’ll talk! I’ll talk!”
[ZH] “若不照实说，再给你加十大板。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen raised a hand, halting the blows. “One lie and you get ten more.”

#CH 288
[ZH] 疤子抬头向陈浩东瞥一眼，陈浩东点了一下头，迅速将目光移到别处。
[ZH] 疤子心想：“一百两银子不少，不妨拼一拼。”
[ZH] 于是疤子决定承担下所有罪过：“去年三月初三的晚上，二公子的确没回宅子，春金兰也不是他糟蹋的。是我……是我见春金兰美貌，趁二公子不在想要与她亲热，怕其他兄弟看着眼馋先将他们支走，又担心管家发现，给酒里放了些睡圣散，他没喝几杯就睡着……”
[ZH] 听到这些，春金兰怒不可遏，打算向疤子扑去，被差役按住，只能大声吼道：“你胡说！明明是陈浩宇！”
[ZH] 疤子瞎编道：“我没胡说，你开始不愿意，我告诉你只要从了我，我会悄悄放你回家，以后也会照顾你的生活，你想通后便答应我。大人，我与她之间只能算是你情我愿。完事后，我又想多赚些银子，才又起歹心，将她打晕，卖到扬州青楼。”
[ZH] 再怒吼已经无用，被差役押着，春金兰没有机会掏出匕首以死威胁，只能暂且作罢，努力忍着，希望差役见她安静能很快放开她。
[ZH] 申简辰算是听出来，疤子想护住那位穿黑色披风的人：“穿黑色披风的人又是怎么回事？”
[ZH] 疤子继续编道：“没有此人，是丫鬟胡说。”
[EN] Scarface darted a glance at Chen Haodong, who gave one microscopic nod and looked away.
[ZH] 阿叶强调道：“我没有胡说，明明亲眼看到。”
[EN] *A hundred taels of silver—worth the gamble!*
[ZH] 疤子说：“天黑看错也正常。”
[EN] Taking the whole fall onto his own shoulders, Scarface blurted, “Second Young Master never came back that night, and he never touched Chun Jinlan! It was me! Saw how pretty she was, got itchy while Young Master was away, sent the boys off so they wouldn’t get jealous, and slipped Sleep-Sage Powder into the caretaker’s wine so he’d pass out...”
[ZH] 差役见春金兰老实许多，放开手站回原位。春金兰趁众人注意疤子之时，手伸入袖中，摸牢匕首。
[EN] Fury consumed Chun Jinlan. Lunging at Scarface before constables pinned her down, she screamed, “Liar! It was Chen Haoyu!”
[ZH] 申简辰心想，疤子的话矛盾重重，若真与春金兰你情我愿又何必使那么大力气？又是支人，又是睡圣散？阿叶听到喊叫声，显然也不是你情我愿该有的声音。若调走宅子的下人与此事相关，那位穿黑色披风之人定与疤子有勾结，而且此人一定是陈家人。陈浩宇已被排除在外，陈浩飞更与此事无关，一切迹象指向陈浩东，只是苦于没有证据。
[EN] Scarface spun his tale smooth. “Not lying! You fought at first, till I swore I’d sneak you home and look after you if you gave in—so you agreed! Your Lordship, it was consensual! Only afterward I got greedy for silver, knocked her out, and sold her to Yangzhou!”
[ZH] 申简辰又觉疤子实在狡诈，硬生生把强奸罪说成你情我愿，历来男女之事最不好断定，尤其对这种没有留下实锤证据的案子。好在已证明陈浩宇无罪，算是替千里寻解决一件忧虑之事。
[EN] Pinned tight, Chun Jinlan couldn’t reach her sleeve. Biting back her rage, she went limp, waiting for the constables to loosen their grip.
[ZH] “你刚才还说穿黑色披风的人是陈浩宇，现在为何否认没有此人？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen saw straight through Scarface’s cover-up for the cloaked man. “And the man in the black cloak?”
[ZH] “大人，小人前面说的都是假话，现在句句属实。”
[EN] “Never existed—the maid made him up!”
[ZH] 听了疤子的话，陈浩东又轻松许多。
[EN] “I didn’t make it up!” Aye cried. “I saw him with my own eyes!”
[ZH] 其他人沉浸在思考疤子的话哪句是真，哪句是假，以及穿黑色披风的人到底谁，并未注意到春金兰的变化。
[EN] “Easy to see shadows in the dark,” Scarface shot back.
[ZH] 直到春金兰说：“陈浩宇若不纳我为妾，我就死在公堂之上！”
[EN] Seeing Chun Jinlan calm down, the constables stepped back to their posts. While all eyes stayed on Scarface, Chun Jinlan slid her hand into her sleeve and wrapped her fingers around the dagger hilt.
[ZH] 春金兰张口闭口将死挂在嘴边，除了陈浩东其他人都未当回事，想着她只是说说而已，连差役也十分不屑。既是陈浩东的计谋，他更希望如此，自然不会阻拦。
[EN] Shen Jianchen spotted the holes in Scarface’s story a mile away: consensual, yet he sent his crew away, drugged the caretaker, and Aye heard terrified screams? Tie that to the transferred staff, and the cloaked man had to be Scarface’s accomplice—a Chen family insider. Haoyu was cleared, Haofei had no motive, every finger pointed straight at Chen Haodong—yet without proof, his hands were tied. Crafty rat that Scarface was, twisting rape into “consensual” made proving a capital charge impossible with no physical evidence. At least Chen Haoyu was cleared, lifting a huge stone off Qianli Xun’s chest.
[ZH] 谁知春金兰话音刚落，从袖中抽出匕首，刺向自己的胸前。当匕首真的被她刺入时，她盯着温信，心中疑惑，他为何不救她？还未搞清楚时，已向下倒去……
[EN] “A minute ago you swore the cloaked man was Chen Haoyu—now you claim he never existed?”
[ZH] 周围人被眼前的景象惊住。
[EN] “I was lying before, Your Lordship—every word now is gospel truth!”
[ZH] 申简辰看向陈浩东，他好像有种如释重负之感。
[EN] Relief washed over Chen Haodong. Everyone else sat mulling over the contradictions—missing the sudden shift in Chun Jinlan.
[ZH] 温信因未将春金兰的话当回事，慢了一步，等他冲到跟前，春金兰已倒在地上。他蹲下，把手指放在鼻息前，还有气息。
[EN] Until she cried out, “If Chen Haoyu won’t take me as his concubine, I’ll die right here in court!”
[ZH] 春金兰努力睁开眼，用几乎听不到的声音说：“你……为何……不救我？”
[EN] Having heard her cry wolf since yesterday, nobody took her seriously—not even the constables. And since Chen Haodong had planted the idea, he sure wasn’t stopping her.
[ZH] 春金兰使尽最后的力气说完心中疑惑，咽气而亡。
[EN] Before the echo faded, Chun Jinlan whipped the dagger from her sleeve and drove it straight into her own chest. As cold steel sank home, her eyes locked onto Wen Xin in stunned disbelief—*why didn’t he save me?*—before her knees gave out and she crumpled.
[ZH] 闹出人命，陈浩宇更是悔恨不已，要是当初没有将春金兰抓到宅子里，就没有后来的事，他虽无罪，却良心难安。
[EN] Gasps froze the hall. Shen Jianchen shot a look at Chen Haodong—catching a unmistakable flash of relief across his face.
[ZH] 温信吩咐两名差役将春金兰的尸体抬出大堂。
[EN] Caught half a step slow because he hadn’t believed her, Wen Xin reached her side as she hit the floor and checked her nose—still breathing faintly.
[ZH] 疤子认罪，春金兰自杀而亡，真正的罪人还逍遥法外，此事并未真正结束，但也需告一段落。
[EN] Forcing her eyelids open, Chun Jinlan whispered barely above a breath, “Why... didn’t you... save me?” With that final puzzled question, her breath stopped.
[ZH] 申简辰宣布道：“本案虽未完全了结，案情却已有眉目，陈家二公子陈浩宇无罪，将疤子暂且收押，等待司理院进一步审理。”
[EN] Blood on the floor crushed Chen Haoyu with remorse. Innocent of the crime, sure—yet if he hadn’t snatched her from that creek, she’d still be alive.
[ZH] 温信带着差役将疤子押了出去。
[EN] Wen Xin ordered two constables to carry the body out.
[ZH] 陈浩宇从地上爬起来，他无罪释放，却高兴不起来，刚才的一幕搞得他心情沉重，不想说一句话。
[EN] With Scarface confessing, Chun Jinlan dead, and the real mastermind still walking free, the case wasn’t over—yet this chapter had to close.
[ZH] 陈江林带着两个侄儿上前行礼：“感谢仪王替我儿洗去冤情，春金兰之事多少与浩宇有关，陈家愿承担她身后之事，也愿帮她安顿好家人。”
[EN] “While loose ends remain, the core facts stand clear,” Shen Jianchen announced. “Second Young Master Chen Haoyu is acquitted. Hold Scarface in custody pending further inquiry.”
[ZH] “陈老爷此举也算是对春金兰有个交代。”申简辰说完，面向一旁的韩谷，“麻烦韩师爷带人退下，我有事与陈老爷和陈家公子说。”
[EN] Wen Xin and his constables hauled Scarface out. Chen Haoyu climbed to his feet, cleared of all charges yet too sick at heart to crack a smile.
[ZH] 韩谷应声“是”，将差役和证人带出大堂。
[EN] Chen Jianglin stepped forward with his two nephews and bowed. “Thank you for clearing my son’s name, Your Highness. Since Haoyu played a part in Chun Jinlan’s tragedy, the Chen family will pay for her burial and provide for her kin.”
[ZH] 申简辰看向陈浩东，强烈的第六感告诉他，穿黑色披风之人就是陈家大公子。唯一的证人疤子不肯道出实情，揽下所有罪责，除了继续审问疤子，动用刑罚逼他说出实情外，别无他法，而眼下只能放陈浩东一马。可他撺掇春金兰一事已是实锤，不如先让他在此事上吃些苦头。
[EN] “A decent gesture toward the dead, Master Chen.” Turning to Clerk Han, Shen Jianchen added, “Take the constables and witnesses outside. I need a word with Master Chen and the young masters.”
[ZH] “大公子是打算亲口说呢？还是让本王替你说？”
[EN] Han Gu cleared the hall.
[ZH] 仪王的做法本就令陈江林疑惑，他的话更是令他不解。接着大侄子“扑通”一声跪在他面前，更是令他错愕，一时惊得忘记说话。
[EN] Shen Jianchen fixed his eyes on Chen Haodong. Every instinct screamed he was the cloaked man, yet with Scarface eating the blame, he’d have to break Scarface in interrogation later. Still, Chen Haodong’s meddling in bringing Chun Jinlan to Chen Manor was already proven—high time he tasted some heat.
[ZH] 陈浩宇依旧沉浸在后悔中，懒得关注发生的一切。
[EN] “Will Eldest Young Master tell him yourself, or shall I tell him for you?”
[ZH] 陈浩飞则惊讶地问：“东哥，你这是怎么了？为何要给大伯跪下？”
[EN] Before baffled Chen Jianglin could even process the question, his eldest nephew dropped flat to his knees with a loud thump, stunning him speechless. Haoyu stayed lost in guilt, while Haofei stared in shock. “Brother Dong, what’s going on? Why kneel to Eldest Uncle?”
[ZH] 陈江林这才说：“浩东，有话起来说，不必下跪。”
[EN] “Get up and speak, Haodong,” Chen Jianglin said. “No need to kneel.”
[ZH] 陈浩东声泪俱下：“大伯！我做了错事，您若不原谅侄儿，侄儿绝不起来，呜呜……！”
[EN] Tears poured down Chen Haodong’s cheeks. “Uncle! I did wrong! Until you forgive me, I’m not getting up! Waaah!”
[ZH] 申简辰心中骂道：“事儿还没说，就逼着别人原谅你，实在不地道！”
[EN] *Forcing forgiveness before even confessing—shameless!* Shen Jianchen fumed inside.
[ZH] 陈江林劝道：“在陈家小辈中你出类拔萃，从未做过错事，今日为何要如此说？”
[EN] “You’re the best of our younger generation and never put a foot wrong,” Chen Jianglin coaxed. “What’s all this?”
[ZH] “呜呜，大伯先答应不怪我，我才说，呜呜……”
[EN] “Promise you won’t blame me first, Uncle, or I can’t say it!”
[ZH] “好，我答应不责怪你。”
[EN] “All right, I promise I won’t blame you.”
[ZH] 陈浩东收住哭声：“有一次我去乡下的宅子，见春金兰在宅子外张望，上前训斥两句，她把我当成浩宇，向我袭击而来，还好从宅子里出来几名下人，将春金兰控制住，我告诉她我是浩宇的大哥，她才将她的经历告诉我。她实在太可怜，侄儿不由心生怜悯，加之一心认为是浩宇所为，想着如果她跟了浩宇，有好日子过，浩宇也不用担任何罪责，便给她出了到陈府闹的法子。都是我的错，我不该帮她，呜呜……”
[EN] Turning off the sobs on a dime, Chen Haodong spun his yarn. “One day at the country house, I caught Chun Jinlan peeking around outside. I scolded her, she mistook me for Haoyu and attacked, and servants restrained her. Once she learned I was Haoyu’s cousin, she told me her tragic story. My heart went out to her—and thinking Haoyu really did it, I figured if she joined his household, she’d have a good life and Haoyu would avoid prosecution, so I suggested she make a scene at Chen Manor. All my fault for trying to help!”
[ZH] 陈江林心情复杂，很想骂浩东两句，可已答应不责怪他，又一想只怪浩东太善良，要说错也是浩宇有错在先。陈江林只能用深深的一声叹息来回应：“唉！”
[EN] Stuck by his own promise—and blaming Haodong’s soft heart plus Haoyu’s own recklessness—Chen Jianglin could only let out a heavy sigh.
[ZH] 陈浩宇自责道：“东哥，你起来吧，此事不能怪你，都是我的错，我不该去乡下乱抢姑娘。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu spoke up in self-reproach. “Get up, Brother Dong—none of this is on you. I’m the idiot who went snatching girls in the countryside.”
[ZH] 申简辰看不下去，陈浩东演戏的水平实在高明：“陈大公子想哭，还是回到陈家，跪在长辈面前好好哭一场。”
[EN] Disgusted by the masterclass in theater, Shen Jianchen remarked dryly, “If Eldest Young Master wants a good cry, save it for kneeling in front of your elders at home.”
[ZH] 陈江林认为仪王是提醒他家事还需回家处理，行礼道：“让仪王见笑，我这就带陈家晚辈回去处理家事。”
[EN] Taking the cue to settle dirty laundry behind closed doors, Chen Jianglin bowed. “Forgive the spectacle, Your Highness. I’ll take the boys home to sort out family affairs.”
[ZH] 申简辰说：“陈老爷请便。”
[EN] “Go right ahead, Master Chen.”
[ZH] 陈浩飞扶起东哥，兄弟三人拜谢过仪王后，跟随陈江林离开司理院。
[EN] Chen Haofei helped his cousin up, and the three young men bowed to Prince Yi before trailing Chen Jianglin out.

#CH 289
[ZH] 回到陈府，陈江林并未把长辈们聚集到一起，当众将陈浩东撺掇春金兰一事说了。既是家丑，还需掩盖。
[ZH] 他只把陈浩东一人留在会客厅，让下人把门关好。
[ZH] 陈浩东再次跪下：“大伯，我错了，您想怎么惩罚我都认。”
[ZH] 陈江林坐在椅子上叹口气：“唉！可是因陈家产业一事给闹的？”
[ZH] 已没有必要再隐瞒，陈浩东大胆承认：“是，我担心陈家长辈会选浩宇，致使我从小到大的心血白白浪费。”
[ZH] 陈江林劝道：“浩东啊，你也不想想？浩宇哪一点能与你比？他虽比以前好些，论做生意的本事不及你万分之一，又何必把他当成眼中钉？以后陈家要交到你们年轻人手中，你们三个兄弟要齐心协力，陈家的未来才有希望，而不应因为一点利益互相争斗，搞得家宅不安。”
[ZH] 陈浩东装作羞愧的样子：“大伯，我以后再也不会做傻事。”
[ZH] 陈江林显得很沉重：“行了，回去吧，此事不要对你爹娘提起，就当没发生过。”
[ZH] 大伯不想将事情闹大，说明他不愿让他的人生有污点，看来大伯还是倾向于选他陈浩东成为陈家产业的继承人。
[ZH] 陈浩东窃喜之余爬起来拜别大伯，他要赶紧回去把此事告诉爹娘，也好让他们有个应对之策。
[ZH] 陈江林在会客厅坐了一会儿，想了一会儿，才回到自己的院子。
[ZH] 姚轻琰迎出来，急切地问：“案子审理得如何？”
[ZH] “浩宇无事，给放了。”
[ZH] 姚轻琰担忧的神色一扫而光：“既然给放了，为何不来见见我这当娘的？”
[ZH] “春金兰死了，他心里愧疚，先回墨香苑，等他缓过来，再拜见长辈。”
[ZH] “死了？怎么死的？”
[ZH] “进屋，坐下我详细给你说。”
[ZH] 两人进屋后，在榻上坐好，陈江林将案子审理的经过详细向姚轻琰说了。
[ZH] 姚轻琰听后高兴不起来：“说到底还是怪浩宇，他若不把人家姑娘抢来，哪有后面的事？愧疚也是应该的，我们现在只能帮那姑娘处理好身后事。”
[ZH] “此事我会安排妥当。现下有一事，需与你商议，想让你帮我拿个主意。”
[ZH] “什么事？”
[EN] Back at Chen Manor, Chen Jianglin didn’t summon the elders to air Chen Haodong’s meddling—family shame stayed behind closed doors. Keeping only Chen Haodong in the reception hall, he had servants shut the doors.
[ZH] “关于陈家产业继承人的事。”
[EN] Chen Haodong dropped right back to his knees. “I was wrong, Uncle—give me whatever punishment I deserve.”
[ZH] “继承人是大事，你应该跟母亲还有二弟三弟商议。”
[EN] Sitting back with a sigh, Chen Jianglin asked, “Was this over the family business inheritance?”
[ZH] “我会与他们商议，可我想先听听你的意思。”
[EN] No point hiding it now. “Yes. I feared the elders might pick Haoyu, throwing away everything I’ve worked for since childhood.”
[ZH] “你既让我说，我便说说，从我心里来讲，当然希望是浩宇，可与浩东比起来他差远了，浩飞又志不在此，最适合的只能是浩东，浩宇往后能帮上浩东我也就心满意足。”
[EN] “Use your head, Haodong,” Chen Jianglin reasoned. “Where does Haoyu even measure up to you? Improved as he is, he doesn’t have a fraction of your business savvy—why treat him as a rival? One day Chen Manor passes to your generation. You three brothers have to pull together, not tear each other apart over scraps and wreck the house.”
[ZH] 陈江林点点头：“你能如此想，我便放心。产业继承人一事，本来要到明年才确定，我想提前到今年。”
[EN] Hanging his head in mock shame, Chen Haodong swore, “Never again, Uncle.”
[ZH] “提前？选继承人的时间都是定好的，祖上可有提前的先例？”
[EN] “Enough. Go back—and don’t breathe a word to your parents. Consider it forgotten.”
[ZH] “那倒没有，不过时间是我成为继承人那日定下的，改日子也可由我说了算。”
[EN] Covering it up meant Uncle wanted his record spotless—which meant Uncle still backed him for heir! Thrilled inside, Chen Haodong bowed out to hurry straight home and brief his parents.
[ZH] “既如此，你定吧，只要其他长辈不反对，我没意见。不过，为何突然要提前？”
[EN] Sitting alone in the hall a while to think, Chen Jianglin finally headed back to his own courtyard, where Yao Qingyan rushed out to meet him. “How did the hearing go?”
[ZH] 向来任何事陈江林都不会瞒着姚轻琰，可此事他不想说，省得给她平添烦恼，只怕说了也会影响妯娌之间的关系。
[EN] “Haoyu’s cleared and released.”
[ZH] “官家管理天下，需尽快定下太子，以安臣民之心；陈家也需尽快定下产业继承人，方可安家宅，家宅安，子孙宁，子孙宁，家宅兴。”
[EN] Relief flooded her face. “Then why didn’t he come greet his own mother?”
[ZH] 姚轻琰十分认可：“你想怎么做我都支持。”
[EN] “Chun Jinlan died, and guilt’s eating him alive. He went straight to Moxiang Courtyard—he’ll pay respects once he collects himself.”
[ZH] 征得夫人同意，陈江林才从榻上下来，携着姚轻琰一起向老太太汇报案件审理的情况，顺便说说产业继承人之事。
[EN] “Died?! How?”
[ZH] 陈江树刚从铺子里回来，喝完一口茶，儿子陈浩东急匆匆进来，话还未说，先将下人屏退。
[EN] “Come sit inside and I’ll tell you everything.”
[ZH] “你这是做什么？案情审理得如何？”
[EN] Seated on the couch, Chen Jianglin walked her through the entire trial. Yao Qingyan’s smile faded. “Still Haoyu’s own fault—if he hadn’t snatched her, none of this happens. He ought to feel guilty. Least we can do is give her a decent burial.”
[ZH] 陈浩东没有着急回答，下人都出去后，他把门关上，又将陈江树手中的茶杯接过在桌上放好。
[EN] “Already seeing to it. Now there’s another matter I want your take on: choosing the business heir.”
[ZH] “正喝着呢？给我放下是几个意思？”
[EN] “That’s huge—shouldn’t you ask Mother and your brothers?”
[ZH] 陈浩东依旧没有回答，直接说：“大伯已经知道春金兰是我撺掇的。”
[EN] “I will, yet I want your thoughts first.”
[ZH] 陈江树的手颤抖一下，才明白儿子为何会拿走自己手中的茶杯。
[EN] “Since you ask: as a mother, of course I’d wish for Haoyu, yet he’s miles behind Haodong, and Haofei has zero interest. Haodong is the only real fit. If Haoyu just pitches in to help him, I’ll be content.”
[ZH] 包氏从里屋出来，担心地问：“大哥既已知道，会不会影响我儿继承陈家产业？”
[EN] Chen Jianglin nodded. “Glad you see it that way. The selection wasn’t due till next year—I want to move it up to this year.”
[ZH] 陈江树沉默不语，担心一直以来的努力会因此化为泡影。
[EN] “Move it up? Is there any precedent for changing the schedule?”
[ZH] 陈浩东说：“大伯不清楚你们知道此事，还叮嘱我不要对你们提起。”
[EN] “None, yet I set the date the day I took over, so I can change it too.”
[ZH] 陈江树这才开口说：“看来你大伯不想将此事闹得人尽皆知，我儿还是很有希望。也是，三个孩子中浩东出类拔萃，大哥没得选，才不希望传扬出去损害浩东的名声。”
[EN] “Fine by me if the elders agree. Only why the sudden rush?”
[ZH] “早知如此，何必在背后做那么多事？”包氏放心地坐在椅子上。
[EN] He rarely kept secrets from his wife, yet airing Haodong’s scheme would only upset her and sour relations with her sister-in-law. “Just as His Majesty names a Crown Prince early to steady the realm, naming our heir early steadies the house. A steady house keeps the juniors at peace—and peace makes the family prosper.”
[ZH] “当然有必要，既搞得浩宇更没希望，也逼得大哥早做决断。如此想来让他知道是浩东撺掇的也并非坏事。”陈江树轻松许多，又问起案情审理之事，“案子审理得如何？浩宇可愿纳商妓为妾？”
[EN] “You’ve got my full backing.”
[ZH] 陈浩东回道：“仪王着实厉害，很快找出一位证人，证明浩宇那天晚上根本没回到乡下的宅子，逼得疤子认下所有罪行。春金兰别无他法，以死相逼，结果死在公堂之上。”
[EN] With her blessing, Chen Jianglin escorted Yao Qingyan to report the trial outcome to Old Madam Xia and raise moving up the selection.
[ZH] 包氏说：“没想到是这么个结果，事情竟然是疤子做下的，浩宇可真是幸运，采了那么多花，唯独这朵带刺的没采成。”
[EN] Meanwhile, Chen Jiangshu had just returned from his shops and taken a sip of tea when Chen Haodong rushed in and waved the servants out.
[ZH] 陈江树说：“不管是什么结果，陈家子弟无事便好。官府出面我也为浩宇捏了一把汗，想着若真是他做下，一定要劝说他同意纳商妓为妾，才可保住性命。浩东，你记着，再筹谋也不能算计自家子弟的性命。”
[EN] “What’s the fire? How’d the trial go?”
[ZH] “儿子记下。”
[EN] Shutting the door once the help cleared out, Chen Haodong lifted the teacup right out of his father’s hand and set it on the table.
[ZH] “这个结果算是两全其美，浩宇既安全回来，也因此在族人中名声更不好，已无法成为我儿的阻力，我们就此罢手。”
[EN] “Hey—I was drinking that! What’s the idea?”
[ZH] 陈浩东心想，是该让蒋爷出手的时候。
[EN] “Eldest Uncle knows I put Chun Jinlan up to it.”
[ZH] ……
[EN] Chen Jiangshu’s hand twitched—now he saw why his son took the cup away. Stepping from the inner room, Madam Bao asked in panic, “If Eldest Brother knows, will it sink your inheritance?!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇带着青瓜回到墨香苑，墨香苑里一切如故，他的心情却与以往大不相同。
[EN] Chen Jiangshu sat grim, fearing years of groundwork had blown up.
[ZH] 若说几日前他还自信迟早会搞定娘子，现在他是一点自信都没有，甚至觉得根本配不上娘子。早知如此，他便不会做下许多荒唐事。
[EN] “Uncle doesn’t know you two were in on it,” Chen Haodong added. “Even warned me not to tell you.”
[ZH] 不用娘子惩罚，陈浩宇主动走到素心草堂门外双膝跪下。
[EN] Chen Jiangshu’s shoulders dropped in relief. “That means he’s keeping it under wraps—you’re still in the driver’s seat! Makes sense: out of the three boys, you tower over the rest. Eldest Brother has no other choice, so he won’t tarnish your name.”
[ZH] 青瓜理解公子的心情，也跟着跪下。
[EN] “If it was already in the bag,” Madam Bao sighed, sinking into a chair, “why pull all those stunts behind the scenes?”
[ZH] 青瓜陪陈浩宇待在司理院，已令他十分感动，现在又陪着跪下，陈浩宇更是于心不忍：“你又没犯错误，为何跪下？”
[EN] “Needed them!” Chen Jiangshu argued. “Sunk Haoyu for good and nudged Eldest Brother into deciding early—letting him know Haodong pulled the strings wasn’t even bad. Wait, how did the hearing end? Did Haoyu take the courtesan as concubine?”
[ZH] “我是公子的人，公子跪下我便跪下。”
[EN] “Prince Yi’s sharp—dug up a witness proving Haoyu wasn’t there that night and forced Scarface to take the fall. Out of moves, Chun Jinlan threatened suicide and killed herself right in court.”
[ZH] “你不必陪我，还是快去找丑丫，报个平安。”
[EN] Madam Bao clicked her tongue. “Scarface did it?! Haoyu’s got devil’s luck—picked every flower in sight and dodged the one with thorns.”
[ZH] 提到丑丫，青瓜已跪不住，爬起来：“我看完丑丫再来陪公子跪着。”
[EN] “Never mind who did it—good that a Chen lad came home safe,” Chen Jiangshu said firm. “When the yamen stepped in I broke a sweat too—if he’d been guilty, I’d have urged him to take her as concubine just to save his head. Remember this, Haodong: scheme all you want, never gamble with a kinsman’s life.”
[ZH] 瞧着青瓜跑向西院的背影，陈浩宇苦笑一下：“真羡慕你，从司理院回来还能坦然面对喜欢的人，可你家公子我……”
[EN] “Understood, Father.”
[ZH] 已进入夏季，太阳越来越毒辣，尽管稍微偏西一些，那股子热劲儿依然未散，刚跪一会儿，头上已冒出热汗。
[EN] “Worked out best for us anyway—Haoyu’s home safe, yet his reputation with the clan is mud. Nobody stands in your way now, so quit while we’re ahead.”
[ZH] 千里寻正坐在书桌前写话本，几日的经历令她感触颇深，头脑中构织出新的情节，不如趁着等待案件审理结果之际，将故事写下来，也免得一直沉浸在替陈浩宇担忧的情绪中。
[EN] *Time to let Master Jiang make his move,* Chen Haodong thought silently.
[ZH] 苦荞已知门外发生了什么，走到书桌前，小心提醒道：“姑爷回来了，在外头跪着。”
[EN] Over at Moxiang Courtyard, Chen Haoyu arrived with Qinggua, everything looking the same while his heart sat heavy as lead. Days ago he felt sure he’d win his wife over; now he felt unworthy to stand in her shadow.
[ZH] 千里寻停下笔，替自己感到高兴，他既回来说明事情不是他做下的。又一想若陈浩宇不抢人，也没有后面的事，他愿意跪着就跪着，总得为做下的错事付出些代价。于是她继续提笔写话本。
[EN] Without waiting to be scolded, Chen Haoyu dropped to both knees right outside Suxin Thatched Hall. Understanding his mood, Qinggua knelt beside him.
[ZH] 苦荞见小姐不言语，猜到她心中有气，不再去打扰，也去忙丫鬟该忙的事。
[EN] Touched after Qinggua had already kept him company at the Judicial Office, Chen Haoyu scolded, “You didn’t do anything wrong—why kneel?”
[ZH] 陈江林和姚轻琰到老太太处将案情说完后，夏老太太再也绷不住对孙子的关怀之情，要亲自去见孙子。
[EN] “I’m your man, Young Master. You kneel, I kneel.”
[ZH] 众人扶着她踏进墨香苑，看到孙子跪在院子里，夏老太太用手阻止大家：“算了，我们还是回去吧，他们小两口之间还有事情要解决。”
[EN] “Don’t bother—go check on Chouya and let her know you’re safe.”
[ZH] 姚轻琰见儿子跪在日头底下甚是心疼：“大太阳底下，跪着也不是个事儿。”
[EN] Mention of Chouya popped Qinggua straight up. “I’ll check on Chouya and come right back to kneel with you!”
[ZH] “太阳底下跪着总比吃牢饭没了性命强。”夏老太太说完转身往出走。
[EN] Watching him dash off toward the west wing, Chen Haoyu smiled wry. “Lucky guy—walks straight back to the girl he likes with a clear conscience, while your master...”
[ZH] 姚轻琰看一眼儿子，也跟着老太太走出墨香苑。
[EN] Early summer sun beat down fierce even as afternoon slanted west, beading sweat across his brow in minutes.
[ZH] 千里寻大约写了两刻钟，想起陈浩宇手臂上有伤，太阳底下跪着容易出汗，伤口复发更难以愈合。她心有不忍，放下笔，起身走到门口。
[EN] Indoors, Qianli Xun sat at her desk writing her huaben—the last few days had sparked fresh plot twists, and writing beat stewing over Chen Haoyu.
[ZH] 陈浩宇低头跪着，满额头都是汗，衣衫显然换过，已没有血渍，伤口应该在里面包扎着。
[EN] Aware of the scene outside, Kuqiao stepped up and whispered, “Young Master’s back, Miss—kneeling out in the yard.”
[ZH] “起来吧，进屋说话。”
[EN] Qianli Xun paused her brush, relieved—home meant cleared. Still, none of this would’ve happened if he hadn’t snatched girls in the first place. Let him kneel and pay a little penance. She dipped her brush and kept writing. Seeing her temper still up, Kuqiao left her be.
[ZH] 娘子的声音如同凉爽的夏风吹进陈浩宇的内心，他高兴地“哎”了一声，爬起来往屋里走，双腿跪得发麻，走起路来一瘸一拐。
[EN] Soon after, Old Madam Xia arrived with Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan, unable to stay away once she heard her grandson was cleared. Spotting him kneeling in the sun, Old Madam Xia raised a hand to halt everyone. “Leave them be—the young couple has scores to settle.”
[ZH] “苦荞！去扶一下姑爷！”
[EN] Yao Qingyan winced. “Kneeling in this blazing sun...”
[ZH] 苦荞答应着出来，小心扶着姑爷向屋内走。
[EN] “Better kneeling in the sun than rotting in jail awaiting the axe!” Old Madam Xia turned right back out, Yao Qingyan trailing with a backward glance.
[ZH] 娘子第一次在下人面前用“姑爷”称谓他，说明她还认可他作为夫君的身份。
[EN] After half an hour of writing, Qianli Xun remembered his stabbed arm—sweating in the heat would infect the wound. Softening, she set down her brush and opened the door.
[ZH] 苦荞扶着姑爷在椅子上坐好后，到茶桌前为姑爷做茶。
[EN] Head bowed and brow drenched in sweat, Chen Haoyu wore a fresh robe over his bandaged arm.
[ZH] 千里寻也坐回书桌前，盯着写好的书稿，淡淡地问：“伤可好些？”
[EN] “Get up and come inside.”
[ZH] “有青瓜照顾，好多了。”
[EN] Her voice hit like a cool summer breeze. “Coming!” Scrambling up on numb legs, he hobbled toward the door.
[ZH] “给我说说案子审理的情况。”
[EN] “Kuqiao! Help Young Master in!”
[ZH] “疤子认罪，春金兰身上藏着匕首，自杀而亡。”
[EN] Kuqiao hurried out to steady him—and Chen Haoyu beamed inside: the first time his wife had called him “Young Master” in front of a servant, owning him as her husband!
[ZH] 千里寻想到春金兰找上门来闹事与陈浩东相关，本想说，话到嘴边又咽了回去。还是算了，麻烦刚解决，陈浩宇看上去心情并不好，又何必让他知道兄弟之情不可靠，再平添烦恼。
[EN] Once Kuqiao seated him and went to whisk tea, Qianli Xun returned to her desk and asked flat, “Arm any better?”
[ZH] “以后不要再做荒唐事。”
[EN] “Much better with Qinggua looking after me.”
[ZH] “娘子放心，我不会再做，你能否原谅我？”
[EN] “Tell me how the trial went.”
[ZH] “原不原谅已不重要，重要的是我已嫁给你，只能认栽。”
[EN] “Scarface confessed. Chun Jinlan hid a dagger and killed herself in court.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇终于露出轻松的笑容：“只要娘子不闹着跟我和离，让我做什么都行。”
[EN] Qianli Xun almost mentioned Chen Haodong’s hand in sending Chun Jinlan over, then bit it back. Trouble had just blown over, and Haoyu looked beat up enough without learning his own cousin had knifed him. “Quit pulling reckless stunts.”
[ZH] 千里寻被气到：“你竟如此看我？我虽和离过一次，也是逼不得已。哪家姑娘会拿自己的婚姻开玩笑？动不动把和离挂在嘴边？再说我舍得你也舍不得奶奶与公婆，以后少在我面前提这两个字！”
[EN] “Never again, I swear! Can you forgive me?”
[ZH] “不提不提，绝对不会再提。”
[EN] “Forgive or not doesn’t matter—I married you, so I’m stuck with my bed.”
[ZH] 苦荞已将茶做好，亲手端过来，送至姑爷面前：“姑爷，喝盏茶。”
[EN] A relieved grin broke over his face. “Long as you don’t ask for a mutual divorce, I’ll do anything you say!”
[ZH] 在外面跪了良久，出了不少汗，还真有些口渴。陈浩宇接过茶盏一口气喝完，把茶盏递给苦荞：“很快娘子的茶都由我来做。”
[EN] Her temper flared. “Is that what you think of me?! I only went through a mutual divorce once because I had zero choice! What woman treats marriage like a joke, tossing divorce around every five minutes? Even if I didn’t mind leaving you, I’d never bear leaving Grandmother and my in-laws—don’t bring up those words around me again!”
[ZH] 苦荞笑道：“姑爷还要学习做生意，哪有时间做茶？”
[EN] “Never! Swear I won’t breathe them!”
[ZH] “等我跟着茶师再学一段时日，技术纯熟后，只要我在家，做茶的事你不许跟我抢。”
[EN] Kuqiao brought over the freshly whisked tea. Parched from the sun, Chen Haoyu downed it in one gulp and handed back the bowl. “Soon I’ll be making all my wife’s tea.”
[ZH] “放心，我绝不跟姑爷抢。”
[EN] Kuqiao teased, “Young Master’s busy learning business—where will you find time to whisk tea?”
[ZH] 苦荞说着，到茶桌前将茶盏放下，又去收拾手稿。姑爷在跟前，小姐肯定不会再写话本，还是收起来得好。
[EN] “Give me a few more days with the tea master—once I’m sharp, whenever I’m home, don’t you dare fight me for tea duty.”
[ZH] 千里寻说：“春金兰也是可怜人，再怎么说是你招惹而来，她的身后事你需上心，还有她的家人该照顾的也需照顾。”
[EN] “Deal—all yours!” Setting the bowl down, Kuqiao tucked away her miss’s huaben manuscript discreetly.
[ZH] “娘子放心，爹已经安排下去。”
[EN] “Chun Jinlan was a pitiable soul,” Qianli Xun said. “At the root, you dragged her into this—see to her burial and look after her family.”
[ZH] 两人之间出现短暂沉默。
[EN] “Don’t worry—Father already arranged everything.”
[ZH] 千里寻突然说道：“陈浩宇，你既然说让你做什么都行，我正好有一事……”
[EN] A brief hush fell before Qianli Xun spoke up. “Chen Haoyu, since you said you’ll do anything I ask, I’ve got one condition...”
[ZH] “娘子尽管说。”
[EN] “Name it, Wife.”
[ZH] “我本打算快些喜欢上你，然后与你圆房，若没发生这些事，估计我们应该已经……，可偏偏事与愿违，你我什么时候能圆房，我不得而知……”
[EN] “I’d been trying to warm up to you and consummate our marriage—if none of this had hit, maybe we already would have. Now... who knows when I’ll feel ready.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇又变得心情沉重。
[EN] His shoulders sagged.
[ZH] 千里寻继续说：“我给你两年时间，若两年内你不近女色，愿意为我守身如玉，两年后我便答应与你圆房。”
[EN] “I’ll give you two years,” she went on. “Keep away from other women and stay chaste for me for two full years, and at the end of two years, I’ll consummate our marriage.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇像是在黑暗中看到希望：“我同意！就当是用两年的时间洗去我一身污垢，我一定会努力，让自己成为能配得上娘子之人。”
[EN] Hope flared like sunrise in the dark. “Deal! Think of it as two years to scrub every stain off myself—I’ll work my tail off to become a man worthy of you!”
[ZH] 千里寻干脆地回道：“成交！”
[EN] “Shaken and sealed!”
[ZH] 苦荞早已收拾完书稿，站在小姐身旁，听到两人的对话，担忧不已。
[EN] Standing behind her miss with the stacked manuscript, Kuqiao frowned in worry.
[ZH] 陈浩宇起身，向娘子行礼：“以后我会断绝女色，与娘子相敬如宾，绝不越雷池一步，直到娘子满意的那天为止。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu rose and bowed solemn as a scholar. “From this day on I swear off all women and treat my wife with utmost respect, never stepping a single inch out of line until the day you’re satisfied.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇突然正儿八经的样子，令千里寻意外，也极其不适。她没有做出任何回应，呆呆地看着他走出素心草堂。
[EN] His dead-serious formality threw Qianli Xun right off balance, leaving her blinking dumbly as he marched out of Suxin Thatched Hall.

#CH 290
[ZH] 姑爷走后，苦荞实在忍不住：“小姐，两年会不会太长？到时别人家的孩子都两岁，您却什么都没有。”
[ZH] “陈浩宇以前干那些龌龊事可不止两年，给他两年的时间算是优待。在这个婚姻不能自主的时代，感情这玩意儿我还做得了主，我可不想把自己的感情随随便便交给一个不靠谱的人。时间最能检验一个人，若他经得起时间检验，才是一个值得我托付身心之人。”
[ZH] “恐怕陈家长辈不会同意。”
[ZH] “经过春金兰一事，哪怕长辈的心里再不同意，也会尊重我的想法。”
[ZH] 小姐这么一说，苦荞终于放心：“您心里有数便好。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇平安归来，千里寻悬着的心也算落地，她想出去走走，自然想到神柳，也该去看望它。
[ZH] ……
[ZH] 到达山脚下时，千里寻甚是感慨，此处离幽林别居很近，以前她可毫无顾忌去那里寻找朋友，现在觉得它近在咫尺却如同远隔千山万水。
[ZH] 沿着山脚下的路离神柳越来越近时，穿越千年的情愫自然而然涌现在心头。
[ZH] 细数穿越而来的时日，已整整一年，一年的经历让她有种已过了半个世纪的感觉。
[ZH] 她不仅有了亲人，有过闺蜜，还经历过感情，现在连婚姻都有。到底哪些真正属于她？有些已失去，有些正在拥有，失去的令她惋惜的同时已不再执著，唯一担心现在拥有的有一日也会失去。
[ZH] 她现在既是被人称颂的女词人，也是偷偷写话本的千里寻才子。哪个才是真实的她？她依然搞不明白。尽管她努力让自己走上千里寻的人生之路，她也相信已走上，可还是不明白。
[ZH] 就像眼前挂满红绸的神柳，尽管她执着地认为它就是穿越前那棵，其实已不同。穿越前没有人给它的枝条上系红绸，穿越后它却披上红装。
[ZH] 她在心中劝慰自己，不必执着于想不通的事，无论是谁，做好当下该做的事。就像神柳，明明拥有神力，却将自己掩饰得如同一棵普通的柳树，任由大家在它的枝条上系红绸，它毫无怨言默默承受一切。
[ZH] 千里寻很欣慰，她也要变得毫无怨言，心甘情愿接受命运给她安排的一切。既然无力改变，不如顺着命运。也许有那么一日会发现顺着命运，以积极的心态面对一切问题，用正确的方法解决，命运才能被真正改变。
[ZH] 神柳果然有灵性，来到它跟前，千里寻一下子想通人生难题。
[ZH] 她抬起轻松的眼眸向神柳顶端红色的外衣瞧去，发现高处的枝条上拴着一条红绸。心想：“竟然有人学我？该不会是他？”
[ZH] 在好奇心的驱使下，千里寻用轻功跳上神柳的高处，将红绸翻开，上面有她与申简辰的名字。
[ZH] “都已跟梦容圆房，还把我的名字跟你的名字挂在一起，是几个意思？就不怕梦容来到神柳下，红绸正好掉落在她面前？要是被她知道，我跳进黄河都洗不清。为以防万一，我还是将它收了。”
[ZH] 千里寻解下红绸，卷好，将腰间挂的荷包取下，把红绸放进荷包里，收好后，继续将荷包挂回腰间，用轻功飞身而下。
[ZH] 刚站稳，不知从哪里出现一群人，穿着玄色衣裳，蒙着面，个个手中握着大刀，向她围攻而来……
[ZH] 千里寻顾不上想为何会招来这帮人，先向一旁的苦荞喊道：“赶快躲到神柳后面！”
[ZH] 苦荞吓得六神无主，听到小姐的提醒，才醒悟过来，赶快跑到树后躲好。因担心小姐，探出半个脑袋观战。
[ZH] 千里寻手无寸铁，拳头却厉害，并未将围攻而来的人放在眼里。
[EN] The moment Young Master left, Kuqiao couldn’t hold back. “Miss, isn’t two years way too long? Everybody else’s kids will be toddlers while you still have nothing!”
[ZH] 对方一起用明晃晃的大刀向她砍来，千里寻迅速闪身，躲过一刀，紧接着反击，一拳直击一名蒙面人的胸口，将其击退。
[EN] “Chen Haoyu spent way longer than two years pulling sleazy stunts—giving him two years is cutting him slack! In an era where women don’t pick their own husbands, at least I still control my own heart, and I’m not handing it to an unreliable guy on a whim. Time tests a person best; if he passes two years, then he’s earned me.”
[ZH] 然而，蒙面人太多，她不得不连续后退，巧妙地避开刀锋。她左躲右闪，拳脚并用，一时间，拳影重重，刀光拳影。
[EN] “What if the Chen elders object?”
[ZH] 千里寻眼见时机成熟，突然施展出一记强劲的腿功，将一名蒙面人踢飞出去，撞在神柳的树干上。
[EN] “After Chun Jinlan’s mess, even if they hate it inside, they’ll respect my call.”
[ZH] 苦荞吓得赶紧躲好，过了一会儿，她偷偷瞧那撞到树上的人，已掉在地上，口吐鲜血，气喘吁吁，根本无力还击。
[EN] Relieved, Kuqiao nodded. “So long as you know what you’re doing, Miss.”
[ZH] 其他蒙面人见状，纷纷涌上前来，试图再次围攻千里寻。
[EN] With Chen Haoyu home safe, a weight lifted off Qianli Xun’s chest. Wanting fresh air, her mind jumped straight to the Divine Willow—high time she paid it a visit.
[ZH] 她身形灵活，如同游龙，在人群中穿梭，每一次出手都精准无误。
[EN] Reaching the foot of the hill, a swell of nostalgia hit her. Youlin Retreat sat right nearby; once she could stroll right up to see her friend without a care, yet now a stone’s throw felt like oceans away.
[ZH] 她抓住一名蒙面人的手腕，他手中的刀掉落在地，千里寻借力打力，将其摔倒在地，紧接着一脚踢向另一名蒙面人的腰间，使其疼痛难忍，无法再战。
[EN] Walking up the path toward the willow, a full year’s worth of memories since crossing over washed through her—one year that felt like half a century. She had family now, had known close friendship, tasted love, even married. Some things were gone, others still in her hands; she no longer clung to what she’d lost, only feared losing what she still held.
[ZH] 尽管如此，千里寻还是不可避免地受了一些小伤。她的手臂被刀锋擦过，留下一道血痕，她毫不在意，瞬间加速，如同疾风般穿梭在蒙面人之间，拳拳到肉，声声震耳。
[EN] Celebrated poetess in public, secret huaben author Scholar Qianli Xun on the sly—which one was the real her? She still couldn’t tell, just like the Divine Willow draped in red silk ribbons before her. She insisted it was the same willow from her modern world, yet back then nobody tied red ribbons onto its branches.
[ZH] 连续击倒数名蒙面人后，剩下的几人见势不妙，纷纷掉头逃跑。
[EN] *Stop obsessing over riddles with no answers,* she told herself. *Just like this willow—hiding its mystic power behind plain bark, letting mortals tie ribbons all over its limbs without complaint. Accept what fate hands you, meet every twist with a clear head, and maybe that’s how you actually reshape your destiny.*
[ZH] 千里寻并未追赶，她站在原地，喘着粗气，看了着自己手臂上的伤口。
[EN] The Divine Willow really worked wonders—standing beneath it untangled the knots in her head.
[ZH] 苦荞从神柳后来到小姐身边，掏出巾帕，绑在小姐的受伤的手臂上：“疼吗？”
[EN] Lifting her eyes to the red-draped crown, she spotted a single ribbon tied high up in the upper boughs. *Someone copied me? Could it be him?*
[ZH] “这点儿伤不算什么。”
[EN] Curiosity tugging, she sprang up into the high branches with her light-foot technique and flipped the ribbon open—her name and Shen Jianchen’s stared back.
[ZH] “他们是什么人？为何会出现在这里？刚才的情形显然是冲着小姐来的。”
[EN] *Already consummated with Mengrong and still hanging our names together? What if Mengrong walks under here and it drops right in her lap? I’d never wash my name clean! Better take it down just in case.*
[ZH] 苦荞都能看出的状况，千里寻岂能看不出？神柳下还躺着一位受伤较重没有及时逃跑的，不如过去问问。
[EN] Untying the ribbon, she rolled it neat, slipped it into the pouch at her waist, retied the pouch, and floated back to the ground.
[ZH] 苦荞见小姐向地上躺着的人走去，她快走几步，上前在那人屁股上踢了一脚。
[EN] Her feet had barely touched dirt when a pack of black-clad, masked men brandishing heavy sabers swarmed out of nowhere, encircling her!
[ZH] “让你欺负我家小姐！有本事再起来打啊！”
[EN] No time to wonder who sent them—Qianli Xun barked at Kuqiao, “Get behind the Divine Willow—now!”
[ZH] 那人“哎吆”一声，根本爬不起来，任凭苦荞撤去他脸上的黑布。
[EN] Shaken out of her freeze, Kuqiao sprinted behind the huge trunk and poked half her head out to watch.
[ZH] “大白天的敢来欺负我家小姐，蒙着脸算什么？”
[EN] Unarmed yet confident in her fists, Qianli Xun didn’t flinch as gleaming blades slashed in from every angle. She ducked one sweep and drove a punch square into an attacker’s chest, sending him staggering back. Outnumbered, she pivoted and retreated in bursts, weaving between saber arcs with fists and kicks blurring through the dust.
[ZH] 千里寻盯着露出的那张脸，实在普通，她从未见过：“你们是什么人？为何要杀我？”
[EN] Spotting an opening, she unleashed a thunderous kick that launched one masked man straight into the willow’s trunk. Kuqiao ducked back behind the bark, peeking a moment later to see the thug slumped on the grass coughing blood, done for the fight.
[ZH] 苦荞已将那人落在地上的大刀拿在手中，指着他的脸：“快说！不说我就在你脸上划几刀！”
[EN] The rest surged back in. Slipping between them like a darting dragon, Qianli Xun struck with pinpoint precision—snatching one man’s wrist to drop his blade, using his own momentum to slam him down, then slamming a heel into another’s ribs until he doubled over.
[ZH] 那人被吓到，连连求饶：“女侠饶命！我说了，你得答应放过我。”
[EN] Even so, a blade nicked her arm, slicing a shallow red line. Ignoring the sting, she kicked into higher gear, whipping through the ring like a gale as heavy blows landed thud after thud. After she dropped several more, the surviving thugs saw the tide turn and bolted.
[ZH] 千里寻露出不屑的表情：“你的命对我来讲一文不值。”
[EN] Letting them run, Qianli Xun stood catching her breath and eyed the gash on her arm.
[ZH] 苦荞继续用刀指着：“快说！”
[EN] Kuqiao dashed out from behind the tree and bound the cut with her handkerchief. “Does it hurt, Miss?”
[ZH] 那人说：“我是钱塘帮蒋爷的手下，有人买你的命，蒋爷才派我们前来杀你。”
[EN] “Just a scratch.”
[ZH] 千里寻第一个想到梦容，看来她真的想让她消失，莫名的悲伤袭上心头。她没有说一句话，转身准备离开。
[EN] “Who were those men? Clearly they came straight for you!”
[ZH] 苦荞把大刀扔在地上，又踢那人一脚后，紧跟上小姐。
[EN] Noting the downed thug groaning beneath the willow, Qianli Xun walked over. Kuqiao darted ahead and kicked his rear. “Dare jump my miss?! Get back up and try again if you’ve got the guts!”
[ZH] 刚走出几步，被乔梦容挡住去路，她的身后跟着邹嬷嬷和喜兰。
[EN] Groaning unable to rise, he let Kuqiao rip off his black mask. “Ambushing my miss in broad daylight behind a mask—coward!”
[ZH] 乔梦容见朱淑真只是手臂上受伤，不满道：“这些人真没用！你竟然还好好活着！”
[EN] Staring at the plain, unfamiliar face, Qianli Xun asked, “Who are you people, and who sent you to kill me?”
[ZH] 千里寻不想再说任何劝解的话，现在的梦容充满仇恨，根本不愿回到从前，说什么都没用。于是她露出冷冷的表情：“想让我死，没那么容易。”
[EN] Kuqiao scooped up his dropped saber and leveled the point at his nose. “Spill it, or I’ll carve lines across your mug!”
[ZH] 乔梦容靠近朱淑真，冷漠地看着她：“是不容易，但我会想方设法让事情变得容易些，今日算是热热身。”
[EN] Terrified, the man begged, “Mercy, heroine! Promise you’ll let me go if I talk!”
[ZH] 苦荞实在看不下去，壮着胆子插嘴道：“我家小姐已嫁人，也打算跟姑爷好好过日子，不会对仪王妃有任何影响，贵人为何还要盯着我家小姐不放？”
[EN] Qianli Xun sneered. “Your life isn’t worth a single copper to me.”
[ZH] 乔梦容生气道：“什么时候轮到你个下人说话？喜兰！掌嘴！”
[EN] “Talk!” Kuqiao jabbed the blade closer.
[ZH] 喜兰走上前，准备去扇苦荞，被千里寻一把挡住：“我的丫鬟还轮不上他人教训！即使贵人也不行！”
[EN] “We’re Master Jiang’s men from the Qiantang Gang! Somebody paid for your head, so Master Jiang sent us!”
[ZH] 千里寻将喜兰推到一边。
[EN] Mengrong—straightaway Qianli Xun knew. A hollow ache washed through her chest. Saying nothing more, she turned to leave; Kuqiao dropped the blade, gave the thug one parting kick, and hurried after her.
[ZH] 喜兰趔趄一下，努力站稳。
[EN] Three steps down the path, Qiao Mengrong stepped out to block their way, Matron Zou and Xilan right behind her.
[ZH] 乔梦容深知对方功夫厉害，凭她身份再尊贵，此时也无济于事。
[EN] Seeing only a bandaged cut on Zhu Shuzhen’s arm, Qiao Mengrong clicked her tongue. “Useless fools! Still alive and kicking, eh?”
[ZH] “你的拳头厉害，我暂且不与她计较。不过，她刚才说的话根本不对，谁说你家小姐不会对我有影响？她今日跑到神柳下便是别有用心！”
[EN] Any plea would bounce off a wall of hatred. Qianli Xun met her gaze cold and flat. “Killing me won’t be that easy.”
[ZH] 被人误会，千里寻当然不愿吃瘪，必须将话摆在明面上说清楚。
[EN] Stepping closer, Qiao Mengrong stared icy-eyed. “Not easy, no—yet I’ll find ways to make it easier. Consider today a warm-up.”
[ZH] “仪王妃此言差矣，您与仪王还未到临安时，我便时常来神柳下，何来别有用心？”
[EN] Unable to bite her tongue, Kuqiao piped up bold. “My miss is married and just wants a quiet life with Young Master—she’s no threat to Princess Consort Yi! Why won’t Your Highness leave her in peace?!”
[ZH] “此一时彼一时，自从住进幽林别居，哥哥时常来此，你敢说你现在来此不是为了偶遇哥哥？我比谁都了解你，说一套做一套，明明是个婊子，还想给自己立牌坊，实在可笑！”
[EN] “Who let a servant run her mouth?!” Qiao Mengrong snapped. “Xilan—slap her!”
[ZH] 要是他人说出此话，千里寻绝对会一拳上去将对方打个稀巴烂，面对梦容，她的拳头根本无力伸出。
[EN] Xilan stepped up to strike, only for Qianli Xun to block her arm hard. “Nobody touches my maid—not even royalty!” Shoving Xilan aside, she sent the maid stumbling back to catch her balance.
[ZH] “随你怎么说，我内心坦荡。”千里寻控制好情绪，屈膝行礼，“此处景致甚好，贵人慢慢欣赏，家中还有事，淑真先行一步。”
[EN] Knowing rank meant nothing against those fists out here, Qiao Mengrong sneered. “Strong fists—I’ll let your maid slide for now. Still, she’s talking garbage! No threat to me? Then what are you doing sneaking up to the Divine Willow today?!”
[ZH] “怎么？被我说中，想逃走？”
[EN] Refusing to swallow false accusations, Qianli Xun shot back, “Wrong, Princess Consort Yi. I visited the Divine Willow long before you and Prince Yi ever set foot in Lin’an—what scheme is there in that?”
[ZH] 千里寻没有言语，不等贵人同意，带着苦荞绕过贵人，打算离开。
[EN] “Times changed! Since moving into Youlin Retreat, Brother comes here all the time—dare swear you didn’t come hoping to ‘accidentally’ bump into him? Nobody knows you better than me: preaching virtue while playing the harlot behind closed doors! Laughable!”
[ZH] 结果被邹嬷嬷挡在面前：“我该如何称呼你？”
[EN] Anyone else spitting that filth would have caught a fist to the jaw; against Mengrong, Qianli Xun’s hands stayed heavy at her sides.
[ZH] 千里寻第一次见邹嬷嬷，她的年纪看上去与韦嬷嬷相仿：“嬷嬷想怎么称呼都行。”
[EN] “Think whatever you like—my conscience is clear.” Reining in her hurt, she curtsied. “Enjoy the view, Your Highness. I have duties at home—excuse me.”
[ZH] “老听他人称呼你幽栖居士，那我也如此称呼。你我第一次见面，先自我介绍。我是仪王的奶娘，从小把他带大，目睹了仪王妃如何亲力亲为照顾卧在病榻上的仪王。那时的仪王对王妃一往情深，可仪王起死回生来到临安，心里却有了别人，听说仪王心里之人正是幽栖居士。你我既有幸碰上，我倚老卖老叮嘱你两句……”
[EN] “Hit a nerve and running away?”
[ZH] 一切在他人看来都是事实，千里寻解释再多也无用，她以沉默回应。
[EN] Ignoring the jab, Qianli Xun stepped past with Kuqiao—only for Matron Zou to block her path. “How should I address you?”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷继续说：“官家的儿子娶妻纳妾，对女子都有严格要求。听说你和离过一次，在临安的名声也不好，现在也已嫁为人妇，除家世尚可，人长得漂亮点儿，没有一条符合要求，还是不要再有不切实际的幻想。如若你老老实实，我会劝说仪王妃饶你不死。”
[EN] First time meeting her—around Matron Wei’s age. “Call me whatever you please, Matron.”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷说话让人听着甚不顺耳，千里寻因想快些离开，懒得与对方生气，没有给梦容说的那些劝解的话，不如说给她听。
[EN] “Everyone calls you Youqi Jushi, so I’ll use that. Allow me to introduce myself: I’m Prince Yi’s wet nurse. I raised him from infancy and watched Princess Consort Yi tend his sickbed day and night with her own hands. Back then His Highness had eyes for her alone—until he recovered, came to Lin’an, and put someone else in his heart: Youqi Jushi. Since we crossed paths, let an old woman offer some advice.”
[ZH] “嬷嬷说得在理，我有自知之明，从未有过不切实际的幻想。我现在只想安安稳稳过好自己的日子，既不想打扰别人，也希望别人不要来打扰我。还请嬷嬷多劝说仪王妃，不要把我当成假想的敌人。我不是怕死，只是不想无缘无故被拽入他人的生活中。还有，往后我不会再来神柳下。”
[EN] Every word looked like fact from the outside—arguing was pointless. Qianli Xun kept silent.
[ZH] 千里寻说完，带着苦荞从邹嬷嬷身旁穿过。
[EN] “Imperial sons follow strict rules when taking wives or concubines,” Matron Zou went on. “You’ve already had one mutual divorce, your reputation in Lin’an is tattered, and you’re another man’s wife now. Aside from decent birth and a pretty face, you don’t fit a single mark. Drop any wild dreams. Keep your head down, and I’ll persuade Her Highness to spare your life.”
[ZH] 乔梦容对着她的背影笑道：“呵呵……！好个无缘无故！说得好像自己一点儿错都没有！诗会时你明明承认喜欢上哥哥！”
[EN] Grating as the lecture was, Qianli Xun wanted out fast—and decided to aim her parting plea at the matron instead of deaf Mengrong. “Sound advice, Matron. I know my place and never harbored wild dreams. All I want is a peaceful life—neither bothering anyone nor being bothered. Please urge Princess Consort Yi to stop treating me as an imaginary rival. I’m not afraid to die; I just hate being dragged into someone else’s drama for nothing. And don’t worry—I won’t come back to the Divine Willow again.”
[ZH] 千里寻走出几步后，面对贵人之言她不能不作出回应，否则会让人觉得她对贵人不敬，可她不想再说任何话，只是站住，回过身再次行礼。
[EN] She brushed past Matron Zou with Kuqiao.
[ZH] 小姐行礼，苦荞自然也跟着行礼。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong laughed bitter at her back. “For nothing! Talking like you’re pure as snow—when you admitted right at the poetry salon that you fell for Brother!”
[ZH] 千里寻行完礼，果断地转过身，快速离开。
[EN] Etiquette forbade ignoring a royal’s words outright. Halting a few paces on, Qianli Xun turned back, curtsied silent with Kuqiao beside her, then turned clean away and strode off.
[ZH] 乔梦容感到失落，只为失去的友情。再也没有人与她睡在一张闺床上聊到很晚，第二天睡到午时才醒来。
[EN] A pang of loss pricked Qiao Mengrong—no more nights sharing a bed, talking till midnight and sleeping till noon.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷劝道：“我看她对王妃已构不成威胁，不如放过她？也放过自己？”
[EN] Matron Zou sighed. “She looks like zero threat to you now, Your Highness. Why not let her go—and give yourself peace too?”
[ZH] “她三言两语让嬷嬷改变态度，难道不能说明她很厉害吗？”乔梦容根本不认同邹嬷嬷的话，“她不仅善于狡辩，勾引男子的技术更是一流，化有形为无形。乔公子当初被她勾引得闹到绝食的地步，哥哥又因她不顾身份跑去抢亲，就连陈浩宇，一个纨绔子弟，不是流连青楼赌坊，就是抢别人家的姑娘，现在为了她竟然变好？嬷嬷，你说说她厉害不厉害？”
[EN] “She talked you round in three sentences—doesn’t that prove how dangerous she is?!” Qiao Mengrong snapped. “Slick tongue, and unmatched at hooking men without lifting a finger! Hooked Young Master Qiao till he went on a hunger strike, got Brother risking disgrace to raid a wedding, and even turned a playboy rake like Chen Haoyu into a reformed saint! Tell me she isn’t dangerous!”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷继续开解：“她再厉害也已嫁人，我看她那样子也是奔着安分过日子去的。”
[EN] “Dangerous or not, she’s married and clearly settling down.”
[ZH] “不！你根本不了解她！”乔梦容依然固执己见，“无论在任何场合，哪怕她跟哥哥不说一句话，也会将哥哥的魂魄勾走，我又岂能放心？她必须死！”
[EN] “No—you don’t know her! Anywhere, any time, she doesn’t even have to speak a word to steal Brother’s soul! How could I ever sleep easy? She has to die!”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷叹口气：“唉，王妃固执己见我说什么你都听不进去，以后我也不说了，你想怎样就怎样，只要你高兴。今日我算看出，她功夫不错，恐怕没那么容易对付。”
[EN] Matron Zou sighed in defeat. “Stubborn as you are, nothing I say gets through—I’ll quit preaching. Still, you saw her martial skills today; taking her down won’t be easy.”
[ZH] 乔梦容朝地上躺着的人看了一眼：“哼！蒋爷手下的人都是一群草包，那么多人连一个女人都杀不了，看来得另寻他人，还请嬷嬷再见一次陈浩东。”
[EN] Glancing at the groaning thug on the grass, Qiao Mengrong spat, “Hmph! Master Jiang’s men are useless trash—a whole mob couldn’t kill one woman! We need tougher killers. Go see Chen Haodong again, Matron.”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷回道：“明日我再去趟永泰茶楼。”
[EN] “I’ll head to Yongtai Teahouse tomorrow.”

#CH 291
[ZH] 第二天一早，陈浩宇在门外告诉娘子他要去绸缎庄学习后，千里寻让苦荞将红豆找来。
[ZH] 问过施粥棚的情况，确信施粥棚以前的下人能够顺利应对，便吩咐红豆今日不用带人去，把九名下人全部唤来，跟自己去趟钱塘帮。
[ZH] 红豆回道：“有两人跟着韦嬷嬷一早出去办事。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷管理墨香苑的大事，经常要出去应对各类事务，带几个下人出去再正常不过，千里寻当然不会说什么：“无妨，加上你共八，足以应对。”
[ZH] 很快，红豆将七名下人唤来，有阿升、阿美、丑丫、三宝、嘉祥、福禄和阿运。
[ZH] 千里寻问：“阿运，平常你也跟着韦嬷嬷，为何今日在家？”
[ZH] 阿运回道：“嬷嬷说，今日不需要太多人，让我留下听小姐差遣。”
[ZH] 千里寻点点头：“今日我要找蒋爷好好聊聊，说不定会打一架，钱塘帮人多，你们怕不怕？”
[ZH] 下人们齐声回道：“不怕！”
[ZH] 千里寻命令道：“好！现在就跟我出发！”
[ZH] 自从主动承担施粥棚一事，出门无需请示长辈，省去不少麻烦。况且今日是出去闹事，让长辈知道只会担心，还是不说为妙。
[ZH] 千里寻直接带领下人出了陈府，乘坐两驾马车奔赴钱塘帮。
[ZH] 她虽不愿与梦容计较，可钱塘帮欺负到她头上，必须上门教训一番。
[ZH] 苦荞有自知之明，她不会功夫，去了只能添乱，还是留在墨香苑整理小姐的闺房。
[ZH] 昨日派去的一拨人并未要了女词人的命，还一个个带伤回来，蒋爷还未说什么，春金沫从昨晚到今日一大早，只要是醒着，会一直劝说蒋爷再派些厉害的手下，无论如何要结果了女词人。
[ZH] 蒋爷对春金沫不错，把她看作心尖儿上的人，可面对大事，他毫不含糊。
[ZH] 女词人是知府家千金，若除去她，肯定会得罪官府，对钱塘帮来讲没有多少益处。再说蒋爷已搞清楚女词人与仪王的关系，他更不愿再派人出手。
[EN] First thing next morning, right after Chen Haoyu called outside the door that he was off to the silk shop, Qianli Xun had Kuqiao summon Hongdou. Confirming the veteran hands could run the congee shed fine alone, she told Hongdou to skip it today and round up all nine courtyard servants—they were paying the Qiantang Gang a visit.
[ZH] 自从娶回春金沫，蒋爷早起的习惯没了，喜欢赖在床上把春金沫搂在怀里。
[EN] “Two went out early with Matron Wei on errands,” Hongdou reported.
[ZH] 春金沫趁机继续撒娇：“陈浩东背后可是仪王妃，你除掉幽栖居士，会攀上贵人，还用怕知府大人？”
[EN] “No matter—eight including you is plenty.”
[ZH] 蒋爷在春金沫脸上亲一口：“我问你，仪王和仪王妃谁更厉害？”
[EN] Moments later Hongdou assembled seven more: Asheng, Amei, Chouya, Sanbao, Jiaxiang, Fulu, and Ayun.
[ZH] 春金沫想想说：“当然是仪王更厉害，不过仪王妃是仪王的人，仪王厉害，她自然也厉害。”
[EN] “You usually go with Matron Wei, Ayun—why home today?” Qianli Xun asked.
[ZH] 春金沫显然不懂，蒋爷耐心说：“你以前待在春香楼，自然不了解临安富贵人家之间的事，现在嫁给我，也算有些身份，没事多跟有头有脸的夫人们聚聚，自然会听到许多有趣的八卦，别小看这些八卦，里面可包含着重要信息。”
[EN] “Matron said she didn’t need extra hands and left me for Miss to use.”
[ZH] 春金沫一把推开蒋爷，躺到自己的枕头上，背对着蒋爷：“你还好意思给我说这些？钱塘帮是黑帮，根本上不了台面，再说我出身青楼，身份尊贵的夫人们能瞧上我？”
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded. “Today I’m having a long chat with Master Jiang, and fists might fly. The Qiantang Gang has numbers—anybody scared?”
[ZH] 蒋爷又上前搂住春金沫：“只要我顺利攀上仪王，不愁别人瞧不上你。”
[EN] “Not scared!” all eight shouted.
[ZH] “哼！你连仪王妃的事情都不尽心办，还想攀上仪王？做梦去吧！”
[EN] “Good—move out!”
[ZH] “你可知陈浩宇成亲那日仪王抢亲之事？”
[EN] Since taking over the congee shed, she came and went without asking the elders’ leave, sparing endless questions—better not worry them over a brawl anyway. Boarding two carriages with her eight servants, she rolled straight for the Qiantang Gang. She wouldn’t lay hands on Mengrong, yet Master Jiang had crossed her line and owed her a reckoning. Knowing she couldn’t fight and would only get in the way, Kuqiao stayed back tidying the quarters.
[ZH] 春金沫立刻转过身，惊讶地问：“仪王抢亲？抢幽栖居士？”
[EN] Over at the Qiantang Gang, the ambush squad had limped back bruised and empty-handed the day before. Before Master Jiang even spoke, Chun Jinmo had harped in his ear from dusk till dawn, nagging him to send his best killers to finish the poetess off.
[ZH] 蒋爷点点头：“仪王妃为何要找我杀她，你现在应该明白了吧？”
[EN] Much as Master Jiang doted on his new wife, he didn’t lose his head over big stakes. Killing the Prefect’s daughter meant war with the yamen—bad business—and once he dug up her link to Prince Yi, he wanted zero part of it.
[ZH] “我看她就该杀！竟然跟贵人抢男人！”
[EN] Since marrying Chun Jinmo, Master Jiang had quit rising early, preferring to lounge in bed holding her close.
[ZH] “要想攀上仪王，她可杀不得。我已经打听过，仪王妃是小时候被仪王捡回来的，官家见她照顾仪王有功，加之当时仪王拖着病恹恹的身体，才下旨给他们赐婚。如果仪王那时没生病，仪王妃的位置根本轮不到她，现在她即使贵为仪王妃，在朝中也没个靠山，攀上她一点儿用处都没有。”
[EN] Chun Jinmo pouted against his chest. “Princess Consort Yi backs Chen Haodong! Wipe out Youqi Jushi, hitch yourself to royalty, and who cares about the Prefect?”
[ZH] “陈家大公子为何愿意替仪王妃办事？”
[EN] Kissing her cheek, Master Jiang asked, “Tell me—who’s stronger, Prince Yi or Princess Consort Yi?”
[ZH] “这我就不得而知，反正你听我的准没错，幽栖居士杀不得。除了她爹是知府大人，钱塘帮不能得罪外，仪王更不能得罪。你都不知道，陈浩东让我设计逼陈浩宇娶女词人之事被仪王知道后，他亲自来钱塘帮闹过一回，当时未表露仪王的身份，自称申公子，话里话外明显是为朱家姑娘出头。事后我差人打听，才知道是仪王。你好好想想，什么样的人值得仪王如此动怒？肯定是对他来讲再重要不过的人。”
[EN] “Prince Yi, obviously—yet she’s his wife! If he’s powerful, she is too.”
[ZH] “陈公子岂不亏死？娶个被贵人惦记的人。”
[EN] Master Jiang chuckled patient. “Living in Chunxiang Brothel, you didn’t see how Lin’an’s elite circles work. Now that you’re my wife with status, mingle with respectable ladies—gossip carries real intelligence.”
[ZH] “陈浩宇的事你我管不上，还是想想怎么不得罪贵人。仪王妃我算是应付过去，他让我派人我也派了，只不过我派去的人功夫一般，根本杀不了女词人，仪王妃见钱塘帮的人不行，以后肯定不会再来找我。”
[EN] Shoving him away, Chun Jinmo rolled onto her pillow facing the wall. “Save it! The Qiantang Gang is underworld trash, and I’m ex-brothel—what noble lady would give me the time of day?”
[ZH] 蒋爷又过去搂住春金沫：“说不定今日会有贵客临门……”
[EN] Master Jiang wrapped his arm back around her. “Once I hook onto Prince Yi, nobody will dare look down on you.”
[ZH] 话还未说完，门外传来手下的声音：“帮主，有人来钱塘帮闹事，自称幽栖居士。”
[EN] “Hmph! Won’t even lift a finger for Princess Consort Yi and dreaming of Prince Yi? Keep dreaming!”
[ZH] “刚说贵客，贵客就来。”蒋爷放开春金沫，从床上爬起来，“你也起来出去迎接，人家怎么着也是江南有名的女词人，又是临安贵妇之一，跟她搞好关系，对你打入贵妇圈儿十分有利。”
[EN] “Ever hear about Prince Yi raiding Chen Haoyu’s wedding?”
[ZH] 幽栖居士抢走陈公子，春金沫本就心中不满，又岂会想着与她搞好关系？可蒋爷的话句句在理，不好反驳。她面露不屑，也爬起床，倒要瞧瞧这位女词人前来如何闹事。
[EN] Spinning back around, Chun Jinmo gaped. “Prince Yi raided the wedding—for Youqi Jushi?!”
[ZH] 蒋爷收拾好后，来不及吃早饭，带着几名手下，满脸堆笑迎了出去。
[EN] “Now you get why Princess Consort Yi wants her dead?”
[ZH] 钱塘帮的大门外，千里寻站在下人中间，盯着门口，想象着蒋爷会带一群人冲出来，如若将她和下人们包围，她该如何应对。
[EN] “All the more reason to kill her—stealing a royal’s husband!”
[ZH] 没想到蒋爷只带几名手下，满脸堆笑走出钱塘帮的大门，根本不像要打架的样子。
[EN] “If I want Prince Yi’s favor, touching a hair on her head is suicide.
