I asked around: Prince Yi picked the Princess Consort up as a kid, and His Majesty only decreed the match because she tended him while he lay sick. Without that illness she’d never have made Princess Consort—she’s got zero court backing. Hooking onto her gets us nothing.”
[ZH] 尤其是一见面蒋爷的问话，更让千里寻摸不着头脑：“女侠可用过早饭？若没用，钱塘帮都预备好了，你我一起用？”
[EN] “Then why is Eldest Young Master Chen working for her?”
[ZH] 千里寻收起疑惑的表情正色道：“老娘已用过早饭，即使没用过，又岂会与杀我之人同桌吃饭？你若识相，跪在我面前承认错误，发誓以后不会再派你的手下干杀人的勾当，老娘尚可与你一谈，否则今日老娘非打得你趴在地上跪地求饶不可！”
[EN] “No idea—just trust me, Youqi Jushi is untouchable! Not only is her dad Prefect, Prince Yi himself is off-limits. Know what happened when Prince Yi heard Chen Haodong paid me to trap Chen Haoyu into marrying her? He stormed into my hall in person—calling himself Young Master Shen—all to stand up for Miss Zhu! Only dug up later that he was Prince Yi. Think: how huge does a woman have to be to get a prince swinging fists for her?”
[ZH] 谁知蒋爷真的跪下：“女侠，我错了，不该派人杀你。我发誓，以后带着弟兄们好好开赌坊，不再干那些刀尖上舔血的日子。”
[EN] “Poor Young Master Chen—married a woman a prince won’t let go of.”
[ZH] 千里寻甚觉纳闷，这家伙为何如此听话？
[EN] “Chen Haoyu’s problem, not ours. Me, I just keep out of Prince Yi’s crosshairs. I already brushed off the Princess Consort—sent weak scrubs who couldn’t touch the poetess so she’ll give up on my gang.” Pulling her close again, he grinned. “Bet a VIP shows up at our door today—”
[ZH] 蒋爷起身，走到跟前，小声说：“女侠也要理解我，昨日派人杀你出于无奈，可我派去的人功夫差些，否则女侠怎么可能今日前来找我算账？”
[EN] Right on cue, a subordinate knocked outside. “Chief! Somebody’s at the gate making trouble—calls herself Youqi Jushi!”
[ZH] 来时的气焰去了大半，千里寻问道：“你想做什么？”
[EN] “Speak of the VIP!” Master Jiang rolled out of bed. “Get dressed and come greet her—she’s Jiangnan’s top poetess and one of Lin’an’s premier ladies. Getting friendly with her opens every door in high society for you.”
[ZH] 蒋爷回道：“想请女侠到钱塘帮与我坐下心平气和聊聊天儿。”
[EN] Bitter over her stealing Chen Haoyu, Chun Jinmo bristled, yet couldn’t argue with his logic. Sneering, she got up to see what kind of fuss the poetess planned to kick up.
[ZH] 千里寻盯着蒋爷又胖又黑的脸，倒是诚意满满，可仍旧担心被对方算计：“进去就免了，不如在钱塘帮门外的凉亭下坐坐。”
[EN] Skipping breakfast, Master Jiang marched out with only a few men, grinning ear to ear.
[ZH] “女侠还是不信任我，既如此，听你的。”蒋爷转身对手下说，“把我的早饭端到凉亭下，拿壶酒酿，给女侠喝，再将夫人唤出来。”
[EN] Outside the gate, Qianli Xun stood flanked by her servants, braced for an armed mob to pour out—only to blink in bafflement as Master Jiang strolled out beaming like a host welcoming kin.
[ZH] 千里寻担心对方有诈，还是不饮酒得好，立刻拒绝：“老娘不饮酒，上茶即可。”
[EN] His first line baffled her even more. “Had breakfast yet, heroine? If not, our kitchen’s got a spread ready—eat with me?”
[ZH] “那就来壶西湖龙井？”蒋爷伸出手，“女侠，请。”
[EN] Snapping out of her daze, Qianli Xun turned stern. “Already ate—and even starving I wouldn’t share a table with a guy who tried to kill me! Be smart: get on your knees, own up, and swear off sending thugs to murder people, and maybe we talk. Otherwise I’m pounding you flat till you beg!”
[ZH] 千里寻向亭子走去，红豆等下人也都跟上，站在亭子外时刻关注小姐的安危。
[EN] To her shock, Master Jiang dropped straight to his knees. “I was wrong, heroine! Shouldn’t have sent men after you! I swear I’ll stick to running gambling houses with my boys and quit blood-for-hire for good!”
[ZH] 钱塘帮的手下将早饭和茶摆好后，蒋爷并未让他们守在亭子外。
[EN] *Why’s this mob boss folded so fast?*
[ZH] 千里寻见状向红豆等人说：“站着又累又热，你们回马车里歇着。”
[EN] Rising and stepping close, Master Jiang murmured, “Cut me some slack, heroine—had no choice yesterday, so I picked my sorriest fighters on purpose, or how else would you be standing here today?”
[ZH] 小姐既发话，八人便回到马车里待着。
[EN] Half her steam deflated. “What’s your angle?”
[ZH] 蒋爷将茶满上：“女侠请慢用，我先吃两口。”
[EN] “Just want to invite you inside for a calm chat.”
[ZH] 对方不着急说事，千里寻也懒得理会：“别称呼我女侠，听着怪别扭。”
[EN] Sizing up his broad, dark face—earnest enough, though caution never hurt—she refused. “Skip going inside. Let’s sit in the pavilion right outside your gate.”
[ZH] 蒋爷边吃边说：“那我就跟着我夫人称呼您幽栖居士。”
[EN] “Still don’t trust me—fair enough, your call.” Turning to a henchman, he barked, “Bring my breakfast to the pavilion, grab a jug of rice wine for the heroine, and call Madam out!”
[ZH] 千里寻象征性地端起茶盏呷了一口，又将茶盏放下：“蒋爷到底想与我聊什么？”
[EN] “No alcohol—tea only,” Qianli Xun cut in.
[ZH] 蒋爷放下筷子：“听说幽栖居士与仪王关系不一般，可否帮我牵个线？好让我搭上仪王这层关系。”
[EN] “West Lake Longjing tea, then! After you, heroine.”
[ZH] 原来是为此事，千里寻总算明白对方的用意，难怪他态度如此好。
[EN] Hongdou and the other seven trailed to stand guard outside the pavilion, until Master Jiang dismissed his own thugs after tea and breakfast were set, and Qianli Xun waved her eight back to rest out of the sun inside the carriages.
[ZH] “你帮仪王妃办事，想搭上仪王这层关系实在简单，又何必舍近求远？”
[EN] Pouring her tea, Master Jiang grabbed chopsticks. “Help yourself while I grab a bite.”
[ZH] 蒋爷拿起勺子开始吃粥：“我见过仪王，相貌堂堂，一看就知是堂堂正正之人，仪王妃找我办的事不好拿到台面上来说，找她牵线恐怕不妥，反而会引起仪王怀疑。而找幽栖居士大不相同，除了你我之间没有交易外，重要的是仪王对你十分上心，你说话他自然会听。”
[EN] “Quit calling me ‘heroine’—sounds weird.”
[ZH] 对方的话中流露出对她与仪王关系的猜测，估计很多人已在背后议论，千里寻甚觉厌恶。
[EN] “Then I’ll call you Youqi Jushi like my wife does.”
[ZH] “蒋爷如此说，我若帮了你，岂不坐实大家的猜测？我已嫁到陈家，对婆家十分满意。陈家长辈体恤我，给我自由，允许我外出，我更不能在外做些有损个人声誉之事，恐怕蒋爷要失望。”
[EN] Taking a polite sip, Qianli Xun cut straight to it. “What do you want to talk about, Master Jiang?”
[ZH] 对方一口回绝，蒋爷失望片刻，很快又露出笑脸：“无妨无妨，你既不愿出面帮我，大家交个朋友也是好的，只要你愿意跟我做朋友，我见到仪王也好说话。”
[EN] Setting down his chopsticks, he came clean. “Word is you’re tight with Prince Yi. Could you put in a good word and introduce me to him?”
[ZH] “我交的朋友要么是大家闺秀，要么是文人才子，还从未与蒋爷这号人来往过。”
[EN] *Aha! Now the polite act makes total sense.* “You already work for Princess Consort Yi—getting to Prince Yi through her should be effortless. Why take the long way round?”
[ZH] 又被对方拒绝，蒋爷还是不生气：“没关系，不愿与我交朋友也无妨，今儿你我能坐在一起聊天儿也是好的开始。”
[EN] Scooping congee, Master Jiang explained. “I’ve seen Prince Yi—sharp, upright man. The dirty work Princess Consort Yi hires me for can’t see daylight; going through her would only make Prince Yi suspicious. Going through Youqi Jushi is clean, no shady deals—plus Prince Yi hangs on your every word.”
[ZH] 正说着，传来春金沫的声音：“吆！我当是哪里来的贵客？原来是幽栖居士！”
[EN] Hearing him hint at gossip buzzing behind her back soured her stomach. “If I help you after you put it like that, I just confirm the rumors! I’m happily married into the Chen family. My elders treat me well and grant me freedom—I won’t risk my good name outside. Sorry to disappoint.”
[ZH] 春金沫收拾完，在屋里随便用了几口早饭，才出来见幽栖居士。除了因蒋爷派人叮嘱外，更因春金兰的案子如何进展她还不得而知，想从幽栖居士口中打听一二，更重要的是需抓住机会当面奚落她几句。
[EN] Rejected flat-out, Master Jiang stayed sunny. “No harm done! Even if you won’t make the intro, making friends with you works too—makes talking to Prince Yi easier.”
[ZH] 夫人到来，蒋爷即刻起身，上前牵起春金沫的手：“娘子，小心台阶。”
[EN] “My friends are noble ladies and scholars—never ran with gang bosses.”
[ZH] 蒋爷对待夫人的态度，倒让千里寻产生些许欣赏。
[EN] Turned down twice, he still grinned. “Fair enough—sitting down for a chat today is already a great start.”
[ZH] 春金沫扭着腰肢来到亭子里，在千里寻旁边坐下，一边扇着团扇，一边瞅着她，用不屑的目光研究着。
[EN] Right then Chun Jinmo’s syrupy voice cut in. “Well! Wondered who the VIP was—Youqi Jushi herself!” Having dressed and grabbed a quick bite, she had come out both on Master Jiang’s orders and to dig up Chun Jinlan’s trial results—plus take a fresh dig at her rival.
[ZH] 千里寻很不习惯，为了不让对方继续盯下去，还需说些话：“江夫人好福气，上个台阶都有人扶，而且还是自己的夫君。”
[EN] The second his wife appeared, Master Jiang hopped up and rushed to take her hand. “Watch the steps, Wife.”
[ZH] 蒋爷搭话道：“对自己的娘子好是应该的，别看我外表像个粗人，对女人心可细着呢。”
[EN] Watching the tough gang boss fuss over his wife stirred unexpected warmth in Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 春金沫懒得理会蒋爷：“幽栖居士不必羡慕我，陈公子为你而改变才羡煞旁人。”
[EN] Swaying into the pavilion, Chun Jinmo sat beside her, waving a round fan and eyeing her up and down with disdain. To break the awkward stare, Qianli Xun remarked, “Lucky Madam Jiang—having your husband steady your arm over a single step.”
[ZH] 蒋爷立刻说：“各人有各人的归宿，不必再念着陈浩宇，你若真跟了他，最多是个妾室，哪比得上当我的正室？”
[EN] “Treating one’s wife right is only proper,” Master Jiang beamed. “I look rough, yet I’m tender with my woman.”
[ZH] 春金沫面露厌恶的表情，眼睛瞧向一边，体会着内心的无奈。
[EN] Ignoring him, Chun Jinmo sneered, “No need to envy me, Youqi Jushi—turning Young Master Chen into a saint has everyone green with envy.”
[ZH] 千里寻不由对蒋爷生起怜悯之情，曾经她认为的好色之徒在喜欢的人面前也是做小伏低。
[EN] “Each person has their own path,” Master Jiang cut in gently. “Quit pining for Chen Haoyu—with him you’d only be a concubine, nowhere near being my primary wife.”
[ZH] “蒋爷，我答应你，若有机会碰到仪王，帮你说句话。”
[EN] Disgust flashed across Chun Jinmo’s face as she looked away, trapped in silent bitterness.
[ZH] 蒋爷高兴不已，起身拱手致谢：“谢过幽栖居士！要是帮我引荐成功，你就是我钱塘帮的恩人！”
[EN] A pang of pity hit Qianli Xun—even a rough underworld boss turned humble before the woman he loved. “All right, Master Jiang. If I run into Prince Yi, I’ll put in a word for you.”
[ZH] 千里寻端起茶盏又抿一口茶：“你把我当恩人，江夫人未必如此看待。”
[EN] Overjoyed, Master Jiang leaped up and bowed hands clasped. “Thank you, Youqi Jushi! Pull that off and you’re the savior of the Qiantang Gang!”
[ZH] 蒋爷又坐下，瞧一眼背对着自己的夫人：“幽栖居士见谅，娘子刚嫁给我，还不适应，时间一长自然好。我……还有一事需幽栖居士帮个忙。”
[EN] Taking another sip, she glanced sideways. “You call me savior—doubt Madam Jiang agrees.”
[ZH] 对方得寸进尺又有求于她，千里寻本想拒绝，想到他对老婆不错，还是勉强听听：“什么事？说吧。”
[EN] Sitting back down with an apologetic look at his wife’s turned back, Master Jiang murmured, “Bear with her, Youqi Jushi—newly wed, still adjusting, she’ll come around. Actually... one more tiny favor?”
[ZH] 蒋爷先一通夸赞：“幽栖居士名闻江南，是鼎鼎有名的女词人，又是知府千金，妥妥的大家闺秀，还嫁到陈家，在临安的贵妇中地位肯定非同一般。我娘子没法跟您比，她既然嫁给我，我需想方设法提高她的地位，还请幽栖居士带带她，也让她融入你们的圈子。”
[EN] *Give an inch, take a mile!* Still, moved by his devotion to his wife, she relented. “Spit it out.”
[ZH] 原来是为此事，千里寻觉得好笑，可又觉得野鸡想要变凤凰没有什么错，只是蒋爷努力错方向，不如提点他几句。
[EN] “Youqi Jushi’s fame spans Jiangnan, Prefect’s daughter, married into Lin’an’s richest clan—top tier among Lin’an’s noble ladies. My wife can’t compare, yet since she married me, I want to lift her standing. Could you take her along and ease her into high-society circles?”
[ZH] “蒋爷对夫人如此上心，令我感动。蒋爷有没有想过，我之所以能融入贵妇的圈子，是因为我父亲是临安知府，我的婆家又是临安首富。若我是普通人家的姑娘，也不会有这等机会。所以江夫人想要融入，还需蒋爷自己努力才行。”
[EN] Amused—yet sympathetic to a man wanting better for his wife—Qianli Xun offered a gentle reality check. “Your devotion to your wife moves me, Master Jiang. Still, think about why I move in those circles: my father is Prefect of Lin’an, and my in-laws are the wealthiest family in town. Were I an ordinary girl, nobody would let me in either. If you want Madam Jiang accepted, you’ll have to earn that standing yourself.”

#CH 292
[ZH] 蒋爷听出些言外之意来，无非暗含着钱塘帮是黑帮，上不了台面。
[ZH] “您说得对，自从娶了娘子，我的想法也改变，想让钱塘帮改头换面，赚些正经银子，也好让我娘子出去有面子。”
[ZH] 还别说，千里寻又被感动。
[ZH] “说句心里话，贵妇聚会我很少参加，一来我不喜欢议论家长里短，二来不过是吃吃喝喝。诗会我倒经常参加，若江夫人乐意，等下次有人请我参加诗会，带带也无妨。”
[ZH] “多谢！”蒋爷又一阵开心，立刻提醒春金沫，“娘子还不快感谢幽栖居士？”
[ZH] 谁知春金沫一口回绝：“我想融入贵妇的圈子自有人带，至于诗会我毫无兴致。”
[ZH] 蒋爷尴尬道：“幽栖居士莫见怪，只怪我自作主张，没与娘子商议好。”
[ZH] 对方拒绝再好不过，两人本来就不是一类人，硬要拉扯到一起，谁都会觉得别扭。
[ZH] 千里寻轻松道：“有人带江夫人再好不过。”
[ZH] 春金沫终于面向蒋爷：“我有事与幽栖居士说，你能否回避一下？”
[ZH] 蒋爷的目的已达到，没必要再留着，满口答应：“娘子发话，我岂有不听之理？幽栖居士，失陪。”
[ZH] 千里寻点点头，心中感慨，本着打一架的目的来钱塘帮，结果却喝着茶与蒋爷聊天儿，还发现蒋爷一大优点：对老婆好。
[ZH] 见蒋爷走远，春金沫直接说：“你看上去心情不错，该不会春金兰没当成陈公子的妾室？”
[ZH] “令江夫人失望，陈浩宇无罪释放，春金兰因愧疚当场自刎身亡。”
[ZH] “什么？死了？难道她不是被陈公子糟蹋？”
[ZH] “疤子认下所有罪行。”
[ZH] 春金沫有些许伤感：“青楼女子的命就是卑贱，说没就没了。”
[ZH] “江夫人应该珍惜现在的日子，蒋爷对你不错，看得出他是真心待你。”
[ZH] “可惜，我并不爱他，嫁给他是身不由己。”
[ZH] “江夫人不觉得在这个时代想跟喜欢的人在一起是件很奢侈的事吗？”
[ZH] 春金沫终于找到奚落对方的话：“怎么？幽栖居士还惦记着仪王？差点因此连命都没了，我劝你还是不要企图跟贵人抢男人。”
[ZH] 千里寻后悔一时忘情与对方说些真情实感，面前的人根本不是一个可以与她谈心之人。
[EN] Catching the hint—an underworld gang had zero respectability—Master Jiang nodded. “Dead right. Ever since marrying my wife, I’ve wanted to turn the Qiantang Gang legit, earn clean silver, and give her real face when she steps outside.”
[ZH] “江夫人不知从哪里听来些污言秽语？你喜欢说，我未必喜欢听，失陪。”千里寻起身就要离开。
[EN] Touched all over again, Qianli Xun softened. “Truth is, I rarely attend society teas—hate gossip and empty feasting. Poetry salons, though, I go to plenty. If Madam Jiang wants, next time I’m invited, I don’t mind bringing her along.”
[ZH] 春金沫在背后大声说：“别以为你逃过一劫以后还会如此幸运，仪王妃不会放过你！”
[EN] “Thank you!” Thrilled, Master Jiang nudged his wife. “Thank Youqi Jushi, Wife!”
[ZH] 千里寻停下脚步，并未转身：“可惜，你的夫君蒋爷已放过我。”
[EN] Chun Jinmo shot it down cold. “I’ve got someone else to bring me into high society—and zero interest in poetry salons.”
[ZH] “哼！我夫君是个孬货！这世上比我夫君狠的人多的是，仪王妃有的是钱，不怕请不到人！”
[EN] Flushing awkward, Master Jiang apologized. “Forgive me, Youqi Jushi—spoke out of turn without asking my wife.”
[ZH] 千里寻这才转过身：“仪王妃还会请谁我并不关心，即使来了老娘也不怕。只希望江夫人珍惜眼前人，别辜负了蒋爷的好。”
[EN] Relieved—dragging an unwilling rival to poetry gatherings would’ve been torture—Qianli Xun smiled easy. “Even better if she already has a sponsor.”
[ZH] 千里寻说完，转身离开，边走边为蒋爷惋惜。又一想，蒋爷曾经做过不少坏事，遇到没有真心对待他的夫人算是报应，自己又何必为他人的因果而流露情感。
[EN] Chun Jinmo turned to her husband. “Give Youqi Jushi and me a moment alone.”
[ZH] 春金沫对着幽栖居士的背影啐道：“呸！横什么横！以后有你好果子吃！”
[EN] His goal already met, Master Jiang rose without hesitation. “My wife speaks, I obey! Excuse me, Youqi Jushi.”
[ZH] 千里寻白白带着人跑一趟，没有揍成蒋爷，还因他对自家娘子好而感动，忍不住答应帮他与仪王牵线搭桥。
[EN] Watching him stroll off, Qianli Xun marveled: came here spoiling for a brawl, wound up sipping tea and discovering the mob boss was a hopeless romantic.
[ZH] 人生常常如此戏剧化，事情的结果往往与预期的设想背道而驰。不过千里寻还是感到欣慰，起码蒋爷以后不会再帮梦容害她。
[EN] Once Master Jiang was out of earshot, Chun Jinmo cut straight to business. “Looking pretty cheerful—so Chun Jinlan didn’t become Young Master Chen’s concubine?”
[ZH] 希望此事后梦容能放弃残忍的念头，不要再将注意力放在最不想破坏她幸福的人身上。
[EN] “Sorry to disappoint, Madam Jiang. Chen Haoyu was acquitted, and Chun Jinlan killed herself in court out of shame.”
[ZH] 千里寻回到陈府南门口时，透过马车的窗户瞧见东方尘泽身边的小厮化雨，他手中拿着请帖在门口向里张望。
[EN] “What?! Dead?! Wasn’t Young Master Chen the one who ruined her?”
[ZH] 难道东方公子又要举办诗会？
[EN] “Scarface confessed to the whole thing.”
[ZH] 红豆等人先跳下马车，再伸手将小姐扶下。
[EN] A shadow crossed Chun Jinmo’s eyes. “A courtesan’s life—worth less than dirt, gone in a blink.”
[ZH] 化雨早已瞧见，迎上来：“真巧，在门口遇见幽栖居士，省得等苦荞姐姐前来。”
[EN] “Cherish what you have, Madam Jiang. Master Jiang treats you sincerely.”
[ZH] “你找我何事？”千里寻瞧着化雨手中的请帖，“该不会东方公子又要举办诗会？”
[EN] “Too bad I don’t love him—I had no choice.”
[ZH] 化雨将手中的请帖递到幽栖居士面前：“您猜得没错，我家公子是要举办诗会，差我亲自将请帖送来。”
[EN] “Don’t you think marrying for love in this era is a rare luxury anyway?”
[ZH] 千里寻接过，发现请帖上的地址写的是聚香楼：“还是常请的几位朋友吗？”
[EN] Chun Jinmo jumped on the opening with a smirk. “Still pining for Prince Yi, Youqi Jushi? Nearly cost you your neck already—quit trying to steal a royal’s man!”
[ZH] “这次诗会请的人必须在临安的诗坛有些名头，虽也请些身份尊贵之人，但仅限临安官员。以前我家公子常邀请的人中，乔公子必然会请，曹公子和安公子这几年因品评幽栖居士的诗词混出些名气，也在邀请之列，幽栖居士您更不必说。只有苗夫人没有名气，公子并未邀请她。”
[EN] Kicking herself for getting philosophical with someone incapable of real talk, Qianli Xun stood. “No idea where you pick up such gutter talk. Enjoy talking to yourself—I’m leaving.”
[ZH] 化雨的一番解释足以表明此次诗会非同以往，千里寻没有任何拒绝的理由，欣然答应：“有劳，我定会按时参加。”
[EN] Chun Jinmo called after her, “Don’t think dodging one bullet means you’re safe! Princess Consort Yi won’t let you off!”
[ZH] “我家公子说了，还要劳烦幽栖居士写首新词拿到诗会之上。”
[EN] Pausing without turning, Qianli Xun replied, “Too bad your husband already did.”
[ZH] “那是自然。”
[EN] “Hmph! My husband’s a spineless coward! Plenty of killers out there ten times deadlier, and Princess Consort Yi has oceans of silver to hire them!”
[ZH] 化雨拜别后满意离去。
[EN] Turning back one last time, Qianli Xun said coolly, “Whoever she hires, bring them on—I’m not scared. Just treasure the man right in front of you and stop spitting on Master Jiang’s heart.”
[ZH] 千里寻又将请帖翻看一番。虽说长辈不怎么限制她自由，可此次诗会男子居多，她前去参加还需向长辈禀明。
[EN] Walking back to her carriages, she pitied Master Jiang—then shrugged it off: the guy had done plenty of dirt in his life, maybe an unloving wife was his karma. Behind her, Chun Jinmo spat, “Cocky bitch! Wait till you get what’s coming!”
[ZH] 也应给陈浩宇说一声，他既尊重她，每日出门或回来都会打声招呼，她也需将他放在眼里，征求他的同意。
[EN] Funny how life twisted—marched out to beat up a gang boss and ended up volunteering to play matchmaker with Prince Yi. At least Master Jiang was off Mengrong’s payroll now; maybe Mengrong would finally cool down.
[ZH] 打定主意后，千里寻让下人们先回墨香苑，她拿着请帖打算去请示姚轻琰。这个时间婆婆应该在老太太的院子，正好过去征得所有长辈同意。
[EN] Rolling back up to Chen Manor’s south gate, Qianli Xun spotted Dongfang Chenze’s page boy Huayu peeking in with an invitation card.
[ZH] 这两日说话艺人身体不适告了假，三个儿媳妇每日照常按时陪老太太说话聊天儿。今日每个人都有高兴事，心情看上去均不错。
[EN] *Another poetry salon?*
[ZH] 夏老太太坐在榻上，将三个媳瞧一遍：“昨晚浩宇来看过我，几日功夫他变化甚大，比以前稳重许多，口口声声向我保证往后绝不会再做荒唐事。坏事中也有好事，我们陈家最让人操心的后辈跟换了个人似的，这是陈家之福。你们三个牢牢记住，要教他们三兄弟和睦相处，无论以后干什么心都要往一处使。”
[EN] Stepping down with Hongdou’s help, she greeted Huayu as he hurried over. “Lucky timing—saved waiting for Sister Kuqiao! Young Master is hosting a poetry salon and sent me to deliver your invitation personally.”
[ZH] 三位儿媳齐声回道：“儿媳记下！”
[EN] Opening it, Qianli Xun saw the venue: Juxiang Tower. “The usual crowd?”
[ZH] “还有一事，告诉你们也无妨，让你们心里也好有个数。”夏老太太端起茶盏喝了一口，“昨日，江林告诉我想在今年将产业继承人给定下，我同意了，再说也没有反对的理由。”
[EN] “Not this time! Only poets with real standing in Lin’an’s literary circles—plus high-ranking Lin’an officials. From the old crowd, Young Master Qiao is invited of course, and Young Masters Cao and An made names for themselves critiquing your verse, so they’re in—and Youqi Jushi goes without saying. Madam Miao has no literary standing, so she wasn’t invited.”
[ZH] 包氏心喜，正如夫君预料，商妓前来闹事逼得大哥早作决断。就目前情形来看，族中的长辈必然会选自己的儿子。
[EN] Clearly a major event—no reason to decline. “Tell your master I’ll be there on time.”
[ZH] 再高兴也不能过分表露，包氏装作不理解的样子：“如果我没记错还有一年的时间，为何要着急提前？”
[EN] “Young Master also asks that you bring a brand-new ci lyric for the occasion.”
[ZH] “江林自有他的道理，只管听他的，不必多问，再说你们手中也没有关键的一票，何必管那么多？知道有此事即可。”
[EN] “Of course.”
[ZH] 夏老太太的一番话搞得包氏不好再言语。
[EN] Seeing Huayu off, Qianli Xun tapped the card against her palm. Even though her in-laws granted her freedom, this salon would be packed with men—proper respect meant clearing it with the elders and checking with Chen Haoyu too, since he reported his comings and goings to her every day. Sending the servants back to Moxiang Courtyard, she walked straight to Old Madam Xia’s courtyard where Yao Qingyan would be sitting.
[ZH] 崔氏对继承人之事并不上心，主要跟自己儿子没什么关系，等浩飞考上功名，说不定陈家的生意还得仰仗她的儿子，要知道在朝为官比商贾的身份不知要尊贵多少倍。
[EN] With the storyteller out sick two days running, the three daughters-in-law were keeping Old Madam Xia company, everyone in high spirits today.
[ZH] 可总不能一直紧闭嘴巴不说话，为了彰显自己的存在，随口问道：“母亲，不知大哥将日子定在什么时候？”
[EN] Seated on the couch, Old Madam Xia looked over all three. “Haoyu came to see me last night. Only a few days and he’s matured leaps and bounds—swore he’ll never pull foolish stunts again. Good came out of bad: our most worrisome boy turned into a new man! Remember, all three of you: teach the three cousins to stick together and pull as one.”
[ZH] “具体日子还未定，等江林与族中各位长辈通过气后，大家共同商议，挑选一个吉利的日子。”
[EN] “Yes, Mother!” all three chorused.
[ZH] 老太太话音刚落，一位丫鬟走进来：“老太太，二少夫人前来拜见。”
[EN] “One more thing so you aren’t caught off guard.” Sipping tea, Old Madam Xia went on, “Yesterday Jianglin asked to name the business heir this year instead of next. I approved—no reason to object.”
[ZH] “请她进来。”
[EN] Joy flared inside Madam Bao—just as her husband predicted, the courtesan scandal pushed Eldest Brother to act fast, and her son had the clan elders locked up! Hiding her smirk, she played puzzled. “Isn’t there still a full year left? Why rush?”
[ZH] 丫鬟出去后，不一会儿千里寻走进屋，向诸位长辈行完礼后，拿出请帖：“奶奶，婆婆，我刚到家门口，东方公子差人送来请帖，邀请我明日参加诗会。听化雨说，此次诗会邀请的都是有头有脸的人物，我不好直接拒绝，暂时接下请帖，再来请示长辈。诗会中男子居多，淑真前去恐怕再惹非议。”
[EN] “Jianglin has his reasons—just go along without questioning. None of you hold a deciding vote anyway; just know it’s happening.” That shut Madam Bao right up.
[ZH] 夏老太太并未吩咐丫鬟去接请帖：“不必拿给我瞧，人家送来请帖是瞧得上你，既是给你面子，也是给陈家面子，为何不去？男子居多又如何？谁想说什么让他说去，奶奶支持你去。”
[EN] Unbothered since her bookish son wasn’t competing—once Haofei passed the civil exams and took court office, the Chens would be leaning on him anyway—Madam Cui still chimed in to stay visible. “Has Eldest Brother picked a date yet, Mother?”
[ZH] 姚轻琰自然也不会阻拦：“淑真，想去就去吧，诗会的事没必要给长辈们说，自己做主即可。快，别站着，过来坐下。”
[EN] “Not yet. He’ll sound out the clan elders and pick an auspicious day together.”
[ZH] 千里寻走到离姚轻琰较近的椅子旁坐下，心想：“我虽早料到是这样的结果，可说与不说效果完全不一样。说了，长辈不仅会觉得我懂事，对我也会更加放心；若不说，她们嘴上不言语，心里多少会不满。”
[EN] Just then a maid announced Second Young Madam. Inviting her in, Old Madam Xia listened as Qianli Xun curtsied and presented the invitation. “Grandmother, Mother-in-law, Young Master Dongfang just sent an invitation to a poetry salon tomorrow. Huayu says every notable figure in town is going, so I accepted tentatively to ask your leave first—with mostly men attending, I wouldn’t want to stir up gossip.”
[ZH] 崔氏小心问：“淑真啊，你说的诗会是不是在聚香楼举行？”
[EN] Old Madam Xia didn’t even glance at the card. “No need to show me! Inviting you honors both you and Chen Manor—of course you go! Mostly men? Let tongues wag! Grandmother backs you.”
[ZH] 千里寻回道：“正是。”
[EN] “Go right ahead, Shuzhen,” Yao Qingyan agreed. “No need to ask us over poetry salons—decide for yourself. Come sit.”
[ZH] 崔氏高兴道：“浩飞一大早也拿着个请帖，说明日要去聚香楼参加诗会，看来你们参加的是同一个诗会，到时你可要帮忙照顾他。”
[EN] Taking a seat beside Yao Qingyan, Qianli Xun smiled inside: sure she knew they’d say yes, yet asking first showed respect and won even more trust.
[ZH] 千里寻说：“自家兄弟，我自然会多与他在一处。”
[EN] Madam Cui leaned over eager. “Shuzhen, is that salon at Juxiang Tower?”
[ZH] 老太太慨叹道：“年轻时我也曾羡慕文人墨客举办的诗会，怎奈我对诗词一窍不通，从未奢望有机会参加。没想到我们陈家竟然有两人被邀请，淑真更不必说，浩飞现在也成了有头有脸的文人，实在可喜可贺啊！”
[EN] “Yes, Third Aunt.”
[ZH] 崔氏更是喜上眉梢，为了夸儿子，还需强调一下本次诗会有多不一般：“母亲猜猜明日的诗会背后的金主是何人？”
[EN] Madam Cui beamed. “Haofei got an invitation this morning too! Same salon—look out for him tomorrow, will you?”
[ZH] 老太太以逗乐的心态随口说：“瞧你那表情，难不成是仪王殿下？”
[EN] “Of course—we’re family.”
[ZH] “母亲不愧是母亲，一语猜中。我听浩飞说，仪王出资，要让东方公子的诗会成为临安第一。明日仪王肯定会去，看在他的面子上临安哪个官员敢不去？浩飞有机会参加这等诗会，我这当娘的脸上也有光。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia sighed fondly. “Used to envy literary salons when I was young, only I don’t know a rhyme from a radish! Now Chen Manor has two guests invited—Shuzhen goes without saying, and Haofei’s made a name for himself too. Wonderful news!”
[ZH] 一旁的包氏只觉心中涌起一股酸酸的醋意来，心想：“参加诗会又能如何？哪比得上我儿子学习做生意的本事来得实在？看你眉飞色舞的样子，若浩飞考不上功名就等着哭吧！”
[EN] Puffed with pride, Madam Cui added, “Guess who’s bankrolling tomorrow’s salon, Mother!”
[ZH] 千里寻感到疑惑，仪王为何要出资帮东方尘泽举办诗会？她担心诗会上遇到梦容，若她也去，自己还是尽量躲避为妙。
[EN] Old Madam Xia played along. “By that grin, don’t tell me it’s Prince Yi?”
[ZH] “既与仪王有关，请帖送上门来，更不能拒绝。”夏老太太说完，不想再继续诗会的话题，迅速将话锋一转，“淑真，你可是刚从施粥棚回来？”
[EN] “Bull’s-eye! Haofei says Prince Yi put up the silver to make Young Master Dongfang’s salon number one in Lin’an! With Prince Yi attending, what Lin’an official dares skip? Having Haofei invited makes me proud as punch!”
[ZH] 千里寻不想让长辈们担心，并未照实说：“是……是刚从施粥棚回来。”
[EN] Sour envy twisted Madam Bao’s stomach. *Poetry salon—big deal! Wait till Haofei flunks the exams and see who’s crying!*
[ZH] “仪王利民之举，百姓称赞，要不了多久，陈家的施粥棚也该撤了。你公爹说知府大人让现在就关了施粥棚，把省下的银子拿去建土屋和窑洞，我没同意。土屋和窑洞建好前，流浪之人还需有个填饱肚子的地方，官府需要的银子陈家一个字儿不少给添上，不能为省几个钱饿着可怜的百姓。因此，你还需辛苦些时日。”
[EN] Qianli Xun frowned inside: why was Prince Yi funding Dongfang Chenze’s salon? Worse, if Mengrong showed up, she’d have to steer wide clear.
[ZH] 老太太的一番言论，令千里寻觉得陈家不愧是义商，心中更是敬佩得五体投地：“自从懒人村的人回山里后，施粥棚好多了，我即使不去也不会有事，根本谈不上辛苦。”
[EN] “All the more reason not to decline if Prince Yi’s behind it,” Old Madam Xia said, then changed topics. “Just back from the congee shed, Shuzhen?”
[ZH] “那就好。”
[EN] Unwilling to worry them with the truth, Qianli Xun fibbed, “Yes... just back.”
[ZH] 老太太又想起浩宇的事，向姚轻琰使眼色。
[EN] “Prince Yi’s relief project has the whole city cheering—before long our congee shed can wind down. Father-in-law says the Prefect suggested shutting it now to put the silver toward building cottages and cave dwellings, and I said no! Until those roofs are up, homeless folks still need hot meals. Chen Manor will cover the government’s share down to the last copper without starving the poor. Sorry to keep you busy a while longer.”
[ZH] 姚轻琰明白母亲的意思，开口说：“淑真啊，这几日浩宇的事让你又费心又受惊的，现在一切归于平静，老太太与我，还有你公爹商量，以后你跟浩宇的事我们不再插手，你们的日子你们过，只要是往好里过比什么都强。”
[EN] Respect for the Chens’ integrity swelled in Qianli Xun’s chest. “Since the Lazybones Village folks went back to the hills, the shed runs smooth even without me—no trouble at all.”
[ZH] 千里寻被感动，陈家的长辈再次给予她自由。
[EN] “Good.” Old Madam Xia shot Yao Qingyan a glance.
[ZH] 昨日她要求给陈浩宇两年的时间，事后担心了好一阵，怕陈家长辈知道后出面阻拦，没想到他们如此善解人意，搞得她更不舍得离开陈家。也正是因为这份不舍，她才愿意跟陈浩宇继续努力。
[EN] Catching her cue, Yao Qingyan said gently, “Shuzhen, Haoyu’s messes put you through plenty of stress and fright these past days. Now that peace is back, Grandmother, Father-in-law, and I agreed we won’t meddle between you and Haoyu anymore. Live your marriage your way—so long as you’re building toward good days, nothing matters more.”
[ZH] 千言万语只化作一句话：“淑真……谢过长辈，也会将长辈的话牢记在心。”
[EN] Emotion tightened Qianli Xun’s throat. She had fretted over how the elders would react to her two-year chastity pact, and instead they handed her total trust—making her want to stay in this family and give Haoyu a real shot more than ever. “Shuzhen thanks Grandmother and Mother-in-law—I’ll take every word to heart.”

#CH 293
[ZH] 司理院的审讯室内，申简辰亲自坐镇，他想尽快将陈浩东揪出来。
[ZH] 审讯室里放置着各种刑具，只看那么一眼足以令人吓得胆寒心怯。
[ZH] 疤子坐在椅子上，上身被粗绳捆 绑，耷拉个脑袋。
[ZH] 韩谷坐在一旁，提笔展纸，准备录口供。
[ZH] 温信手里拿着粗鞭，指着疤子：“把头抬起来，老实回答大人的问题，否则先打你二十鞭。”
[ZH] 疤子抬起耷拉的脑袋，看到眼前的鞭子以及周围的刑具，有些后悔揽下所有罪责。想起一百两银子，决定再坚持一回，否则将陈大公子抖搂出来，他被抓起来，银子必然泡汤。
[ZH] 他将目光投向与他面对面正襟危坐的参军大人，也是仪王，临安城的节度使，心有百姓，大家都传他是位好王爷，不如装得可怜些，说不定他心一软，把自己给放了。
[ZH] 疤子装出哭腔：“呜呜，救苦救难的王爷，我也是个可怜人，也是个男人，家里给娶不上媳妇，见到漂亮姑娘心里痒得难受，才对春金兰动起歪心思。我发誓，我跟她绝对是你情我愿，她当时还乐得不行，她就是想给陈家二公子当妾室才不愿承认，要说我有错，就是不该把她卖到妓院，呜呜，王爷您想怎么处置我都行，千万别要了我的命，我家还有一位七十岁的老母等着我养活，呜呜……！”
[ZH] 疤子以为自己装得情真意切，面前的王爷怎么说也会心有不忍。
[ZH] 谁知传来严肃而冷漠的声音：“少来这一套，再哭，先给你来一套辣椒水尝尝。”
[ZH] 疤子立刻收起哭声：“王爷想知道什么？尽管问。”
[ZH] “穿黑色披风的人是谁？”
[ZH] “没……没有此人，是丫鬟阿叶胡说。”
[ZH] “阿叶没有任何理由编假话，倒是你，假话连篇。”
[ZH] “我……我说的全是真的，不敢欺瞒王爷。”
[ZH] “哼！有两处你说了假话，一处是你跟春金兰你情我愿。她现在死了，无法再与你对证，但有一个细节便能证明你说的是假话。让阿叶进来。”
[ZH] 温信打开审讯室的门，阿叶从外面走进来，第一次来此，被周围的刑具吓得不敢抬头，更不敢主动说话。
[ZH] 申简辰用温和的声音说：“别怕，又把你叫来，是想问问你当时听到春金兰的喊叫声是什么样的？”
[ZH] 阿叶不解，照实说：“大喊大叫……”
[ZH] 疤子插嘴道：“她跟我在一起开心，当然会大喊大叫……”
[EN] Inside the Judicial Office interrogation room, Shen Jianchen presided in person, determined to smoke out Chen Haodong.
[ZH] 温信用鞭子指着：“再开口小心我抽你！”
[EN] Row upon row of torture gear lined the dim walls, chilling the blood at a glance. Bound to a chair with thick rope, Scarface hung his head while Han Gu poised brush and paper to take notes.
[ZH] 疤子赶紧闭口，不敢再随意开口。
[EN] Wen Xin tapped a heavy whip against his palm. “Head up and answer His Lordship straight, or you get twenty lashes first!”
[ZH] 申简辰继续问：“我是想问，她的喊叫声是那种享受的大喊大叫？还是恐惧害怕，充满愤恨的大喊大叫？”
[EN] Eyeing the whip and irons, Scarface half regretted taking the fall—then pictured those hundred taels of silver and dug his heels in. Turn on Eldest Young Master Chen and he’d never see a copper!
[ZH] 阿叶总算听明白：“是第二种，那声音我至今忘不了，还能听到那姑娘说，我要杀了你！”
[EN] Eyeing the prince—rumored across Lin’an as kindhearted and compassionate—Scarface tried turning on the waterworks. “Merciful Prince! I’m a poor lonely guy whose family can’t afford a wife! Seeing a pretty girl made me weak, so I slipped up with Chun Jinlan! I swear it was consensual—she loved it, just denied it hoping to snag Second Young Master! My only crime was selling her to a brothel! Punish me however you want, just spare my life—I’ve got a seventy-year-old mother to feed! Waaah!”
[ZH] 申简辰埋怨道：“为何在公堂不说得详细些？”
[EN] Cold steel rang in Shen Jianchen’s voice. “Drop the act. One more fake sob and we pour chili water down your nose.”
[ZH] 阿叶胆怯地回道：“大人……大人没问得……细致。”
[EN] Scarface shut off the tears on the spot. “Ask whatever you want, Your Highness.”
[ZH] 申简辰心想，这倒怪起他来？
[EN] “Who was the man in the black cloak?”
[ZH] “你可以回去了。”
[EN] “Nobody! Aye made him up!”
[ZH] 温信又将审讯室的门打开，他心中觉得好笑，公堂之上，王爷不好当着众人的面问得太过细致。
[EN] “Aye has zero reason to lie—you, on the other hand, are spinning fairy tales.”
[ZH] 待阿叶出去后，关好门，瞧见王爷投来的眼神，温信不好再偷乐，也即刻明白王爷的意思，命令差役端来一盆水。
[EN] “Every word’s true—I’d never fool Your Highness!”
[ZH] 温信亲自解开疤子身上的绳子，把疤子押到水盆跟前，使劲儿将他的头按进水里。
[EN] “Hmph! Two blatant lies. First, claiming it was consensual. Chun Jinlan’s dead, so she can’t face you, yet one detail blows your story sky-high. Bring Aye in.”
[ZH] 开始疤子还能忍住，时间一长，憋不下去，连连求饶：“饶了我吧！饶了我吧！我说！我说！”
[EN] Wen Xin opened the door. Terrified by the torture rack, Aye stepped in with eyes glued to the floor.
[ZH] 温信松开按住头的手，疤子立刻把头从水里抽出来，深深吸口气，只觉无比舒畅。温信将他押到仪王面前，在他腿上踢了一脚：“跪下！老实交代！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen softened his voice. “Don’t be scared. Tell me what Chun Jinlan’s screams sounded like that night.”
[ZH] 疤子心想，绝不能承认是自己糟蹋了春金兰，否则连小命都没了。为了一百两银子也不能将陈大公子交代出来，到底该如何编排？疤子灵机一动：“我跟春金兰的确不是你情我愿，但她也不是我糟蹋的，是……是……”
[EN] Puzzled, Aye replied, “Just... loud screaming...”
[ZH] 申简辰肯定道：“否认有穿黑色披风之人，便是你第二处假话，快说！穿黑色披风的人到底是谁？”
[EN] Scarface cut in, “Having fun with me, of course she screamed—”
[ZH] “是……是……是黑煞虎。”疤子觉得自己实在聪明，瞬间拉来黑煞虎当垫背的。
[EN] Wen Xin flicked the whip. “One more peep and you taste leather!” Scarface clamped shut.
[ZH] 申简辰没听过这个名字：“黑煞虎？”
[EN] “What I mean,” Shen Jianchen clarified, “did she sound like she was enjoying herself, or terrified and furious?”
[ZH] 温信解释道：“黑煞虎是天目山上的土匪头目，集结一帮人，烧杀抢掠，无恶不作。”
[EN] Aye got it at once. “Terrified and furious! I’ll never forget that voice—she was shrieking, ‘I’ll kill you!’”
[ZH] 无意中牵扯出一恶人，并非申简辰关注的重点，他也根本不相信：“疤子，你以为扯出黑煞虎我便信你？再不老实交代，荆条伺候！”
[EN] “Why didn’t you say that in court?”
[ZH] 荆条打在屁股和腿上比板子还疼，疤子害怕的同时，继续扯着谎言：“小人说的句句属实。黑煞虎本名叫李虎，跟我是邻居，后来犯了事儿，被官府捉拿，跑到山上集结一帮人当起匪寇。去年三月初一他从山上下来，偷偷住在我家，逼我给他搞个娘们儿。正好三月初三那日陈家乡下的宅子没人，我才想方设法灌醉管家，偷偷把黑煞虎带进宅子，为了不被人发现，他才披上黑色披风。所以，春金兰是黑煞虎糟蹋的，不是我！”
[EN] Aye shrank back. “Your Lordship... didn’t ask that detailed...”
[ZH] 疤子为自己编得圆满而满意，谁让黑煞虎以前就是他的邻居，确实也曾下山找过他。将事情赖在他的身上，他与陈大公子便可相安无事，官府拿黑煞虎没办法，面前的王爷估计也无计可施，最后只能将他无罪释放，到时一百两银子到手，岂不美哉？
[EN] *Blaming me now?* “All right, you may go.”
[ZH] 申简辰虽不相信，还是追问道：“既是此人，为何不早些交代？反而替他隐瞒？”
[EN] Opening the door for Aye, Wen Xin hid a grin—obviously the prince couldn’t grill a maid over bedroom noises in open court. Shutting the door again, he caught the prince’s look and signaled constables to bring in a tub of water.
[ZH] 疤子的头几乎贴在地上：“小人是怕官府给我定下勾结匪徒的罪名，才……才一再隐瞒。”
[EN] Untying Scarface, Wen Xin dragged him to the tub and shoved his head straight under. Scarface held out a few seconds before lungs burned and he thrashed, gasping as Wen Xin hauled him up. “Mercy! I’ll talk! I’ll talk!”
[ZH] 疤子说得滴水不漏，没有丝毫证据指向陈浩东。申简辰不由佩服起来，陈家大公子实在厉害，任何把柄和痕迹都未留下，连疤子这等见财忘义之人也能拿捏住。
[EN] Kicking the backs of his knees to drop him before Prince Yi, Wen Xin barked, “Talk straight!”
[ZH] 想到“见财忘义”四个字，估计陈浩东许诺疤子不少好处，他才宁可拉出黑煞虎当挡箭牌，也不会牵扯陈浩东半分半毫。
[EN] Confess to rape and he swung; rat out Chen Haodong and bye-bye hundred taels. Thinking fast, Scarface pulled a scapegoat out of thin air. “Fine—it wasn’t consensual, yet I didn’t touch her either! It was... it was...”
[ZH] 案子审到这里看似只能带人奔上天目山将黑煞虎抓起来。申简辰明知对方胡诌，又岂能上当？不如顺着疤子，关他些时日，再将他放出去，差人跟踪，只要发现他与陈家大公子有钱财交易，最好能抓个现行，便可坐实陈浩东的罪名。
[EN] “Denying the cloaked man was lie number two! Out with it—who was he?”
[ZH] “既是黑煞虎做下，我定集结临安兵力，奋力剿了天目山的土匪。你是帮凶，死罪可免，活罪难饶，加之买卖人口，罪加一等，除了奖励你二十大板，还需在狱中关押一个月。”
[EN] “Black-Fiend Tiger!”
[ZH] 疤子瘫在地上，命总算保住。他已打好主意，从狱中出去定要向陈大公子再多讨些银子，毕竟替他既挨板子还吃牢饭。
[EN] Shen Jianchen blinked. “Black-Fiend Tiger?”
[ZH] 过来两名差役，一起将疤子押了下去。
[EN] “Bandit chief up on Mount Tianmu,” Wen Xin explained. “Leads a mob burning, looting, and murdering.”
[ZH] 温信说：“王爷，我总觉得陈浩东很可疑，可疤子的证词并未提到他，难道此案就此了结？”
[EN] Unconvinced, Shen Jianchen snapped, “Dragging in a mountain bandit to fool me? Fetch the thorn switches!”
[ZH] 韩谷也说：“我听说黑煞虎有一习惯，只要他动过的女子必然会带到天目山上，如果传言是真，疤子一定是说谎，穿黑色披风之人定是另有其人，我也怀疑陈浩东。”
[EN] Worse than paddles—Scarface scrambled to sell his lie. “Every word’s true! Black-Fiend Tiger’s real name is Li Hu—used to be my neighbor before he ran afoul of the law and turned bandit! On the first of the third month last year he slipped down the mountain, hid at my place, and demanded I get him a woman. Knowing the Chens’ country house sat empty on the third, I drugged the caretaker and snuck him in—he wore the black cloak so nobody’d spot him! Black-Fiend Tiger defiled Chun Jinlan, not me!”
[ZH] 申简辰肯定道：“你们二人怀疑得没错，只能等疤子放出来后，再派人盯着，不相信抓不到陈浩东的把柄。”
[EN] *Brilliant!* Li Hu really had been his old neighbor and really had dropped by once. Blame an untouchable mountain bandit, keep Chen Haodong spotless, walk out alive, and pocket a hundred taels!
[ZH] 二人这才理解仪王的筹谋，齐声说道：“王爷英明！”
[EN] Skeptical, Shen Jianchen pressed, “Then why hide his name earlier?”
[ZH] 申简辰半开玩笑半认真地说：“宋律上关于买卖人口的罪处罚得也太轻微，要我说就该判处死刑。不过，今日也算帮了我，疤子活着，我们也可利用他，顺藤摸瓜。”
[EN] Scarface’s forehead kissed the floor. “Terrified you’d charge me with colluding with bandits!”
[ZH] 韩谷和温信不好说什么，毕竟宋律是经过官家允许才定下。
[EN] Airtight—zero thread linking back to Chen Haodong. Shen Jianchen had to hand it to Eldest Young Master Chen: not a single loose end, even holding a greedy rat like Scarface in check. Clearly Haodong had dangled a fat payout.
[ZH] 二人陪同仪王刚走出审讯室，一名差役前来递上一张请帖：“王爷，有位东方公子差人送来请帖。”
[EN] No point chasing ghosts up Mount Tianmu right away—better lock Scarface up a spell, turn him loose, and tail him straight to the silver payoff to catch Chen Haodong red-handed.
[ZH] 申简辰接过，翻开看了一眼，自己答应的事，还掏了银子，竟然差点儿忘记，只怪这几日为了案子太过忙碌，不妨趁着诗会放松放松。
[EN] “If Black-Fiend Tiger did it, I’ll muster Lin’an’s garrison to wipe out Mount Tianmu! As his accomplice—plus trafficking a free citizen—you escape death yet not punishment: twenty strokes of the paddle and one month in jail.”
[ZH] 本次诗会申简辰不想带梦容前去，但也不愿瞒着她。司理院已无大事，不如回趟幽林别居，将诗会之事告知她，省得事后听别人提起，胡乱猜测一番。
[EN] Collapsed on the floor, Scarface exhaled—alive! Once he got out, he’d hit Eldest Young Master up for extra hazard pay!
[ZH] 正是午饭时间，哥哥竟然回来，自从他去司理院后，还是第一次回来得这么早。乔梦容兴奋之余，见依然没有白风的影子，疑惑道：“哥哥回来得早，说明司理院不忙，为何白风没跟哥哥一起回来？”
[EN] Once constables hauled Scarface out, Wen Xin frowned. “Chen Haodong stinks to high heaven, Your Highness—are we letting it drop here?”
[ZH] 申简辰不想正面回答，开玩笑道：“我伤心了。”
[EN] Han Gu agreed. “Rumor says Black-Fiend Tiger drags every woman he touches back up Mount Tianmu. If true, Scarface is lying—the cloaked man is Chen Haodong.”
[ZH] 乔梦容不解：“为何？”
[EN] “Dead right,” Shen Jianchen confirmed. “Once Scarface gets out in a month, we put a tail on him. No way Chen Haodong stays clean.”
[ZH] 申简辰解释道：“我一进门，你只管问白风，却没问我一句，难道我不该伤心？”
[EN] Both lit up. “Brilliant plan, Your Highness!”
[ZH] 乔梦容不再关心白风：“哥哥说的哪里话？我心里全是哥哥，问白风也是担心哥哥身边没人伺候。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen added half-joking, half-grave, “Song penalties for human trafficking are way too soft—ought to carry the death penalty. Still, today it works for us: keeping Scarface alive gives us bait to reel in the big fish.”
[ZH] 申简辰不愿梦容继续关心白风拉起她的手：“来，坐下，我有事给你说。”
[EN] Neither official commented on imperial law. Walking out of the interrogation room, a constable handed over an invitation card from Young Master Dongfang.
[ZH] “已是饭点儿，我让丫鬟把饭摆上，你我饭桌上说。”
[EN] Opening it, Shen Jianchen chuckled—he had funded the salon and nearly forgot amid the case! He didn’t want to bring Mengrong tomorrow, yet hiding it would blow up worse if she heard later. With work slow, he headed back to Youlin Retreat at noon to tell her straight.
[ZH] “也好。”
[EN] Surprised to see him home so early—a first since taking the Judicial Office post—Qiao Mengrong lit up, then noticed Bai Feng still missing. “Home early means work’s light—why didn’t Bai Feng come back with you?”
[ZH] 喜兰命丫鬟将饭摆了。
[EN] Shen Jianchen teased, “Now my feelings are hurt.”
[ZH] 申简辰牵着梦容的手在饭桌旁坐定后，问道：“怎么不见邹嬷嬷？”
[EN] “Why?”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷一早去永泰茶楼见陈浩东，还未归来。乔梦容自然不会将实情告知。
[EN] “I walk through the door and all you ask about is Bai Feng—not one word about me?”
[ZH] “嬷嬷刚来临安，对一切都好奇，尤其喜欢临安的风景，她今日一早去了西湖，估计天黑后才回来。”
[EN] She dropped Bai Feng at once. “Don’t be silly—my heart’s all yours! Only asked because I worry nobody’s tending you.”
[ZH] 申简辰不再多问，亲自为梦容盛碗汤，放在她面前：“汤最是滋补，你多喝些。”
[EN] Taking her hand, he led her over. “Sit—got something to tell you.”
[ZH] 乔梦容露出幸福的笑容：“哥哥有什么重要的事对我说？”
[EN] “Just in time for lunch—let’s eat and talk.”
[ZH] 申简辰拿出请帖：“今日接到东方公子给的请帖，邀请我明日参加诗会。”
[EN] Xilan had maids lay the table. Sitting beside her, Shen Jianchen asked, “Where’s Matron Zou?”
[ZH] 乔梦容兴奋地接过：“哥哥是不是想带我一起去？”
[EN] Matron Zou had gone to Yongtai Teahouse to see Chen Haodong, yet Qiao Mengrong covered smooth. “Still new to Lin’an and enchanted by the scenery—went out to West Lake this morning, won’t be back till dark.”
[ZH] 看到乔梦容兴奋的样子，申简辰有些张不开口。衡量再三，小心说：“这次诗会与以往不同，除了邀请在临安诗坛有地位的人以外，也邀请临安官员。诗会不是宴会，带家眷去不合适。”
[EN] Letting it pass, Shen Jianchen ladled her a bowl of nourishing soup and pulled out the invitation. “Young Master Dongfang sent this for tomorrow’s poetry salon.”
[ZH] 乔梦容脸上的笑容立刻消失，哥哥今日回来得早就是为了解释明日的诗会为何不带她。
[EN] Her eyes sparkled as she took it. “Taking me along, Brother?”
[ZH] 她将请帖合好，还给哥哥：“既然不让带家眷，哥哥又何必拿给我瞧？”
[EN] Seeing her excitement made his words catch. Choosing them with care, he said, “This salon’s different—strictly literary figures and Lin’an officials, not a social banquet. Bringing wives isn’t proper.”
[ZH] 申简辰接过请帖，继续装好：“东方公子想让自己的诗会成为临安第一，必然只会邀请诗坛名流以及官场之人。”
[EN] Her smile vanished on the spot. *So that’s why he came home early—to ditch me!* Shutting the card, she handed it back. “If wives aren’t allowed, why even show me?”
[ZH] 乔梦容噘起嘴：“东方尘泽只是位普通公子，他凭什么能请得动名流和临安官场之人？该不会大家都是看在哥哥的面子上才去？”
[EN] “Young Master Dongfang aims to make his salon number one in Lin’an, so he’s limiting invites to poets and officials.”
[ZH] 被梦容猜中，申简辰老实交代：“这次诗会由我出资替他捧场。”
[EN] She pouted. “Dongfang Chenze is just an ordinary gentleman—how’d he pull in every poet and official in town? Everybody’s going for your face, aren’t they?”
[ZH] 乔梦容露出疑惑的表情：“哥哥虽与他有交情，还达不到如此卖力的地步，哥哥为何要这么做？”
[EN] Caught dead-on, he admitted, “I put up the silver to back him.”
[ZH] 说出实情只怕梦容不快，申简辰只能选择善意的谎言：“我这人你又不是不了解？见不得人求，他求到我的门上不好不帮。”
[EN] Suspicion sharpened her gaze. “You’re friendly with him, sure, not close enough to bankroll him like that. Why go to such lengths?”
[ZH] “既如此哥哥带我去也没什么，我相信东方公子绝不会嫌弃。”
[EN] Admitting the salon aimed to clear Zhu Shuzhen’s name over the pamphlets would detonate a bomb, so he told a white lie. “You know me—soft touch when friends beg.”
[ZH] 梦容的话算是说到重点，申简辰之所以不愿带她去，是因诗会上大家会针对书卷的内容为幽栖居士的佳作正名。书卷与梦容有关，只怕她听到会不高兴。
[EN] “All the more reason to take me! Young Master Dongfang wouldn’t dare mind.”
[ZH] 申简辰继续着善意的谎言：“话虽如此，可临安的诗会有诗会的规矩，不好打破，尤其是我，更不能带头破坏规矩，希望你能理解。”
[EN] “Local poetry salons have strict traditions, Mengrong—even more so for me, I can’t be the one breaking rules. Try to understand.”
[ZH] 哥哥的话说得在理，乔梦容不好再纠缠。她拿起筷子夹一块鸡肉放进哥哥的碗里：“她可去？”
[EN] Unable to fault the logic, she picked up a piece of chicken with her chopsticks and set it in his bowl. “Is she going?”
[ZH] 申简辰瞬间猜到是谁，却装作不知的样子：“谁？”
[EN] Playing dumb, he asked, “Who?”
[ZH] “朱淑真。”
[EN] “Zhu Shuzhen.”
[ZH] 申简辰也拿起筷子为梦容夹了一道菜，装作从容的样子：“应该会去吧，毕竟是江南女词人。”
[EN] Setting a morsel in her bowl in return, he kept his face calm. “Most likely—she is Jiangnan’s poetess.”
[ZH] 乔梦容拿起勺子舀了一口汤啜入口中，咽下后，问道：“哥哥会不会在诗会上与她单独说话？”
[EN] Sipping her soup, Qiao Mengrong asked, “Will you talk to her alone there, Brother?”
[ZH] 申简辰不知该如何回答。
[EN] He hesitated half a beat too long.
[ZH] “哥哥难道是默认？”
[EN] “Silence means yes?”
[ZH] “梦容，你别乱怀疑，你我已圆房，我会一心一意待你。”
[EN] “Stop imagining things, Mengrong—we’ve consummated our marriage, and my heart is devoted to you.”
[ZH] “我信哥哥，可我不信她。哥哥要么别去，要么带上我。”
[EN] “I trust you, Brother—I don’t trust her. Either skip the salon, or take me with you.”
[ZH] 面对乔梦容无理取闹，申简辰无可奈何，只能答应：“好，我带上你。”
[EN] Cornered with no way out, Shen Jianchen gave in. “Fine. I’ll take you.”

#CH 294
[ZH] 陈浩宇从外面回来时，天色已晚。他按照惯例到素心草堂门外汇报一日的情况。
[ZH] “娘子，我回来了，秋管事说后天让我到账房学管账。还有，我今日去永泰茶楼学做茶，女茶师夸我进步很快，再学几日，我就可以为娘子做茶。再告诉你一个好消息，永泰茶楼已归陈家，里面挂上新的字画，娘子若喜欢可以抽时间去瞧瞧。”
[ZH] 门被打开，娘子出现在眼前。
[ZH] “进来吧，我有话跟你说。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇巴不得进去，刚抬脚想进去，被青瓜拽住衣裳。
[ZH] “公子，我能不能不跟着进去？”
[ZH] “你进不进去一点儿不重要。”
[ZH] “我去找丑丫？”
[ZH] “滚。”
[ZH] “这就滚。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇很享受骂青瓜的感觉，如果说以前还有半分真骂在里面，现在的骂则充满认可和喜爱。
[ZH] 当陈浩宇走入屋内，苦荞已将一盏茶递到他面前。
[ZH] 陈浩宇接过，在茶汤中瞧了瞧，开始挑刺：“雪沫乳花不够厚也不够白。”
[ZH] 他端到嘴边品一口：“味道尚可，只是水与茶粉的比例没把握好，水有些偏多，茶粉不够细腻。”
[ZH] 苦荞噘起嘴：“我给小姐做了多年茶，没见她说任何不满的话来，姑爷才学几日，茶还没做上一盏，倒先学会挑刺，往后我再也不给您做。”
[ZH] “你这丫头，要虚心接受他人的意见，否则永远进步不了。别看我才学几日，水平可比你强。”
[ZH] “姑爷就知道说大话，您要做得好，不如给小姐现做一盏，也好让我瞧瞧您的水平。”
[ZH] “现在不行，等我再学些时日，定让娘子惊艳一回。”
[ZH] 姑爷嘴上挑着毛病，还是将茶喝完。没等姑爷递茶盏，苦荞将茶盏抢走。
[ZH] “你这丫头也忒没礼貌。”
[ZH] “姑爷要能在茶汤中作画那才叫本事。”
[ZH] 苦荞说着去茶桌前放好茶盏，打开木盒，用小木勺取些茶粉放在手指间摩擦片刻，还真不够细腻，于是坐下重新碾茶粉。
[ZH] 陈浩宇发现自己的话起到作用，不再与苦荞斗嘴，转向娘子。她一直都坐在书桌前欣赏他与苦荞斗嘴，面色和谐。
[ZH] “娘子，我还真会在茶汤中作画，只是技术不够醇熟。”
[ZH] 千里寻无心谈论做茶之事，拿起请帖：“看这个。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇上前接过请帖，打开瞧了一眼：“娘子要去参加诗会？”
[ZH] “嗯，你可否同意？”
[ZH] “我哪有资格不同意？娘子想去尽管去。”
[ZH] “诗会男子居多，你不担心？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇把请帖合了，放在娘子的书桌上：“自然担心，可自打成亲后，与娘子相处得多，了解你的为人，我深信娘子与其他男子相处最知分寸。”
[ZH] 千里寻竟然感动起来，说出连她自己都意想不到的话：“明日可否向秋管事告假，随我一同去？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇兴奋地问：“可以吗？”
[ZH] “我在临安诗坛地位不容小觑，带个把人参加诗会不成问题。”
[ZH] “可我……根本不懂诗词，怕去了给娘子丢脸。”
[ZH] “我带你去并非想让你在诗词方面替我长脸，你只要待在我身边，便可免去许多口舌是非。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu got home after dusk and checked in outside Suxin Thatched Hall as usual. “I’m back, Wife! Steward Qiu says I move to the accounting room day after tomorrow to learn bookkeeping. Plus the female tea master at Yongtai Teahouse praised my progress—give me a few more days and I’ll whisk tea for you! Oh, and good news: Yongtai Teahouse belongs to the Chens now, hung with fresh paintings and calligraphy if you want to browse sometime.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇想起自己传播谣言一事，惭愧片刻后说道：“娘子不必在意他人说什么。”
[EN] The door swung open, his wife standing inside. “Come in—need to talk to you.”
[ZH] “难道你不乐意陪我去？”
[EN] Eager to step in, Chen Haoyu felt Qinggua yank his hem. “Can I skip going in, Young Master?”
[ZH] “当然乐意！”
[EN] “Nobody cares if you come in.”
[ZH] “那就别再废话！”
[EN] “Go see Chouya?”
[ZH] “收到！”陈浩宇的脸上洋溢着笑容，“娘子早些休息，我先回后院。”
[EN] “Scram.”
[ZH] 千里寻点点头，目送陈浩宇离开。
[EN] “Scramming!”
[ZH] 苦荞用小木勺将碾好的茶粉一点一点放入茶盒：“小姐能带姑爷一起参加诗会再好不过。”
[EN] Odd how cursing Qinggua felt warm now—less temper, more affection.
[ZH] 千里寻站起来，伸了一个懒腰：“你又何必因姑爷一句话又把茶粉碾一遍？”
[EN] Inside, Kuqiao handed Chen Haoyu a freshly whisked bowl of tea. Peering into the bowl, he started nitpicking right away. “Foam’s neither thick nor white enough.” Taking a sip, he tutted. “Taste is passable, only the water-to-powder ratio is off—too much water, and the powder’s coarse.”
[ZH] “小姐都说是‘姑爷’，他的意见我敢不听？”
[EN] Kuqiao stuck out her lip. “I’ve whisked tea for Miss for years without one complaint! Young Master’s studied three days, hasn’t even made a bowl yet, and already acts like a critic—never making tea for you again!”
[ZH] “太困了，该收拾睡觉。”千里寻朝里屋拐去。
[EN] “Gotta take honest feedback or you’ll never improve! Three days or not, I’m already better than you.”
[ZH] 苦荞快速装好茶粉，赶去里屋伺候小姐休息。
[EN] “Big talk! Whisk one for Miss right now and prove it!”
[ZH] ……
[EN] “Not yet—wait a few more days and I’ll blow my wife’s mind.”
[ZH] 话说自征得仪王同意后，东方尘泽在两位公子的帮忙下开始筹备诗会。既然要办大，不能太吝惜钱财，仪王大方，给予充足的资金支持，筹备起来三位公子也是信心十足。
[EN] Griping all the while, he still drained the bowl—which Kuqiao snatched straight out of his hand.
[ZH] 筹备得差不多时，又借着仪王的名头邀请京城的达官贵人参加。为官之人腹中多少有些文墨，他们前来并不与诗会的宗旨相违背。况且要办成临安第一，还要靠达官贵人的宣扬和支持。
[EN] “Watch the attitude, girl!”
[ZH] 然后又向临安的文人墨客广发请帖，当然选择之人需在临安诗坛有一定的名声，不拘男女，不拘年纪，均在被请之列。
[EN] “Draw a picture on tea foam, then we’ll talk!” Setting the bowl down at the tea table, Kuqiao pinched some tea powder between her fingers—okay, maybe a hair coarse—and sat down to re-grind it.
[ZH] 东方尘泽给诗会取了一个响亮的名字：东方诗会。他希望今年之后，东方诗会成为临安文人趋之若鹜的盛大聚会，更希望今后大大小小的诗会尽无，只剩东方诗会。
[EN] Smug his jab worked, Chen Haoyu turned to his wife, who had watched their bickering from her desk with an amused smile. “Actually, Wife, I really can paint on tea foam—just need more polish.”
[ZH] 东方尘泽的父亲也是临安官员之一，一直不满儿子不求功名，喜欢花钱办只出不进毫无用处的诗会，常常限制他的花销。
[EN] Skipping tea talk, Qianli Xun held up the invitation. “Look at this.”
[ZH] 可本次诗会背后的支持者是仪王，东方老爷也帮儿子操办起来，更为儿子能交上仪王这等朋友而自豪。
[EN] Taking it open, he blinked. “Going to a poetry salon?”
[ZH] 诗会这日千里寻并未细心装扮，甚至连脂粉都未施，主打一个素面朝天。她并不认同参加活动时女子只有化妆才是对他人的尊重，尊重放在心里，靠行为和语言展示，脂粉是外物，靠它彰显岂非可笑？
[EN] “Mm. Mind?”
[ZH] 况且诗会中男子居多，她作为女子有机会参加在许多道学者的眼中已是出格，若再精心打扮会被认为别有用心。
[EN] “Who am I to mind? Go wherever you please.”
[ZH] 衣裳还是要精心选择一番，一定要端庄典雅，颜色素淡的，最好是一出场不会让所有人的目光聚集在自己身上的那种。
[EN] “Mostly men—not worried?”
[ZH] 刚准备好，门外传来陈浩宇的声音：“娘子，我已经准备好，现在可否出发？”
[EN] Closing the card on her desk, he said earnest, “Course I worry—yet after living with you, I know your character. You keep strict boundaries with other men.”
[ZH] 苦荞把门打开，千里寻从屋内走出，眼前一亮，陈浩宇竟然穿着一身红色衣衫，衬得他肤色更加白皙，五官也更加立体。
[EN] A warm rush caught her off guard, prompting words she hadn’t even planned. “Ask Steward Qiu for tomorrow off and come with me?”
[ZH] “又不是去当新郎，为何如此装扮？”
[EN] His eyes lit up. “Really? I can come?”
[ZH] “这件衣裳可不是新郎装，是我两年前做的一身常服，只穿过一回，既然是陪娘子出去抛头露面，得让别人知道我是谁的人，只有穿得鲜艳些，别人才能注意到我是江南女词人带去的人。”
[EN] “With my clout in Lin’an’s poetry circle, bringing one guest is nothing.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇脸上不由露出得意的表情。
[EN] “Only... I don’t know a thing about poetry—what if I embarrass you?”
[ZH] 千里寻被他得表情惹得微微一笑：“青瓜呢？为何没跟着？”
[EN] “Not bringing you to compose verse—just stand beside me and it shuts down half the gossip.”
[ZH] “我要陪娘子，总得有人替我去绸缎庄请假，再说青瓜现在对当伙计十分上心，我都担心他以后不愿给我当跟班。”
[EN] Remembering his own rumor-mongering, he winced. “Ignore what people wag their tongues about.”
[ZH] “从被人动不动又踢又骂的跟班变成伙计，青瓜的人生算是向前迈进一大步。”千里寻故意调侃，不等陈浩宇应对直接往出走。
[EN] “Don’t want to come?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇紧跟上：“娘子要用进步的眼光看人，我已经好久没踢过他，再说你教他幻影闪移，我已拿他没办法。”
[EN] “Course I do!”
[ZH] 千里寻想象着陈浩宇踢青瓜时，青瓜躲开，再将他推倒的样子，觉得甚是好笑。
[EN] “Then quit jabbering!”
[ZH] 公子与少夫人一同出去的情景被丫鬟映雪无意中瞧见，她小跑着回到夫人的房中。
[EN] “Yes, ma’am!” Grinning ear to ear, he backed toward the door. “Rest early, Wife—I’m heading to the rear courtyard.”
[ZH] “夫人！夫人！天大的喜事！”
[EN] Watching him go, Kuqiao spooned fresh-ground powder back into the caddy. “Bringing Young Master to the salon is the best idea ever, Miss.”
[ZH] “什么事？搞得你如此慌张？”
[EN] Stretching her arms overhead, Qianli Xun teased, “Why re-grind the whole batch over one comment from him?”
[ZH] 姚轻琰又与陈江林下棋，刚落下一子，映雪突然进来，想好的棋局被她一喊全忘记。
[EN] “Well, even Miss calls him ‘Young Master’ now—how dare I ignore him?”
[ZH] 映雪回道：“公子与少夫人一起出去了。”
[EN] “Sleepy—time for bed.” Heading into the inner chamber, she let Kuqiao wrap up and help her turn in.
[ZH] 姚轻琰已顾不上棋局，露出惊喜的表情：“一起出去？”
[EN] Meanwhile, backed by Prince Yi’s generous purse, Dongfang Chenze had gone all-out with Cao Ban and An Mingxuan, inviting Lin’an’s top officials and every noted poet regardless of age or gender under a bold new banner: the Dongfang Poetry Salon. Aiming to swallow every rival gathering in town, he even won over his own official father, who had once griped about his son wasting silver on salons yet now beamed with pride over Prince Yi’s backing and pitched in to help.
[ZH] 映雪的头点得跟个拨浪鼓似的：“千真万确，我亲眼看到。”
[EN] Salon morning arrived. Qianli Xun skipped makeup altogether—no powder, no rouge—believing respect came from conduct, not paint, and knowing a painted woman at a male-heavy literary gathering invited snide remarks. She picked a plain, refined gown designed to blend in rather than steal eyes.
[ZH] 姚轻琰已无心下棋：“害我们担心半天，怕淑真不愿再理浩宇，没想到人家小两口好好的。”
[EN] Right on cue, Chen Haoyu called outside, “All set, Wife! Ready to roll?”
[ZH] 陈江林收起手中的黑子：“儿孙自有儿孙福，你我别跟着瞎操心。淑真是个明理又有主意的孩子，知道怎么做对自己的人生更有利。”
[EN] Opening the door, Qianli Xun blinked—Chen Haoyu stood dressed head to toe in vibrant crimson, setting off his fair skin and sharp features.
[ZH] “搞得好像你没担心过一样？是谁昨晚上问我淑真会不会嫌弃浩宇？”
[EN] “Not a groom today—why red?”
[ZH] 陈江林“嘿嘿”笑了两声：“偶尔担心一下也正常。”
[EN] “Not wedding robes—a casual outfit I tailored two years back and only wore once! If I’m accompanying my wife in public, folks need to know who I belong to—wear bright red and everybody spots the Poetess of Jiangnan’s husband!” Smug pride practically glowed off him.
[ZH] “淑真今日要去参加诗会，她该不会把浩宇带去？”
[EN] Amused despite herself, she asked, “Where’s Qinggua?”
[ZH] 陈江林认可道：“八成是。”
[EN] “Sent him to the silk shop to request my leave—plus he’s so hooked on clerking I worry he’ll ditch being my valet.”
[ZH] “呵呵……，我得赶紧去告诉老太太，也让她高兴高兴。”
[EN] “Going from kicking-post lackey to shop clerk is a huge step up for Qinggua,” she teased, striding ahead.
[ZH] 姚轻琰已坐不住，从榻上下来，穿好鞋。
[EN] He trotted alongside. “Give me credit for growth! Haven’t kicked him in ages—plus since you taught him that phantom-step dodge, I couldn’t land a kick if I tried!”
[ZH] 夫人要去，陈江林不能不陪着：“别着急，我陪你一起去。”
[EN] Picturing Qinggua sidestepping and tripping Haoyu flat on his rear drew a quiet laugh from her.
[ZH] 陈府的南门外已停好一辆马车，陈浩飞站在马车旁等待二嫂。见二嫂与浩哥一起出来，立刻迎上去。
[EN] Spotting the pair heading out together, Yao Qingyan’s maid Yingxue bolted straight back to her mistress’s quarters. “Madam! Madam! Huge news!”
[ZH] “二嫂，马车我已套好，我们赶紧出发。”
[EN] Right as Yao Qingyan set a Go stone against Chen Jianglin, the shout scattered her whole strategy. “What’s the panic?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇装作不满的样子：“你眼里还有没有二哥？不是你们，是我们。”
[EN] “Second Young Master and Second Young Madam just went out together!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇一边说着，一边靠近娘子。
[EN] Dropping the game on the spot, Yao Qingyan beamed. “Together?!”
[ZH] 陈浩飞诧异道：“难道浩哥也要去？”
[EN] Yingxue bobbed her head like a rattle-drum. “Saw them with my own eyes!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇质问道：“怎么？不可以吗？有江南女词人带我去，谁敢说个不字？”
[EN] “Here we were fretting Shuzhen would freeze him out, and look at the lovebirds!”
[ZH] 陈浩飞立刻醒悟过来：“原来如此，嫂嫂带你去，肯定没人敢说什么。我也好久没见二哥，正好路上跟你好好聊聊。”
[EN] Gathering his black stones, Chen Jianglin smiled. “Kids sort out their own blessings. Shuzhen’s got a level head—she knows what’s best for her future.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇批评道：“你我兄弟想聊天儿什么时候都行，唯独今日不行，我要与娘子同乘一辆马车，你最好再套一辆来。”
[EN] “Acting like you didn’t fret too! Who asked me last night whether Shuzhen would dump Haoyu?”
[ZH] 陈浩飞不好意思道：“是弟弟考虑不周，这就去再套一辆马车来。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin chuckled sheepish. “Allowed to worry once in a while.”
[ZH] 千里寻故意说：“我喜欢热闹，三个人坐一辆，说说笑笑正合我意。”
[EN] “She’s off to that poetry salon—must be taking Haoyu along!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇很快转变强调：“娘子说什么就是什么，浩飞，不必再套马车，我们坐一辆。”
[EN] “Ten to one.”
[ZH] 陈浩飞很是无语，浩哥成亲后像变了个人，他开始相信一物降一物并非只是世人的说法，还真有其事。
[EN] “Wait till I tell Mother—she’ll be over the moon!” Slipping on her shoes, Yao Qingyan hurried out with Chen Jianglin tagging along.
[ZH] 陈江林与姚轻琰来到老太太的院子，将丫鬟映雪看到的说了。
[EN] Outside Chen Manor’s south gate, Chen Haofei waited beside a hitched carriage and stepped up as they emerged. “Carriage is ready, Second Sister-in-law—let’s go.”
[ZH] 夏老太太更是笑得合不拢嘴：“呵呵……，好！甚好！淑真带浩宇去诗会，说明她从心底不嫌弃浩宇，也是想让我们放心，她一个姑娘家参加诗会绝不会给我们陈家招惹是非。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu stuck out his chest and sidled right up to his wife. “Blind to your second brother? Not ‘you two’—‘us’!”
[ZH] 姚轻琰认可道：“母亲说得是，淑真这孩子就是有心。”
[EN] Chen Haofei blinked. “Second Brother’s coming too?”
[ZH] 夏老太太笑够后，问起正事：“产业继承人的事安排在什么时候？”
[EN] “Why not? With Jiangnan’s poetess bringing me, who dares say peep?”
[ZH] 陈江林回道：“儿子问过族中长辈，将时间提前一年他们没有任何异议，具体日子让我来定，儿子想放在下个月。”
[EN] “Ah—fair enough! Haven’t caught up with you in ages anyway, Second Brother—we’ll chat on the ride!”
[ZH] “下个月就下个月。就目前情形来看，浩东胜算最大。这孩子从小跟着你学习做生意，又是陈家后辈中的老大，也算合适。唉！唯一美中不足便是对安雅不够好，你得多规劝规劝他。”
[EN] “Chat any other day, not today! I’m riding with my wife—go hitch your own carriage.”
[ZH] “儿子记下了。”
[EN] Flushing, Chen Haofei murmured, “My oversight—I’ll grab another rig.”
[ZH] 陈江林想到春金兰之事，浩东算计陈家子弟，按说是产业继承人的大忌。可纵观三个后辈，浩宇根本没有资格，浩飞这孩子心性纯良，可对做生意不感兴趣。只有浩东，无论从哪一个方面讲都是最佳人选。
[EN] Qianli Xun grinned. “I like company—three in one carriage chatting and laughing suits me fine.”
[ZH] 陈江林不想因春金兰之事影响浩东在长辈心中的印象，否则陈家连个合适的继承人都没有，他作为当家人如何对得起祖先？
[EN] Chen Haoyu flipped his tone in half a second. “Whatever my wife says goes! Skip the second carriage, Haofei—hop in!”
[ZH] 他思来想去，为避免陈家子弟互相盘算，也为给祖先有个交代，早些定下继承人才是正事。
[EN] Speechless, Chen Haofei decided the old saying about every beast having its tamer was gospel truth.
[ZH] 马车上，陈浩宇没有心情理会浩飞，一直盯着娘子看，像从来没见过她一般。
[EN] Back inside, Old Madam Xia laughed till her eyes crinkled at Yingxue’s report. “Splendid! Bringing Haoyu proves Shuzhen truly accepts him—and sets our minds at rest that attending a salon won’t spark gossip.” Turning to business, she asked Chen Jianglin, “When’s the heir selection?”
[ZH] “娘子为何今日打扮得如此素淡？连脂粉都未施？”
[EN] “Clan elders approved moving it up a year and left the date to me—I’m setting it for next month.”
[ZH] “又不是参加选美，为何要涂脂抹粉？”
[EN] “Next month it is. Looks like Haodong has the best shot—trained under you since childhood and the eldest. Only flaw is treating Anya poorly; lean on him to do better.”
[ZH] “娘子素颜也挺好看，越看越耐看。”
[EN] “I will, Mother.” Chen Jianglin thought briefly of Chun Jinlan—sabotaging a cousin was a major strike, yet Haoyu wasn’t ready and Haofei had no head for trade, leaving Haodong the sole viable heir. Keeping that stain hidden and locking the selection down early was the only way to keep the clan united.
[ZH] 陈浩宇又一阵花痴。
[EN] Inside the rolling carriage, Chen Haoyu ignored Haofei completely, staring at his wife like he’d never seen her before. “Why dress so plain today—not even a dab of powder?”
[ZH] 陈浩飞开始后悔与二人同坐一辆马车，搞得自己跟个透明人似的，只能装作看车窗外。
[EN] “Not a beauty pageant—why cake on powder?”
[ZH] 千里寻觉出浩飞的不自在，有意找话题与他聊天：“浩飞，你什么时候到京城参加考试。”
[EN] “Bare-faced suits you even better—grows prettier every second.”
[ZH] 二嫂主动搭话，陈浩飞不再瞧向窗外：“回二嫂，今年冬季先去汴梁，来年春天参加会试，合格后才可参加殿试。”
[EN] Feeling invisible as a ghost, Chen Haofei stared out the window regretting his choice of ride.
[ZH] “殿试之后是不是就可以在朝为官？”
[EN] Noticing his awkwardness, Qianli Xun drew him in. “When do you head to the capital for the exams, Haofei?”
[ZH] “需进前三甲才可在朝为官。”
[EN] Grateful, he turned back. “Heading to Bianliang this winter for the spring metropolitan exam—pass that, and it’s on to the palace exam.”
[ZH] 千里寻鼓励道：“嫂嫂相信你能一举夺魁。”
[EN] “And after the palace exam you become a court official?”
[ZH] 陈浩飞谦虚道：“一举夺魁不敢奢望，考个第三甲倒是绰绰有余。”
[EN] “Top three tiers earn court posts.”
[ZH] 千里寻有种强烈的荣耀感，比跟仪王在一起时还要荣耀：“这么说来我现在正与一位朝廷官员坐在一起。”
[EN] “I bet you take first place!”
[ZH] 陈浩飞被夸得不好意思起来。
[EN] “First place is a stretch—third tier is well within reach.”
[ZH] 想到这个时代，想到现在的朝廷，千里寻无法再荣耀下去：“浩飞，你若在朝为官，千万不能当个昏庸的贪官。”
[EN] A burst of awe lit her face—prouder than sitting next to a prince! “So I’m riding right beside a future court official!” Then remembering the rot of late Northern Song politics, she turned serious. “Once you wear official robes, Haofei, never become a corrupt crook.”
[ZH] “嫂嫂放心，我最讨厌贪官。”陈浩飞更加不好意思起来，“嫂嫂这话，好像我已经当官似的。”
[EN] “Count on it, Sister-in-law—nothing I hate more than corrupt officials!” Blushing, he added, “Talking like I already passed!”

#CH 295
[ZH] 聚香楼今日被东方公子包下，挂起“闲客免进”的牌子，又在门口临时竖一招牌，上写“东方诗会”四个大字。
[ZH] 千里寻到达后，同陈浩宇和陈浩飞一同进入。
[ZH] 一楼的正面搭着一个不大不小的台子，横批为“东方诗会”，上联与下联乃是唐代白居易的两句诗：“闲征雅令穷经史，醉听清吟胜管弦。”
[ZH] 再看那台上，中间放着一个精美的架子，应该是用来悬挂写有诗词的卷轴，一旁放置着一张古琴。
[ZH] 千里寻来得不算晚，许多人竟然比她来得还要早。她向四处瞧瞧，并未见到女子的身影，更觉带上陈浩宇前来实属明智之举。
[ZH] 陈浩飞瞧见认识的朋友，已在哥嫂身边待不住：“二嫂，我过去跟朋友打个招呼。”
[ZH] 千里寻没有任何理由阻拦：“去吧。”
[ZH] 陈浩飞顾不上跟浩哥说话，快步离开。
[ZH] 陈浩宇瞧着浩飞着急见朋友的样子，故意埋怨：“这孩子！什么时候眼中没了我？只给你说一声就跑？等回到家中我定要好好批评他。”
[ZH] 千里寻说：“二婶还担心他，让我多带带，你瞧，他来这里比我认识的朋友都多。”
[ZH] 两人正说着，三位公子迎上来。
[ZH] 东方尘泽带头说：“幽栖居士能来，东方诗会蓬荜生辉！”
[ZH] 顶着江南女词人的名头，千里寻更要保持谦虚的态度：“东方公子严重，今日诗会高朋满座，雅士云集，才令诗会星光熠熠，待会儿贵人与众位官员一到，更是光芒四射，我看我还是找个雅座躲起来得好。”
[ZH] 曹班开玩笑道：“幽栖居士要是躲起来我们还不得钻到地下去？”
[ZH] 说得众人都笑起来。
[ZH] 陈浩宇本瞧不上酸臭文人，可谁让这些假惺惺的话是娘子说出来的，他耐着性子认真听一回，见众人笑，也努力挤出些笑容。打心里来讲真不知道这些人到底为何要笑？反正他觉得一点儿都不好笑。
[ZH] 安明轩问：“幽栖居士可准备好诗词？”
[ZH] “那是自然。”
[ZH] “诗会中有一个临时挥毫泼墨的环节，还请幽栖居士一展风采。”
[ZH] 千里寻以微笑回应。
[ZH] 陈浩宇跟着幽栖居士前来，三位公不好冷落他。
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“陈公子能娶到幽栖居士不知羡煞多少人，今日能光临诗会，定是受到幽栖居士的影响，这才是真正的琴瑟和鸣。”
[ZH] 一番夸赞陈浩宇倒是听进心里，尤其喜欢“琴瑟和鸣”四个字：“我的确是受娘子影响才来诗会，往后她去哪里我都会形影不离。”
[ZH] 三位公子面面相觑，早听说陈浩宇自从成亲像变了一个人，果不其然，还是幽栖居士厉害。
[ZH] 客套话已说完，还要迎接其他人，东方尘泽指着一处位置：“给幽栖居士准备的位置在那边，参加诗会的女子仅有三人，将你和想绒姑娘安排在一处，希望幽栖居士不要介怀。”
[ZH] 千里寻又怎能介怀？
[ZH] “东方公子将想绒姑娘邀请来实在明智，我与她许久未见，正好借此畅聊一回，只是不知另外一名女子是谁？为何不与我们同坐一处？”
[ZH] 安明轩帮着回答：“是仪王妃，她要求一直跟着仪王，故而没有安排她跟幽栖居士坐在一起。”
[ZH] 梦容果然也会来，千里寻很想与她坐在一起，借着畅聊诗词从而畅聊人生，可曾经美好的画面已成过往，只能幽幽地说：“仪王妃是贵人，自然不能与我等同坐一处。三位公子且去忙，我去那边等会儿想绒姑娘。”
[ZH] 三位公子又忙着招呼其他人。
[ZH] 千里寻来到桌前坐下，桌上摆着一壶酒酿，四只酒杯，还多出一盘绿色菜肴，她很是纳闷：“为何摆上一盘菜肴？难道要以此作诗不成？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇也已坐下，推测道：“看着像是山间野菜，听说懒人村居住的山里最适合野菜生长，仪王让岳父大人帮忙谋个出路，我估计岳父大人想借此诗会宣扬一番。”
[ZH] 千里寻歪着脑袋，露出疑惑的表情：“你整日待在绸缎庄，怎么知道这些？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇解释道：“爹去过几次绸缎庄，跟我聊过两回，我听他说的。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze had booked all of Juxiang Tower for the day, hanging a “Closed to Walk-Ins” sign out front beside a bold new banner: “Dongfang Poetry Salon.”
[ZH] “原来如此。”千里寻正说着，见有人过来，都是些她不熟悉的才子，其中不乏年长之人。
[EN] Qianli Xun stepped inside with Chen Haoyu and Chen Haofei. Along the main ground floor stood a tasteful stage crowned by a horizontal scroll reading “Dongfang Poetry Salon,” flanked by a couplet from Tang poet Bai Juyi: “At leisure we trade refined drinking games through classics and histories; tipsy, we hear clear chanting sweeter than pipes and strings.” [TN: Translator’s rendering] At center stage stood a carved rack for hanging poem scrolls beside a guqin zither.
[ZH] 才子们不过是前来打招呼，有人带头说：“久仰幽栖居士大名，今日得见真是三生有幸。”
[EN] Early as they were, scores of guests had already arrived—not a single woman in sight, confirming how smart bringing Chen Haoyu had been.
[ZH] 千里寻变得更加谨慎，唯恐说错话给朱淑真丢脸。
[EN] Spotting friends across the floor, Chen Haofei fidgeted. “Second Sister-in-law, mind if I go say hello?”
[ZH] 她站起来谦虚道：“淑真乃闺阁女子，深受闺阁限制，极少走出来，故而见识浅薄，竟不知诸位，还请见谅。”
[EN] “Go right ahead.”
[ZH] 其中一位年长者笑道：“我等您不必挂怀，这位叶少蕴还是要向幽栖居士介绍。”
[EN] Haofei dashed off without a glance at his brother. Chen Haoyu pouted for show. “Look at him—totally ignores me and only asks you! Wait till we get home, I’m lecturing him.”
[ZH] 千里寻对“叶少蕴”的称谓并不熟悉，一时愣住，盯着眼前四十开外的男子研究不出他到底是谁。只怪穿越前没好好阅读文学史，要不今日也不会偶遇尴尬之境。还有一种可能，也许对方只是在当时的文人中有名，并未载入史册。
[EN] “Third Aunt worried he’d feel lost and asked me to look out for him—turns out he knows twice as many folks here as I do!”
[ZH] 只听对方吟诵道：“落花已作风前舞，又送黄昏雨。晓来庭院半残红……”
[EN] Right then Dongfang Chenze, Cao Ban, and An Mingxuan hurried over.
[ZH] 还未吟诵完，千里寻脱口而出：“您是叶梦得？”
[EN] “Youqi Jushi’s arrival makes the Dongfang Salon shine!” Dongfang Chenze beamed.
[ZH] 叶梦得回道：“正是鄙人。”
[EN] Keeping up her modest poetess front, Qianli Xun replied, “You flatter me, Young Master Dongfang. With this hall packed with distinguished scholars—and royals and officials due any minute—my humble light fades away. Better I tuck myself into a quiet corner.”
[ZH] 叶梦得的诗词千里寻读过几首，还好他念出的句子是她熟悉的。根据时间推算，此时的叶梦得不过四十来岁，应是朝廷任命的官员。
[EN] Cao Ban grinned. “If Youqi Jushi hides in a corner, the rest of us will have to dig holes in the floor!”
[ZH] “按说您是朝廷官员，应该跟着仪王与知府大人一起前来，为何……”
[EN] Laughter rippled through the group. Chen Haoyu had no patience for stuffy scholars, yet since his wife was trading banter, he plastered on a polite smile—no earthly clue what was supposed to be funny.
[ZH] “我从颍昌先去湖州，只因石林谷的别馆落成，后有朋友相约，才来到临安，知府大人和仪王并不知晓，我也落得自在。”
[EN] “Brought a fresh poem, Youqi Jushi?” An Mingxuan asked.
[ZH] 千里寻又在心中仔细推算，叶梦得应该在颍州任知州，官职与朱晞颜不相上下，于是赶紧行礼：“知州大人洒脱，令淑真欣羡不已。”
[EN] “Of course.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇见状也起身行礼：“知州大人。”
[EN] “There’s a live brush-and-ink round later—we’re counting on you to dazzle us.”
[ZH] 叶梦得笑道：“今日没有知州大人，只有文人才子，大家以诗会友。”
[EN] Qianli Xun answered with a smile. Mindful not to snub her husband, Dongfang Chenze turned to Chen Haoyu. “Winning Youqi Jushi’s hand made half of Lin’an jealous, Young Master Chen—and attending a poetry salon under her influence shows true conjugal bliss!”
[ZH] 此话一出，大家都轻松地笑起来。
[EN] That hit the sweet spot—he loved the sound of “conjugal bliss.” “Right you are! Wherever my wife goes from now on, I stick like a shadow.”
[ZH] 笑够后，叶梦得说：“我等还要与其他友人相叙，就不打扰幽栖居士，失陪。”
[EN] The three young masters traded amazed glances—rumors of the wild playboy’s total overhaul were true after all!
[ZH] 千里寻以微笑回礼：“诸位请便。”
[EN] Pointing to a table, Dongfang Chenze added, “We seated you over there. Only three women are attending today, so we paired you with Miss Xiangrong—hope you don’t mind.”
[ZH] 众位文人走后，千里寻与陈浩宇才放心坐下。
[EN] “Mind? Inviting Xiangrong was brilliant—haven’t caught up with her in ages! Wait, who’s the third woman? Why isn’t she seated with us?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇随口问道：“他很有名吗？”
[EN] “Princess Consort Yi,” An Mingxuan answered. “She insisted on sitting beside Prince Yi.”
[ZH] “当然，将来会名载史册，被后人记住。”
[EN] Mengrong really was coming. A wistful pang hit Qianli Xun—how she would’ve loved trading poems and laughs at the same table—yet those days were gone. “Her Highness is royalty, naturally she sits apart. Go tend your guests; I’ll head over and wait for Xiangrong.”
[ZH] “你呢？”
[EN] Taking seats at their table, Qianli Xun spotted a jug of rice wine, four cups, and—oddly—a plate of green stir-fried vegetables. “Why a dish of greens? Are we writing poems about dinner?”
[ZH] “幽栖居士当然也会名载史册。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu peered at it. “Looks like wild mountain greens. Heard Lazybones Village’s soil only grows wild veggies, and Prince Yi asked Father-in-law to open a market—bet Father-in-law is promoting them here.”
[ZH] “你说后人会不会也知道有我这号人？”
[EN] Qianli Xun tilted her head. “Stuck in the silk shop all day—how do you know that?”
[ZH] “为何这么问？”
[EN] “Father stopped by the shop a couple times and chatted with me.”
[ZH] “因为我是你夫君。”
[EN] Before she could reply, a knot of unfamiliar scholars—several graying at the temples—came over to pay respects. “Long admired Youqi Jushi’s fame—meeting you today is an honor!”
[ZH] 千里寻愣了一会儿，给了一个模棱两可的回答：“也许吧。”
[EN] Careful not to tarnish Zhu Shuzhen’s reputation, Qianli Xun rose with a modest curtsy. “Sheltered in the inner quarters, my knowledge is narrow—forgive me for not recognizing you gentlemen.”
[ZH] 接着又有好几拨人前来打招呼，陈浩宇都会跟随娘子一一回礼，今日他算是见识到自家娘子名气不小，跟着她出来一趟甚觉荣耀。
[EN] One older scholar chuckled. “Don’t mind us—yet you must meet Ye Shaoyun here.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇想着再有人来招呼时，他如何说话才能让别人知道他是幽栖居士的夫君，然后对他露出欣羡的表情。
[EN] Blanking on “Ye Shaoyun,” Qianli Xun stared at the man in his forties, kicking herself for skipping literary history in college—unless he was a local celebrity lost to time.
[ZH] 正想着，一位花容月貌的姑娘向他们走来。
[EN] The man smiled and recited softly, “Fallen petals have danced upon the wind, only to send off the dusk in rain; by dawn the courtyard lies half strewn with fading crimson...” [TN: Translator’s rendering]
[ZH] 要搁以前，陈浩宇绝对会多瞧两眼，说不定还会调戏一番。可现在有了娘子，还答她两年之内不碰女色。陈浩宇是认真的，因而对眼前的美女并未产生任何想法。
[EN] Recognition snapped—she blurted before he finished, “You’re Ye Mengde?!”
[ZH] 只见娘子面露笑容，站起来，上前拉住那女子的手，高兴地说：“你怎么才来？我们许久未见，赶紧坐下聊聊。”
[EN] “Indeed I am.”
[ZH] 想绒被幽栖居士拉到桌前坐下，她早已注意到陈浩宇，只是从来未曾见过，坐下后小心问：“这位是？”
[EN] She knew several of Ye Mengde’s lyrics—by the calendar, he’d be in his forties now, serving as a court official! “Wait—as an imperial official, shouldn’t you be arriving with Prince Yi and the Prefect?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇主动介绍：“她是我娘子，我是她夫君。”
[EN] “Traveled from Yingchang to Huzhou for the completion of my retreat in Shilin Valley, then swung by Lin’an to see old friends,” Ye Mengde laughed. “Neither Prince Yi nor the Prefect knows I’m here—much freer this way!”
[ZH] “原来是陈家二公子，幸会幸会！”
[EN] Doing quick math, she curtsied low. “Prefect Ye’s carefree spirit fills Shuzhen with admiration.” Chen Haoyu hopped up to bow too. “Lord Prefect!”
[ZH] “我有这么出名吗？”
[EN] Ye Mengde waved it off with a laugh. “No prefects today—only fellow poets making friends through verse!”
[ZH] “幽栖居士美名在外，她嫁给谁大家自然会关心。”
[EN] Everyone chuckled warm and easy before Ye Mengde excused himself to greet other friends.
[ZH] “人太出名未必是件好事，私事都会被人拿来议论，要我看还是默默无闻得好。”千里寻不由自主慨叹道，接着向陈浩宇介绍，“她是我的好朋友想绒姑娘，弹琴唱曲儿可是一绝，待会儿一定会惊艳到你。”
[EN] Sitting back down, Chen Haoyu asked, “Big celebrity?”
[ZH] 刚才东方尘泽提到想绒的名字时，陈浩宇便已猜到是花云楼的姑娘，毕竟想绒姑娘的名字他听说过不知多少回，只是很少去花云楼而已。
[EN] “Huge—his name goes straight into the history books.”
[ZH] 只因花云楼的姑娘一点儿都不低俗，对于以前的陈浩宇来讲实在无趣。今日一见果真和春香楼的姑娘不同，全身上下透出与大家闺秀不相上下的气质，难怪娘子愿意与她成为朋友。
[EN] “What about you?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇想高雅一回，毕竟是女词人的夫君，不能给娘子丢脸：“李太白有句诗：云想衣裳花想容。想绒姑娘的名字该不会出自这句诗？”
[EN] “Youqi Jushi goes into history too, of course.”
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“真难为他，还能想出李白的诗句来。”
[EN] “Think folks in future centuries will know my name?”
[ZH] 想绒俏皮地向幽栖居士挤挤眼睛，回道：“的确出自这句诗，只是绒字与诗句不同，是绒花的绒。”
[EN] “Why ask that?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇又想出一句有水准的话来：“‘花想容’改为绒花的绒，更是妙不可言。”
[EN] “Because I’m your husband!”
[ZH] 千里寻故意批评道：“想绒是我朋友，跟我还没聊上几句，你的话倒不少。”
[EN] Caught off guard, she offered a vague smile. “Maybe.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇担心娘子误解，凑到她耳边：“我不过是想在你的朋友面前多表现表现，让她觉得我能配得上你。”
[EN] Wave after wave of scholars stopped by next, Chen Haoyu bowing beside his wife, swelling with pride at her star power and scheming how to slip “husband of Youqi Jushi” into every hello.
[ZH] 千里寻也凑到陈浩宇耳边：“不必卖力表现，你是什么人，全临安城的人都知道。”
[EN] Right then a vision of grace strolled up. In his old life, Chen Haoyu would have ogled and flirted nonstop; bound by his two-year vow, he didn’t feel a flicker of stray thought.
[ZH] 陈浩宇不再言语，免得娘子误会他见到美女才变成话痨。
[EN] His wife beamed and jumped up to clasp the newcomer’s hands. “What took you so long? Sit—let’s catch up!”
[ZH] 想绒看着两人，用丝帕捂着嘴笑了一会儿。
[EN] Taking a seat, Xiangrong glanced curious at Chen Haoyu. “And this is...?”
[ZH] 千里寻问：“为何笑？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu chimed in proud, “She’s my wife, and I’m her husband!”
[ZH] 想绒挪开捂着嘴的丝帕：“坊间传言幽栖居士是母老虎，陈公子怕得很，被逼无奈才转变性情。依我看传言一点儿都不准，你们两个甚是融洽，应是妇唱夫随。”
[EN] “Second Young Master Chen! A pleasure!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇体会着“妇唱夫随”，甚觉惬意。
[EN] “I’m that famous?”
[ZH] 只有千里寻清楚她一路走来的无奈，可无论如何，她已心甘情愿与陈浩宇继续走下去。
[EN] “With Youqi Jushi’s fame, everyone naturally follows who she marries.”
[ZH] 还没与想绒聊上几句，朱晞颜带领临安的官员们簇拥着仪王走进来，梦容跟在仪王身边，脸上带着贵气而又满足的笑容。
[EN] “Too much fame brings nothing but gossip—better staying low-key,” Qianli Xun sighed, then introduced her. “My close friend Miss Xiangrong—unmatched on the zither and singing. Wait till she blows you away later!”
[ZH] 所有参加诗会的人起身相迎，千里寻也跟着站起来。
[EN] Recognizing her name from Huayun Pavilion—a refined venue he’d found too tame back in his rowdy Chunxiang Brothel days—Chen Haoyu admired her ladylike poise and decided to flex a little literary polish for his wife’s sake. “Li Bai wrote: ‘Her face is seen in flower and her dress in cloud.’ [TN: Translated by Xu Yuanchong] Miss Xiangrong’s name must come from ‘hua xiang rong’ (花想容, ‘flowers recall her face’), right?”
[ZH] 大家异口同声：“恭迎仪王、仪王妃大驾光临！”
[EN] *Look at him pulling Li Bai out of thin air!* Qianli Xun marveled inside.
[ZH] 仪王站定，向在场所有人说：“诸位请落座！不必拘束！”
[EN] Xiangrong winked slyly at Qianli Xun. “It does come from that line, except my ‘rong’ is written with the character for floss-silk flowers (绒), not appearance (容).”
[ZH] 众人齐声说：“多谢仪王、仪王妃！”
[EN] “Swapping appearance for silk-flower ‘rong’—even cleverer!” Chen Haoyu praised.
[ZH] 待众人落座后，仪王、仪王妃也与众位官员坐在中间准备好的位置。
[EN] Qianli Xun shot him a mock glare. “She’s my friend—I barely got two words in and you won’t stop talking!”
[ZH] 朱晞颜并未落座，他要借此诗会说两句。
[EN] Leaning close to her ear, he whispered, “Just trying to look sharp in front of your friend so she thinks I’m worthy of you!”
[ZH] “诸位！感谢大家光临诗会，仪王为东方诗会倾注不少心力，东方公子也为诗会忙前忙后，按说没我什么事。可因仪王殿下心忧百姓，容我在诗会开始前说两句。”
[EN] She whispered right back, “Save the act—all of Lin’an knows what you’re like.”
[ZH] 朱晞颜将在场众人扫视一遍：“大家的桌上摆着一道菜，请大家尝尝，感受一下味道如何？”
[EN] Shutting his mouth at once lest she think he was sweet on a pretty girl, Chen Haoyu sat quiet while Xiangrong hid a giggle behind her silk handkerchief.
[ZH] 众人拿起筷子品尝起来。
[EN] “What’s funny?” Qianli Xun asked.
[ZH] 想绒尝完赞道：“清淡爽口，是开胃菜中的佳品。”
[EN] Lowering her handkerchief, Xiangrong smiled. “Street gossip claims Youqi Jushi is a fierce tigress who scared Young Master Chen straight. Nonsense—you two are in total harmony, the wife leading and the husband right behind!”
[ZH] 千里寻也点头认可。
[EN] Savoring that phrase, Chen Haoyu glowed, while Qianli Xun felt the quiet truth beneath: hard as the road had been, she was willing to walk it with him.
[ZH] 众人也纷纷赞不绝口。
[EN] Before they could chat more, Zhu Xiyan led Lin’an’s officials into the hall surrounding Prince Yi, Qiao Mengrong walking at his side wearing a proud, regal smile.
[ZH] 朱晞颜见状，继续说：“大家桌上的菜是从懒人村运送而来，再由聚香楼的厨师烹制而成。为了懒人村和临安的流浪百姓能安居乐业，本官在仪王的督促下决定将这道菜作为临安大大小小酒楼饭庄必备的一道菜肴。现下苦于没有一个好听又响亮的名字，便想借此次诗会拜托诸位帮忙想一想。本官就说这么多，接下来交给东方公子。”
[EN] Every guest rose in unison. “Greetings, Your Highness Prince Yi and Princess Consort Yi!”
[ZH] 东方尘泽走到台上，先向仪王和仪王妃以及各位官员行礼，接着又向所有参加诗会的人行礼，今日完全可以看作是他人生的高光时刻。
[EN] Prince Yi raised a hand. “Please be seated—no need for formality.”
[ZH] “诸位，今日诗会的荐诗环节与以往有所不同。以往大家推荐的均是身边之人写的新诗新词，而今日要求只推荐前人所写的诗词，可以是写野菜的诗词，也可是其他，但必须从诗词中提炼出菜名。最终选择哪个，由仪王定夺。”
[EN] Once the royals and officials took the center seats, Zhu Xiyan remained standing. “Gentlemen, thank you for coming! Prince Yi poured heart and silver into the Dongfang Salon, and Young Master Dongfang worked tirelessly—normally I’d step aside, yet for the people’s livelihood, allow me one brief word.” Sweeping the room, he pointed to the tables. “Notice the green dish before you? Please taste it.”
[ZH] 千里寻赞道：“这个环节既新奇又有趣。”
[EN] Chopsticks rose across the hall. “Crisp and refreshing—a superb appetizer!” Xiangrong praised, Qianli Xun nodding along as murmurs of praise rippled around.
[ZH] 她说完，无意中接触到梦容投来的眼神，像是炫耀，更像是警示。
[EN] “These wild greens come straight from Lazybones Village, cooked by Juxiang Tower’s chefs,” Zhu Xiyan announced. “At Prince Yi’s urging to give Lazybones Village and Lin’an’s vagrants an honest trade, I’ve decreed every tavern and inn in Lin’an serve this dish—only it needs a classy, memorable name! I’m imposing on your poetic genius today. Over to you, Young Master Dongfang!”
[ZH] 千里寻以微笑点头回礼。
[EN] Stepping onstage for the shining hour of his life, Dongfang Chenze bowed to the royals, officials, and crowd. “Today’s opening round has a twist! Instead of recommending new verse by peers, nominate classical poems—about wild greens or anything fitting—and draw a dish name from the lines! Prince Yi will choose the winner!”
[ZH] 乔梦容则露出冷漠的表情，转过头不再瞧她。
[EN] “Fresh and clever!” Qianli Xun praised—and caught Qiao Mengrong’s stare from across the floor, half boast, half warning. Qianli Xun returned a polite nod and smile; Qiao Mengrong froze her face and turned away.
[ZH] 陈浩宇一直在人群中搜寻乔文仙的身影，毕竟他目睹过乔文仙与娘子甜蜜的场面，想着他若出现自己该如何在他面前表现得与娘子甜甜蜜蜜。
[EN] Beside her, Chen Haoyu scanned the sea of scholars hunting for Qiao Wenxian—ready to put on a dazzling display of marital bliss the instant he spotted him.

#CH 296
[ZH] 终于在众位才子中发现乔文仙，他正向这边瞧来。
[ZH] 陈浩宇觉得乔文仙之所以没有主动前来打招呼，一定是因为他跟着娘子。
[ZH] 看来他跟随娘子前来实在必要，试想他若没来，乔文仙肯定早已过来打招呼，说不定还会偷偷给娘子抛媚眼。
[ZH] 为了检验乔文仙是否正在看娘子，陈浩宇倒一杯酒酿举起来。
[ZH] 乔文仙根本没注意到，由此断定他正在瞧他的娘子。陈浩宇露出不满的表情，端起酒酿自顾喝了。
[ZH] 有位才子站起来：“诸位！我推荐《诗经•卫风》中‘伯兮’一诗：其雨其雨，杲杲出日。愿言思伯，甘心首疾。焉得谖草，言树之背。愿言思伯，使我心痗。谖草，便是一种野菜，又名忘忧草，不如就给这道菜起名忘忧草，寓意吃过这道菜的人都可忘记忧虑。”
[ZH] 众人都无不称赞。
[ZH] 又有一人站起来：“鄙人推荐唐代邢群的诗《郡中有怀寄上睦州员外杜十三兄》，其中有两句：经冬野菜青青色，未腊山梅处处花。不如取名‘青青色’。”
[ZH] 接着又有几人推荐了各自想到的诗词。
[ZH] 乔文仙站起来：“鄙人推荐唐代诗人李群玉的《沅江渔者》：倚棹汀洲沙日晚，江鲜野菜桃花饭。长歌一曲烟霭深，归去沧江绿波远。我取其中的‘江鲜’与‘绿波’，给这道菜取名‘江鲜绿波’。”
[ZH] 众人也都无不称赞。
[ZH] 叶梦得站起来：“仪王、仪王妃下官有礼，请原谅下官此时才有机会拜见二位贵人。”
[ZH] 申简辰与乔梦容都不认得眼前这位。
[ZH] 朱晞颜起身介绍道：“叶梦得，现任颍昌知州。”
[ZH] 申简辰恍然大悟，一位名载史册之人，尽管脑中想不起他写过哪些诗词文章，可大名如雷贯耳。于是客套道：“原来是叶知州，不必拘礼。”
[ZH] 朱晞颜故意批评：“叶知州既来临安，应该亲自拜见仪王与仪王妃，然后到我府上坐坐，也好让我尽地主之谊，你却一个人躲到诗会，给大家来个出其不意，该罚！”
[ZH] 叶梦得巧妙回道：“我离开颍州是为私事，跑到这里更是为私事，怎敢送上门让贵人和知府大人知道我擅离职守？只能躲得越远越好。怎奈东方诗会因仪王名声远播，将我吸引而来，本想躲在人群里体会体会诗会的盛况，再目睹仪王的风采，怎奈又被仪王和知府大人的善举所感动，再也躲不下去，只好浮出水面。”
[ZH] 叶梦得一番话说得众人都笑起来，朱晞颜指着他：“你这张嘴，让人又爱又恨！”
[ZH] 申简辰倒是欣赏起叶梦得来，他并非趋炎附势之辈，来临安没着急巴结贵人，而是隐身在才子中。想暴露时，又能以极高的情商给自己和他人一个台阶下。
[ZH] “叶知州不如也试着推荐一首诗，给这道菜取个雅致的名字。”
[ZH] “下官谨遵仪王吩咐。”叶梦得站出来本就为此事，他早已想好，“我不荐他人之诗，推荐自己的，诗名有些长，不说也罢，其中有：残春扫馀花，密叶未有蝉。高台略四远，绿野浮芊眠。下官觉得‘绿野浮芊’四字与这道菜甚是相合。”
[ZH] 曹班说道：“叶知州的诗我知道，诗名的确长，在下不才，记性却好，给大家说上一说，此诗名为‘又明日复同惇立总领吴德素运使章思台过天禧寺登雨花台再用前韵 ’，怎么样？是不是有史以来最长的诗名？”
[ZH] 众人都无不认可。
[ZH] 申简辰虽不觉得“绿野浮芊”最合适，可他欣赏叶梦得不趋炎附势的品性，于是说道：“诸位，‘绿野浮芊’四字最佳，就以此作为这道菜的名字。”
[ZH] 仪王已发话，众人不好再说什么。
[ZH] 叶梦得免不了一番感谢：“感谢仪王垂爱。”
[ZH] 朱晞颜又起身说道：“往后希望各位大加宣扬，多多去酒楼饭庄捧场，也要吩咐家中掌管后厨采买之人到菜市场多多买回懒人村种植的野菜，让这道绿野浮芊成为家中常备的一道开胃菜。话不多说，诗会继续！”
[ZH] 东方尘泽继续走上台：“接下来进入旧诗品鉴环节！”
[ZH] 化雨也走上台，将一个大卷轴挂好，原来是苏轼的《定风波·莫听穿林打叶声》。
[ZH] 众人开始认真品鉴。
[ZH] 乔梦容对诗词品鉴并不感兴趣，她时刻关注哥哥，发现他自从进入，还未曾向朱淑真投去过任何目光，她十分满意。既然来了，依旧需警告她两句。
[ZH] 乔梦容从主位起身，直奔幽栖居士。
[ZH] 申简辰以为梦容不过是上前与昔日的闺中密友打招呼而已，并未用心关注。
[ZH] 千里寻瞧见梦容向她走来，心想：“她不乖乖待在仪王身边，跑来找我是何意？自从仪王入场，我都没敢正眼瞧过，更别说逾越之举。”
[EN] At last Chen Haoyu spotted Qiao Wenxian among the scholars—gazing straight toward their table.
[ZH] 乔梦容走近后，三人都站起来行礼，苦荞站在小姐身后也跟着行礼。
[EN] *Bet he hasn’t walked over only because I’m sitting here!* Smug he’d tagged along, Chen Haoyu raised a cup of rice wine to test him. Qiao Wenxian didn’t even blink—eyes locked dead on his wife! Scowling, Chen Haoyu tossed the drink back himself.
[ZH] 乔梦容看向陈浩宇：“陈公子不懂诗词今日也跑来，难道不放心自己的夫人？也是，诗会中才子众多，的确让人不放心。好心提醒陈公子，平日把你的夫人看紧了，她那双眼睛随时会勾引男子。”
[EN] One scholar stood first. “Gentlemen, I nominate ‘Bo Xi’ from the Odes of Wei in the Book of Songs: ‘O for rain! O for rain! But brightly the sun comes forth. Longingly I think of my husband, till my heart is weary, and my head aches. How shall I get the plant of forgetfulness? I would plant it on the north of my house. Longingly I think of my husband, and my heart is made to ache.’ [TN: Translated by James Legge] That plant of forgetfulness—daylily shoots—is a wild green! Why not name the dish ‘Forget-Sorrow Herb,’ wishing every diner forgets their troubles?”
[ZH] 对方的话语明显不怀好意，又是公众场合，又是王妃，陈浩宇不好发作，只能回道：“小人对娘子十分放心，今日有幸前来参加诗会，都是沾了娘子的光，是她主动邀请我来参加。”
[EN] Murmurs of approval rose.
[ZH] 乔梦容懒得再说别的：“麻烦陈公子与这位姑娘到旁边坐会儿，我与幽栖居士说几句体己话。”
[EN] Another scholar rose. “I nominate Tang poet Xing Qun’s couplet: ‘Wild greens through winter keep their verdant green; before twelfth-month cold, mountain plums bloom everywhere.’ [TN: Translator’s rendering] Let’s call it ‘Verdant Green’!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇不放心，犹豫要不要离开。
[EN] Several more followed before Qiao Wenxian stood. “I nominate Tang poet Li Qunyu’s ‘Fisherman on the Yuan River’: ‘Leaning on my oar by the sandy islet as evening falls: river delicacies, wild greens, and peach-blossom rice. One long song into the deepening mist, then homeward across the far green waves of the blue river.’ [TN: Translator’s rendering] Blending two phrases, I propose ‘River-Fresh Green Waves’!”
[ZH] 千里寻小声说：“不会有事。”然后示意他与想绒姑娘到旁边先挤挤。
[EN] Warm applause met his suggestion.
[ZH] 正好一旁有人暂时离开，陈浩宇与想绒坐过去。他的目光一直未曾离开过娘子，诗会中文人才子鉴赏旧作的说辞他是一句未听进去。
[EN] Then Ye Mengde stood and bowed. “Greetings, Your Highness Prince Yi and Princess Consort Yi—forgive me for paying respects only now.”
[ZH] 千里寻旁若无人地坐下：“仪王妃若想来找茬，我奉劝你还是理智些，别在诗会中丢了身份。”
[EN] Neither Shen Jianchen nor Qiao Mengrong recognized him until Zhu Xiyan rose to introduce him. “Ye Mengde, Prefect of Yingchang.”
[ZH] 乔梦容冷笑着也坐下：“不必你提醒，我自有分寸。我来不过是想告诉你，本来今日诗会并未邀请我，哥哥主动要带我前来，你知道这是为何？”
[EN] Recognition hit Shen Jianchen—a giant of Song letters! “No formality needed, Prefect Ye.”
[ZH] “我对贵人的私事不感兴趣。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan teased him half-serious, “Sneaking into Lin’an without paying respects to Prince Yi or letting me play host, hiding in a poetry salon to ambush us—you owe a forfeit!”
[ZH] 千里寻打算去听他人鉴赏佳作之语，可还是清清楚楚听到梦容的声音。
[EN] Ye Mengde parried smooth as silk. “Left Yingchang on private errands and came here on private errands—how dare I march up and advertise playing hooky from my post? Better lie low! Only Prince Yi’s fame drew me into this salon hoping to blend into the crowd, until your noble relief project moved me so deep I couldn’t stay hidden anymore!”
[ZH] “你感不感兴趣不重要，重要的是我想对你说。哥哥自从与我圆房后他的心里只有我，再也容不下任何人，还说去哪里都要带上我。”
[EN] Laughter swept the hall. Zhu Xiyan pointed at him. “That silver tongue of yours makes a man love and hate you at once!”
[ZH] 千里寻感动悲哀，不为自己，只为梦容。她明明可以采取更正确的方式维系她的感情，却偏偏跑来故意炫耀，殊不知炫耀的行为更显出她的可悲，看来人的可悲与身份地位的高低没有关系。
[EN] Impressed by a man who didn’t fawn over rank and kept his dignity sharp with wit, Shen Jianchen invited, “Why not propose a poem and dish name too, Prefect Ye?”
[ZH] “我想起一个故事，不妨与仪王妃讲讲。有一个小女孩，到处向其他小朋友炫耀她有娘亲，其他小朋友嘲笑她，小孩子有娘亲不是一件很正常的事吗？为何要拿出来炫耀？后来才知道小女孩有一个后娘，对她并不好。由此可见，一个人越喜欢拿出来炫耀的往往是她最需要而又缺乏的。不知仪王妃可明白？”
[EN] “Gladly, Your Highness—I’ll nominate a poem of my own! The title’s too long to bother reciting, yet four lines read: ‘Late spring sweeps away the lingering blossoms; thick leaves hold no cicadas yet. From the high terrace the gaze sweeps all four quarters, where green plains float in lush expanse.’ [TN: Translator’s rendering] How about ‘Lush Floating Greens’?”
[ZH] 被对方看穿，乔梦容本想恼羞成怒，可在公众场合，还有这么多官员，她不好发作。
[EN] Cao Ban jumped in grinning. “I know that poem! Longest title in history—let me show off my memory: ‘The Day After Tomorrow, Returning Once More with General Manager Dunli, Transport Commissioner Wu Desu, and Zhang Sitai Past Tianxi Temple to Ascend Yuhua Terrace, Using the Former Rhymes Again’!”
[ZH] “小女孩的后娘一定还有孩子，才无法全心全意待她，要我说她应该想方设法将后娘的其他孩子除掉，让她成为唯一的孩子，才有可能获得后娘的母爱。”
[EN] Laughter and nods confirmed the record.
[ZH] “小女孩很善良，她不会这么做。况且后娘知道是她杀死自己的孩子，又岂会真心待她？”
[EN] Even if the name wasn’t his literal favorite, Shen Jianchen admired Ye Mengde’s backbone and called it. “‘Lush Floating Greens’ wins! That’s our dish name.”
[ZH] “善良？人不能太善良，善良的人只会被人欺负，无论身份地位如何，善良都会被人践踏，哪怕是闺中密友。”
[EN] Ye Mengde bowed his thanks, and Zhu Xiyan urged everyone to promote “Lush Floating Greens” across taverns and household kitchens before handing the stage back to Dongfang Chenze.
[ZH] 梦容的声音不大却十分有力，千里寻只觉心痛。
[EN] “Next up: Classic Appreciation!” Dongfang Chenze announced as Huayu unfurled a massive scroll of Su Shi’s lyric “Dingfengbo: Pay No Heed to the Rain Beating Through the Forest Leaves.”
[ZH] “如若我是小女孩，我不会这么做，我会用理智想方设法处理好家中复杂的关系，也许会有好的结果。梦容，请把心思多放在你和仪王的生活上，别再理会我。你瞧，我夫君就在那边坐着，他很爱我，他为我而改变，天下有几个女子能像我这般幸运？我很珍惜。”
[EN] While scholars dove into spirited critique, Qiao Mengrong cared nothing for poetry—watching her brother like a hawk, pleased he hadn’t shot a single glance toward Zhu Shuzhen. Still, she wasn’t leaving without issuing a warning. Rising from the head table, she strode straight toward Youqi Jushi.
[ZH] 这番话令乔梦容动情：“你果真如此想？可你根本不爱陈浩宇？”
[EN] Assuming she was just saying hello to her old friend, Shen Jianchen paid little mind.
[ZH] 千里寻试着要求道：“把手伸过来。”
[EN] *Not a single glance at Prince Yi all morning—what’s she marching over here for?* Qianli Xun wondered, rising with Chen Haoyu, Xiangrong, and Kuqiao to bow.
[ZH] 乔梦容犹豫一会儿将手伸到桌面，千里寻将自己的手握上去。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong aimed a barb straight at Chen Haoyu. “No head for poetry, yet Young Master Chen tagged along—don’t trust your wife? Fair enough, surrounded by handsome scholars! Friendly tip: keep a tight leash on her; those eyes hook men on sight.”
[ZH] 这一刻，乔梦容流下眼泪，淑真的手还像以前一样温暖。
[EN] Swallowing his anger in public before royalty, Chen Haoyu replied stiff, “I trust my wife completely. She invited me along herself.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇终于松口气，两人没事便好。
[EN] “Mind stepping aside with this lady a minute?” Qiao Mengrong dismissed him. “I’d like a private word with Youqi Jushi.”
[ZH] 申简辰也看到，以为二人已和好。
[EN] Hesitating, Chen Haoyu caught his wife’s quiet murmur—“I’ll be fine”—and squeezed over to a nearby vacated seat with Xiangrong, eyes glued to his wife the whole time.
[ZH] 自打仪王妃走向女儿，朱晞颜开始担心，看到她们握在一起的手，才安心听诸位谈论东坡居士的诗词。
[EN] Sitting down unruffled, Qianli Xun spoke flat. “If you came looking for a fight, Princess Consort Yi, keep your cool—don’t embarrass your rank in public.”
[ZH] 苦荞一直站在旁边，她们说的每句话都听得一清二楚，她在心中祈祷小姐与仪王妃从今日起关系恢复正常。
[EN] Smirking as she took the chair across, Qiao Mengrong shot back, “Save your lectures. Just wanted you to know: I wasn’t even invited today—Brother insisted on bringing me. Know why?”
[ZH] 千里寻握着梦容的手，这一刻她自信二人可以恢复到从前。
[EN] “Royals’ private lives don’t interest me.” Qianli Xun turned her ear toward the scholars, yet Qiao Mengrong’s voice cut straight through.
[ZH] “世上最厉害的是时间，它可以改变一切，以前我不爱陈浩宇，可现在我与以前不同，往后会更不相同。我已打定主意跟陈浩宇白头偕老，也希望你跟仪王琴瑟和鸣。”
[EN] “Interest you or not, hear this: since consummating our marriage, Brother’s heart belongs to me alone, with zero room for anyone else—even insists on taking me everywhere he goes!”
[ZH] 淑真一脸真诚，乔梦容努力让自己相信：“好，我信你一回。”
[EN] Sadness welled in Qianli Xun—not for herself, for Mengrong. Boasting like this only laid bare how terrified she was inside; high rank couldn’t cure an empty heart.
[ZH] 乔梦容抽出手放心离开，继续回仪王身边坐好。
[EN] “Reminds me of a story,” Qianli Xun said softly. “A little girl bragged nonstop to other kids that she had a mother. The other kids laughed—every kid has a mother, why brag? Turns out she had a stepmother who treated her rotten. People always flaunt loudest what they’re starving for most. Follow me, Princess Consort Yi?”
[ZH] 申简辰不敢随便发问，他担心一旦过问，会引起梦容不必要的猜测。
[EN] Stung raw, unable to explode in front of Lin’an’s officials, Qiao Mengrong hissed, “Then that stepmother must’ve had other children stealing her love! If I were that girl, I’d wipe out every other child until I was the only one left—then the mother’s love would all be mine!”
[ZH] 乔梦容刚离开，乔文仙走过来坐在桌旁：“淑真，好久不见。”
[EN] “She was a good kid—she wouldn’t do that. Besides, once a mother knows who killed her children, could she ever love the killer?”
[ZH] 还没等千里寻回答，陈浩宇迅速回到桌旁：“乔文仙，你不去品东坡居士的词，跑到这里来做什么？”
[EN] “Good?! Being good just gets you stepped on, no matter your rank—even by your own best friend!”
[ZH] “刚刚品鉴完，过来与淑真喝两杯，你有什么好介意的？”乔文仙说着给淑真的杯中填满酒酿。
[EN] Quiet words, yet heavy as stones—making Qianli Xun’s chest ache. “If I were that girl, I’d use my head to heal the family instead of burning it down. Mengrong, put your energy into your life with Prince Yi and leave me be. Look over there—my husband loves me and changed his whole life for me. How many women get that lucky? I treasure what I have.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇不满道：“淑真也是你叫的？小心你的一妻一妾不高兴，到时找我娘子麻烦。”
